    - [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]

    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Turtles All the Way Down (John Green novel)** (2×, _literary_) — Green's novel about grief and mental illness is invoked as another resonance of the 'turtles' phrase, connecting the cosmic question to lived emotional pain.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **3am nights with kids and 10pm crying phone calls** (2×, _personal_) — Everyday moments of joy, grief, and friendship are cited to show that human experience matters beyond mere chemistry.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Mother Teresa 'Do It Anyway'** (2×, _literary_) — The 'Anyway' poem illustrates faithful, unrewarded goodness done for God rather than for human recognition.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/miriam-miriam|Miriam, Miriam]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Moral hazard and the 2008 crisis** (2×, _current-events_) — Forgiving debts risks moral hazard, the reckless reliance on bailouts seen in 2008, yet that is exactly what God's forgiveness invites.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-race|The Race]]
- **Balaam and his donkey** (2×, _biblical_) — In Numbers, the wise man Balaam fails to see God's angel but his donkey does, paralleling Herod who cannot see the star the Magi follow.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Jesus weeping over Lazarus** (2×, _biblical_) — Jesus grieving Lazarus moments before raising him shows that we grieve because we love.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Formula 1 and Drive to Survive** (2×, _pop-culture_) — Getting hooked on F1 via the Netflix series sets up the points system and unjust last-race endings as a picture of life that seems unfair.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **John Wesley's Aldersgate conversion** (2×, _historical_) — Wesley's strict early moralism, failed Georgia mission, and his heart 'strangely warmed' at Aldersgate model the shift from faith-as-duty to faith-as-grace.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Bob Dylan 'Gotta Serve Somebody'** (2×, _pop-culture_) — Dylan's line that you've got to serve somebody—the devil or the Lord—illustrates that there are no neutral positions in discipleship.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-communion-of-the-saints|I Believe in the Communion of the Saints]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/come-to-me-all-you-who-are-weary|Come to Me, All You Who Are Weary]]
- **Party that never gets old** (2×, _other_) — The new creation is the best party imaginable that never gets old, tiring, or boring, illustrating embodied eternal joy.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting-2|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Teaching kids to say thank you** (2×, _personal_) — His children practicing manners and gratitude at Christmas models responding to the gift rather than the object.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **John Milton, Upon the Circumcision of the Lord** (2×, _literary_) — Milton's poem frames Christ emptying his glory to satisfy the covenant we transgress.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **John Chrysostom's Christmas sermon** (2×, _historical_) — An early preacher's astonished sermon on the Ancient of Days becoming an infant lifts up the wonder of the Nativity.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Simeon's song (Nunc Dimittis)** (2×, _biblical_) — Simeon's spontaneous song, sung nightly worldwide since at least 516 AD, models worship as thanks.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Anna the prophetess** (2×, _biblical_) — Anna, who prayed in the temple for years, becomes the first evangelist sharing the redemption found in Jesus.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Bob Dylan, 'Gotta Serve Somebody'** (2×, _pop-culture_) — Dylan's line that 'you gotta serve somebody' illustrates that no choice is itself a choice about following God.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **George Mallory and Everest ("because it's there")** (2×, _historical_) — Mallory's deadly drive to climb Everest illustrates the human pursuit of the impossible that finds its true answer at the Lord's table.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **The Ship of Theseus (Plutarch)** (2×, _literary_) — Plutarch's puzzle of the rebuilt ship dramatizes the substance-versus-accident question underlying transubstantiation.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]

    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/who-is-christ|Who is Christ?]]
- **Superhero / Iron Man suit of grace** (2×, _pop-culture_) — God does not zap you with 'superhero juice' to do whatever you want; grace walks with you rather than leaving you where you are.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Paul Simon, '50 Ways to Leave Your Lover'** (2×, _pop-culture_) — The 1975 song's litany of escape routes frames the sermon's 12 ways we 'leave our lover'-the God who loves us-by avoiding resurrection.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Joseph Ratzinger quotation** (2×, _literary_) — Quoted passage that the Christian faith stands or falls on Christ being risen, else Jesus is merely a failed religious leader.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **John Updike, 'Seven Stanzas at Easter'** (2×, _literary_) — Poem insisting on the literal bodily rising-'let us not mock God with metaphor'-against spiritualizing the resurrection.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **John Chrysostom Paschal homily** (2×, _historical_) — The 1600-year-old 'Christ is Risen' acclamation proclaiming death annihilated and life liberated.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Instagram-worthy Sunday meals** (2×, _current-events_) — Contrasts treating resurrection as a nice idea shared between curated brunches versus all cosmic reality being overturned.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Hell Swallowing the Body of Christ** (2×, _literary_) — Hell takes a body and discovers God, takes earth and encounters Heaven, depicting death undone by the one it could not see.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-s-paschal-homily|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom's Paschal Homily)]]
- **Lines we draw around our behavior** (2×, _personal_) — We all have things we will and won't do; faith in Christ dissolves those self-drawn limits.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Learning to ride a bicycle** (2×, _personal_) — You can't learn from a book and must fall a few times, illustrating that prayer is an uncomfortable practiced habit.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Adrian Newey's How to Build a Car** (2×, _literary_) — The Red Bull designer's book and his four chassis goals (stable, light, low-drag, balanced downforce) model what a strong church foundation must be.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **The Church's One Foundation hymn** (2×, _literary_) — The hymn naming Jesus Christ as the church's one foundation is affirmed as true, with Jesus's parable adding another way to see that foundation.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
    - A Foundation of Water
- **Parable of the Sower** (2×, _biblical_) — Jesus' parable of seed on path, rock, thorns, and good soil illustrates the cost of being tilled by God so we can bear fruit.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **The world's media versus the narrow way** (2×, _other_) — Modern media saying 'be happy, take it easy, follow the broad way' contrasted with Christ's call to the narrow way of struggle.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **The Rescue / Thai Cave Disaster** (2×, _current-events_) — The 2018 Thai cave rescue documentary clip (boys trapped, divers searching) frames salvation as being trapped and lost and needing help to be free.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **William Temple on original sin as empirically proven** (2×, _historical_) — The wartime Archbishop of Canterbury's line that original sin is the only empirically proven doctrine, seen in hearts and institutions alike.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **John Milton's Paradise Lost** (2×, _literary_) — Milton's Satan looks mighty at first but devolves into nothingness, and Jesus in Book 6 puts forth only half his strength, showing the heavenly battle is no fair fight.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **Azusa Street revival out of Methodism** (2×, _historical_) — The 1906 birth of Pentecostalism grew out of holiness traditions and Methodism, proving revival requires continuity, not total rupture.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Rich young man told to sell everything** (2×, _biblical_) — Jesus' undetailed command to "sell everything" shows how his teachings usually lack specifics, making the very precise colt instructions strikingly odd.
    - Palm Sunday: From Hosanna to Passion
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Herbert McCabe on Easter as meaning of the cross** (2×, _literary_) — The quoted theological passages (Easter as the meaning of the cross, resurrection as the seal on incarnation) ground the claim that we must go through Good Friday to reach Easter joy.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Mandatum word origin of Maundy** (2×, _literary_) — The word 'Maundy' from the Latin mandatum (Greek entole) explains why this day is named for Christ's new commandment to love.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Augustine's Restless Hearts** (2×, _literary_) — Augustine's 'our hearts are restless until they rest in you' names humanity's search for meaning.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **Augustine on holy desire** (2×, _literary_) — Quoted line that the Christian life is an exercise of holy desire, preparing us to see and be satisfied when Christ comes.
    - [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Estalena and Donald came from nothing** (2×, _personal_) — Donald an orphan raised in an orphanage and Estalena born at the height of the Depression — both rose from poverty.
    - Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
    - A Service of Death and Resurrection for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Raising chickens and vegetables in Jolleyville** (2×, _personal_) — Childhood self-sufficiency, growing food with family after moving to Jolleyville.
    - Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
    - A Service of Death and Resurrection for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Joseph Sold into Slavery** (2×, _biblical_) — Joseph keeps faith and thrives rather than complaining after his brothers sell him into slavery, illustrating childlike humility.
    - [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Parable of the houses on rock and sand** (2×, _biblical_) — Hearing and doing the word builds on rock that withstands wind and water; hearing without doing builds on sand that is swept away, illustrating that loving Jesus means keeping his commandments.
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
    - A Foundation of Water
- **Prevent Defense in Football** (2×, _pop-culture_) — Teams late in a game play a fearful prevent defense focused on tomorrow's outcome, like Christians who mitigate injustice instead of boldly confronting it in the present.
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Gideon and the Day of Midian** (2×, _biblical_) — Gideon's 300 men attacking the Midianites by faith, not running a prevent defense, models bold faithful action that breaks the oppressor's rod.
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Christ Fills Your Bucket List** (2×, _other_) — Christ has already filled your bucket list, so all life's small joys are a bonus next to the joy of being chosen and loved by God.
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Moses and the Burning Bush** (2×, _biblical_) — Standing on holy ground, told to remove his shoes, exemplifies encountering the presence of God beyond ourselves.
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/in-the-presence-of-god|In the Presence of God]]
    - [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]

## All illustrations (A–Z)
- **"The Church of Christ in Every Age" hymn** _(literary)_ — Hymn quoted (the church Spirit-led amid change, the servant church as partner in Christ's sacrifice) to ground the call to be Christ's body without bearing the burden of saving anyone.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **"Thousands of future Hitlers" thought experiment** _(other)_ — Tests whether imagining the slain children as future monsters makes their killing less horrendous  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **'Do I have to or I'll go to hell?'** _(other)_ — Reducing faith to bare necessity ('will I go to hell?') misses Jesus, who says not 'do this or else' but 'do this in remembrance of me.'  → [[covenant-umc/2024/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **'Give Me Jesus' spiritual** _(literary)_ — The old spiritual frames starting each morning by choosing Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]], [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **'Hell' from Norse mythology vs Gehenna/Hades/Tartarus** _(historical)_ — the English 'hell' imposes a structured system the Bible's varied terms don't support  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **'If I don't do this, will I go to hell?'** _(personal)_ — Reducing faith to bare necessity ('not for me unless required') misses Jesus' invitation to do this in remembrance, not under threat.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **'If you cannot preach like Peter' hymn** _(literary)_ — The old hymn verse illustrates that ordinary believers, not just apostles, can tell the love of Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/miriam-miriam|Miriam, Miriam]]
- **'No' song lyric** _(pop-culture)_ — The pop refrain 'my name is no, my sign is no, my number is no' voices the rational refusal Mary could have given Gabriel.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **1 & 2 Enoch and Jubilees on the origin of demons** _(literary)_ — Non-canonical texts (not the Bible itself) are the actual source of most popular ideas about where demons come from.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **1.4 Times a Month Attendance** _(current-events)_ — The shift from weekly attendance a generation ago to 1.4 times a month shows worship becoming a fickle, low-priority duty.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **168 hours in a week** _(other)_ — Reminder that only 1 hour is spent in Sunday worship versus the other 167 hours, pressing the question of what convictions are actually lived out.  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **1744 Methodist conference agenda** _(historical)_ — The doctrine/discipline/practice questions and ground rules from the first Methodist conference model holy conferencing as shared storytelling of ministry.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **18th-Century French Bread** _(historical)_ — Pre-modern peasants baking bread once a year and eating it stale all year illustrates how radically the human diet has changed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **18th-century Methodists accused of antinomianism** _(historical)_ — Early Methodists wrongly charged with lawlessness shows that true faith deepens, not erases, the consequences of our choices.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-do-we-hope-for|What do we hope for?]]
- **18th/19th-century spiritualists, theosophists, and Freemasons** _(historical)_ — Popular fascination with spiritualism, Swedenborgianism, and Masonic symbolism (the pyramid and eye on the dollar bill) shows people have always had unanswered questions about the spiritual realm.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **19th-century factory synchronization buzzwords** _(historical)_ — Trains, factories, and clocks made 'synchronization' a business buzzword like synergy or ROI, birthing chronos dominance.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **2.7 seconds on a bull named Fu Man Chu** _(pop-culture)_ — Bucket-list thrills like riding a bull are mere imprints of Christ's love found at the table.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **2006 Rose Bowl, Vince Young and the Texas Longhorns** _(personal)_ — The preacher's lifelong fandom culminating in UT's last-second national championship win shows how real and joyful celebration feels.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **2019 Nationals Sleep Consultant** _(current-events)_ — The Washington Nationals' World Series run, aided by a sleep consultant who reconfigured locker rooms, blue/red light, and travel sleep, illustrates how good rest prepares you to perform when the moment comes.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **2019 World Series Game 7 loss** _(personal)_ — The Astros leading then losing Game 7 to Juan Soto's hits shows how disappointing and not-fun losing is.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **2021 Astros World Series loss** _(personal)_ — Knowing the Astros would lose once Yordan Alvarez stopped hitting illustrates life moments that feel like a blowout rather than a close game.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **2021 F1 title decided on the last lap** _(current-events)_ — The race director changing rules so Verstappen could pass Hamilton illustrates injustice and a bad ending decided at the very end.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **23andMe DNA test on Jesus** _(other)_ — A genetic test on Jesus would show neither Joseph's nor the Spirit's DNA, illustrating that covenant names, not blood, mattered.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **30 Rock handsome doctor** _(pop-culture)_ — Liz Lemon's handsome but incompetent doctor boyfriend who coasted through life and became lost, showing how being shielded from consequences leaves us lost.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/when-you-re-lost-god-will-find-you|When You're Lost, God Will Find You]]
- **30 Rock's handsome incompetent doctor (Jon Hamm)** _(pop-culture)_ — A man never rejected never learns his faults, showing we are shaped by the no's more than the yes's  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-harvest|The Harvest]]
- **350-square-foot apartment with no microwave** _(personal)_ — Reheating coffee on the stove in a near-empty apartment shows the meager circumstances that did not define his future.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **3am nights with kids and 10pm crying phone calls** _(personal)_ — Everyday moments of joy, grief, and friendship are cited to show that human experience matters beyond mere chemistry.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **80/20 organization** _(other)_ — The 20%-do-80%-of-the-work pattern illustrates what the church must transcend by everyone responding to God's yes.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **9/11 at UT and the Candlelight Vigil** _(personal)_ — As a freshman in the gym under DKR he watched the tower burn; at the mall vigil singing Amazing Grace he felt an unseen hand and a peace that God is God amid a broken world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **9/11 candlelight vigil at UT** _(personal)_ — As a UT freshman the preacher saw the towers burning and at a candlelight vigil felt an unseen hand and a God-given peace while singing Amazing Grace, showing how God meets us in tragedy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **99 out of 100 odds** _(other)_ — Sports and business success rates (basketball, baseball, sales) show 99% is great by human standards but God won't settle for losing one.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/when-you-re-lost-god-will-find-you|When You're Lost, God Will Find You]]
- **A Boss Who Made Bad Decisions** _(personal)_ — Trusting a leader's decisions even when they seem to turn out badly, like Jesus' and Stephen's deaths appearing to be failures.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]]
- **A child learning to speak and read** _(nature)_ — The astonishment of a child picking up words and learning to read shows the power of naming to open up the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/god-told-adam-and-eve|God Told Adam and Eve]]
- **A Colonel whose only order is 'receive this gift'** _(other)_ — Imagining a commander whose sole order is to receive a gift illustrates the nature of Jesus' mandatum.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **A decade of peanut butter and jelly** _(personal)_ — Eventually losing his taste for PB&J shows how fading desire turns enjoyment to displeasure, unlike the redeemed desires of eternity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/new-creation|New Creation]]
- **A failed 1970s real estate deal becoming the church** _(historical)_ — Bee Creek's origin in a failed seventies real estate deal turned 'beacon on a hill' shows God redeeming the church's past.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **A few ups and downs with his brother** _(personal)_ — Same words mean something light or deep depending on the hearer, like communion's words  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **A Roman legion (6,000 soldiers) compared to a US Army brigade** _(historical)_ — concretizes how vast the demonic force possessing one man was  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **A teacher's saying about the resurrection** _(personal)_ — A former teacher's remark that if you believe Jesus rose, believing the rest of the Bible isn't hard, anchors faith in the resurrection.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **A Teacher's Words on Learning to Die** _(personal)_ — A former teacher's warning against worshiping a series of truths rather than Jesus reframes faith as learning to die.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]]
- **A Viking with a Chihuahua** _(historical)_ — Old Norse Rakki had no concept of a Chihuahua, a humorous image showing names develop within the limits of their world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/god-told-adam-and-eve|God Told Adam and Eve]]
- **A year spent too much at home** _(current-events)_ — The COVID year of being stuck at home grounds the call to reset the home and stay home to begin the practice.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Abide With Me (hymn)** _(literary)_ — Henry Francis Lyte's evening hymn opens the sermon, framing the plea for Christ to stay near as darkness deepens, echoing the Emmaus travelers' 'stay with us.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/abide-with-me|Abide With Me]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **Acoustic resonance of the triad** _(nature)_ — The physics of C, E-flat, and G frequencies resonating after notes are played pictures how the lives of saints keep resonating with us.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Acts 29 Network (Village Church, Mars Hill)** _(current-events)_ — The Reformed church-planting network's name (disagreeing with its Dort theology) illustrates God writing 'Acts 29' through the church now.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Adam Bede / Dinah Morris** _(literary)_ — George Eliot's female Methodist preacher character illustrates the historical reality of women preachers Wesley sent out.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Addict's first day sober** _(other)_ — Being sober one day shows the first step is not the last step in salvation's ongoing transformation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-salvation|What is Salvation?]]
- **Adjusting Eyes in a Dark Room** _(nature)_ — The slow, wall-crashing process of eyes adjusting in darkness illustrates how we dimly see Jesus until moments of bright clarity.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **Adrian Newey's How to Build a Car** _(literary)_ — The Red Bull designer's book and his four chassis goals (stable, light, low-drag, balanced downforce) model what a strong church foundation must be.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Advice for the Graduate** _(personal)_ — The flood of advice given to young people (what to study, where to go to college) frames how to discern good counsel from bad.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/you-are-strong|You Are Strong]]
- **Aerodynamics as the church meeting the world** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor: the church, like a car, meets the world's air; minimizing drag on the Holy Spirit and maximizing downforce lets it move and handle better.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Aesop's boy who cried wolf** _(literary)_ — The boy who lost credibility by raising false alarms illustrates fear as respect that is forfeited when one cannot be heard when telling the truth.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Aesop's tortoise and the hare** _(literary)_ — Contrasts fables with exhaustible morals against parables, which can be preached again and again without exhausting their meaning.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **Aggie and Longhorn arguing over fans** _(other)_ — Rival-fan bickering illustrates how unreasonable online political dialogue has become.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Aggie symbols: Century Tree, Aggie Ring, Reveille** _(personal)_ — Campus symbols show how signs help members recognize and bond with one another.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Aggies in the building** _(personal)_ — A joking aside about Texas A&M fans present shows the congregation is not united by shared affinity but by Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Aging and the deficit mindset** _(other)_ — As bodies change, people dwell on what they used to do rather than who they were created to be.  → Every Act of Faith
- **AI as the ultimate cheating tool / Bill Gates AP Bio bet** _(current-events)_ — OpenAI built to pass tests (Gates' AP Bio wager) shows banking-model education's emptiness versus real transformation.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-parables|The Parables]]
- **Air-conditioners, chairs, children, skies and sparrows** _(nature)_ — Everyday existing things prove there had to be a source of all things.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]]
- **Airport arrivals pickup line** _(personal)_ — Everyone in the no-parking arrivals line thinks they are the exception to the rule, illustrating the human tendency from original sin to see ourselves as the exception.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/justification|Justification]]
- **Al-Khwarizmi, Origin of 'Algorithm'** _(historical)_ — The word 'algorithm' derives from a 9th-century Persian mathematician, demystifying the 'magic box' of social media.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Alanis Morissette "Ironic"** _(pop-culture)_ — The song (and the endless "well, actually it's not really ironic" debates about it) illustrates how irony is a disconnect between what we see and what is really there, like the cheering crowd that will soon shout "Crucify him."  → Palm Sunday: From Hosanna to Passion
- **Alasdair MacIntyre on justice as virtue** _(literary)_ — MacIntyre's three-fold account (desert, practices, common good) frames justice as a cultivated virtue, not mere theory.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Alaska plane crash survival** _(other)_ — Surviving an emergency crash long enough to be rescued shows survival is aimed at rescue, not the journey.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Albert Schweitzer on the Mirror-Image Historical Jesus** _(historical)_ — The Nobel-winning scholar-doctor's observation that seekers of the historical Jesus tend to see a reflection of themselves (liberal German, conservative Texan, liberation theologian), warning that our Jesus can be a construct of our own minds.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Albert Schweitzer's Quest for the Historical Jesus** _(historical)_ — Schweitzer's observation that the historical Jesus becomes a mirror reflecting the seeker's own ideology, showing 'who was Jesus?' is really 'who are we?'  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **Albert Schweitzer, The Quest of the Historical Jesus** _(literary)_ — Schweitzer's point that seekers of the historical Jesus find a face resembling their own (conservative, liberal, radical).  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
- **Albert Tucker's Prisoner's Dilemma** _(historical)_ — The classic dilemma where rational betrayal beats silence, used to contrast real life's countless small chances to love a neighbor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-of-god-love-of-neighbor|Love of God, Love of Neighbor]]
- **Aldersgate heart strangely warmed** _(historical)_ — Wesley's experience hearing Luther's Romans preface embodies assurance that 'even I' am loved by God  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Alina's Catholic school Mass and the host** _(personal)_ — Seventh graders can't leave once the host is revealed, illustrating eucharistic reverence  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **All Quiet on the Western Front and Wilfred Owen** _(literary)_ — The only reading material left was war literature and Owen's 'Bent double, like old beggars under sacks,' which did nothing to relieve the suffering.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **All Saints Day candles for five departed church members** _(personal)_ — Candles for Bob Bowen, Bill Kiely, Elzie Williams, Susan Williams, and Dick Littleton embody remembering the saints of the congregation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **All Saints Day tradition dating to Charlemagne** _(historical)_ — The 1200-year-old tradition frames the day's act of remembrance.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **All Things Considered realization at age 10** _(personal)_ — A childhood one-time realization that 'All Things Considered' meant all things being considered, contrasted with faith as an ongoing continuum rather than a single moment.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **All-You-Can-Eat Buffet and Diminishing Marginal Utility** _(other)_ — Trying to game a buffet with avocado and sushi runs into the law of diminishing marginal utility, illustrating that worldly satisfactions have limits while God's love is inexhaustible.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/you-can-never-have-too-much-love|You Can Never Have Too Much Love]]
- **Always room for pie** _(personal)_ — Kids too full for broccoli always have room for ice cream, showing the gap between need and want.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Am I my brother's keeper?** _(biblical)_ — Cain's evasive question used to name the temptation to fixate on ourselves and refuse to help nearby neighbors in need.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Ambrose's defense of Abraham and Hagar** _(literary)_ — Ambrose's four reasons (pre-Law, zeal for progeny, Sarah's request, Pauline allegory) defending Abraham's union with Hagar.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/hagar-sarah-and-the-birth-of-a-people|Hagar, Sarah, and the Birth of a People]]
- **American Graffiti and the keep-it-real rebel** _(pop-culture)_ — The rebel who does what he wants in a small town illustrates the cultural celebration of rebellion that John reframes as sin against God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/sin-as-rebellion-the-freedom-not-to-rebel|Sin as Rebellion: The Freedom Not to Rebel]]
- **American Republic Without a King** _(historical)_ — The U.S. as a kingless commonwealth/republic frames how we should understand praying for Christ's kingdom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/thy-kingdom-come-thy-will-be-done-on-earth-as-it-is-in-heaven|Thy Kingdom Come, Thy Will Be Done, on Earth as It Is in Heaven]]
- **Amos uninvited from dinner parties** _(biblical)_ — Imagining people dodging Amos's harsh prophecies shows that the truth we need to hear about our direction is often unwelcome  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Amos's plumb line** _(biblical)_ — The weighted string testing whether a wall is upright illustrates righteousness/justification as being built straight before God  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Amy Kenny, My Body Is Not a Prayer Request** _(literary)_ — Kenny renames the unnamed blind man 'Zach' to humanize him and reframes disability as a place of encounter with God's glory rather than a symbol of sin.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/in-the-presence-of-god|In the Presence of God]]
- **Amy Laura Hall and the Texas Wesley three at Duke** _(personal)_ — Dr. Hall's talk drew only three students, all of whom ended up at Duke, weaving the pastor's path into the church's origin through DS Robert Hall.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **Ananias and Sapphira and church stuff** _(biblical)_ — Acts 5's deception shows the early church going off the rails, paralleling the painful 'church stuff' in every congregation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Ananias and Sapphira withholding the proceeds** _(biblical)_ — The couple struck dead for keeping back money illustrates the sin of churches and Christians who put limits on what they give to God.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Ancient Cornerstone Masonry / Joseph Aspdin and Portland Cement** _(historical)_ — Ancient masonry cornerstones (Psalm 118:22) had to be perfectly square or the whole building failed, until 19th-century English bricklayer Joseph Aspdin invented Portland cement, replacing scarce volcanic-ash concrete.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Ancient Egyptian glass beads** _(historical)_ — Illustrates the ancient origins of glassmaking, older than most written languages  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Ancient generals and battles** _(historical)_ — Alexander the Great, Hannibal at Cannae, Scipio, Marius, Sulla, and Julius Caesar exemplify the worldly military victory that Jesus deliberately refused.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **Ancient Greek Orator with Pebbles** _(historical)_ — The tradition of an orator shouting at the sea with pebbles in his mouth to train articulation, linked to debate practice of talking with a pencil in the mouth.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-to-me-all-you-who-are-weary|Come to Me, All You Who Are Weary]]
- **Ancient sun-reckoned hours** _(historical)_ — First hour at dawn, sixth hour at noon shows pre-modern time was set relative to the sun and seasons.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **Ancient Virtue of Prudence** _(historical)_ — The ancients' practiced virtue of prudence illustrates judging good from bad as a learned skill, not an inborn trait.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/you-are-strong|You Are Strong]]
- **Andrew of Crete on spreading ourselves before Christ** _(literary)_ — Andrew of Crete urges spreading not withering olive branches but ourselves, clothed in Christ's grace, before his feet on Palm Sunday.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Andrew of Crete on spreading ourselves before Christ** _(literary)_ — Quoted homily urging believers to be the garments spread before Christ, clothed in him rather than waving withering branches.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Andrew of Crete on the centrality of the cross** _(historical)_ — the church father's meditation that without the cross there is no immortality, freedom, or paradise underscores Christ's saving action  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]], [[covenant-umc/2024/take-up-your-cross|Take Up Your Cross]]
- **Andrew of Crete's Palm Sunday homily** _(literary)_ — Quoted patristic call to 'run to accompany' Christ toward his passion, prostrating ourselves by humility rather than spreading garments and palms.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Andrew Tate on Jesus not being manly** _(pop-culture)_ — The social-media influencer's claim that Islam is better because Jesus isn't strong or manly enough exemplifies the false desire for a conquering rather than suffering savior.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **Andy Williford's life** _(personal)_ — Eulogy of Andy: Shreveport upbringing, Air Force service, 25 years as DFW air traffic controller, family, and golf at Lake Tahoe, witnessing to a full and happy life.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Andy Williford
- **Anesthesia and aesthetics share a Greek root** _(other)_ — Both derive from Greek aesthesis (judgment); anesthesia is the impairing of judgment, illustrating how judging others numbs our own self-judgment  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]]
- **Angels dancing on the head of a pin** _(historical)_ — The medieval question masks a real distinction about whether angels are material or non-material beings.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **Angels dancing on the head of a pin / TikTok** _(other)_ — Mocking overcomplicated angel cosmologies to recover the plain meaning of angelos as simply 'messenger.'  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-he-is-not-here-among-the-dead|Easter: He Is Not Here Among the Dead]]
- **Animal House character epilogues** _(pop-culture)_ — The 1978 film's closing cards (Bluto becoming a US Senator) show resolved conflict revealing who characters become.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i-in-christ|Who am I in Christ?]]
- **Anna Jarvis and the founding of Mother's Day** _(historical)_ — The Methodist woman who founded Mother's Day to honor others' grieving mothers, not herself, illustrating love that does not seek its own.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-does-not-seek-its-own|Love Does Not Seek Its Own]], [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Anna Jarvis, Methodist founder of Mother's Day** _(historical)_ — A Methodist laywoman started Mother's Day over a century ago to honor her mother, showing the day is about reaching out to another, not oneself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Anna Karenina opening line** _(literary)_ — 'happy families are all alike'—contentment is less dramatic than struggle, like sanctification vs justification  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Anna the prophetess** _(biblical)_ — Anna, who prayed in the temple for years, becomes the first evangelist sharing the redemption found in Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]], [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Anna the prophetess, first evangelist** _(biblical)_ — Anna sharing news of redemption models thanking God by proclaiming the good news.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **Annual Conference as a dull word** _(personal)_ — The pastor's own unexciting experience of UMC annual conferences sets up the contrast with conferencing at its best in Acts 2.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Anonymous early Christian on the infinite river** _(historical)_ — An ancient writer marvels that the unfathomable fountainhead of life was immersed in the shallow waters of this transient world.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **Anselm on Mary the mother of re-creation** _(literary)_ — Anselm's meditation that God is Father of creation and Mary mother of the re-created world.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Anthony Burgess Acts novelization (big lungs)** _(literary)_ — A novelized Acts suggesting Jesus survived because of big lungs, used to dismiss the swoon theory and affirm a real death.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Anthony Burgess, 'The Kingdom of the Wicked'** _(literary)_ — The novel's premise that Jesus didn't really die, cited as a way of answering 'no' to the resurrection.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Anthony Burgess, Kingdom of the Wicked** _(literary)_ — The swoon theory in novel form-Jesus didn't really die-as a way of answering 'no' to resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **AP physics vectors and forces** _(personal)_ — His high-school physics memory of centripetal/centrifugal forces becomes a metaphor for life's constant directional movement and the inward-curved heart turning outward.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i-in-christ|Who am I in Christ?]]
- **Apologetics: addressing misconceptions** _(personal)_ — The preacher's own reflection that he can't make a non-believer believe but can clear away misconceptions, illustrating how the church 'makes it easier to be good.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Appalachian Trail backcountry shelters** _(personal)_ — Hiking Maine-to-Georgia, he ditched his own tent to use public trail shelters, illustrating that real shelter means accepting community and strangers.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/shelter|Shelter]]
- **Appalachian Trail blisters and bog logs** _(personal)_ — Hiking the hundred-mile wilderness in Maine, waterproof boots trapped water and caused festering blisters that only healed once uncovered and dried out, illustrating how sin must be uncovered and healed by love rather than ignored.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins-2|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Appalachian Trail thru-hike** _(personal)_ — The preacher's southbound AT hike from Mount Katahdin shows how preparation transforms experience  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]], [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Appalachian Trail thru-hike and caloric density** _(personal)_ — Hiking 25 miles a day with a 12-pound base pack changed his relationship to food, forcing him to seek calorie-dense junk (18-inch pizzas, grape soda, peanut butter Ben and Jerry's), illustrating how survival reshapes desire.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/food|Food]]
- **Appalachian Trail water hiking** _(personal)_ — The preacher's post-college months hiking south from Maine, learning to trust abundant mountain springs after Texas water-scarcity, illustrating thirst, suspicion, and appreciation for water.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/give-me-water|Give Me Water]]
- **Apple/orange tree maturation time** _(nature)_ — An apple tree takes 7-10 years and an orange up to 15 to bear fruit, showing fruitfulness requires patient investment, not a rush job.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Appreciating indoor plumbing** _(personal)_ — How sustained outdoor life renewed his wonder at simply turning a faucet to drink, illustrating gratitude for water as gift.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/give-me-water|Give Me Water]]
- **Apprentice plumber/woodworker** _(other)_ — An apprentice plumber's goal is to become a plumber, illustrating that the disciple's goal is to become like Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Aquinas's Five Ways** _(historical)_ — The classic proofs (Prime Mover, First Cause, etc.) Russell tried to dismiss, vindicated by an expanding universe.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
- **Archaeologists finding Noah's Ark** _(current-events)_ — News reports of finding the ark prompt the point that faith rests in God, not in wooden remains.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Archimedes in the Bathtub** _(historical)_ — Archimedes discovering volume by water displaced becomes the central image for how God's kingdom displaces worldly kingdoms and our desires.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/thy-kingdom-come-thy-will-be-done-on-earth-as-it-is-in-heaven|Thy Kingdom Come, Thy Will Be Done, on Earth as It Is in Heaven]]
- **Archimedes shouting Eureka** _(historical)_ — God's time is NOT like bathwater displacing us; rather we live and move in God, the source.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **Aristotle's Four Causes and the Table** _(other)_ — Aristotle's material/formal/efficient/final causes illustrated by a table, used to name Christ as the formal cause of all humanity.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **Aristotle's golden mean** _(historical)_ — the right path lies between Manicheanism and pure rationalism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **Arrowhead hunting with Dave** _(personal)_ — Chris and Dave loved getting away to find and trade arrowheads, lingering because it was so nice out there, illustrating shared joy and her zest for life.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Christine Renée Jarl
- **Asterisk by 'Catholic' in the old Methodist hymnal** _(historical)_ — The hymnal's footnote marking 'Catholic' as 'universal' frames the sermon's central word-study.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-holy-catholic-church|I Believe in the Holy Catholic Church]]
- **Astros 2024 playoff elimination relief** _(personal)_ — The pastor's anxiety over the Astros' playoff run vanished once they were eliminated, showing how expectation breeds worry and its removal brings peace.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-we-fill-our-waiting|How We Fill Our Waiting]]
- **Astros and dead sports heart** _(personal)_ — His joy in sports replaced by stress (Astros losing the World Series, Duke in the Final Four) illustrates why he sought a fresh start, paralleling not building the church on old baggage.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Astros and Longhorns** _(personal)_ — Wilson imagines Jesus asking him to lay down his sports teams—'Anything but that!'—naming the everyday loyalties we cling to.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Astros Game 7 2019 with Zach Greinke** _(pop-culture)_ — Pulling Greinke too early cost the Astros the World Series, illustrating history's contingency.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Astros rout the Dodgers 18-1** _(current-events)_ — Dave Roberts 'flushing it down the toilet' shows healthily letting a rejection/loss go  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-harvest|The Harvest]]
- **Astros World Series Game 7** _(personal)_ — The preacher's lifelong Astros fandom inherited from his father and the painful Game 7 loss frames his love of baseball.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Astros World Series Game 7 loss and earlier win over the Dodgers** _(personal)_ — Cheering for one team while the other's fans grieve illustrates how ordinary celebration is zero-sum, unlike the joy Jesus offers.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **Atonement as 'at-one-ment'** _(literary)_ — The old English word atonement broken into 'at-one-ment' to show how we are made one in Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/justification|Justification]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Atrophying the heart by not serving** _(nature)_ — Failing to serve the hurting atrophies your heart of love like an unused muscle, making it harder to lace up your shoes and go out again.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Attendre and Attention** _(literary)_ — The French attendre (to wait) is the root of attention, showing holy patience means actively attending to Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Augustine on conversion** _(historical)_ — Augustine's image of conversion as turning from being curved in on ourselves to standing upright to see the world rightly.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/god-s-love-a-deal-too-good-to-be-true|God's Love: A Deal Too Good to Be True]]
- **Augustine on God Loving Us First** _(historical)_ — Augustine teaches that we can only love because God loved us first and pours love into us by the Holy Spirit.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **Augustine on holy desire** _(literary)_ — Quoted line that the Christian life is an exercise of holy desire, preparing us to see and be satisfied when Christ comes.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]], [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Augustine on loving God** _(literary)_ — We could not love God unless we already possessed him — inclusion is through Christ, not despite him  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **Augustine on saving for the children** _(literary)_ — Augustine's sermon mocking the endless 'saving it for my children' excuse shows that hoarding across generations is nothing new and not true freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **Augustine on the absurdity of the cross** _(historical)_ — Augustine's image of Christ subjecting kings not with a proud army but the absurdity of the cross shows victory by self-sacrifice.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Augustine on the Lord's Prayer** _(historical)_ — Augustine's claim that every petition of Scripture is contained in the Lord's Prayer, so we may use different words but not say different things.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/future-sermon-notes-on-thomas-and-prayer|Future Sermon Notes: On Thomas and Prayer]]
- **Augustine on the millennial age** _(historical)_ — Augustine read the millennium as the church's ongoing struggle between sin and grace, with Satan bound in human hearts and the Antichrist seen wherever people deny Christ by loveless words and actions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/revelation-notes|Revelation Notes]]
- **Augustine on the woman at the well** _(literary)_ — Augustine's reflection that Jesus is both needy and rich, using veiled language to gradually enter the woman's heart, illustrating how God draws us toward living water.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/give-me-water|Give Me Water]]
- **Augustine's 'little easters'** _(historical)_ — Calling Sundays little easters shows weekly worship lets us re-enter the resurrection beyond linear time.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **Augustine's 'make me chaste, but not yet'** _(historical)_ — Augustine's famous prayer captures the appealing delay of the 'not yet' before grace.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Augustine's Ascension Sermon** _(literary)_ — Quoted passage that because we are Christ's body we are already in heaven with him while he still suffers on earth in his members.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-ascended-into-heaven|He Ascended into Heaven]]
- **Augustine's curvatus / curved inward** _(historical)_ — Image of humanity born turned away from God, straightened by grace through baptism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **Augustine's curvatus in se** _(historical)_ — Augustine's image of the heart curved inward (curvatus) versus standing upright (rectitudo) frames life before and after Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i-in-christ|Who am I in Christ?]]
- **Augustine's love-commandments rule** _(historical)_ — Augustine's rule to read scripture seeking love of God and neighbor as an interpretive guide.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **Augustine's prayer for chastity 'but not yet'** _(historical)_ — Augustine's 'Lord make me chaste, but not yet' illustrates our readiness for new life that we keep postponing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]]
- **Augustine's restless heart** _(literary)_ — Augustine's prayer that we are restless until we rest in God — our goal is to be with God, not to collect power.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-world|Reset Your World]]
- **Augustine's restless heart prayer** _(literary)_ — Augustine's 'our hearts are restless until they rest in You' frames the whole series' theological basis for restlessness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]], [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Augustine's Restless Hearts** _(literary)_ — Augustine's 'our hearts are restless until they rest in you' names humanity's search for meaning.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]], [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **Augustine's vestiges of the Trinity / footprints in the sand** _(historical)_ — Vestigium as footprint shows God's presence glimpsed in fleeting traces held onto by faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Augustine's wonderful exchange** _(literary)_ — Quoted Augustine on the exchange where we give Christ the power to die and he gives us the power to live.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-he-is-not-here-among-the-dead|Easter: He Is Not Here Among the Dead]]
- **Augustus Caesar** _(historical)_ — The imperial leader-of-kings Jesus was born under, contrasted with the obscure Jesus of Nazareth.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Augustus Caesar and Virgil's Aeneid** _(historical)_ — The emperor's cultivated image as 'the leader' sets a foil for Jesus of backwater Nazareth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Augustus statues compared to pagan gods** _(historical)_ — Roman emperor sculptures promulgated to rival pagan god statues show the idolatrous God-language Rome used, framing biblical citizenship against empire.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-gate|The Gate]]
- **Aunt Laura's long blessing and the premature Amen** _(personal)_ — The preacher as a child blurting 'Amen' mid-prayer illustrates how knowing when to stop praying is harder than knowing how to begin.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/lead-us-not-into-temptation|Lead Us Not into Temptation]]
- **Austin skyscraper foundations built square** _(nature)_ — A tall building must be built at true 90-degree angles ('justified'/square) from a deep foundation; a crooked building cannot be straightened after the fact without demolition, illustrating that justification is God tearing us down to the foundations to rebuild us upright.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/justification|Justification]]
- **Avett Brothers, 'The Ballad of Love and Hate'** _(pop-culture)_ — Song where Love waits patiently and Hate returns repentant illustrates love undeceived on the far side of the epiphany.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-believes-all|Love Believes All]]
- **Avoiding people over the election** _(current-events)_ — Post-2020-election estrangement is named as a place to practice reconciliation rather than blocking people.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacrifice|Sacrifice]]
- **Azusa Street revival from Methodism** _(historical)_ — Pentecostalism, the fastest-growing Protestantism, was born at Azusa Street and traces back to the Wesleyan/Methodist movement.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Azusa Street revival out of Methodism** _(historical)_ — The 1906 birth of Pentecostalism grew out of holiness traditions and Methodism, proving revival requires continuity, not total rupture.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]], [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Babies and Delayed Gratification** _(nature)_ — Babies are born hungry and tired without delayed gratification, showing patience must be cultivated, not assumed.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Baby, It's Cold Outside** _(pop-culture)_ — The 'creepy' pop song shows that some seasonal songs' lyrics don't match the Christmas spirit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Bacchanals after Bacchus/Dionysius** _(historical)_ — Associating partying with pagan abandon shows how celebration got redefined as losing inhibitions rather than rejoicing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **Bach in Cöthen** _(historical)_ — Bach wrote his great instrumental works under a Calvinist non-liturgical ruler yet still signed them 'to the glory of God,' showing holiness apart from the church calendar.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Bach's Fifth Cello Suite (C minor prelude)** _(other)_ — The C-minor suite, played live by Daniel, illustrates that a sad/minor key is still beautiful, like grief that remains real.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Back-to-school traffic on 620** _(current-events)_ — Changing traffic patterns when school starts illustrate life transitions and the call to keep growing and learning.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Backpacking in the Colorado mountains** _(personal)_ — Waking to fresh snow, bird tracks, and stream coffee illustrates the joy of the journey over comfort.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Backyard barbecue temptation conversation** _(other)_ — An imagined awkward cookout chat shows how temptation is too intimate a topic for casual conversation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **Bacon as personal aversion** _(personal)_ — The preacher finds bacon disgusting though society loves it, showing temptation is particular and unique 'to you.'  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eating-stones|Eating Stones]]
- **Balaam and his donkey** _(biblical)_ — In Numbers, the wise man Balaam fails to see God's angel but his donkey does, paralleling Herod who cannot see the star the Magi follow.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Band of Brothers where-are-they-now epilogue** _(pop-culture)_ — The HBO series showing the real WWII veterans behind the characters illustrates wanting to see who people become after their story.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i-in-christ|Who am I in Christ?]]
- **Bandwagon fan** _(other)_ — Christians can join late, in the fourth quarter, and still be part of the team because Christ already won the victory.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Banking model of education** _(other)_ — Education as mere knowledge deposit, contrasted with Jesus' aim of transformation rather than knowledge transfer.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-parables|The Parables]]
- **Baptism as a one-time car wash for the soul** _(other)_ — Baptism is not a single car wash that keeps the soul clean forever but an entrance into ongoing community.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Baptism is not a paint job** _(other)_ — Baptism is not a paint job needing touch-ups but a new person buried and risen in Christ — hence no rebaptism.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-baptism-of-the-lord|The Baptism of the Lord]]
- **Baptismal pall over the coffin** _(other)_ — The cross-marked pall covering every coffin alike witnesses that all are equal before the table of the Lord, recalling baptism into Christ.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection (Funeral/Memorial Template)
- **Barbecue pit shaped like an ark** _(other)_ — Since God permits eating animals after the flood, a clever welder could build an ark-shaped barbecue pit—illustrating the first ark's promise.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Barefoot Sunday Ministries Connection** _(personal)_ — A planned tie-in to the congregation's Barefoot Sunday and its ministries as a lived expression of being found and giving back.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-found|Lost & Found: Found]]
- **Barmen Declaration** _(historical)_ — Barth's six theses rejecting Nazi control of the church model refusing to subordinate Christ's lordship to earthly powers.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-cost|The Cost]]
- **Bart Ehrman losing his faith but keeping his fundamentalism** _(historical)_ — The scholar's faith collapsed over textual variants because it rested on fundamentalist perspicuity rather than encountering the living Christ in worship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Bart Simpson 'I'm going to be punching'** _(pop-culture)_ — Bart tells Lisa he is going to be punching and if she gets in his way it is her own fault, illustrating the self-as-sole-judge narcissism of choosing your own story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Barth on Christians who wake up** _(literary)_ — A theologian's quote that Christians are those awakened by God, not who wake themselves, illustrating the call to wake up for Advent.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-four-questions-who-is-coming|The Four Questions: Who Is Coming?]]
- **Barth's Strange New World of the Bible** _(literary)_ — Karl Barth's phrase: if Scripture doesn't feel strange and new, you're reading it wrong.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Barton Springs hidden source** _(nature)_ — The hard-to-locate source of Barton Springs illustrates how the Trinity's origin is difficult to pinpoint.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Basil of Caesarea, On the Holy Spirit** _(historical)_ — Basil's 364 treatise against the neo-Arians, quoted to describe what the Spirit does — raising, enlightening, and transforming souls.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Basket of Summer Fruit** _(biblical)_ — Amos's vision of ripe summer fruit signals that the end has come ripe upon Israel.  → Ordinary Time
- **Battle in Germany Where Romans Were Defeated** _(historical)_ — Major events like Rome's defeat in Germania dominate the histories of Augustus's reign, while Bethlehem, Mary, Joseph, and Jesus never appear — yet that is where God acts.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **Be still as keeping your sword sheathed** _(other)_ — The Hebrew military root of 'be still' means a hand on the hilt that does not strike, so stillness is intentionality, not passivity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Bearded Christ catacomb fresco (4th c. Rome)** _(historical)_ — Earliest images of Jesus reveal their culture more than reveal Jesus himself  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Beating the brother-in-law** _(personal)_ — Racing to beat his older, more athletic brother-in-law illustrates the competitive scarcity mindset grace overturns.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-race|The Race]]
- **Beatitudes inverting expectations** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' teaching flips our expectations: blessed are the poor in spirit, those who mourn  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Beautiful complex snowflakes / describe yourself in one thing** _(other)_ — Opening thought exercise showing no one is reducible to a single attribute, framing the sacred story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Beautiful things that overwhelm — Matterhorn, Pietà, David, St Peter's, Grand Canyon** _(nature)_ — Standing before these sublime sights evokes humility, not self-pity — the proper response to beauty that reveals our smallness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-baptism-of-the-lord|The Baptism of the Lord]]
- **Bede's sevenfold outline** _(historical)_ — Bede divided Revelation into seven parts tracing the general flow of church history from the seven Asian churches through conflicts to the New Jerusalem.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/revelation-notes|Revelation Notes]]
- **Bee Creek discernment conversations** _(personal)_ — The abundant resurrection life is offered now, even amid the ongoing discernment conversations at Bee Creek.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Bee Creek mission statement and values** _(other)_ — The church's corporate-style mission/values statements illustrate the move from stated values toward lived habits.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **Bee Creek Village and Christmas offering** _(personal)_ — The $9,608 Christmas offering almost exactly matched the $9,600 survey bid, a sign of the church's call to build local community for those in need.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **Beethoven's Fifth Symphony** _(pop-culture)_ — Begins in C minor and resolves to C major, picturing how all chords of human history finally resolve in God.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Being ordered around by a stranger** _(personal)_ — Our instinctive resistance to commands from strangers sets up the question of why we obey Jesus' command at the table.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Benedictines vs. Franciscans** _(historical)_ — Medieval dispute between asset-gathering Benedictines and poverty-vowed Franciscans, illustrating the freedom of material detachment in imitating Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Bernard Williams and the Tedium of Immortality** _(literary)_ — Williams's philosophical argument that a changeless immortality becomes miserable sets up the contrast with new creation that transforms our very desires.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/new-creation|New Creation]]
- **Bernard Williams on the tedium of immortality** _(literary)_ — A non-Christian philosopher's essay arguing endless life-as-is would become hellish, illustrating how fixating on eternity wrongly makes the present eternal.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/glorification|Glorification]]
- **Bertrand Russell vs. Aquinas and the Big Bang** _(historical)_ — Russell's 1927 book denying the universe needed a beginning was undercut by Hubble and Lemaître proving cosmic expansion, vindicating Aquinas's First Way.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]], [[covenant-umc/2026/three-whats|Three Whats?]]
- **Bertrand Russell's 1927 book refuting proofs of God** _(historical)_ — The atheist's claim that the universe needs no beginning sets up the contrast with later cosmology.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]]
- **Bertrand Russell, Why I Am Not a Christian** _(literary)_ — Russell's use of the turtle retort, invoked as the skeptical infinite-regression challenge to belief in God as creator.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **BFF (best friend forever)** _(pop-culture)_ — The term 'BFF' that emerged for younger generations illustrates the longing for friendship as an eternal, next-level relationship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Bible app reading plans and audio Bible** _(personal)_ — Downloading the Bible app to listen while doing dishes or driving shows a practical, accessible step toward entering Scripture.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Big Bend Backpacking Trip** _(personal)_ — Wilson and Alina taking the kids backcountry into the Chisos Basin in winter 2019, packing too much of some things and not enough of others, illustrating how we set out unprepared.  → Notes from the Pastor: Resetting Yourself with God in the New Year
- **Big Bend Hitchhiking Fisherman** _(personal)_ — A woman cyclist the pastor's family gave a ride to in Big Bend, an Alaskan fisherman planning wilderness counseling on a boat, illustrating how doing hard things together builds trust and opens people up.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/laborers-in-the-vineyard-grace-community-and-mortality|Laborers in the Vineyard: Grace, Community, and Mortality]]
- **Big Bend honeymoon javelina** _(personal)_ — He and Alina spun off the road avoiding a javelina on their honeymoon and needed a park ranger to pull them out — we all need outside help.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-opened|Be Opened]]
- **Big boy / big girl pants** _(other)_ — Facing the consequences of our actions and picking up the pieces  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **Big Sky Daddy** _(other)_ — The image of God as a distant 'sky daddy' who orders and punishes is rejected in favor of God being fully present and for us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Big Yellow Taxi (Joni Mitchell)** _(pop-culture)_ — The 'paved paradise' lyric underscores the hardened, efficiency-trampled road as ground no longer capable of growth.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **Bill Murray in Groundhog Day** _(pop-culture)_ — a selfish man's turnaround illustrates the dramatic conversion climax of justification  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Billboard on Highway 71** _(current-events)_ — A billboard definition of 'life' put up by fellow Christians on Highway 71 that misrepresents the biblical Greek.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/fragments|Fragments]]
- **Biography of Pontius Pilate** _(historical)_ — Pilate's name, equestrian Pontius family from Samnium, military career, and 26-36 AD prefecture under Tiberius anchor the incarnation in verifiable secular history.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **Birders identifying birds by sight and sound** _(nature)_ — contemplation as a practiced habit, like the patient, trained stillness of experienced birdwatchers  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Birthday memory of Dominic and Ephraim** _(personal)_ — Recalling his sons' births and gifting Alina who did the work shows how memory functions as kairos, not linear chronos.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **Bitcoin and GameStop stock** _(current-events)_ — Chasing the next hot investment exemplifies the world's wisdom always seeking 'the next thing.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **Bitcoin, Apple stock, and Super Bowl bets** _(current-events)_ — Wishing to turn stones to bread is like wanting perfect foreknowledge of markets or games to always get what you want.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **BJJ competition victory** _(personal)_ — Winning his one jiu-jitsu match with an arm triangle felt validating, then Jesus 'ruins it' by challenging that desire for worldly vindication.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Black swan events and financial speculation** _(other)_ — Unpredictable black-swan events illustrate the downward spiral of anxious speculation about an unknowable future.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Blaise Pascal and the Pensées** _(historical)_ — Pascal's life, mystical conversion, and wager argument supply the sermon's core framework for betting one's life on God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Bleeding without noticing** _(personal)_ — Finding blood on your pants before feeling pain shows how we can be wounded, especially spiritually, without realizing it.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Blogging and the Australian Atheist Biologist** _(personal)_ — A comment-section exchange on an atheist biologist's blog about a death, showing that secular worldviews lack habits for grieving while the church has practices for death.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]]
- **Blue Laws and the Inflated Sunday Church** _(historical)_ — Southern Sunday business-closing laws artificially boosted church attendance because church was the only thing to do.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **Bluey vs. Citizen Kane** _(pop-culture)_ — Judging the gospel as modern history is a genre mistake, like critiquing Bluey as if it were Citizen Kane  → [[covenant-umc/2025/remember-and-be-thankful|Remember and Be Thankful]]
- **Boarding a plane and missing the flight** _(other)_ — Once you board you're 'in' and latecomers are out of luck, the kind of human grouping the parables refuse to endorse.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-next-thirty-seconds|The Next Thirty Seconds]]
- **Boaz as kinsman-redeemer** _(biblical)_ — In Ruth, redemption is a concrete economic transaction over family land, not eternal salvation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Bob Dylan 'Gotta Serve Somebody'** _(pop-culture)_ — Dylan's line that you've got to serve somebody—the devil or the Lord—illustrates that there are no neutral positions in discipleship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-communion-of-the-saints|I Believe in the Communion of the Saints]], [[covenant-umc/2026/come-to-me-all-you-who-are-weary|Come to Me, All You Who Are Weary]]
- **Bob Dylan, 'Gotta Serve Somebody'** _(pop-culture)_ — the real question of sanctification is not what experience you had but whom you serve  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
