- **Bob Dylan, 'Gotta Serve Somebody'** _(pop-culture)_ — Quoting Dylan's song to underscore that everyone serves someone, so serve God rather than deceivers.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/advent-don-t-be-deceived|Advent: Don't Be Deceived]]
- **Bob Dylan, 'Gotta Serve Somebody'** _(pop-culture)_ — Dylan's line that 'you gotta serve somebody' illustrates that no choice is itself a choice about following God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]], [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Bob's unwelcome advice** _(personal)_ — A dismissive exchange ('Thanks Bob') shows the natural human resistance to being told to change one's heart.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **Body of Christ with different cells and organs** _(biblical)_ — Following Jesus doesn't make everyone identical; the church is a body of varied members, not a military unit.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Body with hands, feet, eyes, and hearts** _(literary)_ — Paul's image of diverse members in one body shows the church as concretely connected and not uniform.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **Boil Water Notice on Just and Unjust** _(current-events)_ — Echoing Jesus, the power outage and boil-water notice fall on just and unjust alike (a nod to the recent Texas winter storm) showing suffering is universal.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Bonhoeffer on faithfulness in daily things** _(historical)_ — Bonhoeffer's line that no one faithful in great things has not first learned faithfulness in daily life underscores day-by-day discipleship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Bonhoeffer's alcoholic and rich man freed but not from themselves** _(literary)_ — Giving up alcohol or money frees you from those things but not from yourself, showing only Jesus' call dissolves our ties and brings true faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Bonhoeffer's Cheap Grace vs Costly Grace** _(literary)_ — Bonhoeffer's distinction in Discipleship between shiny, well-marketed cheap grace that demands no change and the costly grace of the way to the cross.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Bonhoeffer's Discipleship on the cross** _(literary)_ — Bonhoeffer's 'when Christ calls a man, he bids him come and die,' framing discipleship as abandoning worldly attachments.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Bonhoeffer's return to Germany / letter to Niebuhr** _(historical)_ — Choosing to leave the safety of America to share his people's trials shows costly discipleship that cannot be chosen from security.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-cost|The Cost]]
- **Boot Canyon spring at Big Bend** _(personal)_ — A rare living spring emerging from the mountain that he still boils as a father, illustrating both the wonder and the caution toward water sources.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Bootstrap ideology** _(other)_ — the self-reliant 'take care of my own business' mindset names the hubris of thinking we can save ourselves  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]]
- **Borges, 'On Exactitude in Science'** _(literary)_ — An empire's map made full-scale becomes useless, illustrating that a story can't reproduce a life one-to-one.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Borges, 'Pierre Menard, Author of the Quixote'** _(literary)_ — A writer recopying Don Quixote line by line raises the question of what a copy, inspiration, and a faithful telling of a life really are.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Born on third base, thinks he hit a triple (Barry Switzer)** _(pop-culture)_ — Switzer's quip about inherited advantage illustrates life as a genetic lottery rather than a level playing field.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-in-christ|Freedom in Christ]]
- **Borrowing a cup of sugar** _(personal)_ — Childhood memory of knocking on a neighbor's door for sugar or eggs as everyday neighborliness now seen as quaint.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Bosses telling us to do things** _(personal)_ — We obey bosses based on buy-in or survival, framing how Jesus' singular command works differently.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Bowl games and championship-or-bust fandom** _(personal)_ — His mellowing from caring only about national championships toward enjoying lesser bowl games illustrates maturing expectations and the difference between good and bad endings.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Boy Dressed as Jesus at Halloween** _(personal)_ — A six-year-old in robe and sharpie beard came trick-or-treating as Jesus, illustrating how we receive a popular image of Jesus rather than Jesus himself.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **Boys Staying Alive by Staying Together** _(current-events)_ — The trapped boys survived in the dark because they stayed together, illustrating that on the narrow way of the cross we must walk together.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Bracing for Bad News** _(personal)_ — Telling a friend 'my world turned upside down today' makes them brace for bad news, like the empty tomb seemed at first.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Brand Fanatics: Chick-fil-A, Apple, Sports Teams** _(pop-culture)_ — Marketers turning customers into fanatics who don't think straight, illustrating how consumer culture sells us needs and pulls us in many directions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Brazilian jiu-jitsu and assessing power** _(personal)_ — The pastor's family taking up Brazilian jiu-jitsu, learning to judge real power by rolling with bigger/stronger opponents and surviving, illustrating that true power is not the same as size or apparent strength.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/spicy-mustard|Spicy Mustard]]
- **Brighter Than Any Wool a Comber Could Comb** _(literary)_ — The rare biblical word describing Jesus' clothes as whiter than any fuller/wool-comber could achieve in a lifetime, conveying the magnitude of Jesus' revealed glory.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Broken Spoke first meeting** _(personal)_ — Elizabeth and Scott first met at the Broken Spoke dance hall about twenty years ago, marking the long road of patience to their wedding day.  → Elizabeth and Scott
- **Brooklyn wrestling-ring dating mingle** _(current-events)_ — A WWE-style wrestling-match dating event in a Brooklyn warehouse, an adventurous, vulnerable act of faith toward another person.  → Every Act of Faith
- **Bruce's handwritten prayer for compassion** _(personal)_ — Bruce's own prayer asking God for kindness, humility, patience, and grace — showing his humble, growing faith in his own words.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Bruce Martin
- **Bruce's list of achieved life goals** _(personal)_ — A litany of goals Bruce set and met (marry Cheryl, build his own house, daughter Rachel, Master Electrician's License, 33-year business, horses and a barn) illustrating his determination and faithful follow-through.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Bruce Martin
- **Buche de Noel (Yule Log)** _(personal)_ — The exceedingly complicated Christmas yule log he is attempting this year stands for needless complexity we add, contrasted with Jesus' simple 'follow me.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it-2|What Are We Going to Do About It?]]
- **Bucket list / after-the-bucket list** _(other)_ — Reframing the bucket list as an 'after-the-bucket list' to ask what you've done to impact others after you die.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-communion-of-the-saints|I Believe in the Communion of the Saints]]
- **Bucket list origin and film** _(pop-culture)_ — Tracing 'bucket list' via Google nGrams to the 2007 Morgan Freeman film, contrasting life's wants with Christ as the true list.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Buckets / categorizing people** _(other)_ — Humans sort people, foods, and ideas into mental buckets to know what to do with them - framing the question of what makes us unique.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **Buechner: Bled For** _(literary)_ — Buechner's line that we are sinners 'cherished, forgiven, bleeding to be sure, but also bled for' captures gospel as bad news before good news.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Buff 'Yoked Jesus' meme** _(pop-culture)_ — A muscled, supplement-using Jesus shows how we remake him in the image we want  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Buffet and marginal utility** _(personal)_ — His childhood Golden Corral trips and eating dolmas at Dimassi's to get his money's worth illustrate marginal utility and how worldly abundance means consuming past the point of benefit.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/two-economies-heavenly-abundance-and-the-widow-s-mite|Two Economies: Heavenly Abundance and the Widow's Mite]]
- **Building a garden for the long term** _(nature)_ — Building a garden for the long term even at the cost of extra weekends pictures pursuing a life of excellence without cutting corners.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/jesus-our-advocate|Jesus Our Advocate]]
- **Building and decorating a house for the Lord** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor: paint the house with modesty, gild it with good deeds, and crown it with prayer to make the spirit a dwelling place for God.  → Prayer and Converse with God Is a Supreme Good
- **Building bass drum (boom...BOOM)** _(other)_ — An extended metaphor for the mounting tension of Jesus' march to the cross  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Building gardens in exile** _(biblical)_ — Jeremiah's counsel to build and plant in exile, balancing the call to go against reckless rootlessness.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Burgundy grape-harvest laborers** _(nature)_ — Hired pickers gather grapes they didn't grow, illustrating that laborers reap a harvest God already grew (prevenient grace)  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-harvest|The Harvest]]
- **Business-school CEOs and Myers-Briggs tests** _(other)_ — Jesus picks fishermen with no credentials, references, or probation, unlike modern hiring.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Businessweek and FastCompany CEO-routine articles** _(pop-culture)_ — Modern fascination with hot CEOs' daily habits paralleled (and contrasted) with learning from holy lives.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **By Water and the Spirit doctrinal statement** _(literary)_ — UMC baptism document framing humanity's condition, prevenient grace, sacraments, and the baptismal covenant  → [[covenant-umc/2025/baptism|Baptism]]
- **Bûche de Noël yule log** _(personal)_ — An exceedingly complicated Christmas cake recipe illustrates the needless complications we add, unlike Jesus' simple call.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it|What are we going to do about it?]]
- **C.S. Lewis on being 'Surprised by Joy'** _(literary)_ — Lewis's idea that we should be surprised by joy rather than pain supports joy as gift rather than reward.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **C.S. Lewis's Liar, Lunatic, or Lord** _(literary)_ — Lewis's trilemma offered as another threefold formula for grasping who Jesus is.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **C.S. Lewis's The Screwtape Letters** _(literary)_ — demons portrayed as laughable; the temptation is to get stuck in your own head  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **C.S. Lewis, The Great Divorce** _(literary)_ — Lewis's character who delays heaven to finish his research illustrates the temptation to fixate on questions instead of Christ, the answer to every question.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **C.S. Lewis, The Great Divorce — Pam and the ghost mother** _(literary)_ — The ghost mother who can't release her son into God's hands illustrates love distorted into possession and the drag of building life on relationships.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Cabela's or Academy stores** _(pop-culture)_ — You couldn't just buy nets at a sporting-goods store, underscoring how costly and personal the disciples' nets were.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Cain and Abel** _(biblical)_ — John's blunt advice 'don't be like Cain' shows how a heart without love leads to death and to causing death.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/spilling-things-god-offers-us-a-chance-for-a-do-over|Spilling Things: God Offers Us a Chance for a Do-Over]]
- **Cain and Abel** _(biblical)_ — Cain's question 'Am I my brother's keeper?' frames the ancient human struggle over how wide to draw the circle of responsibility.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/responsibility|Responsibility]]
- **Cake vs. chips and salsa** _(personal)_ — Different foods tempting different people illustrates that temptation is personal and always disguised as something good.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/lead-us-not-into-temptation|Lead Us Not into Temptation]]
- **Calling a child 'very ordinary'** _(other)_ — A parent calling a child 'very ordinary' shows how the word is misheard as a put-down, reframing ordinary time as necessary rather than pejorative.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Camp Eagle Minister's Escape Retreat** _(personal)_ — Upcoming family retreat at Camp Eagle in Rock Springs, anticipated as a true rest with no meetings or rules to make.  → October Pastor Report
- **Can opener and toilet brush** _(other)_ — Mundane 'useful' tools to show how calling scripture merely 'useful' undersells it.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **Can't believe they liked that restaurant or movie** _(personal)_ — Judging other people's judgments is easier than judging a person, showing how anesthetized our discernment is  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]]
- **Canaanite woman's faith** _(biblical)_ — The woman who begs Jesus for crumbs is 'good soil' not by status or blood but by desperate faith—an example of faith in desperation.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **Canadian Couple Who Stayed** _(personal)_ — A couple from Canada wept with the preacher and walked him out of the canyon, embodying solidarity that reminds the sufferer he is not alone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Candle that burns down on its own** _(nature)_ — Letting a candle burn all night until it putters out shows that neglect extinguishes the light as surely as covering it.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Capybaras as Lenten fish in Argentina** _(current-events)_ — Catholics not counting fish, or capybaras in Argentina, as meat on Fridays, a humorous example of how fasting rules get drawn.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **Car stuck spinning in mud or sand** _(personal)_ — A functional car spinning its wheels in mud is the most frustrating kind of stuck — illustrating being stuck while everything seems fine.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-opened|Be Opened]]
- **Caravaggio's Entombment of Christ** _(historical)_ — The preacher's high point of baroque depictions of Jesus  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Card in the wallet to prove salvation** _(other)_ — Judgment is not Christ checking a wallet card to verify you said a prayer or did a thing, but how we respond to God's call.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-will-come-to-judge-the-living-and-the-dead|He Will Come to Judge the Living and the Dead]]
- **Carl Henry, Billy Graham, and the rise of 'evangelical'** _(historical)_ — Post-WWII figures who used 'evangelical' to differentiate from fundamentalists, showing how the term shifted sociologically over seventy years.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Carlsbad Caverns Absolute Darkness** _(personal)_ — The shocking experience of absolute darkness in Carlsbad Caverns illustrates how startling true darkness is and why light matters.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/animals-and-god-s-promise-children-s-message-the-true-light-already-shines|Animals and God's Promise (Children's Message): The True Light Already Shines]]
- **Caroline asking daily if it was Sunday** _(personal)_ — Marty's account of Caroline asking every day whether it was Sunday illustrates her deep connection to and delight in worship.  → Caroline Miller (Memorial Service)
- **Caroline welcoming the pastor at her home** _(personal)_ — The pastor's first visit three years prior, when Caroline's joyful welcome surprised him, illustrates her natural, unforced love.  → Caroline Miller (Memorial Service)
- **Carpenter from Nazareth** _(biblical)_ — The image of a carpenter from Nazareth born 2000 years ago illustrates that the highest we can hope to become looks like Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/we-are-god-s-children-but-we-are-not-finished-yet|We Are God's Children, But We Are Not Finished Yet]]
- **Carrying hot water to a neighbor** _(personal)_ — Wilson walked a mile and a half to bring hot water to a stove-less neighbor but could have done more—self-examination of whether God's light shone in him or excused selfishness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Carrying water in the Chisos at Big Bend** _(personal)_ — Hauling your own water in the backcountry teaches you how precious water is, opening the theme of water and baptism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/baptism|Baptism]]
- **Casablanca and the choice between past and future** _(pop-culture)_ — The 1942 film's crux (Rick and Ilsa choosing between rekindling the past or moving toward a better future) frames letting go of the glory days for greater things to come.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
- **Casablanca's Rick and Ilsa** _(pop-culture)_ — A good (their love) competes with a greater good, illustrating rival goods  → [[covenant-umc/2025/no-distinction|No Distinction]]
- **Cassette tapes and letters from deployed husband Donald** _(personal)_ — Pre-FaceTime correspondence during Donald's military deployments depicting the strain and intimacy of military family life.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Cassette tapes and letters instead of FaceTime** _(personal)_ — A military family staying connected across distance through recorded cassettes and letters before modern video calls.  → Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Casting a new rod for the first time** _(other)_ — First attempts at prayer feel awkward like casting a new fishing rod, but practice brings you into the rhythm of God's life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Cat people versus dog people** _(personal)_ — The everyday divide between cat lovers and dog lovers, and how the two animals live differently, introduces the theme of creatures cohabiting.  → Pet Blessing
- **Catching fish with bare hands like a bear** _(nature)_ — You can catch fish barehanded like a bear but can't live on it, so the tools of faith are like nets making discipleship sustainable.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Categorizing into Buckets** _(personal)_ — We sort foods, restaurants, vacations, jobs, and people into liked/disliked buckets to process the world, illustrating the earthly mind.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Catherine of Siena's dialogue with God** _(historical)_ — Catherine's dialogue teaches that we love our neighbors what we cannot repay to God, counted as done for him.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Catherine of Siena's dialogue with God** _(historical)_ — Catherine's dialogue—God redirecting her love toward neighbors since she cannot repay him—models gratitude as actions of mercy toward the unlovable.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Catherine of Siena's dialogue with God** _(historical)_ — God tells Catherine to love neighbors as she cannot repay him, modeling gratitude as mercy toward the unlovable.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **Catholic Marian doctrines explained** _(historical)_ — Distinguishing the Annunciation, Assumption, and Immaculate Conception to clear up Protestant misunderstandings and recover Mary as a biblical witness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Catholic priest confession in movies** _(pop-culture)_ — The cinematic confessional scene illustrates Protestant discomfort with confession and the idea of an intermediary.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Cave paintings at Seminole Canyon near Del Rio** _(personal)_ — Thousands-of-year-old cave art near the Rio Grande shows humanity's ancient power of making images.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/all-peoples-and-creatures|All Peoples and Creatures]]
- **Celebrity at the dinner party** _(pop-culture)_ — Missing the one dinner where Tom Brady, Beyoncé, or Taylor Swift shows up, illustrating both Thomas's unfair miss and his cautious disbelief of his friends.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-concrete-faith-of-thomas|The Concrete Faith of Thomas]]
- **Center never fumbles a snap in a 72-6 loss** _(other)_ — Praising a minor detail done right while losing the whole game illustrates the Pharisees missing the point of faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-opposite-of-hypocrisy|The Opposite of Hypocrisy]]
- **Central Texas Nut Grass** _(nature)_ — Unlike tasty Cypriot weeds, local nut grass won't flavor pasta, underscoring that not all weeds are secretly good but judgment is still not ours.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **CEOs, Senators, and Bishops on power** _(personal)_ — People in authority think more about what they can't do than what they can, showing power is not freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **Changing belief like an outfit** _(other)_ — Picking a belief like choosing to 'wear red today' shows that genuine faith is not fickle and is formed by how we live.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Changing his major from economics to English and history for seminary** _(personal)_ — Switching majors didn't change who he fundamentally was but stopped him lying to himself about what made a life worth living.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Chapel with Bee Loved and songs for the children** _(personal)_ — Writing Bible songs for preschoolers to plant memories of God's action, showing teaching stories matters even if data alone gives the Bible no value.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Charles Ives' Central Park in the Dark** _(literary)_ — Ives' composition of two marching bands crashing in different time signatures as a musical image of the cacophony of life's overwhelming options.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Charles Wesley hymn ''Tis Love! 'Tis Love!'** _(literary)_ — A favorite theologically-rich hymn quoted to show how 18th-19th century hymnody dug deeply into who God is.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **Charles Wesley hymn 'And Can It Be'** _(literary)_ — the preacher's favorite hymn, sung two ways, voices amazement that Christ died and won the crown for us  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]], [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]], Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Charles Wesley hymn 'He emptied himself of all but love'** _(literary)_ — The Wesley hymn line illustrates Christ's self-emptying love as the pattern of being now possible for us through God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/we-are-god-s-children-but-we-are-not-finished-yet|We Are God's Children, But We Are Not Finished Yet]]
- **Charles Wesley hymn 'He left his Father's throne above'** _(literary)_ — The hymn's 'he emptied himself of all but love' models the goal of discipleship: emptying ourselves of everything that isn't love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Charles Wesley's 'And Can It Be'** _(literary)_ — The hymn line 'emptied himself of all but love' frames Christ's self-emptying as the pattern for shedding all that is not love.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-is-christ|Who is Christ?]]
- **Charles Wesley's 'Christ the Lord Is Risen Today'** _(literary)_ — The hymn's 'Where, O death, is now thy sting?' frames the service's triumph-over-death theme.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]]
- **Charles Wesley's 'My Chains Fell Off'** _(literary)_ — The hymn line evoking the liberating release that comes from an encounter with Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **Charles Wesley's hymn 'Come, O Thou Traveler Unknown'** _(literary)_ — Wesley's hymn, quoted throughout, reframes Jacob's wrestling as the pattern of the whole life of faith clinging to God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Charles Weymann and the chassis** _(historical)_ — The aviator-turned-engineer who in 1921 transformed the wooden wagon frame into the modern chassis shows that a church needs a true structural foundation, not just an engine on a wagon.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Charlie's Proverbs class** _(personal)_ — A congregant's well-researched class shows the value of reading scripture together, though no one has the final word.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **Charlton Heston in The Ten Commandments / Ben-Hur** _(pop-culture)_ — Sword-and-sandal film epics show how we mythologize biblical history as distant and unreal, when in fact it intersects our shared human world.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **ChatGPT and Claude as AI** _(current-events)_ — Large language models rebranded as AI raise the agency question—does any object have free choice?  → Drawn by the Father
- **Chattanooga / Missionary Ridge assault** _(historical)_ — The Union's later successful charge up a fortified ridge shows Pickett's Charge was a feasible, contingent gamble, not mere folly.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **Cheap Grace's Social Media Team** _(other)_ — Cheap grace is shiny, slick, well-packaged and 'has a wonderful social media team,' dramatizing how a no-cost faith is marketed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Cheating on the Quiz** _(personal)_ — Older kids cheating to impress the teacher shows how a teacher (like God) can't help if they don't know where you actually are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/start-from-where-you-are|Start From Where You Are]]
- **Check yourself before you wreck yourself** _(pop-culture)_ — The hip-hop catchphrase reframes Wesley's first General Rule, do no harm, as honest self-examination.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Chemical weed killers** _(nature)_ — Modern reliance on chemicals to kill weeds carries unintended consequences, illustrating how the word grows up choked amid competing weeds.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **Chewing rocks / grit in food** _(personal)_ — The painful experience of biting sand or a pebble shows bodies aren't made to eat stones, setting up the temptation to wish stones were bread.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eating-stones|Eating Stones]]
- **Chewing with your mouth closed** _(personal)_ — Being told manners versus seeing food dribble out shows the gap between being told something and embodying it.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/subterranean-hometown-blues|Subterranean Hometown Blues]]
- **Chex-mix for a car, ruby for a paperback** _(other)_ — Lopsided gift exchanges illustrate the humiliation of receiving a gift you cannot reciprocate, like God's love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Chicago Bears beating prevent defense** _(current-events)_ — The Bears scoring easily against weak prevent defenses shows how injustice spreads when we give away 'first down after first down.'  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Chicken or the Egg** _(other)_ — The unanswerable paradox illustrates contradictions that assume a false rivalry between two things on the same plane.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Child crying for its mother's milk** _(other)_ — The praying spirit clings to God like a child crying for its mother and craving the milk God provides.  → Prayer and Converse with God Is a Supreme Good
- **Child learning to tie shoes: 'I do it by myself'** _(personal)_ — the preacher's youngest insisting on doing it alone pictures the prideful 'I've got this' attitude toward God and salvation  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]]
- **Child or friend saying 'don't freak out'** _(personal)_ — A kid or friend prefacing news with 'don't freak out' parallels angels' first words and signals something genuinely alarming.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]]
- **Child tasting cake for the first time** _(nature)_ — A toddler going crazy over their first birthday cake pictures eternal joy and satisfaction that never grows old in God's presence.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/new-creation|New Creation]]
- **Child's bedtime thirst** _(personal)_ — The thirstiest person in the world is every child at bedtime begging for water, beating even a desert traveler.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Childhood as a Modern Concept** _(historical)_ — In ancient Rome and Israel there was no notion of childhood as a distinct stage; the Greek word for child is genderless, underscoring children's low status.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]]
- **Childhood Family Christmas Eve Reading of Luke 2** _(personal)_ — The preacher's family read Luke 2 every Christmas Eve, and the odd name 'Quirinius' always struck him, illustrating how we recite the text without digging into its real historical roots.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **Childhood memorization of Psalm 23 and the Lord's Prayer** _(personal)_ — The KJV Psalm 23 and Lord's Prayer learned as a child hold pride of place in his imagination and link straying to shepherd-and-sheep imagery.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Children Barred from Restaurants and Bars** _(other)_ — Modern spaces that exclude children because they are unpredictable and unruly, illustrating the instinct the disciples had to keep children away from Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]]
- **Children playing together on a playground** _(nature)_ — Kids instantly playing regardless of skin color, language, or status shows the childlike openness Jesus praises over worldly 'wisdom.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Children's number-scaling song** _(other)_ — A child's call-and-response counting song ('more than 10? more than 50?') illustrates the uncapped, ever-growing scale of those Jesus feeds.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/how-many-people-did-jesus-feed|How many people did Jesus feed?]]
- **Children's song about foot washing** _(personal)_ — A children's song the preacher wrote ('If you want to follow me, I have to wash your feet...') distills the heart of following Christ through table and service.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Children's song about the wedding at Cana** _(personal)_ — The pastor's own children's song ('Jesus likes to party... he turns the water into wine') frames celebration as part of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Chimamanda Ngozi Adichie, 'The Danger of a Single Story'** _(pop-culture)_ — The novelist's TED talk and roommate anecdote illustrate how a single story flattens people and obscures complexity, paralleling how we read the Bible.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/miriam-miriam|Miriam, Miriam]]
- **Chips and salsa in college** _(personal)_ — The preacher ate only chips and salsa for dinner in college, hungry for it yet never satisfied.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Chocolate cake vs. chips and salsa** _(personal)_ — Different food cravings illustrate that temptation must actually be tempting to you to count as temptation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/holding-the-faith-doing-what-you-say|Holding the Faith: Doing What You Say]]
- **Choosing a partner** _(personal)_ — Selecting a partner means deselecting others, a zero-sum choice based on attributes, contrasted with God's gracious election.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/election-not-selection|Election, Not Selection]]
- **Choosing restaurants and dishwashers** _(other)_ — Nobody picks the three-star restaurant or the two-star dishwasher over the Wirecutter best, illustrating our aversion to the ordinary.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Chris and Animal Planet** _(personal)_ — Chris loved animals and would take over the TV to watch Animal Planet, illustrating her big-hearted, caring nature.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Christine Renée Jarl
- **Chris's Song 'Half a World Away'** _(personal)_ — A song shared by Chris whose chorus about seeing morning and stars at the same time across distance illustrates how we both find ourselves and hide in music, and stay connected to those far away.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/half-a-world-away|Half a World Away]]
- **Christ Fills Your Bucket List** _(other)_ — Christ has already filled your bucket list, so all life's small joys are a bonus next to the joy of being chosen and loved by God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]], [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Christ the First-Fruits of Those Asleep** _(biblical)_ — The risen Christ as the first-fruits of those who have fallen asleep, picturing resurrection as the harvest's opening promise.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-sermon|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom Sermon)]]
- **Christ's open communion table** _(personal)_ — The pastor's recurring words at the table—'This is Christ's table, all are welcome'—illustrate communion's low barrier of entry.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Christian conferencing as a grow group** _(other)_ — Wesley's 'Christian conferencing' means transformational conversation about where God is working, like a grow group, as a means of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Christian Wiman on wounds and love** _(literary)_ — Wiman's line that life tears us apart but tended wounds let love enter, illustrating how suffering and evil can become channels of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/evil|Evil]]
- **Christians as vultures turning on their own** _(other)_ — When the church is at its worst, members act like vultures and Pharisees toward each other instead of protecting all who enter.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/shelter|Shelter]]
- **Christina Rossetti and 'In the Bleak Midwinter'** _(literary)_ — The poet overshadowed by her brother Dante, later set to music by Gustav Holst, frames the carol whose closing question—give him my heart—answers how we say yes to God.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Christmas candlelight service** _(personal)_ — Holding a candle until it drips and goes out illustrates the forgetfulness that snuffs out God's light.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Christmas Day Falling on a Sunday** _(current-events)_ — Churches canceling worship when Christmas lands on a Sunday illustrates treating the Lord's Day as optional.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **Christmas decoration boxes** _(personal)_ — Finding boxes of stuff you no longer need while decorating, asking what paths in your life need to be made straight.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-four-questions-who-is-coming|The Four Questions: Who Is Coming?]]
- **Christmas Tree as Baptized Pagan Symbol Myth** _(historical)_ — The claim that the Christmas tree is a baptized pagan symbol has no basis in history; Europe was already Christianized.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-cultivation-of-christmas-trees|The Cultivation of Christmas Trees]]
- **Christmas Trees Are Not Historically Accurate to Bethlehem** _(historical)_ — Fir trees don't grow near Bethlehem and there were no trees that first Christmas, raising why we keep the tradition at all.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-cultivation-of-christmas-trees|The Cultivation of Christmas Trees]]
- **Chronos and Kairos** _(literary)_ — Two Greek words for time—linear chronos vs. event-time kairos (Christmas memories, falling in love at 4am, holding a baby)—to argue God is beyond time.  → Drawn by the Father
- **Chronos vs. kairos** _(other)_ — The two Greek words for time distinguish clock-time from the event-time worship aims for.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **Chrysostom's Five Paths of Repentance** _(historical)_ — John Chrysostom's homily laying out five accessible paths of repentance—self-condemnation, forgiving enemies, prayer, almsgiving, and humility—frames the whole sermon.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/where-two-or-three|Where two or three…]]
- **Chuck E. Cheese and Urban Air** _(personal)_ — Kids' play places where freedom means maximizing fun in a constrained bubble, a metaphor for a self-indulgent grown-up freedom cut off from the real world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **Church as Hospital for the Soul** _(other)_ — The church is not a place where everyone has it figured out but a hospital for the weary and heavy laden in need of care and hope.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Church consultant fads** _(other)_ — Parking spaces, contemporary worship, and the 80%-capacity building rule are passing trends ('sand') that change like fashion and cannot be the church's foundation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Church in the 50s** _(other)_ — The new creation doesn't look like church in the 1950s but like a life boldly offered to others through grace.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]]
- **Church Lady ('Could it be Satan?')** _(pop-culture)_ — Dana Carvey's SNL sketch shows how demons became cultural punchlines detached from Scripture  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **Church Membership as Body vs. Country Club** _(personal)_ — Joining the church gives responsibilities not perks, unlike a country club membership at Barton Creek or the Hills of Stonehouse Vineyard with golf and member-only events.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **Church of the Resurrection visitor privileges revoked** _(other)_ — The pastor telling new members their 'visitor privileges have been revoked' illustrates that joining means becoming one who welcomes others.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **Church plant with no legacy costs** _(personal)_ — The preacher's discernment about church planting revealed he was not called to the cleanness of escaping legacy costs.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Church roofs shaped like boat hulls (Noah's ark)** _(historical)_ — The church anciently compared to Noah's ark carrying the word of God illustrates the vessels through which people move through the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Church storm damage and insurance deductible** _(current-events)_ — Recent rains knocked out the AC, internet, and flooded the building with a $100,000 insurance deductible, illustrating not building storehouses but giving in a hard summer giving season.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Churches leaving the UMC over property** _(current-events)_ — People who left the United Methodist Church told the preacher their departure was really about control of property, not human sexuality.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **Churches on the block** _(personal)_ — Baptist, Church of Christ, Buddhist temple, and Imani community church all within blocks illustrate the absurdity-yet-reality of denominationalism.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Churches that run like corporations serving shareholders** _(other)_ — Transactional, member-as-shareholder churches contrast with believing in Christ's body acting beyond what we see.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-holy-catholic-church|I Believe in the Holy Catholic Church]]
- **Clarity of a final sports score** _(personal)_ — the preacher's love of the clear winner/loser at game's end illustrates the human craving for clarity over biblical ambiguity  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]]
- **Classical double bass practice and lessons in Houston** _(personal)_ — The pastor's teenage Saturday drives to study bass illustrate worldly 'sacrifice' as giving something up versus biblical sacrifice.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacrifice|Sacrifice]]
- **Clay jars holding treasure** _(biblical)_ — Paul's image of treasure in fragile clay pots that take blows yet are not broken shows God revealed through our struggle.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Cleaning business customers** _(personal)_ — Chris was a constant, caregiving presence to her long-term cleaning-business customers, many present at the service, illustrating faithfulness toward people.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Christine Renée Jarl
- **Clearer holy books** _(other)_ — The Bhagavad Gita and Quran are comparatively clear, unlike the layered, non-immediate Bible.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-lamb-of-god|The Lamb of God]]
- **Cleveland Marathon** _(personal)_ — The preacher's first marathon in Cleveland, run socially at an easy pace, illustrates life as a race that need not be a competition.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-race|The Race]]
- **Climbing and respecting the mountain** _(personal)_ — Teaching his kids to fear and respect a mountain's power illustrates fear-as-respect versus fear-as-insecurity.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/do-or-do-not|Do Or Do Not]]
- **Climbing films (Free Solo, The Dawn Wall, Meru)** _(pop-culture)_ — The preacher's love of climbing documentaries, especially Meru's unclimbable peak, frames communion as the truly impossible summit offered freely.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Clint Briggs in Spirited** _(pop-culture)_ — The seemingly unredeemable Scrooge-figure PR maven from the film Spirited illustrates how the prodigal could squander himself by using people, not just money.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Closing on a house** _(other)_ — Wanting Jesus to 'just tell us' if he's the Messiah mirrors wanting to reach closing and be done  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **Closing Song: On a Scale of One to Ten Your Love Is Eleven** _(literary)_ — A sung paraphrase of the prayer's opening lines reframing 'hallowed be thy name' as not shaming God and love that 'changed the game.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **Coaching the Packers flag football team** _(personal)_ — Wilson's youth flag football team beating the rude Vikings illustrates rivalry and the call to love one's enemies.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/rivals|Rivals]]
- **Coffee shop deathbed-question evangelism** _(personal)_ — A stranger interrupting the pastor's reading to ask where he'd go if he died that moment, offered as one (off-putting) model of evangelism.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Coffee shop evangelism question** _(personal)_ — A man asked the preacher in high school where he'd go if he died right then, raising the valid question of what happens after death.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting-2|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Coffee shop evangelist** _(personal)_ — A stranger in high school asked the pastor where he'd go if he died right then, raising the valid question of what happens after death.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Coke or Pepsi, Plaid or Stripes, A&M or Texas** _(other)_ — Trivial consumer and team-loyalty choices illustrate how modern freedom-of-choice is really servitude to a sin-limited heart, not biblical freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **Collapse of the Roman Empire** _(historical)_ — A leading theory blames Rome's fall on over-leveraged debt and broken economic communication, not its conversion to Christianity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Colleagues' century-old stained glass nightmares** _(personal)_ — Contrasts huge high-maintenance windows with Covenant's small, focused, timeless ones  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **College kids on the quad or south mall** _(other)_ — How people lingered at the Temple/Portico of Solomon as a casual gathering place  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **College Station vs. Longhorn pride** _(personal)_ — The pastor's hometown of College Station and his UT loyalty parody the 'what good comes from there?' dismissal.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **Colonial Tax Crisis and Romans 13** _(historical)_ — Colonists wrestling with Paul's command to obey authorities versus rebelling against the king illustrates that kingdom-talk is political.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/thy-kingdom-come-thy-will-be-done-on-earth-as-it-is-in-heaven|Thy Kingdom Come, Thy Will Be Done, on Earth as It Is in Heaven]]
- **Colorado Bend camping under a burn ban** _(personal)_ — A family camping trip where a burn ban prevented building a fire, introducing fire's mesmerizing draw and its three components.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Columbus funded by debt instruments** _(historical)_ — Columbus's voyage was financed through complex debt schemes engineered to dodge usury laws.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Combed woolen cloth** _(nature)_ — The Greek image of cloth combed thousands of times to white, picturing the brilliance of Christ transfigured.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Come and see (John 1)** _(biblical)_ — Jesus inviting Philip, Andrew, and Nathanael with the simple words 'come and see' models evangelism as invitation rather than argument or conversion-pressure.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Come O Thou Traveler Unknown** _(literary)_ — The hymn sung the prior week, based on Jacob's wrestling, frames the sermon's meditation on struggle and healing.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Come well across languages** _(other)_ — French/Spanish/German welcome words (bienvenue, bienvenido, Willkommen) share the root 'come well,' showing welcome crosses cultures.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **Comfort is the goal of a mattress company** _(other)_ — Quips that comfort is a mattress company's goal, not faith's, since faith offers something more than ease.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Comfortable life in Spicewood, Texas, 2022** _(personal)_ — God did not create so the congregation could enjoy a comfortable life in Spicewood, illustrating that faith is not a tool for our comfort or status quo.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Commercial Advent calendars** _(current-events)_ — Dog-treat and wine advent calendars mark December, not the Christian calendar, showing the easy-to-sell first meaning of Advent versus the hard second meaning.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Common English Bible rendering of the Beatitudes ('Happy are the hopeless')** _(literary)_ — The jarring CEB translation of makarios as 'happy' reframes the Beatitudes away from impossible ideal toward God's presence.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Communion table's two perspectives** _(other)_ — The plain Hawaiian bread, grape juice, and gluten-free crackers seen as heavenly manna through Jesus' eyes.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Communion's low barrier vs foot washing's high barrier** _(other)_ — Communion has a low barrier of entry (all welcome, no metaphysics quiz) while foot washing has a high one, exposing the limits we set.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Comparing church attendance and preaching to others** _(personal)_ — The pastor admits how hard it is in practice not to measure his church's attendance or his preaching against others.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-in-christ|Freedom in Christ]]
- **Complicated gourmet recipes (Alinea, Noma, Meadowood)** _(personal)_ — The preacher's love of elaborate multi-component restaurant recipes shows complexity is knowable but takes extra time.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it|What are we going to do about it?]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it-2|What Are We Going to Do About It?]]
- **Confirmation Sunday confirmands** _(personal)_ — Three young people confirming their faith are meant to change the church toward Jesus, illustrating the church's call to move rather than conform newcomers to existing tastes.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Conservation of Energy** _(nature)_ — As energy is neither created nor destroyed, humans do not originate love; we only direct the love God gave us first.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **Constantine and Reformation rulers grabbing power** _(historical)_ — From Constantine to Protestant princes, leaders co-opted the gospel for control, yet a faithful witness persisted  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Construction Mission Trips** _(personal)_ — Introverted, rough dads on roofing mission trips who opened up about failures and fears while working, showing how shared labor reveals character and forges community.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/laborers-in-the-vineyard-grace-community-and-mortality|Laborers in the Vineyard: Grace, Community, and Mortality]]
- **Contracts and title agents** _(other)_ — The quid-pro-quo world of closing documents and distrust contrasted with the free gift of salvation in Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Conversation with a strident atheist** _(personal)_ — A first-time encounter with a pastor by a man who knew only addict or mooch Christians, met with listening and love rather than argument.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Cool kids and crushes at elementary school** _(personal)_ — His kids' talk of crushes and the cool kids illustrates the universal in-group/out-group dynamic the church transcends.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-holy-catholic-church|I Believe in the Holy Catholic Church]]
- **Cool Kids vs. Band Geeks** _(other)_ — Whether popular or a weirdo/band geek, every person is worthy of God's love and inclusion.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **Corded telephones and the pre-Internet world** _(personal)_ — How hard it is to convey the past to the present, framing the difficulty of explaining what the Temple meant  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **Costumes We Wear** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor of costumes (gender, ethnicity, victimhood, fixing others) that society makes us perform and that the word of God cuts through.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Council of Jerusalem and James changing his mind** _(biblical)_ — James humbly discerning the Spirit moving against his preconceptions models openness over rigid organizational thinking  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Counterfactual of Gabriel visiting another woman** _(other)_ — An imagined alternate history where another woman refuses or attacks the angel shows how rational 'no' would have been and magnifies Mary's 'yes.'  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Courtroom and government oaths** _(current-events)_ — Swearing 'to tell the truth, the whole truth' and military/government oaths run against Jesus' 'let your yes be yes' — true honesty rests on faith that Jesus is Lord, not on a system to adjudicate truth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-line-jesus-and-the-inversion-of-our-moral-expectations|The Line: Jesus and the Inversion of Our Moral Expectations]]
- **Cousin Ross's dig at Wilson's football knowledge** _(personal)_ — Ross teasing Wilson sets up the self-deprecating coaching story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/rivals|Rivals]]
- **Cousin stationed in Kazakhstan** _(personal)_ — The fear when his cousin flew over enemy airspace illustrates the long, hard cost of 20 years of service since 9/11.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Cousin Stationed in Kazakhstan** _(personal)_ — The fear of his cousin flying over enemy airspace, illustrating the hard cost of 20 years of war and service.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **Covenant campus security plan** _(personal)_ — The church's large-campus security and the calm move-to-the-locked-exit plan illustrating preparedness without panic amid the world's brokenness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Covenant food pantry volunteers** _(personal)_ — an open-handed ministry that thrives by offering many people the chance to do a mitzvah, feeding immigrants from many nations  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **Covenant Preschool Chapel & The Wise Man's House** _(personal)_ — Teaching the preschool chapel song about building on the rock frames love as the foundation that lets the soul survive the storms.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **Covenant Social Club** _(other)_ — If the church stops welcoming, forgiving, and healing, it might as well change its sign to 'Covenant Social Club' rather than be the Body of Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-gate|The Gate]]
- **Covenant stained glass nativity window** _(personal)_ — The manger window at Covenant grounds the sermon's meditation on the incarnation as God-with-us.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Covenant stained glass Resurrection window** _(other)_ — The fourth window in the sermon series, depicting the cross, used as the visual anchor for the gift of Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-jesus|The Gift of Jesus]]
- **Covenant UMC 43rd birthday and its challenges** _(personal)_ — The church's aging campus (roofs, separating stucco, AC units), monthly deficit, and ministry challenges show that hope in a God of resurrection means facing hard realities rather than ignoring them.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/not-easy-to-watch|Not Easy to Watch]]
- **Covenant UMC capital campaign and debt** _(current-events)_ — The church's campaign to pay down debt is a chance for today's members to add their names to the legacy of builders, like Nehemiah refusing to be trapped by past decisions.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/what-s-next-builders-and-names|What's Next? (Builders and Names)]]
- **Covenant UMC pledge cards / stewardship drive** _(current-events)_ — Launching a stewardship drive with pledge cards as an act of faith and discernment, giving for 2054 not just 2024.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **Covenant UMC stained glass windows series** _(personal)_ — Launches a sermon series on the sanctuary's stained glass, beginning with the Ten Commandments window  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Covenant's stained-glass parables window** _(other)_ — The unlabeled lamp window depicts the parables as one of Jesus' gifts in the sermon series on the church windows.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-parables|The Parables]]
- **COVID-19 viral load study** _(current-events)_ — A May study suggesting nasal breathing mitigates COVID viral load supports the health case for proper breathing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Cowboys, Longhorns, Aggies loyalties** _(other)_ — Cities, jobs, families, and sports teams are the assorted burdens people choose to carry.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Crashing the sailboat into the dock on Lake LBJ** _(personal)_ — The preacher finishes last week's story of taking a sailboat out alone, crashing into a dock, and being paddled back humiliated by Dominic, illustrating that we are already embarked in the life of faith and sometimes stuck in endless, blameless shipwrecks.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]]
- **Credit and credo sharing a Latin root** _(other)_ — Banks extending credit ('he/she believes you are good for your word') is the everyday place most people encounter belief, contrasted with credo.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Creeds omit heaven at the end** _(historical)_ — The Apostles', Nicene, and Athanasian creeds never mention heaven as a destination after death, focusing instead on bodily resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Cross vs. fish as Christian symbols** _(historical)_ — Neither symbol was universal in the early church; the true sign of a Christian is mercy, not a badge or emblem on your car  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Cross-Washing** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor: wearing Christian shirts and going through motions to avoid confronting sin and loving neighbors.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **CrossFit / composting / shopping affinity buddies** _(other)_ — Shared-hobby friendships show 'affinity' relationships where you sacrifice nothing of yourself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Crowd shifts from Hosanna to Crucify** _(biblical)_ — The same crowd that shouted Hosanna turned to 'Crucify him' when Jesus drove out money-changers instead of Romans, illustrating a Messiah unlike the one they expected.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Crowd singing Hosanna together** _(other)_ — The good feeling of singing the same song in a group illustrates how the power of crowds can drive praise without knowing why.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Crucifixion of Spartacus's rebels** _(historical)_ — The mass crucifixion of rebellious slaves shows crucifixion was used not only to kill but to intimidate and warn.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Cultural portraits of Jesus** _(historical)_ — Images of Jesus across cultures show we tend to find a Jesus who looks like us rather than the Jesus of the Bible.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
- **Cup of cold water in a broken land** _(biblical)_ — Prevenient grace pictured as mercy given freely, a cup of cold water rather than an act of human will.  → Drawn by the Father
- **Cup refilled at church** _(personal)_ — People say they pour themselves out all week and need their cup refilled at church — a good but shallow answer to why we worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/why-do-we-worship-the-assembly-and-the-bread-of-life|Why Do We Worship? The Assembly and the Bread of Life]]
- **Curly from City Slickers** _(pop-culture)_ — Curly's line 'Day ain't over yet' mocks consoling ourselves with 'at least I didn't kill anyone today.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-line-jesus-and-the-inversion-of-our-moral-expectations|The Line: Jesus and the Inversion of Our Moral Expectations]]
- **Curtain rising at the start of a play** _(other)_ — The stage revealed when the curtain goes up illustrates 'apocalypse' as revelation that Jesus is Lord here and now.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-do-we-hope-for|What do we hope for?]]
- **Curtain rising at the start of a play** _(literary)_ — The stage curtain going up to reveal the set illustrates apokalypsis/revelation as Christ's lordship unveiled here and now.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eternal-life|Eternal Life]]
- **Curvatus / construction or destruction business question** _(literary)_ — The Augustinian image of the self curved inward (curvatus in se) framed as 'Are you in the construction business or the destruction business?' to contrast sin's self-destruction with God's order.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/sin-as-rebellion-the-freedom-not-to-rebel|Sin as Rebellion: The Freedom Not to Rebel]]
- **Cyprian on doing the will of the Father** _(historical)_ — A quoted passage from Saint Cyprian on humility, faith, and standing by Christ's cross defines what it means to be a coheir with Christ and follow him through storms.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Cyprus Cook's Oregano Weed** _(personal)_ — A documentary cook's delicious wild oregano growing between driveway bricks shows that what looks like a weed may be good, since we are not the planters.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Dabbling in Mandarin Chinese** _(personal)_ — The preacher's halting attempt at Mandarin/Japanese characters illustrates how we can use the same word ('sin') yet mean entirely different things from what God means.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Daniel playing the cello** _(personal)_ — A church member studying cello at UT performs the Fifth Suite prelude as the sermon's living example of using one's gifts.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Dante's Inferno vs. the biblical 'placeholder' Hell** _(literary)_ — Dante's detailed geography of Hell is contrasted with the Bible's much vaguer placeholder language (Sheol, Gehenna, Hades), reframing the real question as who (if anyone) is there, not what it looks like.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Dark and stormy night cliche** _(literary)_ — A clichéd line that tells instead of shows, jarring the reader out of imagination.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/cantata-show-don-t-tell|Cantata: Show, Don't Tell]]
- **Dark skies of Fort Davis and Big Bend** _(nature)_ — West Texas star-filled skies evoke the ancient world's far deeper relationship to the stars before light pollution.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Darkness as absence of light** _(nature)_ — There are no 'darkness particles'; darkness is only the absence of light, just as evil is only a corruption or absence of the good.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **Darrell K. Royal** _(historical)_ — Reference to Texas coach Darrell K. Royal's saying that three things happen when you throw the ball (two bad), evoking Glynis's devotion to UT.  → Glynis Frye Memorial
- **Dating apps incentivized against success** _(current-events)_ — Hinge/Match profit from you NOT finding a partner, showing the strangeness of online dating and its required step of faith.  → Every Act of Faith
- **David and Goliath** _(biblical)_ — David's childlike courage, unafraid because he believed God was with him, defeats the giant where seasoned soldiers doubted.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]]
- **David Bentley Hart, The Doors of the Sea** _(literary)_ — In a child's death we see not the face of God but the face of his enemy; hope, not optimism, sustains faith until God makes all things new  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **David Chosen Over His Brothers** _(biblical)_ — God passing over the strong and handsome brothers to choose David the runt, illustrating that God doesn't call the qualified.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **David Hume** _(historical)_ — The philosophical denial that anything supernatural exists, so resurrection couldn't have happened.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **David Hume** _(historical)_ — Hume's denial of the supernatural cited as a philosophical 'no' to resurrection being possible.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **David Hume's argument against miracles** _(historical)_ — The 18th-century philosopher's empirical case against the virgin birth, rebutted as a one-time unrepeatable event.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **David's 200 foreskins for Michal** _(biblical)_ — David doubling Saul's grisly bride-price demand shows the 'weird' turn of David from underdog toward power.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **David's capture of Jerusalem and the Six-Day War** _(historical)_ — David took Jerusalem from the south, a feat unrepeated until the IDF in 1967, showing David's later rise into a powerful general/Goliath figure.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Day-after-Christmas decoration takedown** _(other)_ — Americans rushing to pack up Christmas to move on illustrates how hard it is to stay in the moment during the long Easter season.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **Daylight saving time clock reset** _(current-events)_ — Sleeping through 2:00 AM repeating illustrates how time is something we just add or take away, not permanent or natural.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/new-year-s-eve-christ-the-king-sunday|New Year's Eve (Christ the King Sunday)]]
- **Dead engine and Mario Kart night** _(personal)_ — A car engine died at night, so he let the boys play unlimited Mario Kart — irreparable breakdowns are oddly freeing.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-opened|Be Opened]]
- **Dead moose at a Maine spring** _(personal)_ — An obviously contaminated water source the preacher avoided, illustrating recognizable signs of a bad source.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Dead moose by the spring** _(personal)_ — Passing on a Maine water source because a dead moose lay next to it, illustrating the criteria for discerning safe (life-giving vs. tainted) water.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/give-me-water|Give Me Water]]
- **Dead-end relationship** _(personal)_ — Staying in a doomed relationship to not waste invested time illustrates the sunk cost fallacy in our moral lives.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Debate Team Spreading** _(personal)_ — Wilson's high school Lincoln-Douglas debate experience and the technique of 'spreading' illustrates conquering vs. persuading, and ideological deafness.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-to-me-all-you-who-are-weary|Come to Me, All You Who Are Weary]]
- **Deborah's song and 'when hair is long in Israel'** _(biblical)_ — The judge Deborah deferred honor yet was honored, and her song's 'long hair' image (hair grown in peacetime, not fit for battle helmets) illustrates longing for the peace of Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Defining Gnosticism by Spelling** _(historical)_ — Walking through the G-N-O-S-T-I-C spelling and gnosis as 'knowledge' to explain Model Two's bad/material vs good/spiritual error.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Dementia and confabulation** _(other)_ — Loved ones with dementia unconsciously combining memories shows remembering is about connection and love, not cold accuracy.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Demon thrown out like a baseball** _(other)_ — The demon was thrown out of the daughter like a baseball hit out of the park — vivid image of decisive deliverance.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-opened|Be Opened]]
- **Denver Casket-Building Ministry** _(personal)_ — A ministry that built and gave away wood caskets shows offering dignity in grief beyond money and transactions.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]]
- **Desert fathers and mothers** _(historical)_ — Early-church ascetics who went to the desert to pray without ceasing model accepting the radical simplicity of God's call.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it|What are we going to do about it?]]
- **Desert Fathers and Mothers** _(historical)_ — Early church ascetics who went into the desert to pray without ceasing illustrate accepting the radical simplicity of God's call.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it-2|What Are We Going to Do About It?]]
- **Dietrich Bonhoeffer** _(historical)_ — The Lutheran pastor who resisted the Nazis, wrote The Cost of Discipleship, and died in Flossenbürg embodies costly grace and carrying one's cross.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-cost|The Cost]]
- **Dietrich Bonhoeffer 'when Jesus calls a man he bids him come and die'** _(literary)_ — Bonhoeffer's line frames discipleship as letting die the things that deserve to die.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacrifice|Sacrifice]], [[covenant-umc/2023/what-does-god-ask-of-us|What Does God Ask of Us?]], [[covenant-umc/2024/eating-stones|Eating Stones]]
- **Dietrich Bonhoeffer on cheap grace vs costly grace** _(literary)_ — Bonhoeffer's contrast between cheap grace (forgiveness without repentance, grace without the cross) and costly grace (which costs a person their life because it cost God his Son) frames the cost of discipleship.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/2nd-sunday-in-lent-taking-up-the-cross-and-costly-grace|2nd Sunday in Lent: Taking Up the Cross and Costly Grace]]
- **Difficult films vs. easy action movies** _(personal)_ — Preacher's shift from loving Kurosawa/Truffaut/Godard in his twenties to wanting simple bad action movies at 9 PM on a Tuesday illustrates our reluctance to engage hard, important things like difficult Scripture.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/not-easy-to-watch|Not Easy to Watch]]
- **Dikaiosune Translation in Paul Class** _(personal)_ — The preacher's habit of swapping 'justice' and 'righteousness' when translating Greek shows the two are one word with very different English connotations.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
- **Dinner party with a serial killer** _(other)_ — The fear of letting a serial killer into your home dramatizes how uncomfortably wide and dangerous Jesus's command to host the poor and strangers feels.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/responsibility|Responsibility]]
- **Dirty feet in hard places** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor: our feet going to dirty places mirrors our lives going to hard, shameful places that only grace can reveal, sometimes by simply taking off our shoes.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Disciples Staring at the Sky** _(biblical)_ — After the ascension the disciples are told to stop staring up and return to Jerusalem, showing Jesus isn't found in the clouds.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Disney's Aladdin and the lamp** _(pop-culture)_ — The genie's lamp resembles the stained-glass parables window, but he redirects attention from Aladdin to God's yes.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-parables|The Parables]]
- **Distracted walking listening to a podcast** _(personal)_ — Bumping into others while mentally elsewhere on a walk pictures how we stray from our path and trespass into others' lives.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Diverse animals on the ark (monkeys, bears, platypi, snakes, wolves)** _(nature)_ — God tells Noah to take two of every kind, not just the good kinds, picturing the church's call to welcome all, not the in-crowd.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Divine Megaphone (Binitarian Spirit)** _(historical)_ — Early Binitarians treated the Spirit as merely a mode of God, 'a divine megaphone,' which the sermon corrects by affirming the Spirit as fully God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Do as I say, not as I do** _(personal)_ — Children latch onto what parents actually do, not what they say, showing how hypocrisy undermines trust.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-opposite-of-hypocrisy|The Opposite of Hypocrisy]]
- **Do what I say, not what I do** _(other)_ — The old parenting joke exposes the hypocrisy that disconnects words from actions.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Docetism heresy warning** _(historical)_ — Turning Mary into a passive statue over-spiritualizes Jesus and risks the docetic denial of his true humanity.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Dodo birds eating rocks for digestion** _(nature)_ — Dodos ate rocks to aid digestion and went extinct, illustrating how little good eating stones does us.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eating-stones|Eating Stones]]
- **Dog acquired before the first child** _(personal)_ — Friends who got a dog six to nine months before their first child illustrates pets as companions woven into human life.  → Pet Blessing
- **Domesticating faith on the shelf** _(other)_ — Reducing the gospel to three simple rules or shelf knick-knacks from Canton or Round Top beside a Longhorns photo illustrates the temptation to make Christ comfortable rather than transformative.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/arise-shine-the-light-of-epiphany-and-the-grafting-in-of-the-gentiles|Arise, Shine: The Light of Epiphany and the Grafting In of the Gentiles]]
- **Dominic asks if we are living in a movie** _(personal)_ — The preacher's 5-year-old son asks at breakfast whether life is a movie, prompting the point that God is not a distant observer and we are not the protagonists with everyone else as extras.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Dominique Collin on the Good News becoming an event for me** _(literary)_ — Collin's line that Christ's Good News 'can also become an event for me' shows each person's 'why' for Jesus is personal.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **Don Quixote Tilting at Windmills** _(literary)_ — Seeking ourselves in things and experiences is a quixotic quest—riding donkeys, tilting at windmills—rather than resting in who God created us to be.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **Don't build a three-room cabin** _(other)_ — A survival shelter is temporary, not a hermit's home, because the aim is rescue not permanent residence.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Don't mix fibers vs. don't murder** _(literary)_ — Illustrates that Torah makes no separation between moral, ceremonial, and dietary law  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Don't Panic from The Hitchhiker's Guide to the Galaxy** _(pop-culture)_ — Douglas Adams's phrase frames the sermon, but his nihilistic reason for it is contrasted with Christian hope.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Don't panic when backpacking** _(personal)_ — Wilson telling his boys the first rule in any challenging situation is don't panic, since panic never resolves a situation.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Don't shoot the messenger** _(other)_ — The idiom shows messengers are not responsible for the news they bring — like the angel at the tomb.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-he-is-not-here-among-the-dead|Easter: He Is Not Here Among the Dead]]
- **Don't take what you can't lose** _(personal)_ — Wilson's rule for his kids—only bring to school what you wouldn't mind losing or breaking—illustrating non-attachment, since only people mattered to Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Donating a couch you were going to throw away** _(personal)_ — Giving away something you didn't want isn't a real sacrifice, illustrating that sacrifice means letting go of something good you desire.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Donor pictured walking with Jesus in a window** _(historical)_ — Example of busy self-glorifying window art Covenant's focused windows avoid  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Door-to-Door Salesmen Learned from Evangelists** _(historical)_ — Sleazy salesmanship was learned from preachers, exposing how Christianity has been distorted into a sales pitch.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Door-to-door salesmen modeled on door-to-door evangelists** _(historical)_ — Explains modern squeamishness about evangelism by noting sales tactics borrowed the evangelist's model, tainting the association.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **DoorDash life satisfaction** _(other)_ — Wanting to 'DoorDash' satisfaction and a 'four-hour work week' change illustrates our desire for instant, controlled transformation versus real change that takes time.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **Dorothy Day 'Don't call me a saint'** _(historical)_ — The Catholic Worker founder's refusal of the saint label illustrates how naming something holy can dismiss it as unachievable.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Dorothy Day's Saying** _(historical)_ — 'I only love God as much as I love the person I love the least' — loving God is inseparable from loving the least lovable neighbor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
