- **Dostoevsky's Grand Inquisitor** _(literary)_ — Ivan's prose poem in The Brothers Karamazov frames the three wilderness temptations as encompassing all human history.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **Dostoevsky's The Idiot / Prince Myshkin** _(literary)_ — The innocent Prince Myshkin crushed by society embodies the wish that Jesus still walked the earth as a guileless example.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Doubting Thomas touching the wounds** _(biblical)_ — Thomas refusing to believe until he touches Jesus' wounds, contrasted with Luke where touching still isn't enough.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/opened-eyes|Opened Eyes]]
- **Downloading 1080p instead of 740p** _(personal)_ — A recent personal mistake of downloading the wrong video resolution that lagged badly illustrates how mistakes happen even when we try to do well.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/jesus-our-advocate|Jesus Our Advocate]]
- **Dr. Jacklyn Albin and the Blue Zones** _(current-events)_ — A UT Southwestern physician's article on Blue Zones shows vegetable-heavy, low-meat diets that echo Wesley's advice.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **Dr. Matt on sheep being stupid animals** _(personal)_ — A quoted source that sheep are dumb and untrustworthy explains why night-shepherds existed and were weary.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]]
- **Dr. Pepper 10-2-4 commercial** _(pop-culture)_ — Old Dr. Pepper ad gives the wild-card rule its name and a humorous aside about sugar intake.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-believes-all|Love Believes All]]
- **Draw your mustard seed exercise** _(personal)_ — Congregation is invited to draw a circle and fill it with what they actually believe, then reflect on why they are here rather than elsewhere — a guided self-examination exercise.  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Dressed Up in the Rainstorm** _(other)_ — Peter is like someone all dressed up straining to stay dry in the mud, when accepting you'll get wet is the only peace; we cannot keep ourselves dry from suffering.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Drifting back to the wide road** _(other)_ — If you're not growing in grace you stagnate and drift back to the wide road, avoiding the narrow one.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **Drinking from Lady Bird Lake** _(nature)_ — why ancient people drank wine instead of polluted water, framing the eating/drinking the disciples did  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Driving differently on ice vs spring** _(nature)_ — You drive differently on winter ice than on a spring day, as the church must drive differently in its present cultural season.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Dropping curtain as revelation** _(other)_ — A curtain held up and then dropped illustrates that 'apocalypse' means unveiling reality, not the end of the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/alpha-and-omega|Alpha and Omega]]
- **Drowning in Lake Travis without rescue** _(nature)_ — A swimmer drowning in Lake Travis whom someone reaches but leaves there pictures a God who meets us where we are but refuses to leave us drowning.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Drowning swimmer refusing help** _(other)_ — A swimmer must admit he needs help before accepting rescue, like admitting you need to be saved.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Duke of Buckingham and the English Civil War** _(historical)_ — James I promoting his favorite to Duke of Buckingham set off a chain of contingent events leading to the English Civil War.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Duke the rescue dog on the boat** _(personal)_ — The family's St. Bernard mix enjoying his first boat ride, setting up how memories get told and reshaped.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Duke's Final Four loss to UConn** _(current-events)_ — A dumb pass that cost Duke the game illustrates how the losses that sting most are the ones nearest to victory, paralleling Peter's high before the rebuke.  → Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Dunbar's Number** _(literary)_ — Robin Dunbar's 150-person limit, popularized in Gladwell's The Tipping Point, mirrors how we keep our circle of neighbors small.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Dunking an Oreo in milk** _(other)_ — Illustrates the plain Greek meaning of baptizo (to put into water), distinct from the sacrament  → [[covenant-umc/2025/baptism|Baptism]]
- **Dwayne 'The Rock' Johnson** _(pop-culture)_ — Satan's bulk making the Rock seem small underscores the worldly equation of power with physical size.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Early Christians accused of being atheists** _(historical)_ — Romans called Christians atheists for refusing pagan sacrifice, showing how outsiders tried to control or dismiss the early church  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Early English railroads and coal carts** _(historical)_ — first railroads came because mules couldn't haul coal over muddy washed-out roads, setting up why durable milestones mattered  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **Early Facebook and the Newsfeed Algorithm** _(personal)_ — The preacher's college-era experience of early Facebook and the rise of attention-harvesting algorithms contrasts the gospel with engagement-driven 'news.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Early Methodist boldness** _(historical)_ — Early Methodists let lay people and women preach the scriptures plain, which meant confronting unclean spirits rather than playing the moderate.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **Early Methodist class meetings** _(historical)_ — Early Methodists' transformational small groups model the accountability needed to fight temptation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **Easter Bunny of Christian practices** _(other)_ — Secularized sin reduces baptism to sprinkling magical water with a wink and a nod, like the Easter bunny, an empty gesture.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Easter dating by the spring equinox full moon** _(nature)_ — The astronomical rule for Easter (first Sunday after the first full moon after the spring equinox) explains why Ascension Sunday falls early this year.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Easter dressed up vs. private Good Friday** _(personal)_ — The contrast between Easter's flowering cross and family meals and the unmarked, partyless privacy of Good Friday illustrates the distance between public and private selves.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]]
- **Easter Practices Generator QR Code** _(current-events)_ — A QR code linking to a site that generates a random Easter practice, inviting the congregation to live the Easter season differently.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Eastern Orthodox graduated fast** _(historical)_ — The Orthodox practice of giving up meat, then dairy, then even oil shows that the point of fasting is the why, not the what.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Eastern Orthodox staged fasting (meat, dairy, oil)** _(other)_ — The Orthodox practice of giving up meat, then dairy, then oil illustrates that the point of fasting is the why, not the what.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Easy peasy lemon squeezy all-nighter** _(personal)_ — Fantasy of finishing reports, edits, and a drive to Alaska without sleep illustrates our wish to avoid our need for rest.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Eating Cheetos** _(other)_ — Immediate enjoyment tastes good but is hollow — like Cheetos that don't fill you and leave you sick and starving again.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-lamb-of-god|The Lamb of God]]
- **Eavan Boland's poem 'Quarantine'** _(literary)_ — Boland's poem of a famine-era husband warming his dying wife with his last body heat is read as a poem of resurrection and embodied love pointing to God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunday-works-of-love|Easter Sunday: Works of Love]]
- **Economics major at UT** _(personal)_ — The preacher started college as an econ major before God redirected him to ministry, leaving him attuned to biblical economic language.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Eddie Izzard's 'Cake or Death'** _(pop-culture)_ — Choosing between a good and an obvious bad is no real temptation  → [[covenant-umc/2025/no-distinction|No Distinction]]
- **Edmund Hillary and Tenzing Norgay summit Everest** _(historical)_ — Hillary repeats Mallory's 'because it's there' line, continuing the theme of conquering impossible heights.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Edwin Hubble and Georges Lemaitre proving the expanding universe** _(historical)_ — The 1929 discovery of cosmic expansion implies a beginning, vindicating Aquinas over Russell.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]], [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]]
- **Effortless perfection and the pursuit of needlessness** _(other)_ — Our culture's strongest, richest, most powerful images of needless 'effortless perfection' actually pull us away from reality and from God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **Eight numbering systems of the Decalogue** _(historical)_ — Shows enduring disagreement (Septuagint, Talmud, Calvin, Augustine, Luther, etc.) over how to count the commandments  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Election signs and Facebook fights** _(current-events)_ — Lawn signs and cousin arguments show how 'election' became a secular political word rather than its older theological meaning.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/election-not-selection|Election, Not Selection]]
- **Elite business schools (McCombs, Booth, Wharton)** _(other)_ — The disciples weren't credentialed elites; Jesus required no test or references, just 'follow me.'  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Elizabeth Kübler-Ross's five stages of grief** _(historical)_ — Critiqued because grief is not staged and finished but infinite, since loving someone means their absence is always felt.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Elsa 'Let It Go' from Frozen** _(pop-culture)_ — Elsa's anthem illustrates letting go, with the caveat 'don't be like Elsa' who isolates herself instead of reconciling.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacrifice|Sacrifice]]
- **Elysium and Olympus** _(historical)_ — Heaven-as-resting-place would have sounded to early Christians like pagan afterlife locations rather than biblical hope.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Elysium and Olympus** _(literary)_ — Pagan afterlife locations the early church would have associated with 'heaven as resting place of the dead,' contrasting with bodily resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting-2|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Emily Dickinson, 'Tell all the truth but tell it slant'** _(literary)_ — Dickinson's poem illustrates how God speaks indirectly so we are dazzled gradually rather than blinded by the full truth.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **Emmaus road and Thomas not recognizing the risen Jesus** _(biblical)_ — Even those who knew Jesus best couldn't recognize him after the resurrection, marking Easter as a radically new kind of ministry.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **English common law and early US courts** _(historical)_ — Christian-rooted legal systems developed swearing-in for practical accountability, yet Jesus' words still challenge those pragmatic boundaries.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-line-jesus-and-the-inversion-of-our-moral-expectations|The Line: Jesus and the Inversion of Our Moral Expectations]]
- **English Reformation history** _(historical)_ — Henry VIII's divorce, Cromwell, Cranmer, civil war, regicide, and the Georges trace how Methodism emerged from the Church of England tradition.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Ephraim asking who's the good guy** _(personal)_ — The preacher's son always asking 'who's the good guy?' shows how we sort people into deserving/undeserving buckets.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/election-not-selection|Election, Not Selection]]
- **Ephraim imitating his brother Dominic** _(personal)_ — The preacher's young son copies his brother's good and bad behaviors, illustrating that growing up is 'practicing living' as disciples practice faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **Ephraim wishing to be a grownup** _(personal)_ — The pastor's youngest son wishes to be a grownup so he can watch screens whenever he wants, prompting the lesson that adulthood means responsibility, not unlimited freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/responsibility|Responsibility]]
- **Ephraim's first Astros game** _(personal)_ — Taking his son to a winning Astros game illustrates how winning is fun and we naturally want to succeed.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Ephraim's flag football helmet** _(personal)_ — His youngest son's soft flag-football helmet shows even modest protective gear marks a contest that isn't perfectly safe.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Ephraim's reshaped boat-ride story** _(personal)_ — The preacher's 10-year-old son merging two memories into one better story shows we naturally exaggerate and condense when telling stories.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Ernest Becker's The Denial of Death** _(literary)_ — Becker's book on how contemporary people deny death frames the Christian call not to hide from death.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]]
- **Estalena and Donald came from nothing** _(personal)_ — Donald an orphan raised in an orphanage and Estalena born at the height of the Depression — both rose from poverty.  → Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff, A Service of Death and Resurrection for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Estalena climbing under the house to remove a skunk** _(personal)_ — Illustrates Estalena's fearlessness as a de facto single parent raising her children.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Etymology of 'talent'** _(literary)_ — How the Greek monetary 'talent' became English 'talent' for a gift or ability, framing the question of how we use what God gives.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/do-or-do-not|Do Or Do Not]]
- **Etymology of angelos, mal'ak elohim, daemon, and Satan/Shatan** _(other)_ — Word studies (Greek angelos/daemon, Hebrew mal'ak elohim, Shatan 'the accuser') used to strip speculative baggage from what angels, demons, and Satan actually mean in the text.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Etymology of antidote (anti + didonai)** _(literary)_ — Antidote means 'something given against,' showing the Greek prefix anti- isn't inherently negative and reframing 'antichrist' as 'one against Christ.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/antichrist-becoming-the-villain|Antichrist: Becoming the Villain]]
- **Etymology of devil and Satan** _(historical)_ — Diabolos means slanderer and Satan means accuser, arguing the devil's work is words that tear us down, not cinematic possession.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Eulogos / root of eulogy** _(literary)_ — 'Blessed' (eulogos, 'well said') is the root of 'eulogy'—generations will speak well of Mary.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Everything changes when you have a child** _(nature)_ — The common, inexplicable advice to new parents that life will utterly change illustrates the many unexplainable life-reorientations like conversion.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **Evolutionary view of parent-child love** _(nature)_ — Survival-of-the-fittest only gives a surface-level account of the love between parents and children, which goes beyond understanding.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Exhausting prophets** _(biblical)_ — Jeremiah, Isaiah, and Elijah were exhausting company, so 'no prophets welcome' signs would make sense, yet we're told to welcome them.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **Exodus from Egypt and the waters of baptism** _(biblical)_ — God's deliverance from Egypt and through the Red Sea typifies baptismal salvation, set against the cross prepared for the Savior  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-service-for-good-friday|A Service for Good Friday]]
- **Exodus from Egypt and the waters of baptism** _(biblical)_ — God led the people out of Egypt and delivered them by baptismal waters, yet they prepared a cross for their Savior.  → A Service for Good Friday
- **Explaining Ash Wednesday to Isaac** _(personal)_ — The pastor describing Ash Wednesday to his child Isaac that morning, framing the day's meaning.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Exposure in mountain climbing** _(personal)_ — The climbing term 'exposure' (how far you'd fall) becomes a metaphor for the stakes of the soul's vulnerability in the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/shelter|Shelter]]
- **Eye of the Tiger and We Are the Champions** _(pop-culture)_ — Pop songs with the same minor-to-major trajectory as Beethoven's Fifth, showing the move toward resolution.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Eyes enlightened by light** _(nature)_ — As bodily eyes are lit when they see light, the spirit intent on God is illumined by his infinite light.  → Prayer and Converse with God Is a Supreme Good
- **Ezekiel's four-faced, four-winged wheeled angels** _(biblical)_ — Cited against the popular image of angels as humans with feathery wings, showing Scripture's own descriptions are stranger and less domesticated.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Ezekiel's terrifying four-faced angels** _(biblical)_ — Ezekiel's awesome four-faced, four-winged creatures show how unflinchingly Mary faced the angels at the tomb in her grief.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunday-works-of-love|Easter Sunday: Works of Love]]
- **Ezekiel's Valley of Dry Bones** _(biblical)_ — The sinews and flesh returning to bones depicts resurrection as fleshly and bodily, not disembodied spirit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **F1 fuel ratio change (E5 to E10 ethanol)** _(other)_ — Getting the right fuel mix for the most power at the least weight illustrates the church getting the right source of spiritual power.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **F1 trade secrets and team secrecy** _(other)_ — The secret fuel mixtures hidden between racing teams contrast with the gospel, which is revealed openly to all rather than as secret knowledge for the few.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Facebook outrage and the user as product** _(current-events)_ — Facebook engineering outrage for profit, with users as the product sold, exemplifies the dopamine-driven wisdom of the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **Failing pizza shop** _(other)_ — Pouring more money into a failing pizza shop illustrates the sunk cost fallacy and the need to cut losses.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Faith as a house of cards** _(other)_ — Putting faith in external verification of every Bible verse makes belief a fragile tower that collapses when one card shifts, misplacing faith in technique rather than God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **Faith as a social club** _(other)_ — If Jesus isn't God and didn't rise, faith is merely a pleasant club—eat, drink, and be merry for tomorrow we die.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **Faith is not a possession like a car or leaf blower** _(other)_ — Unlike a car, calculator, or a leaf blower given on Father's Day, faith cannot be possessed or handed over; it must be received and lived.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **Fake it till you make it after a hard night** _(personal)_ — Hiding a rough night behind a good face the next day shows the human instinct to cover woundedness rather than display vulnerability.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]]
- **Falling in love and 50 years of marriage** _(personal)_ — A first date versus decades of marriage shows prayer as a deepening relationship requiring practice.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Falling on rocks at Petrified Forest** _(personal)_ — The pastor's cut thumb (deep, bled, healed fast) vs. shallow palm cut (little blood, healed slow) illustrates that some wounds linger longer than others.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **False summits while mountain climbing** _(personal)_ — You always know when you reach a mountain's top because you can't go higher, illustrating clarity of arrival.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Family Christmas dishes and recipes** _(personal)_ — The universal special foods and family recipes of Christmas, contrasted with not bothering to find a recipe for the most average green beans, illustrating how we seek the best while Mary and Joseph sought only a place to stop.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **Family meals that fall apart** _(personal)_ — A perfect family dinner followed by chaos shows how our good actions are not invalidated by subsequent failure.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **Fantasy football draft** _(pop-culture)_ — Drafting the next football star in a fantasy league exemplifies the world's endless boredom and pursuit of novelty.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **Father Abraham had seven sons** _(pop-culture)_ — The children's song is corrected (Abraham had two sons, not seven) to note how Abraham's hard stories get skipped in VBS and children's Bibles.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **Father Damien of Molokai** _(historical)_ — The Belgian missionary who served and died among the lepers of Hawaii is a physical reminder of holiness and one sanctified in truth who did not fear death.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Father God Versus Heavenly Father** _(personal)_ — The preacher once said 'Father God' because of the people around him, showing how we learn the language of prayer from our community.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **Father Lemaitre and the Big Bang** _(historical)_ — Lemaitre's Big Bang theory overturning belief in an eternal universe illustrates that the universe is not eternal, only God is.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Father on delayed gratification as the mark of maturity** _(personal)_ — His father's saying that instant gratification is childish and delayed gratification is mature frames faithful waiting amid pain and longing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Father Zosima's decaying body** _(literary)_ — Alexei is shaken when his revered saint's corpse decays and stinks, yet through that challenge comes to spiritual awakening.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **Father's Day screwdriver gift** _(personal)_ — The little screwdrivers becoming junk-drawer black holes illustrate that handy or not, everyone bears responsibility to the next generation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Father-in-law's friction-stir welding** _(personal)_ — A genuinely complex engineering specialty shows some things are hard but knowable, contrasting with the simplicity of faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it|What are we going to do about it?]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it-2|What Are We Going to Do About It?]]
- **Faulkner on the heart in conflict with itself** _(literary)_ — Temptation is the internal conflict that makes good writing worth the agony  → [[covenant-umc/2025/no-distinction|No Distinction]]
- **Faulkner: the past is never dead** _(literary)_ — Faulkner's line that the past is not even past shows how prior decisions still shape the present church.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Fearlessness Versus Courage** _(historical)_ — The ancient virtue of courage contrasted with mere fearlessness shows that courage means fearing the right fear.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/you-are-strong|You Are Strong]]
- **Fides quae creditur vs. Fides qua creditur** _(other)_ — Latin theological distinction between the faith that is believed (creed/object) and the faith by which one believes (lived conviction/action).  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Field hospital in a conflict zone** _(other)_ — An extended metaphor of the church as the first line of care present in the midst of battle, caring for the most wounded, contrasted with a clean fortress castle.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/field-hospital|Field Hospital]]
- **Field hospital not a private club** _(other)_ — Pope Francis's image of the church as a field hospital caring for the wounded, not a members-only club.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Fig Tree Lesson** _(nature)_ — Jesus' image of the fig tree's tender branch signaling summer is near, teaching watchfulness for his nearness.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-cultivation-of-christmas-trees|The Cultivation of Christmas Trees]]
- **Filling Judas's vacant slot by casting lots** _(biblical)_ — The first act of the early church was filling a committee slot, choosing Matthias by lot, showing God equips leaders rather than the most credentialed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **Finding the Book of the Law in the temple (2 Kings 22)** _(biblical)_ — Shows the Law had been lost/ignored for so long that its rediscovery during temple renovation was itself the note-worthy event.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Finishing a marathon** _(personal)_ — The satisfaction of completing a marathon, painting a room, or organizing a closet illustrates the joy of accomplishment, paralleling Jesus reaching Jerusalem though the journey isn't over.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Finite games versus life** _(other)_ — Football is a finite game with a known clock, but life is not finite because we don't know when it will end, framing the value of a fresh start.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/spilling-things-god-offers-us-a-chance-for-a-do-over|Spilling Things: God Offers Us a Chance for a Do-Over]]
- **Fire as Trinity Metaphor** _(other)_ — Father as spark, Son as light, Spirit as heat — an extended metaphor for the Trinity where the Spirit is how we feel God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Fire tornado, thunder snow, super flu of 2020** _(current-events)_ — Imagined escalating 2020 catastrophes illustrate how life's challenges and our sense of them keep changing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/holding-the-faith-doing-what-you-say|Holding the Faith: Doing What You Say]]
- **Fire, light, and heat** _(nature)_ — Father as fire, Son as light, Spirit as felt heat — we feel the Spirit's warmth as God's presence.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]]
- **Fire: spark, flame, and light** _(nature)_ — Spark, flame, and light are each distinct yet all one fire — analogy for the three persons of the Trinity.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **First Amendment and in loco parentis** _(historical)_ — Learning students lack free-assembly rights because school stands in loco parentis, illustrating felt loss of agency.  → Drawn by the Father
- **First church committee meeting casting lots for Judas' replacement** _(biblical)_ — The disciples drawing lots to replace Judas as the church's first committee, showing church order matters even if it isn't the gospel's heart.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/opened-eyes|Opened Eyes]]
- **First commandment from Sinai** _(biblical)_ — God's first word 'I am the Lord your God' shows sin is about relationship with God, not a moralistic list of rules.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **First date at Church Under the Bridge** _(personal)_ — The pastor and Alina's first date at this Austin ministry ties his own story into the congregation's outreach history.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **First date hike with the dog** _(personal)_ — Their first date was a hike with Tanner's dog Luna, who had to approve of Rachel or there would be no second date.  → Rachel and Tanner
- **First date with Alina's direct questions** _(personal)_ — His wife Alina, in sweats from soccer practice, opened their first date with deep direct questions, illustrating how direct questions about ultimate things forge real faith and relationship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Fish fry fundraisers** _(nature)_ — Lenten fish fry fundraisers at Catholic churches as the everyday outworking of Friday meat-fasting.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **Five Things About You Slideshow** _(personal)_ — A 'five things about you' slideshow tempts us to list flattering rather than true things, illustrating our urge to present a false self to be liked.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/start-from-where-you-are|Start From Where You Are]]
- **Flannery O'Connor** _(literary)_ — O'Connor's defense that being a Christian let her look at dark places without fear models facing death and grief unafraid.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]]
- **Flannery O'Connor's 'A Good Man Is Hard to Find'** _(literary)_ — The Misfit's claim that Jesus 'thrown everything off balance' by raising the dead dramatizes that the resurrection forces a decision: throw away everything and follow Him, or there's no pleasure but meanness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it-2|What Are We Going to Do About It?]]
- **Flannery O'Connor, A Good Man Is Hard to Find** _(literary)_ — The Misfit's claim that Jesus 'thrown everything off balance' shows the resurrection forces a total either/or: follow Him or chase meanness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it|What are we going to do about it?]], Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Flat vs. overflowing world / 'a chair is only a chair'** _(other)_ — An extended metaphor contrasting a purely materialist 'flat' reality (we are only cells and atoms) with an 'overflowing' world where moments and experiences carry meaning.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Flood myths across cultures** _(historical)_ — Gilgamesh, Choctaw, Cree, Inuit, Egyptian, Indian, and Malaysian flood myths show the flood and boat are not unique to the Hebrews.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Following Someone Before GPS** _(personal)_ — The lost art of following another driver through unexpected turns illustrates discipleship as following Jesus wherever the path turns.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]]
- **FOMO and the uninvited party** _(personal)_ — The sting of hearing friends threw a party without you, framing the human fear of missing out that God's love never subjects us to.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-a-new-commandment-god-s-love-is-old-not-trendy|Not a New Commandment: God's Love Is Old, Not Trendy]]
- **Food pantry cleanup at 5:30pm** _(personal)_ — Concrete church service opportunities show that vectoring outward means signing up and showing up, not just nodding along.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i-in-christ|Who am I in Christ?]]
- **Food poisoning and bodily knowledge** _(personal)_ — Eating something toxic gives you intimate, quick knowledge of your body, illustrating how the tree's fruit could give knowledge by being poison.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Food, Dieting, and Self-Loathing** _(current-events)_ — Modern struggles with diet, body image, and 'that third dessert' illustrate how constant self-satisfaction leads to self-destruction rather than rest, motivating fasting.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-to-me-all-you-who-are-weary|Come to Me, All You Who Are Weary]]
- **Footloose small-town rebellion** _(pop-culture)_ — The 'cut loose, Footloose' rebel against small-town authority shows rebellion only works when there's an order worth defying.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/sin-as-rebellion-the-freedom-not-to-rebel|Sin as Rebellion: The Freedom Not to Rebel]]
- **Ford Ranger broken axle** _(personal)_ — His axle fell driving Austin to College Station — a total breakdown you accept and move on from.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-opened|Be Opened]]
- **Forest fire suppression and ecological renewal** _(nature)_ — fire is both destructive and constructive, renewing ecologies as God's refining fire renews us  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-mercy-of-fire|The Mercy of Fire]]
- **Forgiving $50 vs $500,000 vs a $2.35M house** _(other)_ — Escalating sums show that the larger the forgiven debt, the more radical the forgiveness, like a bank wiping a Zillow mansion's mortgage.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Forgiving a car loan** _(other)_ — Christ doesn't just forgive sin like canceling a car loan and moving on; he overwhelms and covers it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Forgiving someone who tripped your child** _(personal)_ — The hardest mercy of all is forgiving those who have tripped up our children, illustrating that forgiveness is impossible apart from grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Formula 1 and Drive to Survive** _(pop-culture)_ — Getting hooked on F1 via the Netflix series sets up the points system and unjust last-race endings as a picture of life that seems unfair.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Formula One driver development and simulators** _(pop-culture)_ — F1 teams train drivers through divisions and million-dollar simulators to master every track, as Christians must learn to drive attentively.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Formula One power unit** _(other)_ — The custom F1 engine that constantly needs refueling illustrates Scripture as the church's fuel that must be returned to again and again.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Forrest Gump bus seat 'seat's taken'** _(pop-culture)_ — Unlike the bus kids refusing Forrest a seat, the unfinished church always has space for others.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Fossil fuel running out versus Scripture never running dry** _(nature)_ — Petroleum will one day be exhausted, but the Bible as fuel never runs dry and stays as fresh as ever.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Founding of Bee Creek / Pedernales UMC** _(historical)_ — The 2002-2005 church-plant story (letter to the DS, Pastor David Payne, name changes, land donation) embodies the concrete collective body of Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **Founding story of Bee Creek UMC** _(historical)_ — From a failed 1970s real estate deal to charter in 2003 and a 2.4M loan, Bee Creek's existence was contingent and unexpected.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Francis Xavier's mission to Goa and Japan** _(historical)_ — Xavier said yes to the Great Commission and pressed on from Goa to Japan, a saint who is a 'visible gospel' to imitate.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **Francois Rabbath** _(historical)_ — The Lebanese bassist who revolutionized technique, cited in the pastor's musical lineage as a 'cool connection.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacrifice|Sacrifice]]
- **Frank and the porn addiction** _(other)_ — Frank, a churchgoing UT-football fan with a hidden porn addiction, meets Christ, loses the desire, and can humbly confess to friends in Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Frank Wilson and John Wesley Hardin** _(personal)_ — Preacher's namesake sheriff and the outlaw named for Wesley show that a name/lineage doesn't determine character.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Franz Ferdinand's driver and Gavrilo Princip** _(historical)_ — A wrong turn by the driver in Sarajevo put Franz Ferdinand in front of his assassin, sparking World War I.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Fred and Larry who refused the call** _(other)_ — Imagined fishermen Jesus may have called before Andrew and Peter, who said things were fine and wouldn't put down their nets, illustrating clinging to what you have.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/fragments|Fragments]]
- **Frederick Buechner on the Gospel as bad news before good news** _(literary)_ — The quote that we are chicken, phony, slob yet loved, cherished, and bled for, capturing grace beyond mere affirmation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Frederick the Great on defending everything** _(historical)_ — 'He who defends everything, defends nothing' shows fearing everything means missing the right fear.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Free-throw at the buzzer** _(other)_ — The pressure of making the winning shot is contrasted with the gospel, where Christ has already won and the pressure isn't on us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Freed prisoner who shares the news** _(other)_ — Someone locked away for a long time and then set free would naturally share that news, like new parents sharing about a baby, illustrating spontaneous evangelism from freedom in Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Freezing brains and supplements** _(current-events)_ — Modern attempts to preserve ourselves won't last; earthly treasure is impermanent.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **French 'arbre' and its meaning as the cross** _(other)_ — the word for tree in French ranking the cross as a second meaning shows that translation is always a choice between words  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]]
- **Freshman dorm-room God debate** _(other)_ — Contrasts an abstract intellectual debate about God's existence with the lived crisis of a struggling marriage and kids in college, showing why the question matters existentially.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]]
- **Friend hungover on Passover wine** _(personal)_ — A middle-school friend came to school hungover from too much Passover wine, a memory of how the holidays appeared to him as a kid.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Friend's saying about wanting to spend time with God** _(personal)_ — If we don't want to spend time with God now, why would we want to in the future — pointing to present transformation, not deathbed bargaining.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-jesus|The Gift of Jesus]]
- **Frozen Lake LBJ Transformed by Ice** _(personal)_ — The Texas winter storm turned the lake where the pastor learned to swim white, which his boys saw as magical while he saw burst pipes, illustrating how the same scene can be a transformation seen differently.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Frustrated parent telling kids to brush teeth and buckle seatbelts** _(personal)_ — Everyday parental frustration shows that frustration is not the same as rejection or giving up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Galadriel refusing the Ring** _(pop-culture)_ — Power corrupts even one who means to do good with it  → [[covenant-umc/2025/no-distinction|No Distinction]]
- **Galen and the Methodist medical school** _(historical)_ — The ancient 'Methodist' medical rivalry explains the insult's origin: anyone, by method, can be a Christian  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Galen vs. the ancient Methodist medical school** _(historical)_ — The ancient medical 'Methodists' (anyone trained by method could heal, versus Galen's elite) gave the Wesleys their nickname-turned-claim that holiness is offered to all.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **Galileo, Kepler, and Tycho Brahe practiced astrology** _(historical)_ — Pioneers of modern astronomy also practiced astrology, showing Christians have long focused on the stars.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Game theory and the Nash equilibrium** _(historical)_ — Game theory's rational-self-interest logic contrasts with the gospel call to love one's neighbor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-of-god-love-of-neighbor|Love of God, Love of Neighbor]]
- **Gandalf is never late** _(literary)_ — Tolkien's wizard arriving 'precisely when he means to' illustrates that God's time differs from human time.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **Gandalf says no heroes can be found** _(literary)_ — a small, unassuming hobbit becomes the surprising hero, like ordinary believers being made holy  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Garb vs. desire in the Gospels** _(biblical)_ — The street-corner Pharisee condemned and the widow's tiny offering praised show Jesus values inward desire over outward Christian appearance.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Gathering Online During the Pandemic** _(current-events)_ — Longing for the day to gather 'with masks off' to hold and shake hands, naming the COVID-era separation of the congregation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Gaudete Sunday and the pink candle** _(other)_ — The Advent day of rejoicing frames joy as an unearned gift received when we admit the world's brokenness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **Gaudete Sunday pink candle** _(historical)_ — The pink Advent candle marks the church's shift from 'Come, Lord Jesus' to 'The Lord is close at hand.'  → [[covenant-umc/2025/cantata-show-don-t-tell|Cantata: Show, Don't Tell]]
- **General anesthesia and the meaning of judgment** _(personal)_ — The preacher's two times under general anesthesia (wisdom teeth, ankle tumor) frame anesthesia as 'absence of judgment,' a wordplay distinguishing kinds of judgment from God's judgment of souls.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-will-come-to-judge-the-living-and-the-dead|He Will Come to Judge the Living and the Dead]]
- **General Conference and Kansas City delegation trip** _(personal)_ — The UMC's deep debates and the pastor's delegate trip to Kansas City that very afternoon show present-tense church disagreement firsthand.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **General Conference presentation after the service** _(current-events)_ — The pastor's upcoming talk on what really happened at UMC General Conference versus media portrayals, framing the call to read Scripture together humbly.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Genius Bar / Google troubleshooting** _(pop-culture)_ — Tech support always asks first if you've reset it, modeling humble preemptive reset without ego.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **George Mallory and Everest ("because it's there")** _(historical)_ — Mallory's deadly drive to climb Everest illustrates the human pursuit of the impossible that finds its true answer at the Lord's table.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **George Mallory on Everest** _(historical)_ — Mallory's 'because it's there' answer for climbing Everest shows how the reasons we give for doing things sound cool but don't really make sense.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **George McClellan and the lost Confederate orders at Antietam** _(historical)_ — A battle's outcome is contingent, not inevitable: McClellan found the enemy's plans yet failed to press his advantage.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **George Michael's 'Faith' (covered by Limp Bizkit)** _(pop-culture)_ — A Google search for 'faith song' turns up this pop song rather than hymns, illustrating how culture reduces faith to mere hope/expectation.  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **George Strait, "Ocean Front Property"** _(pop-culture)_ — The 1987 song's ironic too-good-to-be-true sales pitch (oceanfront property in Arizona) frames grace as a deal that sounds unbelievable.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/god-s-love-a-deal-too-good-to-be-true|God's Love: A Deal Too Good to Be True]]
- **George the Sheep stuck in the brambles** _(other)_ — An imagined named lost sheep whose rescue feels unfair to the obedient, framing the rule-followers' resentment as economists' moral hazard.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **George Washington declines a third term** _(historical)_ — Washington's 1796 peaceful transfer of power shows America rejected kingship, framing the sermon's question of who should rule.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-not-to-make-jesus-king|How not to make Jesus King]]
- **Gettysburg Address** _(historical)_ — Lincoln's clear, succinct prose exemplifies the dense, get-to-the-point style shared with Mark's gospel.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/binding-the-strongman|Binding the Strongman]]
- **Ghost hunter shows, horror movies, tarot and horoscopes** _(current-events)_ — Contemporary pop-culture fascination with the supernatural (ghost hunting TV, horror films, tarot, crystals) shows the same enduring spiritual curiosity Wesley's audience had.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Giardia parasite** _(nature)_ — The odorless, tasteless parasite that can debilitate you pictures bad spiritual water that slowly destroys the soul.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Gideon and the Day of Midian** _(biblical)_ — Gideon's 300 men attacking the Midianites by faith, not running a prevent defense, models bold faithful action that breaks the oppressor's rod.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]], [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Gideon Bibles and the AirBnB era** _(current-events)_ — Most Americans meet the Bible in hotel rooms, but short-term rentals are ending the Gideons' reach, illustrating the Bible's uneven value to people.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Giving 300% at work** _(other)_ — Exhausting ourselves in external striving illustrates how prayer offers a countervailing way of simply being with God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Giving kids many rules** _(personal)_ — Children don't take a pile of rules as a sign of freedom, showing how commands feel constraining.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Giving the promotion to Sally** _(other)_ — Few willingly yield a deserved promotion, showing how zero-sum and competitive ordinary work life is.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Giving up chocolate for Lent** _(personal)_ — Food tempts precisely because it is good, not bad  → [[covenant-umc/2025/no-distinction|No Distinction]]
- **Gleaning at orchard edges in Israel and California** _(nature)_ — The ancient practice of leaving field edges for the poor and wayfarer illustrates God's provision for Ruth and the outsider.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Glengarry Glen Ross sales contest** _(pop-culture)_ — First prize a Cadillac, second steak knives, third you're fired — the zero-sum, results-oriented misreading of the fig-tree parable  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]]
- **Global Poverty Comparison** _(other)_ — Comparing our lives to world measures of poverty, malnutrition, and persecuted Christians reveals our blessings and challenges our privilege.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **God as Santa Claus** _(pop-culture)_ — Rejecting the naughty-or-nice reward-and-punishment image clarifies why Jesus came to redeem, not to dole out gifts.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **God comes not as king but as a speechless poor child** _(other)_ — Instead of arriving on a chariot or as warlord, king, or tycoon, God enters as the weakest among us, a poor child who cannot speak, illustrating divine self-emptying.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/waiting-on-a-miracle|Waiting on a Miracle]]
- **God enters the chat from the whirlwind in Job** _(biblical)_ — Job's ending, where God speaks from the whirlwind, parallels Jesus' power over the storm, except Jesus speaks to the whirlwind in finite flesh and it obeys.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **God is not a heavenly Santa Claus or puppet master** _(other)_ — Contrasting a gift-dispensing Santa or controlling puppet master with God who desires relationship shows love is more than receiving things.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **God of the gaps and the rise of modern science** _(historical)_ — In the 16th-17th century God became a filler for what new observational techniques couldn't explain, the deist 'God of the gaps' theory rather than the Trinitarian God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **God treated like a pesky in-law** _(personal)_ — Keeping a non-Trinitarian God at a comfortable distance, like an in-law you only tolerate briefly.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **God's glory would burn us up, as it nearly did Moses** _(biblical)_ — Moses nearly being consumed by God's glory shows why God came humbly in Jesus rather than in overwhelming glory.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]]
- **Gods playing bridge** _(other)_ — The image of pantheon gods meeting to play bridge underscores how the God of Abraham differs from one deity among many.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Golden calf and focus groups** _(biblical)_ — Aaron giving impatient people what they want like responding to focus groups, showing we are led astray by getting wants instead of needs.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Gollum hoarding the ring of power** _(literary)_ — Clinging to Christ's peace like Gollum to the ring contrasts with sharing peace freely.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Good Friday scoreboard Rome 1 - Jesus 0** _(other)_ — The metaphor of a scoreboard that didn't hold out shows Jesus' apparent defeat became resurrection victory.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Good Samaritan and ritual purity** _(biblical)_ — Passersby avoided the wounded man to protect their ritual purity, illustrating how fear of contamination makes us see others as threats.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Good Samaritan's Priest and Levite** _(biblical)_ — The priest and Levite passing by the wounded man illustrate how we turn away and give up first downs against injustice.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
- **Grafting into Israel and the sin of persecution** _(biblical)_ — Gentiles grafted into the chosen people turned on Israel with persecution and made them scapegoats for their own guilt.  → A Service for Good Friday
- **Grandfather John Huebel beating Smithville in football** _(personal)_ — A lighthearted family memory of the 1930s quarterback introduces the town of Smithville.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **Grandfather John Huebel, A&M class of 1940** _(personal)_ — Personal family tie to A&M's only national championship illustrates symbols carrying memory.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Grandfather John Huebel, Aggie class of 1940** _(personal)_ — Dropped at a train station in the dark with two more miles to walk, illustrating the preacher's roots in the College Station / Aggie story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Grandfather left a church over a money ask** _(personal)_ — His grandfather quit a Methodist church because the preacher asked for money, explaining the pastor's own aversion to pulpit fundraising.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/shelter|Shelter]]
- **Grandmother and the jetskis** _(personal)_ — His grandmother threatening warning shots at jetskis in their cove colors the family lake setting that frames the sermon's central metaphor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Grandmother's genealogy research** _(personal)_ — Pre-internet family-tree hunting in libraries sets up the theme that genealogy is about relationship, not blood.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Greedo or Han shot first edits** _(pop-culture)_ — Online arguments over George Lucas's special-edition edits illustrate that 'nerds don't like change' and the restless chasing of the next thing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **Greek 'daemon' vs English 'demon'** _(other)_ — source-language word meant a neutral divine power, not an inherently wicked being  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **Greek dikaiosyne in the Paul class** _(other)_ — The single Greek word for justice/righteousness, which the preacher flips when translating, shows justice and righteousness are one reality.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Greek pantheon and Athenian philosophy** _(historical)_ — The sophisticated, increasingly monotheistic thought of Plato and Aristotle shows the Greeks seeking the same God Paul proclaims as now revealed in Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Greek word calypto vs apocalypse** _(literary)_ — The Greek 'calypto' (cover) versus 'apocalypse' (uncover) illustrates that love covers sin while Revelation uncovers Christ's plan.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Greek word study: Calypso / Apocalypse** _(other)_ — Wordplay tracing the Greek for 'cover' (kalyptō) through Calypso and apocalypse/uncovering, illustrating that love covers sin until it is no more, as Revelation uncovers the new heaven and earth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins-2|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Greenwashing** _(current-events)_ — Corporations acting environmental without changing policies, the parallel for Christians' empty performance.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **Gregory the Great on Doubting Thomas** _(historical)_ — Gregory the Great's reflection that Thomas's doubt, resolved by touching Christ's wounds, did more for our faith than the other disciples' belief.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/future-sermon-notes-on-thomas-and-prayer|Future Sermon Notes: On Thomas and Prayer]]
- **Grief is not like a mortgage you pay off** _(other)_ — An extended metaphor contrasting grief with a debt that can be settled and forgotten, since we grieve because we love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Grizzly bear escape joke** _(other)_ — You only have to outrun the slowest person, illustrating the human instinct to save yourself and abandon the weakest.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **Grocery cart returners** _(personal)_ — People who don't return grocery carts illustrate the differing internalized levels of responsibility people carry.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/responsibility|Responsibility]]
- **Grocery-store freedom of fifty pastas** _(other)_ — Worldly freedom as endless consumer choice (fifty kinds of pasta) is unquenchable and constraining, the freedom of the hired hand.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/being-sheep|Being Sheep]]
- **Groggy morning pianist** _(personal)_ — A young pianist who plays best when too groggy to overthink, illustrating the danger of overthinking faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Groucho Marx club argument** _(pop-culture)_ — "I'd never join a club that would have me" frames the argument that God is too great to care about insignificant us.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-baptism-of-the-lord|The Baptism of the Lord]]
- **Group chats and FaceTime vs. hugging** _(other)_ — Digital connection can't replace celebrating together or hugging tightly, showing that bodies matter and a party without bodies is no party.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting-2|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Group chats vs. hugs** _(other)_ — FaceTime and group chats can't replace hugging someone tightly, showing that bodies matter for true celebration.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Group project where someone doesn't do their part** _(personal)_ — A group member who promises to do the work but doesn't, illustrating the disappointment of broken commitments.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/antichrist-becoming-the-villain|Antichrist: Becoming the Villain]]
- **Growing up and realizing others are better than you** _(personal)_ — The childhood-to-adulthood ache of discovering some people are faster, smarter, and more gifted, illustrating the unfairness of unequal talents.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-in-christ|Freedom in Christ]]
- **Growing up in College Station, the Supreme Court dream, and conversion in Saltillo** _(personal)_ — The preacher's ambition to be a Supreme Court justice with a Potomac island dissolved after an encounter with God on a hill outside Saltillo, Mexico, illustrating how dropping your nets enlarges rather than shrinks your purpose.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Growing up with Nintendo and video games** _(personal)_ — Wilson's childhood playing the original 8-bit Nintendo, Super Mario, Goldeneye, and deliberative computer RPGs sets up the value of games that form good decision-making.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eternal-life|Eternal Life]]
- **Guests who never leave** _(personal)_ — Loving people but liking when they go home illustrates why a mere continuation of earthly life would not be heaven.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/new-creation|New Creation]]
- **Guided enemy-prayer meditation** _(personal)_ — Closing eyes to pray for a loved one, then an irritant, then someone you never want to see again, enacts loving enemies because Christ is victorious.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Guided eyes-closed self-examination** _(personal)_ — Congregation imagines what Jesus would notice, highlight, or pass over in them  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Guided meditation as the father/mother** _(other)_ — Invites listeners to feel the parent's grief and mercy at a child who left them for dead and then returned.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-prodigal-son-and-the-ministry-of-reconciliation|The Prodigal Son and the Ministry of Reconciliation]]
- **Guided meditation as the older sibling** _(other)_ — Invites listeners to inhabit the obedient older brother's jealousy and resentment at the celebration.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-prodigal-son-and-the-ministry-of-reconciliation|The Prodigal Son and the Ministry of Reconciliation]]
- **Guided meditation as the prodigal son** _(other)_ — Invites listeners to close their eyes and imagine being the runaway child returning home, to feel repentance and dread.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-prodigal-son-and-the-ministry-of-reconciliation|The Prodigal Son and the Ministry of Reconciliation]]
- **Gustavo Gutierrez's commentary on Job** _(literary)_ — The Peruvian theologian connects Job to Pascal's wager—Pascal confronts history's winners while Job seeks out its losers and nonpersons.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]]
- **Gym 'no judgment zones'** _(current-events)_ — A gym slogan against judging others' bodies is pushed to its limit (someone flinging dangerous weights) to show that some judgment/discernment is necessary, undercutting 'kingdom without judgment.'  → Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **H. Richard Niebuhr on a God without wrath** _(literary)_ — Niebuhr's 'God without wrath...Christ without a Cross' names the church-as-social-club following of the hired hand.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/being-sheep|Being Sheep]], [[covenant-umc/2024/lifted-up-reading-the-bible-as-jesus-reads-it|Lifted Up: Reading the Bible as Jesus Reads It]], Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Hagia Sophia Christ mosaic** _(historical)_ — Iconographic flatness invites the viewer to be drawn up to heaven in prayer  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Haircuts for books at Waldenbooks** _(personal)_ — Pastor's boyhood inability to resist a free book in exchange for a haircut, illustrating his lifelong love of prophecy-laden fiction.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Halloween and the Word Hallowed** _(current-events)_ — Hallowed survives only in the Lord's Prayer and on October 31st, showing how a once-living word has become strange.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **Hamartia as archery term** _(literary)_ — Sin as 'hamartia,' an archery term for missing the target, frames sin as straying from the path of God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Hamlet 'more things than are dreamt of'** _(literary)_ — The allusion that there are more things than dreamt of in our philosophy acknowledges real spirits and powers of this world.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Hamlet's 'more things in heaven and earth'** _(literary)_ — Quoting the Prince of Denmark to frame the guardrail that Scripture's witness exceeds what our philosophy/speculation can pin down.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Hamlet's undiscovered country** _(literary)_ — Shakespeare's image of death as an unmapped land, set against the age of exploration, to show death as a fixed, unknown frontier.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/glorification|Glorification]]
- **Han Solo frozen in carbonite** _(pop-culture)_ — God's word is not frozen and static like Han Solo in carbonite but speaks living truth today.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Handel's Messiah** _(pop-culture)_ — Handel set Isaiah 9:6 ('For to us a child is born') to music, voicing the power of the coming Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Handmade ancient fishing nets** _(historical)_ — Nets were painstakingly handmade prized family possessions lasting generations, so dropping them meant laying down who you thought you had to be.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Hank Williams and Johnny Cash gospel songs** _(pop-culture)_ — Country artists who shared gospel songs, showing praise styles predating the Jesus Freak movement.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **Hannah's song prefiguring Mary** _(biblical)_ — Barren Hannah's song over Samuel parallels Mary's Magnificat, showing God overcoming barrenness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Hannibal crossing the Alps with elephants** _(historical)_ — Ancients went around mountains rather than over them, contrasting with the modern goal of conquering the impossible.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Hashem / Adonai and ho kurios** _(literary)_ — Jewish reverence for the unspoken divine name (Hashem, Adonai) shows that Mark's 'ho kurios' means the colt is needed by the Lord God, not a random master.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Hauerwas on the story you choose when you have no story** _(literary)_ — Stanley Hauerwas's line that modernity produces people who think they should have no story except the one they choose, naming the false self-authored identity of the modern self.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **He died just as he lived — on his terms** _(personal)_ — Bruce arranged to settle Cheryl in a manageable home before dying, facing death brave, strong, and loving — illustrating a life and death of quiet control and devotion.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Bruce Martin
- **Heading downhill to find rescue** _(nature)_ — Going downhill finds people and conserves energy, illustrating wise, energy-conserving pursuit of rescue.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Headlamp leaves your back vulnerable** _(personal)_ — Shining a flashlight forward leaves you blind behind, where a kid could sneak up and grab you, illustrating need for a rear guard.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Hearing a grackle** _(nature)_ — We don't hear God the way we hear a grackle, because God is immaterial and has no body to make sounds.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **Heart Rate Variability and Sleep Biometrics** _(other)_ — Physiological markers of good sleep (low heart rate, low respiratory rate, high HRV) show there are good and bad kinds of rest, not just hours in bed.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **Heartache vs. heart attack** _(other)_ — A one-syllable difference with enormous consequence, paralleling pantheism vs. panentheism.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **Heat (Pacino vs. De Niro)** _(pop-culture)_ — Michael Mann's film about whether you can walk away when the heat closes in; De Niro can't let go and is caught, illustrating the cost of clinging.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Heath's Baptism** _(personal)_ — The baptism performed that morning grounds the sermon's teaching that God, not the human recipient, is the active agent in baptism.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Heavenly chocolate fountain** _(other)_ — An ever-flowing chocolate fountain in the narthex playfully illustrates the rejected hypothesis of an infinitely self-replenishing universe.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]]
- **Heavenly HR department / C-suite / parent** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor warning against putting ourselves in God's seat judging others' worthiness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-believes-all|Love Believes All]]
- **Heavenly tow truck pulling a car out of the mud** _(other)_ — extended metaphor for God freeing us when we are stuck spinning in earthly things so we can drive toward where we need to be  → [[covenant-umc/2025/under-the-wing|Under the Wing]]
- **Hebrew 'satan' as accuser** _(other)_ — the term means accuser, not inherently wicked  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **Hebrew as the only extant ancient document** _(historical)_ — Some Hebrew words appear only once in Nehemiah and nowhere else in human history, so scholars rely on traditional meanings — like the once-recorded builder names that still matter to God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/what-s-next-builders-and-names|What's Next? (Builders and Names)]]
- **Hebrew Bible and the Founding Fathers** _(historical)_ — 17th-century Hebrew scholarship and the English Civil War made monarchy-as-sin thinkable, shaping America's founders who read Israel as a republic.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/thy-kingdom-come-thy-will-be-done-on-earth-as-it-is-in-heaven|Thy Kingdom Come, Thy Will Be Done, on Earth as It Is in Heaven]]
- **Hebrew mayim and shamayim** _(other)_ — The near-identical Hebrew words for water (mayim) and sky (shamayim) show we are surrounded by waters above and below.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **Hebrew shamayim as upper waters over the ocean** _(literary)_ — Uses the Hebrew word for heaven (waters above) and the ocean-sky resemblance to frame creation as pure gift.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]]
- **Hebrew word almah** _(literary)_ — The Hebrew term in Isaiah 7 can mean virgin or young woman, the crux of the translation dispute.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **Hebrew words for heaven and land (shamayim and eretz)** _(literary)_ — Hebrew 'shamayim' (upper waters) and 'eretz' (land) frame creation as pure gift, like ocean meeting sky.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/three-whats|Three Whats?]]
- **Hebrew words shamayim and eretz** _(other)_ — The Hebrew terms for 'waters above/heaven' and 'land' show creation as pure gift, like sea and sky meeting at the horizon.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]]
- **Helicopter or boat rescue** _(other)_ — God's rescue does not always look like a dramatic helicopter or boat rescue from the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Hell deceived by taking Christ's body** _(literary)_ — Hell 'took a body and discovered God... took what it saw and was overcome by what it did not see,' picturing death undone by swallowing the crucified Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]]
- **Hell Swallowing a Body and Discovering God** _(literary)_ — Death takes Christ's body expecting earth but encounters Heaven, overcome by what it could not see, dramatizing the harrowing of hell.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-sunrise-service-the-paschal-homily-of-st-john-chrysostom|Easter Sunrise Service: The Paschal Homily of St. John Chrysostom]]
- **Hell Swallowing the Body and Finding God** _(literary)_ — Hell took a body and discovered God, took earth and encountered Heaven, dramatizing death being undone from within by Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-sermon|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom Sermon)]]
- **Hell Swallowing the Body of Christ** _(literary)_ — Hell takes a body and discovers God, takes earth and encounters Heaven, depicting death undone by the one it could not see.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]], [[covenant-umc/2026/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-s-paschal-homily|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom's Paschal Homily)]]
- **Helping Hands community give-away** _(personal)_ — The Nov 9 clothing/housewares collection for Helping Hands Crisis Ministry is a concrete chance to share with those in need.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Helping Hands ministry** _(current-events)_ — A church-supported community ministry as a concrete way to prepare to love neighbors.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Hen gathering her brood under her wings** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' own metaphor of himself as a mother-hen longing to protect Jerusalem's children  → [[covenant-umc/2025/under-the-wing|Under the Wing]]
- **Henri Nouwen on the prodigal** _(literary)_ — Nouwen's insight that we can find ourselves in each figure: father, older son, and younger son.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/when-you-re-lost-god-will-find-you|When You're Lost, God Will Find You]]
- **Henri Nouwen's Return of the Prodigal Son** _(literary)_ — Nouwen's book placing himself in each figure of the parable models the rich, multi-angled reading of the prodigal son.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Hepburn and Tracy 'antediluvian'** _(pop-culture)_ — Imagining Katharine Hepburn calling Spencer Tracy 'antediluvian' illustrates the word's meaning of something very old, from before the flood.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Herbert McCabe on crucifixion as the Christian's destiny** _(literary)_ — McCabe's claim that Christ-like love inevitably leads to a death conquered by the Spirit grounds the sermon's vision of self-giving love.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Herbert McCabe on distractions in prayer** _(literary)_ — McCabe argues distractions reveal our real desires, so we should pray for what we actually want, as people on sinking ships do.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Herbert McCabe on Easter as meaning of the cross** _(literary)_ — The quoted theological passages (Easter as the meaning of the cross, resurrection as the seal on incarnation) ground the claim that we must go through Good Friday to reach Easter joy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]], [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Herbert McCabe on grace and prayer** _(literary)_ — McCabe's claim that it is God who prays in us reframes prayer as connection rather than productive work.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Herbert McCabe quotation** _(literary)_ — Easter is the meaning of the cross, not its cancellation; the risen Christ is paradise and the new bodily world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Herbert McCabe's eternal parent, child, joy** _(literary)_ — Theologian Herbert McCabe's phrasing of God as eternal parent, eternal child, and eternal joy to express the Trinity.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Hercules myth (Campbell and von Balthasar)** _(literary)_ — Resurrection dismissed as just another mythic dying-and-rising hero story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Hero of your own movie** _(pop-culture)_ — Without the Trinity you stay in control as the hero while everyone else is a supporting character — a self-destructive lie.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]], [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **Herod as Roman warlord** _(historical)_ — Herod's rise via alliances with Julius Caesar, Marc Antony, and Caesar Augustus, his tax-funded building projects and Temple rebuild, illustrating insecure borrowed authority and a scarcity mindset.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Herod the Great's reign and Temple-building** _(historical)_ — Herod's Rome-derived power, heavy taxes, and controversial Temple project illustrate an insecure heart hiding behind grand appearances.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **High Fidelity / Rob's Top Five Lists** _(pop-culture)_ — Nick Hornby's character making top-five side-one track-one lists reveals how list-making and music taste can mask the shallowness of presenting ourselves to the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/half-a-world-away|Half a World Away]]
- **High priest as butcher / hot butcher shop** _(historical)_ — The blood-soaked, un-air-conditioned sacrificial temple resembled a hot butcher shop, reframing what Jesus actually walked into.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-foolishness-of-the-cross-jesus-cleanses-the-temple|The Foolishness of the Cross: Jesus Cleanses the Temple]]
- **High school debate team and spreading** _(personal)_ — Pastor's debate-team days (extemp, LD, CX, and 'spreading' the Constitution preamble) illustrate how argument can overwhelm rather than persuade.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **High school keggers and beer pong vs. Christian kids** _(personal)_ — The stereotype that Christian kids don't party shows the modern, deficient idea that partying means relinquishing inhibitions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **Hiker who goes his own way** _(other)_ — Refusing to walk together on a hike is no humility, illustrating that walking humbly means walking with God's people.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Hiking and Trail Trust** _(personal)_ — Pastor's long-distance hiking days, where he quickly learned who would have his back and make room in a shelter, illustrating earned trust.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **Hippolytus of Rome on Christ's baptism** _(historical)_ — A 2nd-century Roman saint's meditation on the astonishing paradox of the infinite, all-filling Christ being immersed in John's shallow stream, illustrating divine condescension and the healing of Adam's wound.  → Baptism of the Lord
- **His changing account of faith at 8, 23, 43** _(personal)_ — His faith's account differing across ages shows giving an account of faith is a lifelong journey shaped by experience.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Historical Herod the Great** _(historical)_ — Herod's Idumaean origins, Roman collusion, and temple-building show him as a king who saw Jesus as a rival to eliminate.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/rivals|Rivals]]
- **History of Brazilian jiu-jitsu and judo** _(historical)_ — BJJ's roots in judo and its ground-game philosophy set up the idea of a 'dominant position' amid struggle.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **History of Methodism podcast starting with Luther** _(personal)_ — The preacher's podcast begins 200 years before Wesley to show Wesley repackaged others' ideas, making the movement about God rather than one man.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
