- **History of the ancient and modern Olympics** _(historical)_ — The Olympics from 776 BC to today demarcating time shows the enduring human pursuit of being the best.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Hitchcock's MacGuffin** _(pop-culture)_ — The MacGuffin (Maltese Falcon, Death Star plans) is an arbitrary object that drives a plot, like the hollow goals the world hands us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Hitting a wall at Genesis 2 in seminary Hebrew** _(personal)_ — The pastor could translate Genesis 1 but stalled at Genesis 2's different vocabulary, showing the Bible's many human authors versus the Koran's uniformity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Hoarders of the heart** _(other)_ — Cluttered, hoarded hearts cramped with desires illustrate the need to declutter so God and love can be at the center.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **Hobby Lobby and Menoras** _(current-events)_ — Resetting your house isn't spending $300 at Hobby Lobby or displaying religious objects like Herod's menoras to impress people rather than honor God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Hocus pocus from hoc est corpus meum** _(historical)_ — The magic phrase derives from the Latin words of institution, yet communion is not magic  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **Holier than thou** _(other)_ — The cute Southern phrase shows how culture distorts holiness into something undesirable rather than humble acceptance of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Holy Club nickname appropriated from an insult** _(historical)_ — The Wesleys turned the mocking labels 'Holy Club' and 'Methodist' into owned identities of pursuing holiness for everyone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **Holy people you have known** _(personal)_ — The hearer's own experience of seeing genuinely holy people proves the Spirit-empowered life of love is possible, not an impossible ideal.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-is-christ|Who is Christ?]]
- **Holy Spirit as the song of love sung by the Father to the Son** _(literary)_ — A medieval mystic's image of the Spirit as the love-song between Father and Son depicts the Trinity singing life into existence.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]], [[covenant-umc/2026/three-whats|Three Whats?]]
- **Holy Spirit is not a vampire** _(pop-culture)_ — Prevenient grace illustrated by the Holy Spirit not being a vampire who must be invited into the house before entering.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-will-come-to-judge-the-living-and-the-dead|He Will Come to Judge the Living and the Dead]]
- **Holy, Holy, Holy at communion and at the shepherds' fields** _(biblical)_ — The Sanctus sung at communion and by the heavenly host to the shepherds at Christmas connects the church's worship to the song of the elders around the throne.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Home Alone** _(pop-culture)_ — Kevin wandering the store carefree mirrors the child's blissful obliviousness to being lost.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/when-you-re-lost-god-will-find-you|When You're Lost, God Will Find You]]
- **Home Depot trips for home projects** _(personal)_ — Holiday home projects and Home Depot runs as another marker of the busy striving around Christmas.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **Honeymoons end and babies eventually eat solid food** _(personal)_ — Good things in life don't last forever — but the obverse is more encouraging: hard things are likewise not the end of the world.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/get-ready-for-something-big|Get Ready for Something Big]]
- **Hope Floats (Sandra Bullock film)** _(pop-culture)_ — The Smithville-set movie's surface-level 'keep hoping' message exemplifies generic Hollywood hope that is not biblical hope.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **Horizon of Earthly Pursuits** _(other)_ — The horizon limiting what we can and cannot see pictures how Jesus' light reaches beyond and within our present and future.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/animals-and-god-s-promise-children-s-message-the-true-light-already-shines|Animals and God's Promise (Children's Message): The True Light Already Shines]]
- **Horse and dog pedigree records** _(other)_ — Breeding records show how the 'good blood' idea predates genetics and gets wrongly read into the Bible.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Hosting a wedding banquet (place of honor)** _(biblical)_ — Reframes the parable: whom do we seat in the place of honor of biblical authority?  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **House built on sand versus rock** _(biblical)_ — Building one's identity on the sand of relationships or hobbies causes drag, versus the rock of hearing and practicing God's word.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **House Foundations You Never Discuss** _(personal)_ — Homeowners never want to talk about foundations after they're laid, illustrating how foundational assumptions (in marriage, jobs) go unquestioned.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Houston Astros fandom and the sign-stealing scandal** _(personal)_ — The preacher's lifelong, biased loyalty to the Astros (defending them despite the cheating scandal while condemning rival teams) illustrates how we sort people into good/bad buckets and refuse to examine the judgment.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/2nd-sunday-in-lent-taking-up-the-cross-and-costly-grace|2nd Sunday in Lent: Taking Up the Cross and Costly Grace]]
- **Hugh of St. Victor's four arks** _(historical)_ — Medieval Hugh of St. Victor read four arks in Noah's story (Noah's, the church, wisdom, grace) structuring the whole sermon.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Husband betting the mortgage on black** _(other)_ — A gambler losing the family home illustrates the social harms of gambling that made Methodists wary of it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Hypocrisy is like being lazy** _(other)_ — Nobody struggles to do less; hypocrisy comes naturally, requiring no effort to fall into.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-opposite-of-hypocrisy|The Opposite of Hypocrisy]]
- **Hypocrite as Greek Actor** _(literary)_ — The word hypocrite comes from the Greek for one who plays a part, illustrating faith that is performed rather than embodied.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Hypocritical public figures** _(current-events)_ — The family-values politician with an affair, the pastor who embezzles $50,000, the youth caretaker who abuses—recurring hypocrisy that drives people from church.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Hypostasis at the Council of Nicaea (325)** _(historical)_ — The Greek term for faith's 'substance' connects to the trinitarian debates, illustrating faith as foundation rather than culture or heritage.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-jesus|The Gift of Jesus]]
- **I don't know how a computer works** _(personal)_ — Most people use the church without understanding denominational differences, like using a computer they can't repair, and would just switch to another if it broke.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **I like church, I don't like church stuff** _(personal)_ — A line from the preacher's parents' friend distinguishing love of the church from its painful interpersonal mess.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **I Love Cake / Spinach Tamales** _(personal)_ — The way English uses 'love' for everything from spouses to food shows how the word has been hollowed out into mere desire rather than action.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **I Need Thee Every Hour (spiritual)** _(literary)_ — The old hymn voices the sermon's call to admit need and cry out to Christ for rescue.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-should-we-do-to-be-saved|What Should We Do to Be Saved?]]
- **I'm not going to lie today** _(other)_ — A small starting light—committing simply not to lie today—as the ground floor of truthful living.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Ice cube in prenatal class** _(personal)_ — Holding an ice cube to mimic a contraction in a class before his son Dominic's birth illustrates the particular, concrete pain of a particular birth.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Icon controversies (8th century and Reformation)** _(historical)_ — Church fights over icons illustrate the contested power and danger of images of God.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/all-peoples-and-creatures|All Peoples and Creatures]]
- **Idaho soil vs. Texas soil** _(nature)_ — A comparison of soils illustrating the parable of the sower and how costly tilling by God lets us bear fruit.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Idle talk about weather and the Longhorns** _(personal)_ — talk that fills time and doesn't matter, the opposite of how we should speak of God in prayer  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Idol-meat and showing off a good job/marriage** _(other)_ — Don't flaunt your blessings before the suffering, as Paul warns against eating idol-meat before weak believers  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **If it looks like a duck and quacks like a duck** _(other)_ — The duck-test of appearances works for humans but not for God, who looks at the heart rather than outward stature.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-in-christ|Freedom in Christ]]
- **If It's Free, You Are the Product** _(other)_ — The internet adage that free services make users the product frames consumers as commodities with unquenchable desire.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Imagine Jesus as your own child** _(other)_ — Extended perspective-shift asking listeners to see the Pharisees as anxious parents worried their child mixes with the wrong crowd.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-prodigal-son-and-the-ministry-of-reconciliation|The Prodigal Son and the Ministry of Reconciliation]]
- **Imagining Jesus with a Galilean accent** _(other)_ — We picture Jesus speaking like us, but to his contemporaries he was a Galilean healer Pilate's violence should have enraged  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]]
- **Immaculate Reception (Franco Harris, 1972 playoffs)** _(pop-culture)_ — Wordplay distinguishing the famous football catch from the Catholic Immaculate Conception doctrine people confuse with Jesus's conception.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **Immanuel Kant's categorical imperative** _(historical)_ — Kant's no-exceptions deontology shows the rigid system Jesus contrasts by prioritizing love over universal maxims.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Immediacy and the etymology of im-mediate** _(other)_ — Breaking apart the word 'immediate' (im- + medium) to show our culture wants the unmediated and instant.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-lamb-of-god|The Lamb of God]]
- **In the Aggie section but a Longhorn** _(personal)_ — Sitting in the Aggie alumni section at Kyle Field while wanting Texas A&M to lose, illustrating being 'in the world but not of it' as a matter of desire, not garb.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **In the Bleak Midwinter (Christina Rossetti)** _(literary)_ — Rossetti's carol is iconographic, drawing us to God now and modeling giving Christ our heart as the only fitting gift.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Index fund / real estate venture** _(other)_ — You can't put God's love in an index fund or real estate venture—it is given to share, not to hoard.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Indianapolis Colts owner on oppressed billionaires** _(current-events)_ — A billionaire claiming his group is the most oppressed shows how anyone can find a grievance to be angry about.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-foolishness-of-the-cross-jesus-cleanses-the-temple|The Foolishness of the Cross: Jesus Cleanses the Temple]]
- **Indulgences and the Prosperity Gospel** _(historical)_ — The Reformation fought indulgences only for them to return as the prosperity gospel, showing recurring distortion of the faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Infinite heavenly chocolate fountain in the narthex** _(other)_ — Humorously pictures the rejected hypothesis of an infinite source of being versus a single point of origin.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]], [[covenant-umc/2026/three-whats|Three Whats?]]
- **Influencer saying 'you need this supplement'** _(current-events)_ — a marketer turning a choice into a necessity warns us to be wary when something is framed as unavoidable  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]]
- **Influencers and celebrities selling life tricks** _(current-events)_ — Modern voices promising salvation whose paths lead up a mountain but never back down, illustrating idolatry.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Inspiration as inhaling / inspirare** _(other)_ — The Latin root of 'inspire' (to breathe in) ties to lighting candles and using incense to actively inhale and be inspired by the saints.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Instagram and Facebook comparison** _(current-events)_ — Your worth is not based on Instagram, Facebook, neighbors, siblings, or winning, but is grace all the way down.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Instagram-worthy Sunday meals** _(current-events)_ — Contrasts treating resurrection as a nice idea shared between curated brunches versus all cosmic reality being overturned.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]], [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Intermittent fasting can't be branded or sold** _(current-events)_ — Fasting isn't promoted as a weight-loss strategy because, unlike Paleo or cleanses, there's nothing to brand and sell, exposing the world's drive to make us consume more.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]]
- **Intermittent fasting can't be branded or sold** _(current-events)_ — Illustrates how the world's story demands consumption: you can sell cleanses and Paleo, but not 'not consuming.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Interpretation chart as complex as an atom diagram** _(other)_ — Dispensationalist charts being as intricate as a diagram of an atom or protein molecule illustrate the overcomplication of Revelation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/alpha-and-omega|Alpha and Omega]]
- **Introduction to Methodism Class** _(personal)_ — A well-attended adult class sparked by confirmation-class members wishing for 'confirmation for grownups.'  → October Pastor Report
- **Investment banker's favorite verse** _(other)_ — A Christian investment banker reading the parable as a mandate to take financial risk, which the sermon then subverts.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/do-or-do-not|Do Or Do Not]]
- **Irony as an English-class term** _(literary)_ — The hard-to-define notion of irony (a disconnect between what we see and what is really there) frames the gap between Hosannas and 'Crucify him.'  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Isaac means laughter** _(other)_ — The meaning of Isaac's name, 'laughter,' tied to the changing of names and the birth of the promised child.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/hagar-sarah-and-the-birth-of-a-people|Hagar, Sarah, and the Birth of a People]]
- **Isaiah as the fifth gospel** _(personal)_ — A teacher's name for Isaiah, underscoring how centrally it points to the Messiah.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Isaiah Foretelling Hell's Trouble** _(biblical)_ — Isaiah's word that Hell is troubled by encountering Christ below shows the prophesied defeat of death.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-s-paschal-homily|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom's Paschal Homily)]]
- **Isaiah's feast and the bosom of Abraham** _(biblical)_ — Scriptural images (a feast that never grows old, rest in Abraham's bosom, worship before the Lamb in Revelation) offered as images rather than a concrete geography of heaven.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Isaiah's feast and the lion lying with the lamb** _(biblical)_ — Scriptural images (Isaiah 25, Revelation 22) show God gives us pictures, not blueprints, of our open-ended future with him.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **Isaiah's feast with no bloating** _(biblical)_ — The great feast of Isaiah 25 pictures the transformed life to come where one enjoys God's bounty fully.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Israel in Egypt longer than the U.S. has existed** _(historical)_ — A scale comparison — Israelites were in Egypt longer than the U.S. has been a country, and longer than most families in the room have been in North America — conveying the depth of generational estrangement between Pharaoh and Israel.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **Israel in exile** _(biblical)_ — Centuries of toil away from Jerusalem model how to remain faithful to God in exile, as Christians awaiting new creation.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Israeli airport security asking Alina 'why now?'** _(personal)_ — An IDF officer's stern 'why now?' to the preacher's wife on a trip to Israel frames the sermon's recurring question we ask of God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]]
- **Israelites longing to return to Egypt** _(biblical)_ — The Hebrews in the wilderness pining for full bellies in slavery exemplify how fixation on a falsely-golden past blinds us to what God has opened up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
- **It's just bread from HEB** _(personal)_ — A skeptic's mockery that communion elements are merely grape juice and bread from the grocery store dramatizes how difficult the teaching of real presence is.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Ivan and Alyosha in The Brothers Karamazov** _(literary)_ — Ivan's rejection of God over the suffering of innocent children frames the problem of evil; Alyosha answers not with argument but an embrace  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **Jack carrying word from Athens to Sparta** _(historical)_ — An informal ancient messenger of convenience warning King Leonidas the Persians are coming, illustrating the messenger profession.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-he-is-not-here-among-the-dead|Easter: He Is Not Here Among the Dead]]
- **Jack Reacher-sized untrained opponent** _(pop-culture)_ — Size and strength don't determine who prevails in a grapple, just as Jacob need not be the 'burly' one to hold his own with the angel.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Jacob Böhme, the shoemaker of Görlitz** _(historical)_ — A 17th-century mystic who had a vision and 'could not shut up about it,' warning against becoming myopically fixated on visionary experience rather than moving on in faithfulness as Jacob did.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Jacob grasping for blessing** _(biblical)_ — Jacob scheming to get what he wants contrasts with James and John, who had already given up everything for Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Jacob wrestling the unknown figure** _(biblical)_ — Jacob the deceiver is wounded in the thigh yet refuses to let go and is blessed and renamed Israel, modeling how Christ's healing is a wrestling match within the heart.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Jacob's Ladder and Wrestling the Angel** _(biblical)_ — The scheming second son's vision and wrestling match show divine presence breaking into ordinary, undeserving lives.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/in-the-presence-of-god|In the Presence of God]]
- **Jane Austen regency rectors** _(literary)_ — Wealthy marriageable clergy of Austen novels contrasted with the more modest class position of early-18th-century Anglican priests  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **January Gym Memberships and Spring Training** _(current-events)_ — The new-year surge in gym sign-ups and baseball's spring training as the optimistic 'this is the year' feeling we bring to fresh starts.  → Notes from the Pastor: Resetting Yourself with God in the New Year
- **Jason and the Argonauts / Golden Fleece** _(literary)_ — Mythological quests aim at a treasure, like the golden fleece, mirroring how our lives chase a goal.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Jean Valjean's 'Who Am I?'** _(pop-culture)_ — Valjean confessing his true identity rather than letting an innocent man suffer shows identity confronted through grace and the cost of honesty.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i|Who Am I?]]
- **Jeff Bezos leaving you $70 billion** _(pop-culture)_ — An overnight fortune makes your current possessions irrelevant, illustrating not clinging to stuff in light of the kingdom.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Jehoram's evil reign (2 Kings 8:16-18)** _(biblical)_ — Used as a concrete example that Israel's kings, and by extension the people, were rarely faithful to Torah.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Jennifer's divorce** _(personal)_ — A member from Houston, a lawyer, for whom the church was present and supportive during her divorce and a dark day.  → Congregant Notes (Sue, Emily, Jen, Nancy, Brenda, Michael, Rich, Jennifer)
- **Jeremiah 29:11 in its harsh context** _(biblical)_ — The comforting verse sits amid the prophet's announcement of exile and suffering, showing God's faithfulness is found in the midst of brokenness, not in denying pain.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Jeremiah's Potter's House** _(biblical)_ — The potter reshaping the marred clay shows God can remake us even after we've been baked and fixed in our ways.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/subterranean-hometown-blues|Subterranean Hometown Blues]]
- **Jerry Jones putting a Cowboys fan in the game** _(pop-culture)_ — Being called onto the team though unqualified, illustrating the humbling invitation to participate  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **Jesus and the tax collector / woman / cross** _(biblical)_ — Jesus calling Matthew the tax collector, seeing a woman rather than an adulteress, and forgiving from the cross, illustrating love that covers and overwhelms sin until it is gone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins-2|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Jesus as a worldly failure / Nazareth neighbors** _(biblical)_ — Jesus had no retirement, no steady job, and his whereabouts unknown to Nazareth neighbors, showing worldly 'failure' that still bears divine fruit.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/do-or-do-not|Do Or Do Not]]
- **Jesus as your wayward child** _(personal)_ — A thought experiment imagining Jesus as your son or partner hanging out with the wrong crowd, to surface how we judge people who associate with sinners.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/field-hospital|Field Hospital]]
- **Jesus before Pilate** _(biblical)_ — Jesus deflects Pilate's 'Are you the King of the Jews?' by turning the question back, contrasted with the disciples' direct (if inadequate) answers to his own question.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **Jesus calling Peter and Andrew** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' two-word 'follow me' on the Sea of Galilee shows the radical simplicity of God's call versus our self-protective complexities.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it|What are we going to do about it?]]
- **Jesus Calling Peter and Andrew** _(biblical)_ — 'Lay down your nets and follow me' models the radical simplicity and directness of God's call.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-are-we-going-to-do-about-it-2|What Are We Going to Do About It?]]
- **Jesus calling Peter and Andrew to lay down their nets** _(biblical)_ — The simplest yet hardest example of salvation: dropping everything to follow Jesus when he calls.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Jesus Calls Us O'er the Tumult (hymn)** _(literary)_ — The hymn's 'Christian, follow me' ties the whole sermon series' invitations together at communion.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Jesus entering through locked doors** _(biblical)_ — Christ enters despite locked doors, showing he comes with peace even when we shut everyone out of our lives.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/recognized-by-his-scars-the-power-of-pardon|Recognized by His Scars: The Power of Pardon]]
- **Jesus Freak movement and guitar-led worship** _(historical)_ — Southern California's Jesus Freak movement illustrates the emergence of modern guitar-led praise worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **Jesus in Gethsemane** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' own wrestling with God in the garden shows struggle and questioning are not sin but part of faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Jesus is not an influencer or life coach** _(other)_ — Contrasting Jesus with a modern influencer/life coach to insist his words on worry are not self-help tips but a call to repent because he is Lord.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-we-fill-our-waiting|How We Fill Our Waiting]]
- **Jesus Looking Different to Each Person** _(biblical)_ — Imagining the risen Jesus appeared differently to the women than to Peter and John, paralleling how we each encounter neighbors differently.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Jesus Looks Like a Neighbor, Not a Zombie** _(biblical)_ — The risen Jesus is unrecognized, looking like a stranger or neighbor rather than a ghost or zombie.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Jesus Never Body-Shames Peter** _(biblical)_ — Jesus never tells Peter he's gained weight or should be more like John, showing God does not deal in the world's idle comparisons.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Jesus of Nazareth as Smallville** _(pop-culture)_ — Comparing obscure Nazareth to Superman's small hometown to stress Jesus's humble origins.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Jesus of Smallville** _(pop-culture)_ — Nazareth as a backwater 'Smallville' shows Jesus came from an unremarkable place, unlike where leaders are found.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Jesus qualifies the called** _(other)_ — A borrowed preacher's line that God doesn't call the qualified but qualifies the called, affirming the unready.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Jesus recognized by his scars, not his face** _(biblical)_ — The disciples knew the risen Jesus by his wounds, illustrating that resurrection transforms yet bears the marks of suffering.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/recognized-by-his-scars-the-power-of-pardon|Recognized by His Scars: The Power of Pardon]]
- **Jesus Revolution film / Chuck Smith and the Calvary Chapel revival** _(pop-culture)_ — The 1968 Calvary Chapel / Jesus Freak revival depicted in the movie shows revival rippling out across generations.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Jesus silent before Caesar at the passion** _(biblical)_ — Jesus refuses yes/no answers, instead showing who he is on the cross and as a child at Christmas.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/cantata-show-don-t-tell|Cantata: Show, Don't Tell]]
- **Jesus turning over tables** _(biblical)_ — Twisting forgiveness to maintain abusive power structures provokes the same outrage as the cleansing of the temple.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/recognized-by-his-scars-the-power-of-pardon|Recognized by His Scars: The Power of Pardon]]
- **Jesus weeping over Lazarus** _(biblical)_ — Jesus grieving Lazarus moments before raising him shows that we grieve because we love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]], [[covenant-umc/2023/god-s-song-of-love|God's Song of Love]]
- **Jesus' hidden scars shown to Thomas** _(biblical)_ — The risen Jesus had to point out his scars to Thomas rather than walking around displaying bloody wounds, showing even Jesus was not a wallowing mass of vulnerability.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]]
- **Jesus' poor first impression** _(biblical)_ — Jesus calls fishermen to drop everything and doesn't dress for success, illustrating that he gives no good first impression yet offers life.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **Jesus's teleportation narratives** _(biblical)_ — Jesus slipping away and appearing across the lake shows he felt no compulsion to fix everything or organize an elaborate structure  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Jesus's unremarkable appearance** _(biblical)_ — Jesus had no handsome appearance and couldn't bench press, illustrating that true power is in the Lord, not the body.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/food|Food]]
- **Jewish friends in College Station and Shavuot** _(personal)_ — His Jewish childhood friends celebrated visible holidays but not Pentecost/Shavuot, setting up the festival's Jewish origin as the giving of the law at Sinai.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Jewish mikveh and circumcision** _(historical)_ — Ancient Jewish immersion (mikveh) and required circumcision frame the Acts 15 debate over Gentile inclusion.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Jewish mikveh and synagogue excavations** _(historical)_ — Christians took and transformed the Jewish ritual-purification bath into baptism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/baptism|Baptism]]
- **Jewish proximity and European ghettos** _(historical)_ — Keeping Sabbath meant living within walking distance of the synagogue, clustering Jews together - a Sabbath-keeping root of European enclaves.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **JFK moon-race speech ("not because they are easy, but because they are hard")** _(historical)_ — Kennedy's vow to reach the moon shows humanity straining after hard, impossible goals.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Joachim of Fiore and dispensationalism** _(historical)_ — From Joachim de Fiore dividing history's ages, to the Plymouth Brethren Darbyites, to modern dispensationalism's seven dispensations, illustrating the long Christian temptation to predict the end.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]]
- **Job's 'Satan' as troublesome counselor, not paragon of evil** _(biblical)_ — The Satan figure in Job functions more like a critical courtier than the archetypal devil of later imagination, undercutting popular caricatures of ultimate evil.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Joe the unfulfilled good man** _(other)_ — Joe, a 'good' family man with bad-influence friends, encounters the risen Christ and his inner life changes without changing job or wife.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **John 3:16 in stadium end zones and football eye-black** _(pop-culture)_ — The most famous verse ripped from context onto end-zone signs and players' eye makeup, illustrating how 3:16 was never meant to stand alone.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/lifted-up-reading-the-bible-as-jesus-reads-it|Lifted Up: Reading the Bible as Jesus Reads It]]
- **John Brown's raid at Harper's Ferry** _(historical)_ — A landmark passed on the hike, marking the varied American history traversed along the trail.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **John Calvin's idol factory** _(historical)_ — Calvin's line that the human heart is an idol factory illustrates humanity's constant fabrication of false gods.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]], [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]], [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]], [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **John Cassian on acedia** _(historical)_ — Cassian's description of the restless monk gazing at the slow-setting sun illustrates boredom as the spiritual sickness of acedia.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **John Chrysostom on prayer, fasting, and mercy** _(historical)_ — Chrysostom's homily that prayer, fasting, and mercy are inseparable grounds the Lenten call to show mercy before seeking it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **John Chrysostom Paschal homily** _(historical)_ — The 1600-year-old 'Christ is Risen' acclamation proclaiming death annihilated and life liberated.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]], [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **John Chrysostom's Christmas sermon** _(historical)_ — An early preacher's astonished sermon on the Ancient of Days becoming an infant lifts up the wonder of the Nativity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]], [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **John Chrysostom's Christmas sermon** _(historical)_ — Chrysostom's astonished nativity sermon conveys the immensity of God-become-infant entering the covenant.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **John Chrysostom's Easter sermon on Hell in uproar** _(historical)_ — Christ defeated death and hell through humility and the cross, not combat  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **John Darby and the origin of rapture theology** _(historical)_ — Darby's 19th-century invention of the rapture shows that modern prophetic interpretation is not how the earliest Christians read the book.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/alpha-and-omega|Alpha and Omega]]
- **John Huebel on Iwo Jima** _(personal)_ — Grandfather who landed on Iwo Jima told funny stories but left other stories untold, showing some losses are nearly impossible to speak aloud.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **John Milton's Paradise Lost** _(literary)_ — Milton's Satan looks mighty at first but devolves into nothingness, and Jesus in Book 6 puts forth only half his strength, showing the heavenly battle is no fair fight.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]], [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **John Milton's Paradise Lost** _(literary)_ — Milton shaped how Wilson reads the forbidden-fruit passage, though he tries to return to the text afresh.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **John Milton, 'Upon the Circumcision of the Lord'** _(literary)_ — Quoted lines from Milton's poem frame Christ's circumcision as entirely satisfying the covenant humanity transgresses.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **John Milton, Upon the Circumcision of the Lord** _(literary)_ — Milton's poem frames Christ emptying his glory to satisfy the covenant we transgress.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]], [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **John Nash, A Beautiful Mind** _(pop-culture)_ — The mathematician of A Beautiful Mind whose equilibrium concept frames the choice between cooperation and betrayal.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-of-god-love-of-neighbor|Love of God, Love of Neighbor]]
- **John of the Cross on love at life's evening** _(historical)_ — Quoted by Williams: 'In the evening of our life, it will be on love that we are examined.'  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-next-thirty-seconds|The Next Thirty Seconds]]
- **John outrunning Peter to the tomb** _(biblical)_ — John's gloating about beating Peter to the empty tomb contrasts with the rare good line he gives Peter: 'Lord, to whom can we go?'  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **John Updike, 'Seven Stanzas at Easter'** _(literary)_ — Poem insisting on the literal bodily rising-'let us not mock God with metaphor'-against spiritualizing the resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]], [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **John Wesley and the Moravians** _(historical)_ — Wesley learned faithfulness needs no seclusion; grace and sin both find us wherever we are in community  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **John Wesley as an unappealing personality** _(historical)_ — The judgmental, prudish, un-charismatic Wesley shows God works revival through unlikable instruments.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **John Wesley sharing stories of holy lives** _(historical)_ — Wesley offered models of grace-filled living to transform lives, not boost efficiency.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **John Wesley sharing stories of holy lives** _(historical)_ — Wesley retold others' transformation stories as models of faithful living, not for efficiency but transformation.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **John Wesley withholding communion from Sophie Hopkey** _(historical)_ — Wesley's spiteful denial of communion to a woman who married another man shows how the power of pardon is abused when divorced from the Holy Spirit.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/recognized-by-his-scars-the-power-of-pardon|Recognized by His Scars: The Power of Pardon]]
- **John Wesley's Aldersgate conversion** _(historical)_ — Wesley's strict early moralism, failed Georgia mission, and his heart 'strangely warmed' at Aldersgate model the shift from faith-as-duty to faith-as-grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]], [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **John Wesley's coded commonplace-book journal** _(historical)_ — Wesley's devotional diary written in code, undeciphered until broken ~50 years ago by the preacher's teacher Richard Heitzenrater, models the journaling practice he gave the club.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **John Wesley's Covenant Prayer** _(historical)_ — Wesley's New Year covenant prayer ('I am no longer my own, but yours') models total self-surrender over self-care resolutions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **John Wesley's heart strangely warmed at Aldersgate** _(historical)_ — The stodgy failed missionary feeling assurance of grace on Aldersgate Road in May 1738 models conversion as confirmation of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **John Wesley's home health book** _(historical)_ — Wesley's health manual gives plain rules on air, food, light dinners, and exercise so people can show the light of Jesus while healthy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]]
- **John Wesley's preacher lineage and family** _(historical)_ — Wesley born into a family of preachers, showing calling as inherited tradition and particular history  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **John Wesley's rules for singing** _(historical)_ — Wesley's directions to sing lustily, modestly, and spiritually model the desperation and full-hearted offering worship requires.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/why-do-we-worship-the-assembly-and-the-bread-of-life|Why Do We Worship? The Assembly and the Bread of Life]]
- **John Wesley, Oglethorpe, and the failed Georgia mission** _(historical)_ — Wesley came to Savannah with high hopes to convert Native Americans and failed, showing revival comes despite the preacher's shortcomings.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Joining a church in 1950s Texas** _(historical)_ — 70 years ago everyone joined a church and Sundays were closed, showing how the pressure of external expectation persists even as its form changes.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Joke about hearing your own eulogy** _(pop-culture)_ — An old joke/movie-TV trope of someone faking death to overhear their eulogy, used to introduce the strangeness of Jesus asking 'who do people say that I am?'  → [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **Jonah ghosting God** _(biblical)_ — Jonah receiving God's call like a voicemail and fleeing illustrates avoiding God's love and call.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Jonathan Edwards and the Northampton awakening** _(historical)_ — Awakening means waking up to one's own sin and accountability, not a positive feeling  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Jonathan Edwards, 'Sinners in the Hands of an Angry God'** _(literary)_ — Edwards' image of hanging by a thread over the pit illustrates the contingency of life and that only grace delivers us from evil.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/lead-us-not-into-temptation|Lead Us Not into Temptation]]
- **Jonathan hearing David play the harp** _(biblical)_ — Jonathan's initial admiration of David grows into self-sacrificing friendship that defies his own royal self-interest.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Jordan River water in baptism bottles** _(other)_ — Bottles holding a little Jordan water are not magical but holy and sanctified because Christ entered, received, and gave us that water.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Joseph and the Technicolor Dreamcoat** _(pop-culture)_ — Cites the musical as evidence of how strange and dramatic the Joseph story is, even if he isn't a fan of it.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Joseph Campbell / Hans Urs von Balthasar myth comparison** _(literary)_ — The 'no' answer that resurrection is just a myth like Hercules, illustrating an intellectual way of denying the resurrection.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Joseph Martin Cantata foreword** _(literary)_ — The composer's foreword to 'An Invitation to a Miracle' evokes child-like wonder at the unexpected glories of Christmas.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/cantata-show-don-t-tell|Cantata: Show, Don't Tell]]
- **Joseph Ratzinger quotation** _(literary)_ — Quoted passage that the Christian faith stands or falls on Christ being risen, else Jesus is merely a failed religious leader.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]], [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Joseph Sold into Slavery** _(biblical)_ — Joseph keeps faith and thrives rather than complaining after his brothers sell him into slavery, illustrating childlike humility.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]], [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Josephus and the scholarly reappraisal** _(historical)_ — Recent scholarship affirming the authenticity of Josephus's reference to Jesus serves as extra-biblical evidence for the reality of Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **Joshua and the conquest of the promised land** _(biblical)_ — Israel's rescue into an occupied land comes at the cost of the lives and land of those already there.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Journaling shrinks fears on the page** _(personal)_ — Writing down anxieties makes them tiny on paper, a practice for not panicking.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Jubilee and the American economy** _(biblical)_ — If we practiced the biblical jubilee of forgiving debts every fifty years, the American economy would fall apart, showing the tension between the Christian calendar and the world's.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/new-year-s-eve-christ-the-king-sunday|New Year's Eve (Christ the King Sunday)]]
- **Jubilee as a trumpet blast of liberty in Leviticus** _(biblical)_ — The Levitical Jubilee canceling debts and returning land to God models the inefficient, debt-erasing economy of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Judas Maccabaeus and David as war heroes** _(historical)_ — Jewish revolt leader and warrior-king illustrate that victory in battle was the prized mark of a leader, unlike Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Judas perfume-waste meme** _(pop-culture)_ — an internet meme framing Judas's 'why was this not sold?' complaint about wasted resources  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **Judges in MMA, boxing, Olympics gymnastics, and art** _(other)_ — The varied senses of 'judgment' in English, distinguishing scoring/discernment from harmful judgmentalism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]]
- **Judo's origin among displaced samurai** _(historical)_ — Judo emerging as a nonviolent synthesis after the Tokugawa period's end illustrates grappling repurposed for peace, not violence.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Julian and Gregorian calendar reform** _(historical)_ — The arbitrary, human-chosen history of calendars (Caesar, Pope Gregory XIII, England's two-week jump in Wesley's day) shows our timekeeping is a construct, raising the question of who is Lord of our time.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/new-year-s-eve-christ-the-king-sunday|New Year's Eve (Christ the King Sunday)]]
- **Junia mistranslated as Junius** _(historical)_ — Translators changing the female name Junia to male Junius illustrates how women's apostolic roles were erased to defend a male-only priesthood.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/miriam-miriam|Miriam, Miriam]]
- **Junk bond rating** _(other)_ — Even when our soul's credit is junk status, God is willing to bear the risk of forgiving us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Justin Martyr on Plato and Moses** _(historical)_ — Justin Martyr's claim that Plato and Moses conversed shows early Christians' desire to link philosophy and faith as paths to the light.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Justin Martyr's account of Sunday worship** _(historical)_ — Second-century description of the early church gathering on Sunday for Scripture reading, exhortation, prayer, Eucharist, and a collection for orphans, widows, and the needy, modeling the shape of Christian worship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-to-build-a-car-justin-martyr-on-early-christian-sunday-worship|How to Build a Car (Justin Martyr on Early Christian Sunday Worship)]]
- **Kafka's Metamorphosis / Gregor Samsa** _(literary)_ — Gregor waking as a giant cockroach distinguishes metamorphosis (body) from metanoia (mind), the Greek word for conversion.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **Kant's duty-only morality** _(literary)_ — Kant's claim that good must be done from duty alone (not enjoyment) is contrasted with how God shows us to live.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Karl Barth and the journey into the far country** _(historical)_ — Barth's Church Dogmatics IV:1 chapter 'The Journey of the Son of Man into the Far Country' frames the incarnation as God's costly descent to us.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Karl Barth, Church Dogmatics IV.1** _(literary)_ — Barth on Christ becoming a servant and dying in our place grounds salvation wholly in what God does for us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-should-we-do-to-be-saved|What Should We Do to Be Saved?]]
- **Karl Ove Knausgård's My Struggle and auto-fiction** _(literary)_ — Plotless first-person memoir/auto-fiction (vs. Proust) exemplifies the cultural craving for the unmediated and immediate.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-lamb-of-god|The Lamb of God]]
- **Katherine Sonderegger on Christ in the pit** _(literary)_ — Her words that the Helper has tasted the bitter gall and is inside the suffering illustrate Christ's solidarity in our darkest grief.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Keeping up with the Joneses** _(pop-culture)_ — The American saying about social one-upmanship illustrates the futile pressure to keep up with 'the Jones's or the Herod's or the Caesars.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Keeping up with the Joneses (Herods)** _(pop-culture)_ — Riff on the American saying about not needing to keep up with the Joneses, or the Herods.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Keeping up with the Joneses / bucket lists** _(current-events)_ — Everyday drives to have the best kids, healthiest life, fullest bucket list, illustrating how the desire to dominate pervades ordinary life as a lack of faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/spicy-mustard|Spicy Mustard]]
- **Kennedy's moon race speech** _(historical)_ — Kennedy's 'we do these things not because they are easy but because they are hard' illustrates that stated reasons often differ from real motivations and don't fully make sense.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Keyser Söze and Luke's father spoilers** _(pop-culture)_ — The Usual Suspects and Star Wars reveals illustrate how a story's whole truth can be explained in one climactic moment.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Kids and animal noises / ice cream** _(personal)_ — Children delight in animal sounds (which toddlers make before they can speak), framing Noah's story as more than an excuse for animal noises.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Kids learning to sing the naming song** _(personal)_ — The delight children show when they "get" the song mirrors the deeper aha of grasping Christ's love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/god-told-adam-and-eve|God Told Adam and Eve]]
- **Kids sharing favorite toys** _(personal)_ — Children don't naturally share their favorite toy, showing sharing as a practiced letting-go of control.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Kids striving for excellence in school** _(other)_ — Wanting children to succeed in school shows that striving for excellence can be a genuine good that opens future opportunities.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Kids these days nostalgia** _(other)_ — The 'kids these days' refrain illustrates nostalgia that idealizes the past and discounts the present.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Kids with water bottles vs. soda generation** _(personal)_ — Society learning to drink plain water instead of soda illustrates recognizing our continual need for the real thing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Kids' wrist locks and bullies** _(personal)_ — His family does jiu-jitsu for community and confidence (kids can wrist-lock a bully), not to win championships.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Kierkegaard on 'Come here to me'** _(literary)_ — Kierkegaard's Practice in Christianity: truly inviting the burdened to come would overturn one's whole comfortable way of life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
- **Kierkegaard's swimmer in a harness** _(literary)_ — Being suspended in a ceiling harness to mimic swimming strokes shows you can describe faith's movements but cannot actually make the leap for someone else.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **Kierkegaard: the jealous person is the devil's martyr** _(literary)_ — Søren Kierkegaard's saying that the jealous person witnesses to the works of the devil, illustrating how jealousy tears down God's kingdom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-builds-up|Love Builds Up]]
- **Kindling the new fire** _(nature)_ — The opening prayer to sanctify the new fire so hearts are 'kindled with holy desire to shine forth with the brightness of Christ's rising.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]]
- **King James and the translation of the Kings books** _(historical)_ — James I, viewing his kingship as divinely ordained, objected only to harsh depictions of kings in Kings/Chronicles, illustrating the human elevation of earthly kingship.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-not-to-make-jesus-king|How not to make Jesus King]]
- **King Ralph (John Goodman film)** _(pop-culture)_ — Suddenly inheriting the English throne shows you'd leave your stuff behind, like the heavenly kingdom already given to us.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Knowing Jesus returns next Tuesday** _(personal)_ — If we knew the hour of Christ's return we'd procrastinate and set an alarm, but not knowing means living ready now.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Knowledge tree at the Natural Gardener** _(personal)_ — You can't buy a tree of the knowledge of good and evil at the local nursery, underscoring that the Eden fruit meant something beyond food.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **Laborers Arriving at Different Hours** _(biblical)_ — Those who toiled from the first hour and those who came at the eleventh all receive the same reward, illustrating God's grace to early and late alike.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-sunrise-service-the-paschal-homily-of-st-john-chrysostom|Easter Sunrise Service: The Paschal Homily of St. John Chrysostom]]
- **Laborers Hired at Different Hours** _(biblical)_ — Workers arriving at the first, third, sixth, ninth, and eleventh hours all receive their reward, illustrating God's gracious welcome of late and early alike.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]]
- **Laborers of the eleventh hour** _(biblical)_ — Workers arriving at the first, third, sixth, ninth, and eleventh hours all receive their reward, illustrating grace given equally to the late and the faithful.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]]
- **Laborers of the Hours** _(biblical)_ — Workers arriving at the first through eleventh hours all receive the same reward, illustrating God's gracious welcome of latecomers.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-s-paschal-homily|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom's Paschal Homily)]]
- **Laborers Welcomed at Every Hour** _(biblical)_ — Whether one toiled from the first hour or arrived at the eleventh, all receive equal reward, illustrating the lavish grace of the risen Lord.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-sermon|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom Sermon)]]
- **Lake Jackson, Texas — town with no straight streets** _(personal)_ — Bruce's hometown, a WWII Dow Chemical town designed by a Frank Lloyd Wright student so no streets are straight — showing the strange, distinctive place that shaped him.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Bruce Martin
- **Lake time, work time, mountain time** _(personal)_ — How place shapes our sense of time, setting up the contrast between human and God's time.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **Lamb slain for his people** _(biblical)_ — God's character is not a robotic overlord but a slain lamb, an offering freely given so we could be free.  → Drawn by the Father
- **Language as a metaphor for sacred story** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor that living our sacred story has a grammar, vocabulary, and syntax we learn by living it, not by stopping at the alphabet.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Large debt and the freedom of its forgiveness** _(other)_ — A seven- or eight-figure debt that reshapes an organization, and the freedom when it's gone, illustrates sin-as-debt and the relief of forgiveness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Last Will & Testament / testimonial shows** _(current-events)_ — Notes 'testament' surfaces almost nowhere in modern life except Scripture and legal wills (and loosely reality-TV 'testimonials'), to explain Old/New Testament as 'witness' to Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Laud's prayer book and the Bishops' War** _(historical)_ — Charles forcing William Laud's prayer book on the Scots triggered revolt, another contingent cause of the Civil War.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Lay-led circuit preaching** _(historical)_ — Preachers overseeing 60 churches with lay-led hymn-singing shows 19th-century Methodism as a liturgical laboratory.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Lazarus and the Rich Man** _(biblical)_ — Cited as the Bible's clearest example of eternal consequence tied to how one lived, grounding the otherwise speculative topic of judgment.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Learning a language / deceptive fluency** _(personal)_ — No language is easy to learn; thinking you understand a place because it speaks English is 'deceptive fluency' — a metaphor for living our sacred story, which has its own grammar and vocabulary that must be practiced daily.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Learning ASCII charts to type term papers after spilling coffee on his laptop** _(personal)_ — Rather than repair his broken keyboard, he stubbornly taught himself ASCII to type without certain keys, dramatizing the refusal to admit a need.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **Learning basketball like LeBron James** _(pop-culture)_ — You can't wake up expecting to play like LeBron; you start where you are and keep moving, like growing in love of neighbor.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i-in-christ|Who am I in Christ?]]
- **Learning bicycle, car, instrument** _(other)_ — Justice and prudence must be practiced like riding a bike or playing an instrument, an infused habit that requires doing.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i|Who Am I?]]
- **Learning Hungarian for Alina** _(personal)_ — Pruitt knowing no Hungarian when he met his wife Alina and visited Hungary illustrates how learning our sacred story is like learning a new language.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Learning to ride a bicycle** _(personal)_ — You can't learn from a book and must fall a few times, illustrating that prayer is an uncomfortable practiced habit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]], [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Learning to Ride a Bike** _(personal)_ — Once you learn to ride a bike the instructions become internalized, just as God's love and forgiveness become part of us once truly learned.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/learning-to-abide-the-means-of-grace|Learning to Abide: The Means of Grace]]
- **Left Behind series** _(pop-culture)_ — The Rapture/Anti-Christ novels the preacher grew up around exemplify popular Dispensationalist readings of Revelation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/alpha-and-omega|Alpha and Omega]]
- **Legal System and Children's Responsibility** _(other)_ — Modern law holds children below an age unaccountable for their actions and free of financial liability, reflecting Jesus's point that children are not mature or responsible.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]]
- **Lent Shares a Root with Lento (Slow Tempo)** _(other)_ — The musical term lento (play slowly) shares a root with Lent, framing the season as a time to slow down.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Leo the Great on the incarnation** _(literary)_ — Quoted patristic meditation on God coming within our grasp and becoming subject to death.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Liam Neeson in Taken** _(pop-culture)_ — David's 'particular set of skills' as a shepherd compared to the movie's hero, the worldly reading of David's power.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Lieutenant as 'place-holder'** _(other)_ — The French etymology (lieu + tenir) shows ancient 'officers' were just place-holders telling untrained farmers where to go.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Life Before Phones and GPS** _(personal)_ — Growing up having to ask directions and read maps illustrates how modern tools deceive us into thinking we know where we are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Life's blows are not the end of the world** _(personal)_ — Job loss, breakups, divorce, abusive relationships, and disease diagnoses are real but not the end of the world for those anchored in Christ's coming glory.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/get-ready-for-something-big|Get Ready for Something Big]]
- **Lifeboat theology** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor for reducing faith in Jesus to merely escaping hell, which the sermon rejects in favor of present salvation.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/glorification|Glorification]]
- **Lifting and setting down a heavy load** _(other)_ — Weights, backpacks, and furniture you set down to rest picture the burdens everyone must bear.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Lincoln's House Divided Speech** _(historical)_ — Lincoln's 1858 'house divided' speech against slavery illustrates that evil is united on its purpose and cannot be opposed with half-measures.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/binding-the-strongman|Binding the Strongman]]
- **Lincoln's Second Inaugural Address** _(historical)_ — Lincoln noting both sides 'read the same Bible and pray to the same God,' reframing the question from 'is God on our side?' to 'are we on God's side?'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-world|Reset Your World]]
- **Lincoln-Douglas debates ideal** _(historical)_ — The lost hope of Lincoln-Douglas style persuasion contrasts with today's regurgitated soundbites.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Lines we draw around our behavior** _(personal)_ — We all have things we will and won't do; faith in Christ dissolves those self-drawn limits.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]], [[covenant-umc/2024/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Liquidating a 401k to give it away** _(personal)_ — The pastor's own admission that he wouldn't give away his retirement savings exposes how modern property assumptions contradict Acts taken literally.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Little Longhorn reunion** _(personal)_ — Refusing to let fear control her, Elizabeth went to the Little Longhorn on Burnet where she reunited with Scott and 'time had disappeared.'  → Elizabeth and Scott
- **Little Nicodemi coming by night** _(literary)_ — We are all 'little Nicodemi' bringing earnest, doubting clarifications to Jesus about whether he really means his hard commands.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Living on a mountaintop** _(nature)_ — Mountaintops are exposed to violent storms, so you must come down to live, illustrating why God saves us for the ordinary everyday rather than peak experiences.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Liz Lemon's speed-dating on 30 Rock** _(pop-culture)_ — Liz ruins every speed date by correcting her dates because 'I can't just let them be WRONG,' illustrating the human compulsion to judge.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]]
- **Local service ministries** _(personal)_ — Church Under the Bridge, Mobile Loaves and Fishes, Marble Falls rescue mission, Partners in Hope, and Blueprint Ministries become familiar through repeated service.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Logos in French as la Parole** _(other)_ — The French translation of Logos as 'speech' frames Jesus as the speech of God who gives speech before giving bread.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/how-many-people-did-jesus-feed|How many people did Jesus feed?]]
- **Lone Ranger and Tonto 'what do you mean we'** _(pop-culture)_ — Tonto's 'what do you mean we, kemosabe?' frames the central question of who 'we' are in the church and the two ways of being surrounded (allies vs. enemies).  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-communion-of-the-saints|I Believe in the Communion of the Saints]]
- **Longhorns football season hopes** _(current-events)_ — Preferring a proper, decisive win or loss over an agonizing near-miss sets up the sermon's theme that losses closest to success hurt the most.  → Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Longhorns loss to Washington (Michael Penix)** _(personal)_ — After the Longhorns blew the game by not tackling Penix, the pastor felt relaxed in the championship game with 'no skin in the game'—worry tied to expectation.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-we-fill-our-waiting|How We Fill Our Waiting]]
- **Looking for a body in a cave / hide and seek** _(other)_ — Common-sense scene of checking a tomb for a corpse, emphasizing the literal reality that Jesus is not there.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-he-is-not-here-among-the-dead|Easter: He Is Not Here Among the Dead]]
- **Looking for God like a house, car, or job** _(other)_ — We tend to search for Jesus the way we shop for a house, car, or job, but God cannot be possessed that way.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **Looking for the sucker at the poker table** _(other)_ — The poker adage that if you can't spot the sucker at the table it's you, illustrating how deceivers look for someone to fool.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/advent-don-t-be-deceived|Advent: Don't Be Deceived]]
- **Looking in a mirror and forgetting** _(biblical)_ — James's image of glancing in a mirror and forgetting one's face illustrates how we forget who we are without doing God's word.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Looking in the mirror and forgetting** _(biblical)_ — James's image of one who looks in a mirror and forgets his appearance, illustrating that unpracticed faith fades back into tearing people down.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-builds-up|Love Builds Up]]
- **Lorenzo Valla and the Donation of Constantine** _(historical)_ — A Renaissance scholar exposing the Donation of Constantine as a forgery, illustrating the rise of historical criticism applied to church tradition and eventually the Gospels.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/priest-prophet-king|Priest, Prophet, King]]
- **Losing your keys** _(other)_ — Turning a lost set of keys into 'I am a loser' shows how losing something feels like moral failure, but you are not a lost coin.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/when-you-re-lost-god-will-find-you|When You're Lost, God Will Find You]]
- **Lost at the Galleria Mall** _(personal)_ — Wilson, age 5-6, wandering off from his mother's hand while Christmas shopping, illustrating how we often don't notice we're lost.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/when-you-re-lost-god-will-find-you|When You're Lost, God Will Find You]]
- **Lost on a family vacation** _(personal)_ — You won't ask for directions until you admit you're lost, paralleling admitting you are tired.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **Lost sheep as bad business / tax write-off** _(other)_ — Leaving the 99 is terrible efficiency, illustrating that Jesus seeks the lost and never treats anyone as a write-off.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-parables|The Parables]]
- **Lost's disappointing finale vs. well-crafted mysteries** _(pop-culture)_ — Lost's empty ending versus The Usual Suspects and Knives Out illustrates the difference between a good ending and a bad one.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Loudest Christians in the public square** _(current-events)_ — Non-Christians in 2025 picture Christianity as politicians loudly claiming faith, contrasting Jesus' call to quiet, secret devotion.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Love is like riding a bike** _(other)_ — Love compared to riding a bike or long-distance running shows that love is a practice and habit you get bad at if you stop doing it.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Love is like riding a bike / long-distance running** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor that love is a habit and practice you get bad at if you stop doing it, like an athletic skill.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Loved more than a PlayStation 5** _(pop-culture)_ — God's love outweighs any coveted material gift, freeing us from childish desire to receive.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Loved ones in addiction can always go lower** _(personal)_ — What looks like the last straw for an onlooker is not the bottom for the suffering person, who can always sink further.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Lover, Beloved, and Love** _(literary)_ — Augustine's image of God as Lover (Father), Beloved (Son), and Love (Spirit) as a favorite illustration of the Trinity.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Lover, Beloved, and the Love Between** _(literary)_ — The Augustinian analogy of Father as Lover, Son as Beloved, and Spirit as the Love between them, the preacher's favorite picture of the Trinity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Lover, beloved, and the love between them** _(literary)_ — Augustinian love analogy — Father as lover, Son as beloved, Spirit as their love that we are drawn into.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Loving or fearing the spotlight** _(other)_ — Some love the stage spotlight and others fear it, illustrating how people respond differently to Christ the light.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]]
- **Low blood sugar in the pastor's family** _(personal)_ — The preacher's family lashes out and blames others when hungry, illustrating how hunger weakens our responses.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/first-attempt-temptation-of-jesus|First Attempt (Temptation of Jesus)]]
- **Low-stakes prom movie vs. Star Wars** _(pop-culture)_ — The high stakes of Christian doctrinal disagreement are less like a small-town prom romance and more like Star Wars where the whole universe hangs in the balance.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **Luna and Rachel's bond with her Father** _(personal)_ — Seeing how Tanner connected with the dog Luna reminded Rachel of her relationship with her Father.  → Rachel and Tanner
- **Luther Cutting the Bible to the Literal Sense** _(historical)_ — Luther narrowing reading to the literal sense sets up the problem of how to read Jesus's heavily figurative language.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Luther's swaddling clothes** _(historical)_ — Luther's image of scripture as dirty swaddling clothes holding the baby Jesus shows it can be complicated yet hold the greatest gift.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]], [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Lutron, the Greek word for ransom** _(other)_ — The word ransom (lutron) meaning a payment that releases from bondage shows Jesus frees us by love, not power.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Lydia Patterson Institute / SMU Lawsuit** _(current-events)_ — October trip to El Paso for the jurisdictional mission council, plaintiffs in the lawsuit against SMU.  → October Pastor Report
- **Lydia's baptism in Macedonia** _(biblical)_ — The Spirit pushing Paul across a border to baptize Lydia and her household illustrates the Spirit doing radical, boundary-crossing things.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **Lyle Lovett's 'If I Had a Boat'** _(pop-culture)_ — The song's image of riding a pony on a boat as the fantasy of freedom unburdened by obligations to others.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **Mack Pruitt and the death camp** _(personal)_ — Grandfather who helped evacuate a death camp never spoke of it, illustrating the ineffability of certain experiences.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **MADD and drunk-driving laws** _(historical)_ — Mothers Against Drunk Driving changed the law so you must be in the right frame of mind to drive, like attentiveness is required to drive the church.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Madeline lying under the doorframe** _(personal)_ — The pastor's niece, told to stay in her room after bedtime, lay under the doorframe to go right up to the line — illustrating how we push limits to find the boundary.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-line-jesus-and-the-inversion-of-our-moral-expectations|The Line: Jesus and the Inversion of Our Moral Expectations]]
- **Magnificat as revolution** _(other)_ — Casting down the powerful from thrones is a political revolution, moving from governance to redistribution of resources.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Mahoosuc Notch Shoulder Dislocation** _(personal)_ — On the Appalachian Trail in 2004 the preacher dislocated his shoulder in the treacherous Mahoosuc Notch, becoming so restless with pain he no longer cared what happened as long as it changed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Maim and sh'maim (Hebrew for water and sky)** _(other)_ — Sky meaning 'waters above' illustrates the creation distinction between waters above and below.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **Maimonides' 613 commandments** _(historical)_ — Numbering Torah focuses on trees instead of the forest, taking away from the whole  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]], [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Mainframe computers and switchboard operators** _(historical)_ — Older generation's hard-to-explain tech parallels the inexplicable centrality of the Temple  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **Making Matzah While Preaching** _(personal)_ — The pastor bakes unleavened Passover bread during the sermon to embody bread as a real, material gift.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
- **Making the bed** _(personal)_ — Some feel compelled to make their bed daily, others never would, showing different baseline levels of responsibility in everyday life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/responsibility|Responsibility]]
- **Making Wikipedia Pages for Friends in Grad School** _(personal)_ — The preacher's prank of making Wikipedia pages for friends (flagged in three minutes) illustrates that most of us, like Mary and Joseph, will never enter the history books.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **Mal Blum, "Love and Hate"** _(pop-culture)_ — Song lyrics personifying Love and Hate as two people: Love writes warm letters and sails singing through the sky while Hate throws the letters away and walks the streets greeting strangers with a serious face, dramatizing love's persistence against hate's cold indifference.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/mal-love-writes-a-letter-and-sends-it-to-hate|Mal: Love writes a letter and sends it to Hate]]
- **Mandatum word origin of Maundy** _(literary)_ — The word 'Maundy' from the Latin mandatum (Greek entole) explains why this day is named for Christ's new commandment to love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]], [[covenant-umc/2026/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Manger as French word for 'to eat'** _(literary)_ — The manger is the French word 'to eat,' a food trough, so the promised child is laid in a place for animal feed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **Manichaeism and Zoroastrianism as dualist counterfeits** _(historical)_ — Ancient dualistic religions pictured good and evil locked in eternal combat; the sermon contrasts this with Christianity's claim that the battle is already over in Christ.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Manifest your will and find your crystals** _(current-events)_ — The world's gospel of defining your own truth, manifesting your will, and finding crystals is set against the harder, real message of Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Manna in the wilderness** _(biblical)_ — Manna that rotted when hoarded shows God's love and peace are given to share, not to store up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Manna in the Wilderness** _(biblical)_ — God gives just enough manna each day and extra rots, teaching reliance on God for daily provision.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
- **Manna in the wilderness as the bread of heaven** _(biblical)_ — Forty years of manna and Christ's own body given as the bread of heaven, contrasted with the church's betrayal  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-service-for-good-friday|A Service for Good Friday]], A Service for Good Friday
- **Manners are like riding a bicycle** _(other)_ — Manners as practiced habits that become second nature, like a learned skill.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Manners as habits like riding a bicycle** _(other)_ — Manners become second nature through practice, illustrating how gratitude is a learned, repeated posture of love.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **Marathon begun by running a mile** _(other)_ — A New Year's resolution to run a marathon starting with a single mile illustrates that conversion always begins with a small step of grace.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/new-year-s-eve-christ-the-king-sunday|New Year's Eve (Christ the King Sunday)]]
- **Marathon running and community** _(personal)_ — Running two marathons among 30,000 people, and a two-year injury that stole his prayer-running, show athletics as koinonia/fellowship not winning.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Marathon training** _(other)_ — You don't run a marathon on day one - growth in grace is gradual; walk before you run, start where you are.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
