- **Marcion and the angry-OT/happy-NT God** _(historical)_ — The Marcionite error of an evil Old Testament God versus a loving New Testament God, which the early church rejected because it would make creation evil and Jesus a liar.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-a-new-commandment-god-s-love-is-old-not-trendy|Not a New Commandment: God's Love Is Old, Not Trendy]]
- **Marie Antoinette 'let them eat cake'** _(historical)_ — The queen's distance from starving subjects contrasts with God bridging the distance to humanity in Jesus Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Marie Kondo sparking joy** _(pop-culture)_ — Contrasts Kondo's 'spark joy' decluttering with the real question of whether objects draw you toward or away from God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Marines surviving at Parris Island** _(other)_ — Intentional, time-bound survival training illustrates that survival always has a point beyond itself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Marketing segments and brackets** _(other)_ — The world's question 'which segment are you in, how can we get you to spend more' illustrates how consumer culture divides and reduces us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]]
- **Marketing segments and the 'under 10' bracket** _(current-events)_ — Shows the world's thirst to segment people for profit, unlike Jeremiah who fits no marketing bracket.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Marketing that makes you feel inadequate** _(current-events)_ — Ad tactics convince you that without a certain thing, car, or body you aren't a real man or woman, a lie about what is good for us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **Marlo Stanfield and Omar from The Wire** _(pop-culture)_ — HBO's depiction of Baltimore gang rivalry ('when you come at the king, you best not miss') models the threat Herod felt from Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/rivals|Rivals]]
- **Marriage as an act of faith (a teacher's saying)** _(personal)_ — A teacher's line that 'you never marry the right person' because you can't know who they'll become shows marriage itself is a step of faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Marriage counseling and the vows** _(personal)_ — Couples taking vows for better or worse never know what they're getting into, yet the vows are not false—like participating in the Trinity without full understanding.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Marriage to Alina and humble beginnings** _(personal)_ — His wife saw the hope of God's call on his life despite his poverty and stalled career, illustrating sight by the light of God's call.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **Married to a teacher** _(personal)_ — His teacher wife's lack of classroom freedom shows the predetermined ordering of life extends into adulthood.  → Drawn by the Father
- **Marsha caring for mom after Donald's death** _(personal)_ — Devoted family care, with Marsha staying with and tending to her mother since her husband passed in 2011.  → Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Martin Luther on Christian freedom** _(historical)_ — Luther's paradox that the Christian is utterly free master of all yet utterly dutiful servant of all frames the gospel's paradoxical freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **Martin Niemoller's 'First they came' poem** _(literary)_ — The poem about silence in the face of Nazi persecution illustrates the cost of failing to speak out.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-cost|The Cost]]
- **Mary and Joseph walking to Bethlehem without a donkey** _(biblical)_ — Re-telling the nativity journey on foot with Joseph carrying weary Mary dramatizes the gritty particularity of Jesus' arrival.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Mary and Martha** _(biblical)_ — Mary as contemplation and Martha as service/celebration, both good in their own time  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]], [[covenant-umc/2025/glorification|Glorification]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]], [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Mary and the angel Gabriel** _(biblical)_ — Mary's fear and perplexity at the annunciation parallels John's unworthiness — both admit insufficiency before God's glory.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-baptism-of-the-lord|The Baptism of the Lord]]
- **Mary anointing Jesus' feet at Bethany** _(biblical)_ — In John 12 Jesus receives a blessing from Mary; on Maundy Thursday he reverses it by giving the blessing through foot washing.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Mary at Jesus' feet vs. Jacob wrestling the angel** _(biblical)_ — Reading scripture sometimes feels like restful sitting at Jesus' feet, other times like wrestling all night.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **Mary Magdalene at the Empty Tomb** _(biblical)_ — The two Marys discovering the empty tomb and passing the news to Peter and John illustrates the unbroken tradition of the gospel handed down to today.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Masanobu Fukuoka's The One-Straw Revolution** _(literary)_ — Japanese farmer's integrated natural-farming method frames the sermon's central metaphor of not separating what belongs together.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **Massachusetts Bay Colony and John Winthrop's city on a hill** _(historical)_ — The Puritan colony meant to be a city on a hill instead shone the light of division, praising God for a smallpox outbreak that killed 40% of indigenous people, illustrating prideful self-righteous religion.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/field-hospital|Field Hospital]]
- **Mastered skills like riding a bike or brushing teeth** _(other)_ — Things fully understood fade into autopilot; parables resist this so meaning stays alive and transforming.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-parables|The Parables]]
- **Matinee movies and dilated pupils** _(personal)_ — Stepping from a dark theater into bright sunlight contracts the pupils painfully; pupils dilated in darkness are ready to absorb the true light, like the Magi straining to see in a dark world.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/arise-shine-the-light-of-epiphany-and-the-grafting-in-of-the-gentiles|Arise, Shine: The Light of Epiphany and the Grafting In of the Gentiles]]
- **Matthew Brady moving Civil War dead** _(historical)_ — A photographer rearranging corpses shows that every image is manipulated and incomplete.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/all-peoples-and-creatures|All Peoples and Creatures]]
- **Matthew McConaughey's 'tie your shoes' running advice** _(pop-culture)_ — Eliminating excuses to start running illustrates taking the simple first step of following Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]], [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Maxed-out retirements and 529 plans can't save you** _(other)_ — Even full retirement accounts and college funds cannot rescue anyone from death and brokenness, pointing to the need for a savior.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **Maximus of Turin on Christ sanctifying the water** _(historical)_ — The early church bishop taught Christ was baptized not to be made holy but to make the water holy.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **McDonald Observatory and Webb Telescope** _(personal)_ — The pastor's family trip to West Texas to view stars and Hubble/James Webb images, evoking the beauty of space and the ancients' view of the heavens.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-ascended-into-heaven|He Ascended into Heaven]]
- **Meals counted across a lifetime** _(other)_ — A 40-year-old has eaten ~43,830 meals and an 80-year-old ~87,660, showing how relentlessly food and worry occupy a life.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-jesus|The Gift of Jesus]]
- **Medici and Fugger banking wealth** _(historical)_ — Banking families amassed unprecedented non-royal wealth, illustrating the seductive accumulation of riches the gospel passage confronts.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eye-of-the-camel|Eye of the Camel]]
- **Medieval fourfold method of reading Scripture** _(historical)_ — Literal, allegorical, moral, and eschatological senses, and Luther's partial pushback, frame the difference between literal and serious reading.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/lifted-up-reading-the-bible-as-jesus-reads-it|Lifted Up: Reading the Bible as Jesus Reads It]]
- **Medieval Fourfold Reading of Scripture** _(historical)_ — Literal, allegorical, tropological, and anagogical senses illustrate the richer pre-modern way of reading the Bible beyond bare literalism.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Medieval noble pedigrees and marriages** _(historical)_ — Warlord nobles checking bloodlines (Gunther and Hilda) illustrate the mistaken assumption that good blood makes a person good.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Medieval sin of gluttony as insatiable desire** _(historical)_ — Gluttony defined as an impossible-to-satisfy desire illustrates how worldly hungers can never finally be filled.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/food|Food]]
- **Meeting Alina at the Church Under the Bridge** _(personal)_ — The pastor's timid, fear-of-rejection courtship of his wife under the I-35 overpass illustrates why we approach Mary's annunciation expecting 'no.'  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Meeting Alina before online dating** _(personal)_ — The preacher and his wife met by being set up, just before online dating took over, illustrating that any relationship is a step of faith.  → Every Act of Faith
- **Meeting and marrying Alina** _(personal)_ — The preacher's unexpected courtship of his wife illustrates love as showing up daily rather than fitting tidy assumptions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunday-works-of-love|Easter Sunday: Works of Love]]
- **Membership Vows: Prayers, Presence, Gifts, Service, Witness** _(other)_ — The five membership promises, treated as mere obligations, deaden into dry works that keep us from God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **Men who forget to drink water** _(personal)_ — An older generation, especially men, whose bodily thirst triggers don't fire, used to illustrate our often-unfelt thirst of the soul.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/give-me-water|Give Me Water]]
- **Mercedes losing the constructors' championship** _(current-events)_ — After eight years of dominance Mercedes won't win this year, showing it takes different skill to drive a sub-optimal car, as the church now drives in a less privileged season.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Methodism born in Bristol fields and St. Paul's Evensong** _(historical)_ — Methodist worship's dual origins show the tradition holds space for both low and high church.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Methodist circuit riders on the frontier** _(historical)_ — Circuit riding let one pastor plant many frontier churches, explaining Methodism's rapid early American growth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-holy-catholic-church|I Believe in the Holy Catholic Church]]
- **Methodist sweater and Episcopalian section in heaven** _(other)_ — There will be no denominational divisions in eternal life; structures are signs of this world's brokenness, not the point  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Methodists vs Galen** _(historical)_ — The ancient Methodist physicians who rivaled Galen and taught that anyone could be a doctor became a slur for the egalitarian Wesleyan movement.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Michael and Fredo in The Godfather Part II** _(pop-culture)_ — Michael's cold rejection of Fredo ('you're nothing to me now') contrasts the prodigal's father who runs out to embrace his broken son instead.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Michael Jordan remembering who made varsity first** _(pop-culture)_ — Jordan using slights as motivation contrasts with Jesus, who was not petty and didn't keep score of those who said no.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Michelangelo chiseling the sculpture from marble** _(historical)_ — repentance chips away superfluous material to reveal who God created us to be  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-mercy-of-fire|The Mercy of Fire]], [[covenant-umc/2023/who-is-christ|Who is Christ?]]
- **Michelangelo the Teenage Mutant Ninja Turtle** _(pop-culture)_ — self-deprecating aside on the preacher's first cultural encounter with the name Michelangelo  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-mercy-of-fire|The Mercy of Fire]]
- **Michelin Guide and its stars** _(historical)_ — Michelin's three-star rating marks a place 'worth the trip on its own,' as John the Baptist was worth traveling to the Jordan to see.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **Midgard Serpent of Tail Risks** _(literary)_ — The Norse world-serpent names the enormous tail-risk manufacturers fear in self-driving liability.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Milestones as ordinary road markers** _(historical)_ — literal English road milestones were average markers to keep washed-out-road travelers on the path, illustrating that kingdom markers are ordinary, not grand events  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **Military and law enforcement gear** _(other)_ — Putting on extra gear before a situation signals an antagonist or enemy, paralleling the spiritual struggle Paul describes.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Military command structure** _(other)_ — Soldiers obey superior officers because of shared mission, illustrating buy-in to following a command.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Military commissioning of officers** _(historical)_ — The military's use of 'commission' as a verb (to send with) recovers the active sense of the Great Commission.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]]
- **Military commissioning of officers** _(other)_ — Officers are commissioned (a verb, sent-with) rather than passively appointed, recovering the active sense of the Great Commission.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Military leadership books (Clausewitz, Napoleon, Sun Tzu, Book of Five Rings)** _(historical)_ — Old leadership literature centered on generals and strategy, contrasted with Jesus calling ordinary followers.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]], [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Military order** _(other)_ — A commanding officer's order is immediate and uninterpreted — the kind of clarity we sometimes wrongly want from God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-lamb-of-god|The Lamb of God]]
- **Military triage** _(historical)_ — The 19th-century military medicine triage question 'Can they walk? Can they not walk? Are they dead?' as the speaker self-assessed his wounded feet, illustrating honest assessment of one's condition.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins-2|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Milo Hamilton and Larry Dierker radio broadcasts** _(personal)_ — Listening to Astros games on car-ride radio as a child grounds the preacher's formation as a baseball fan.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/2nd-sunday-in-lent-taking-up-the-cross-and-costly-grace|2nd Sunday in Lent: Taking Up the Cross and Costly Grace]]
- **Milo of Croton and the calf** _(historical)_ — The Olympic wrestler who carried a growing calf into a full-grown cow illustrates bearing one another in love.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Milton's Paradise Lost** _(literary)_ — literature filled gaps in biblical revelation about demons  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **Milton's Paradise Lost and the impotence of Lucifer** _(literary)_ — Wesley himself edited Paradise Lost for school use; its power lies in showing how ultimately powerless Lucifer is, reinforcing that the cosmic battle is already won.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Miriam throwing rocks at crocodiles** _(biblical)_ — Imagined image of Miriam protecting the floating basket illustrates the brave, behind-the-scenes love that saved Moses and the people.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/miriam-miriam|Miriam, Miriam]]
- **Mirror That Forgets Its Reflection** _(biblical)_ — James's image of forgetting your face in the mirror illustrates hearing the good news but returning to harmful living.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Missing car seat in Geneva** _(personal)_ — On a trip to visit his sister in Switzerland, his wife Alina crossed a DO NOT CROSS line to retrieve their child's car seat, finding a gate where none seemed to exist.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-gate|The Gate]]
- **Mission creep (Somali Civil War origin)** _(historical)_ — The 1990s military term for an intervention's scope quietly expanding past its original goal explains how temple commerce overran holy ground.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-foolishness-of-the-cross-jesus-cleanses-the-temple|The Foolishness of the Cross: Jesus Cleanses the Temple]]
- **Mitch Hedberg broken escalator joke** _(pop-culture)_ — A broken escalator is just stairs, illustrating that a car (or church) without a driver goes nowhere.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **MLB vs NFL** _(pop-culture)_ — Two sports leagues that are rivals, allies, and collaborators illustrate how denominations differ organizationally yet share a common enterprise.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Model T Ford** _(historical)_ — A best-seller that was loud, noisy, and structurally limited illustrates that popularity is not the same as a sound foundation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Modern news media as selling something** _(current-events)_ — The litany of outlets (NYT, Fox, CNN, MSNBC, etc.) and marketing industries that didn't exist in Paul's day, all now selling something, contrasts ancient 'good news' with today's distrust of news.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Modern shibboleths: hook 'em vs gig 'em, who you voted for** _(current-events)_ — Political shibboleths illustrate the temptation to judge 'good' vs 'bad' Christians.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Money changers and the graven-images problem** _(historical)_ — Changing coins bearing rulers' faces helped diaspora Jews keep the commandment against graven images, showing the money changers served a real religious need.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-foolishness-of-the-cross-jesus-cleanses-the-temple|The Foolishness of the Cross: Jesus Cleanses the Temple]]
- **Moneyball and Billy Beane** _(pop-culture)_ — Billy Beane looked like a ballplayer but didn't succeed; good looks don't help hit a pitch, illustrating that we don't need the 'not loves' or external markers to follow Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Moral hazard and the 2008 crisis** _(current-events)_ — Forgiving debts risks moral hazard, the reckless reliance on bailouts seen in 2008, yet that is exactly what God's forgiveness invites.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-race|The Race]]
- **Moralistic Therapeutic Deism** _(historical)_ — An early-2000s study of 3,000 teenagers found a distant 'just be good' God who would never ask anything odd, like stealing a pony, contrasting the demanding God of Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Moravians and the First Candlelit Fir Tree** _(historical)_ — Moravian and Pietist Protestants who influenced Wesley first put candles on a fir (Tannenbaum) to bring light into a dark season.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-cultivation-of-christmas-trees|The Cultivation of Christmas Trees]]
- **Moravians calm during the Atlantic storm** _(historical)_ — The German pietists praying and singing unafraid in a violent storm while Wesley was terrified shows the peace of grace-rooted faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
- **Moravians singing in the Atlantic storm** _(historical)_ — Their calm faith amid the storm exposes what Wesley lacked and points toward assurance  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Mortgages, amortization schedules, and moral hazard** _(other)_ — Long-term financial planning and the economist's 'moral hazard' contrast the predictable economy of man with the inefficient economy of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Moses and Elijah present for their own sake** _(biblical)_ — The prophets appear not merely for the disciples but so they themselves can behold and worship the God-man Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/who-is-jesus-the-transfiguration|Who Is Jesus? The Transfiguration]]
- **Moses and the bronze serpent in Numbers 21** _(biblical)_ — The lifted bronze serpent that healed snakebitten Israelites is Jesus' own figurative type for himself being lifted up to heal sin.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/lifted-up-reading-the-bible-as-jesus-reads-it|Lifted Up: Reading the Bible as Jesus Reads It]]
- **Moses and the Bronze Serpent in the Wilderness** _(biblical)_ — The snake Moses lifted up in the wilderness prefigures the Human One being lifted up so believers find eternal life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-found|Lost & Found: Found]]
- **Moses and the burning bush** _(biblical)_ — God telling Moses to remove his shoes on holy ground illustrates holiness as being set apart for God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Moses and the Burning Bush** _(biblical)_ — Standing on holy ground, told to remove his shoes, exemplifies encountering the presence of God beyond ourselves.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/in-the-presence-of-god|In the Presence of God]], [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **Moses and the Red Sea / Jordan Crossing** _(biblical)_ — Moses and the prophets split waters to pass through, contrasted with Jesus who instead walks over the waters entirely.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Moses Maimonides numbering the law** _(historical)_ — The Torah wasn't divided into discrete numbered rules until Maimonides, showing the law was meant as a whole life oriented to God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]]
- **Moses Maimonides Numbering the Law** _(historical)_ — The Torah wasn't divided into discrete numbered commands until Maimonides in the Middle Ages, showing dietary and moral law were once seen as one whole.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **Mother Night (Vonnegut)** _(literary)_ — The novel's line 'be careful who you pretend to be because in the end you are who you pretend to be' illustrates that how we live, not our intentions, shapes who we are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]], [[covenant-umc/2024/2nd-sunday-in-lent-taking-up-the-cross-and-costly-grace|2nd Sunday in Lent: Taking Up the Cross and Costly Grace]]
- **Mother Teresa 'Do It Anyway'** _(literary)_ — The 'Anyway' poem illustrates faithful, unrewarded goodness done for God rather than for human recognition.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/miriam-miriam|Miriam, Miriam]], [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Mother Teresa's 'Do It Anyway' poem** _(historical)_ — The poem from her Calcutta wall closes the sermon: give your best though it's never enough, for it is between you and God anyway.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **Mother's food drops** _(personal)_ — His mother mailing him food drops on the trail shows care and sustenance, though still not enough for his hunger.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/food|Food]]
- **Mountain climber: courageous vs foolhardy** _(other)_ — A courageous climber takes extra care at the riskiest spots while a foolhardy one climbs every spot the same and falls, illustrating the Greek distinction between courage and fearlessness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Mountain spring: source, flow, and creek** _(nature)_ — Water's source, flow, and creek as three inseparable things — analogy for Father, Son, and Spirit as one God.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Mountaintop experience of forgiveness** _(other)_ — A powerful mountaintop moment of forgiveness is not the end; like a mother wanting her child to grow, God wants to complete us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-does-not-seek-its-own|Love Does Not Seek Its Own]]
- **Mouth-breathing vs. nasal breathing** _(nature)_ — The medical benefits of nasal breathing become a metaphor for prayer as breathing with God and resetting the soul.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Movie depictions of demon possession** _(pop-culture)_ — Horror-film tropes (head-spinning, screeching) are used to contrast pop-culture Satan imagery with the biblical sense of 'satan' as accuser, applied to Peter's rebuke of Jesus.  → Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Movies that replay the last scene at the start** _(pop-culture)_ — Acts replaying Jesus' ascension that ended Luke is like a sequel that reopens with the previous film's final scene.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/get-ready-for-something-big|Get Ready for Something Big]]
- **Moving-mountains mustard seed parable vs. the growing-bush parable** _(literary)_ — Contrasts the more famous 'move this mountain' parable (self-centered, impractical) with Matthew 13's growing-bush parable (faith expanding to make room for others).  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Mr. Bienkowski's NYPD diver tryout** _(personal)_ — 32 people trying out for four slots (only two made it) shows how life sorts people to find the best, leaving most out.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **Mr. Christ greeting** _(other)_ — A joking 'good afternoon, Mr. Christ' shows Christ was a title (anointed), not Jesus' surname.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
- **Mr. John Huebel and the Lake movie theater** _(personal)_ — The theater owner who patrolled with a swinging flashlight to stop teens making out turns out to be the preacher's own granddad — revealing the surprising personal connection between pastor and the family.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Bruce Martin
- **Mr. Rogers' Neighborhood** _(pop-culture)_ — The 'won't you be my neighbor' theme as a generational image of what being a neighbor means.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Ms. Brenda's confirmation class** _(personal)_ — Referenced as the local teacher of Methodist origins to the congregation's confirmands.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Multilingual congregation understanding each other** _(personal)_ — English, Hungarian (Alina), Korean (Younee), and Spanish (Alexander) speakers all comprehending one another illustrates being fully understood while remaining fully yourself.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Murder on the Orient Express twist** _(literary)_ — The shocking reveal that all the passengers did it illustrates how forgiveness and mercy arrive as an unexpected, justice-bearing twist.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/mercy-revealed|Mercy Revealed]]
- **Mustard seed growing into a tree** _(biblical)_ — Jesus's parable shows God's love expanding from something insignificant into the redemption of the world.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]]
- **Mustard seed multiplication / grocery store mustard jars** _(nature)_ — One mustard bush yields thousands of seeds and nearly every grocery store carries jars of mustard, illustrating how small faith multiplies abundantly.  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Mustard seed transformed into mustard** _(nature)_ — A tiny mustard seed, hard to clean up when spilled but tasty after being smashed with vinegar and turmeric, illustrating hidden power and transformation in small things.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/spicy-mustard|Spicy Mustard]]
- **Mustard-Seed-Sized Faith** _(literary)_ — Asking the mass or volume of faith shows the absurdity of pressing figurative language into wooden literalism.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Mystery stories and the act of faith (P.D. James)** _(literary)_ — Reading a mystery is an act of faith that a hidden truth exists and will be revealed, mirroring faith that God's mercy is the true twist at the heart of the world.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/mercy-revealed|Mercy Revealed]]
- **Name-repeating trick when meeting someone** _(personal)_ — Repeating a new acquaintance's name ('Nice to meet you, Jack...') to remember it shows we hold on to names that matter to us — as God holds the builders' names.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/what-s-next-builders-and-names|What's Next? (Builders and Names)]]
- **Naming our own children** _(personal)_ — We name our kids but don't control them, countering the claim that to name is to control.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/god-told-adam-and-eve|God Told Adam and Eve]]
- **Nathaniel and Nazareth** _(biblical)_ — Nathaniel's 'what good can come out of Nazareth?' shows Jesus wasn't from the right kind of place.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **Natural Explanations for Walking on Water** _(other)_ — Rationalizing theories (thin ice, near shore) are dismissed as failing to fit the text, preserving the miracle as a sign of Christ's divinity.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Nazareth Not on Early Maps** _(biblical)_ — Jesus raised in backwoods Nazareth, a town so insignificant it didn't appear on early maps, underscoring God choosing the lowly.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **Nazi Pervitin methamphetamine** _(historical)_ — WWII soldiers given meth (Panzerschokolade) to skip sleep won early but eventually crashed, showing rest cannot be eliminated.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/rest|Rest]]
- **NBA playoffs and the let-down after winning** _(personal)_ — The excitement of the playoffs lives in anticipation; once a championship is won or lost, the thrill fades fast — like the brief joy after the Astros' World Series.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/get-ready-for-something-big|Get Ready for Something Big]]
- **Nehemiah as patron saint of structural engineers** _(other)_ — A relatable career-style calling (rebuilding) contrasted with Jesus's prophetic, identity-rooted calling  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Neighbor who won shot put gold in 1968** _(personal)_ — A College Station neighbor, a gold-medal shot putter, as a treasured connection to 'best in the world' status.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **New Year's Day and summer as future-markers** _(other)_ — Writing a new year on a check or the start of summer become moments when change and the future become apparent.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]]
- **New Year's resolution as a better start date** _(other)_ — Deferring change to 'next week, New Year's Day, the year of the new me' shows our excuses for not claiming now.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]]
- **Newborn babies as soft, sticky, on-off communicators** _(nature)_ — Babies' helpless, all-or-nothing crying illustrates the humble form God chose in the incarnation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/christmas-eve-why-now|Christmas Eve: Why Now?]]
- **Newborns announce their need unlike independent teenagers** _(other)_ — God came as a needy infant who, unlike self-sufficient teenagers, openly signals need, modeling the admission of need for God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **NFL Draft Scouts** _(pop-culture)_ — A foil for Samuel's mission: God did not scout David for his potential like draft scouts predicting future stars, but chose him to reveal God's glory.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/in-the-presence-of-god|In the Presence of God]]
- **Nicodemus and being born again** _(biblical)_ — Jesus tells Nicodemus he must be born of water and spirit, framing salvation as ongoing new birth, not a momentary event.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-does-not-seek-its-own|Love Does Not Seek Its Own]]
- **Nicodemus and the second birth** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' talk of being born again to Nicodemus illustrates that beyond our one physical birth, faith offers a daily new beginning through repentance.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/spilling-things-god-offers-us-a-chance-for-a-do-over|Spilling Things: God Offers Us a Chance for a Do-Over]]
- **Nicodemus asking about being born again** _(biblical)_ — Nicodemus's literal question about re-entering his mother's womb introduces new birth through the Holy Spirit into the world made new.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Nicodemus born again** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' night conversation about being born of the Spirit sets up the theme of totally new life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Nicodemus taking Jesus' words to the letter** _(biblical)_ — Nicodemus imagining re-entering the womb shows literalism that misses the transformative, born-from-above meaning Jesus intends.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/lifted-up-reading-the-bible-as-jesus-reads-it|Lifted Up: Reading the Bible as Jesus Reads It]]
- **Nieces 18 and 21 years younger** _(personal)_ — The preacher's much-younger nieces introduce the BFF concept and frame how different generations conceive of 'forever.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Nietzsche and the death of God** _(historical)_ — The 19th-century philosopher's claim, contrasted with how Christianity makes the death of God the central action of faith rather than a scandalous taunt.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Niles Crane scoring a basketball shot on Frasier** _(pop-culture)_ — An unathletic character's lucky basket illustrates 'moral luck'—accidental good doesn't make us good.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/all-peoples-and-creatures|All Peoples and Creatures]]
- **Nine Inch Nails T-Shirt in 6th Grade** _(personal)_ — The preacher recalls wanting to be the kind of kid who wore a NIN shirt ('I don't listen to the lyrics'), showing music taste as a performance of identity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/half-a-world-away|Half a World Away]]
- **Nine Inch Nails t-shirt phone call** _(personal)_ — His sixth-grade defense 'I just listen to the music, not the words' illustrates that music is a private experience requiring trust.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Nintendo and childhood video games** _(personal)_ — The preacher's boyhood love of the original Nintendo and slow role-playing games sets up the central cheat-code metaphor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-do-we-hope-for|What do we hope for?]]
- **Nitrogen and phosphorus for plants** _(nature)_ — Without phosphorus a plant may look pretty but never flower, illustrating that humans need 'heavenly nitrogen and phosphorus' (the Spirit) to truly flourish.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Nixon's 'if the president does it, it's not illegal'** _(historical)_ — Nixon's line illustrates antinomian self-justification, treating one's own status as a license for lawless action.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eternal-life|Eternal Life]]
- **No Candles in the Pool** _(personal)_ — Oddly specific establishment rules arise from specific past harms, just as Jesus' new commandment responds to the disciples' behavior.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **No church, potlucks, or planning meetings in heaven** _(personal)_ — Heaven has no preachers, sermons, potlucks, or leadership meetings because the church exists only for the not-yet.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **No Confetti Karaoke Rule** _(personal)_ — A karaoke bar's 'no confetti' rule illustrates how rules answer past harm, and how unloving selfishness creates an exhausting mess for others to clean up.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **No political party, sports team, or job fills the God-sized hole** _(other)_ — Nothing worldly can fill the God-shaped void inside us, underscoring why Jesus is necessary.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **No solicitation sign** _(other)_ — Posting 'no solicitation' signs illustrates how we put easy conditions on whom we welcome.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **No such thing as a free lunch** _(other)_ — The economics adage that all actions have hidden costs contrasts the world's transactional logic with God's freely given abundant life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **No True Scotsman fallacy** _(other)_ — The sugar-on-porridge logical fallacy, used in a Scottish accent, to warn against endlessly qualifying who counts as a 'true Christian'.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Noah and the ark** _(biblical)_ — A model of salvation where only a few are saved and everyone else is left out or sacrificed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Noah and the Flood as the response of 'no'** _(biblical)_ — The destruction of the Flood is the counterfactual divine 'no' to sin, against which the Incarnation is God's 'yes' to the world.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Noelle the disillusioned teen** _(other)_ — Fifteen-year-old Noelle, who sees only hypocrites at church, encounters God and is freed to speak truth knowing she is of sacred worth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Norse mythology of Thor, Odin, Asgard and Midgard** _(literary)_ — Norse gods who are part of the world rather than its creator contrast with the biblical Creator God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Nostradamus and Conan O'Brien's 'In the Year 2000'** _(pop-culture)_ — Contrasts cartoonish future-telling with prophets whose words address present injustice.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Not a mutant serum** _(pop-culture)_ — The disciples weren't injected with a superhuman forgiveness serum; the power is the indwelling Holy Spirit, not a magic ability.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/recognized-by-his-scars-the-power-of-pardon|Recognized by His Scars: The Power of Pardon]]
- **Not a superhero, not the Messiah** _(pop-culture)_ — You can't save yourself; admitting that is where the good news begins  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **Not checking Yelp or finding the best OB/GYN** _(current-events)_ — Mary and Joseph weren't checking Yelp or seeking the best hospital or OB/GYN, just a place to stop, contrasting our search for the best with their bare need.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **Not inviting cousin so-and-so** _(personal)_ — Party guest lists with their in-groups and out-groups contrast with Jesus, for whom there is no outgroup.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **Not tempted by murder confession** _(personal)_ — The pastor jokes that 'thou shall not kill' is easy for him, unlike the harder command to welcome.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **NYC wine-club dating service** _(current-events)_ — A wine influencer's pay-for-women-free-for-men dating table as a 'third space' where people risk connecting.  → Every Act of Faith
- **Obi-Wan Kenobi 'who's the more foolish' line** _(pop-culture)_ — Obi-Wan's retort to Han Solo ('Who's the more foolish, the fool or the fool who follows him?') frames foolishness as a comparative term.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **Obsolete jobs replaced by technology** _(historical)_ — Typewriter repairman, switchboard operator, lamplighter, town crier, ice cutter — vanished professions setting up the lost art of the messenger.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-he-is-not-here-among-the-dead|Easter: He Is Not Here Among the Dead]]
- **Ocean's Eleven Heist** _(pop-culture)_ — Heist films center on a treasure as the goal, like the worldly goals that motivate us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Offering song 'Lord I'm lost, be my way back'** _(literary)_ — The offering song's plea ('Lord I'm lost, be my way back') illustrates Jesus finding and sheltering the lost.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/big-boat|Big Boat]]
- **Oliver Twist's plea** _(literary)_ — A thirsty child becomes Oliver ('Please sir, can I have some more?'), illustrating insatiable craving.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **One solution, three problems (industrial agriculture critique)** _(other)_ — Extended critique of how removing cattle-crop rotation for chemicals created cascading ecological harms, mirrored later onto fragmented Christian life.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **One Weird Trick web ads** _(pop-culture)_ — The bogus internet 'one weird trick' advertising gimmick (shut down by the FTC) frames the series' question of whether one real change could transform a life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **One-word names of military history (Alexander, Caesar, Napoleon, Wellington)** _(historical)_ — The remembered great commanders are those who found an advantage and pressed it, unlike McClellan.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **Online discourse and PR talking points** _(current-events)_ — Internet keyword-policing and PR coaching ('answer the question you wish was asked') illustrate the un-loving ways of responding to others' words.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-believes-all|Love Believes All]]
- **Online niche affinity groups (17th-century satirical poetry)** _(current-events)_ — The internet's ability to match obscure niche interests illustrates how affinity can mimic but fall short of true friendship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Open kitchen cabinets confession** _(personal)_ — The pastor confesses he leaves every kitchen cabinet open, illustrating how varied and personal our senses of responsibility are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/responsibility|Responsibility]]
- **Opening Day and Spring Training Optimism** _(personal)_ — Every team starting 0-0 with hope of a new season frames Lent as a fresh start: 'Is this going to be your year?'  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Oprah giving away cars** _(pop-culture)_ — A car dealer pulling an Oprah giveaway every week would go bankrupt, illustrating the inefficiency of divine forgiveness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **Oracle of Delphi 'know thyself'** _(historical)_ — Greek philosophy's self-knowledge maxim contrasted with Jesus's command to follow him instead.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]], [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Ordination interview on evil** _(personal)_ — Pressed by an angry examiner before 15 pastors, the preacher defended that evil is not a 'thing' lest God be made the author of evil.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **Ordination questions on going on to perfection** _(personal)_ — Being asked at ordination whether he is going on to perfection and would reach it in this life, framing perfection as completion by God, not sinlessness  → [[covenant-umc/2025/baptism|Baptism]]
- **Origin of biblical chapters and verses** _(historical)_ — Monks divided the Bible into readable chapters and later verses for commentary, showing the divisions are human and context still matters.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/lifted-up-reading-the-bible-as-jesus-reads-it|Lifted Up: Reading the Bible as Jesus Reads It]]
- **Origin of the Methodist name and the Holy Club** _(historical)_ — The Oxford Holy Club's insult-name 'Methodist'—rooted in a Galen-era medical dispute over who could be a doctor—shows that everyone, not a select class, can seek a transformed heart.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-holy-catholic-church|I Believe in the Holy Catholic Church]]
- **Origin of the name 'Methodists' / Galen and the ancient doctors** _(historical)_ — The Methodists were an ancient democratic school of doctors believing anyone could be a doctor; Wesley embraced the insult because anyone can be made holy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Origins of Memorial Day** _(historical)_ — Memorial Day began after the Civil War as Decoration Day to honor forgotten and vandalized military graves, tied to remembering ineffable loss.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Outdoor worship and Travis County drought** _(personal)_ — Rain ruining the preacher's outdoor services made him resent the water from above, blind to the drought's need for it.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **Over-specifying a dinner restaurant** _(personal)_ — Adding too many requirements to a Mexican restaurant choice illustrates how loading meaning into a text leaves God less space to move.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Owning a house means your neighbor does not** _(other)_ — Owning a house excludes your neighbor from owning it, contrasting the world's zero-sum scarcity with God's non-exclusive, non-scarce economy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/having-the-son-living-a-more-real-life-by-faith|Having the Son: Living a More Real Life by Faith]]
- **Oxford college kids asking John Wesley for guidance** _(historical)_ — The early Methodists' question 'How is it with your soul?' frames the sermon's central self-examination.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Oxford English Dictionary** _(literary)_ — English readers can trace a word's first use and history, unlike rare Hebrew words known only from a single biblical mention.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/what-s-next-builders-and-names|What's Next? (Builders and Names)]]
- **P=p-i performance equation** _(other)_ — Performance equals potential minus interference, applied to how self-doubt interferes with hope.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Pagan creation myths** _(historical)_ — Greek (Uranus/Gaia), Babylonian, Japanese, and Norse creation stories contrast with the unique Jewish/Christian God who creates from nothing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Pagan Pantheons and the God Uranus** _(historical)_ — Greek and Sumerian gods' sexualized identities contrast with the Father language Jesus uses, which is about relationship not procreation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **Pagan weather gods including Storm of the X-Men** _(pop-culture)_ — Thor, Zeus, and Marvel's Storm illustrate humanity's tendency to assign storm-power to gods, contrasted with Jesus' real power as creator rather than a mutant ability.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Painting a room and organizing a closet** _(personal)_ — Small finished projects that give a satisfying sense of visible accomplishment, like Jesus reaching Jerusalem on Palm Sunday.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Painting and prep-work** _(personal)_ — The rookie mistake Wilson made painting was skipping prep; the best prep leaves the least evidence, as John's work is hidden in the life of Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-four-questions-who-is-coming|The Four Questions: Who Is Coming?]]
- **Paleo diet and crossfit hipsters** _(current-events)_ — The 'paleo' diet is a modern invention that skips 10,000 years of development, illustrating how recent our food assumptions really are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **Palm Sunday crowd to Holy Week jeers** _(biblical)_ — The same crowd cheering Jesus later cried 'crucify him,' showing laurels and crowds turn quickly.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Palm Sunday crowds turning to 'crucify him'** _(biblical)_ — The same crowd that cheered Jesus later jeered him, warning against trusting crowd approval.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Papier-Mâché Foundation** _(other)_ — Any foundation built apart from Christ is like papier-mâché — it only takes one splash to collapse.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Parable of the 99 sheep and the one lost** _(biblical)_ — The shepherd leaves the 99 to rescue the wandering sheep not to reprimand but to bring it home, modeling love that does not seek its own.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-does-not-seek-its-own|Love Does Not Seek Its Own]]
- **Parable of the houses on rock and sand** _(biblical)_ — Hearing and doing the word builds on rock that withstands wind and water; hearing without doing builds on sand that is swept away, illustrating that loving Jesus means keeping his commandments.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]], A Foundation of Water
- **Parable of the Sower** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' parable of seed on path, rock, thorns, and good soil illustrates the cost of being tilled by God so we can bear fruit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Parable of the talents** _(biblical)_ — The servant who buries his talent out of fear shows how we can bury rather than offer the light of Christ.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Parable of the two debtors** _(biblical)_ — The man forgiven 500 denarii loves more than the one forgiven 50, showing that seeing how much Jesus saved us from draws us closer to God's love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
- **Parable of the Two Sons (Matthew 21)** _(literary)_ — Contrasts the son who refuses then obeys with the one who agrees then doesn't, to define the specific hypocrisy Jesus condemns — saying without doing.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Parable of the workers in the vineyard** _(biblical)_ — Workers hired at different hours all receive the same denarius, showing grace is given equally regardless of when one comes to faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Parents who only give stuff** _(other)_ — Parents who merely hand over things without teaching you to be yourself illustrate that giving alone is not love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **Parents' forbidden liquor cabinet** _(personal)_ — The adolescent pull toward the forbidden cabinet illustrates the false assumption that God withholds something good via the tree.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Paris Summer Olympics family watching** _(personal)_ — The pastor's family enjoying different Olympic sports illustrates the human drive to strive for excellence and greatness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Party that never gets old** _(other)_ — The new creation is the best party imaginable that never gets old, tiring, or boring, illustrating embodied eternal joy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting-2|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Pascal's 'you would not seek me if you had not already found me'** _(literary)_ — Pascal's line frames the Spirit as the consolation that inspires us to seek God again and again.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ascension-sunday-mother-s-day-where-did-jesus-go|Ascension Sunday / Mother's Day: Where Did Jesus Go?]]
- **Pascal's Christ saying 'you would not seek me'** _(literary)_ — Pascal's line that we'd not seek Christ unless already found, illustrating that seeking the kingdom is itself a sign it's present  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **Pascal's console yourself** _(literary)_ — You would not seek me if you had not already found me — we cannot short-circuit the act of faith  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **Pascal's wager / hedging bets** _(historical)_ — The rich giver diversifying his investments is framed as hedging his bets on God's existence, unlike the widow who bets everything on God.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/two-economies-heavenly-abundance-and-the-widow-s-mite|Two Economies: Heavenly Abundance and the Widow's Mite]]
- **Pascal's wager sermon series and the Mystery of Jesus** _(literary)_ — Last month's Pascal series plus Pascal's 'you would not seek me if you had not found me' frame seeking the true light.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Paschal Homily of St. John Chrysostom** _(historical)_ — The ancient Easter sermon proclaiming Christ's victory and welcome of all laborers regardless of when they came, framing the whole service.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-sunrise-service-the-paschal-homily-of-st-john-chrysostom|Easter Sunrise Service: The Paschal Homily of St. John Chrysostom]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/easter-sunrise-service|Easter Sunrise Service]], [[covenant-umc/2024/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-sermon|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom Sermon)]], [[covenant-umc/2026/easter-sunrise-service-chrysostom-s-paschal-homily|Easter Sunrise Service (Chrysostom's Paschal Homily)]]
- **Passover and the death of Egypt's firstborn** _(biblical)_ — The Exodus killing of innocent Egyptian sons is recapitulated in Herod's slaughter, exposing the assumption that some deserve death  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **Passover and the Exodus** _(biblical)_ — Salvation of the Hebrews at the cost of Egyptian children and soldiers, another model where others are sacrificed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Passover and the Exodus Flight** _(biblical)_ — The Hebrews flee Egypt without waiting for bread to rise, the origin of unleavened bread and freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
- **Passover house-cleaning before Jesus's visit** _(biblical)_ — Bethany household clearing out leaven illustrates the awkward, upside-down moment in which Mary's extravagant act breaks in  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **Passover Seder Dayenu** _(literary)_ — 'It would have been enough' becomes a frame for God's abundant overflow in creation and salvation.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **Passover Seder's four questions** _(biblical)_ — The child asking why this night is different prompts us to ask why this morning of worship is different.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Pastor learning to run after college** _(personal)_ — Wilson's struggle to build a running and morning rhythm models the daily, ongoing choice to follow Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Pastor Wilson's military family lineage** _(personal)_ — His grandfathers (WWII Europe and Iwo Jima), great-grandfather (WWI France), father-in-law (IDF), and ancestors back to the Revolution illustrate his fascination with concrete military history despite never serving himself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **Pastor's advice to tell the story of your fears** _(personal)_ — Counsel to write down or share fears with a trusted friend shows that naming fears aloud makes them manageable and dispels them.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Pastor's family move to Bee Creek during the pandemic** _(personal)_ — Difficulty meeting new neighbors and the church amid distanced greetings and Evensong illustrates how hard neighboring has become.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Pastor's Friday day off vs Sabbath** _(personal)_ — He takes Fridays off since he works Sundays, but insists Friday isn't his Sabbath - Sabbath is God's, never possessed by man.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **Pastor's morning routine and Alina's joke about sleeping till 8:45** _(personal)_ — His prayer, workout, and language routine model starting each day with gratefulness and rhythm.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Pastor's nonstandard ordination path** _(personal)_ — Going through UMC ordination ~15 years ago, he fit neither the seminary nor second-career bucket, illustrating not fitting easy categories.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **Pastor's own version of Simeon's song** _(personal)_ — His recently written paraphrase ('All of my life... now I can sleep') reimagines the Nunc Dimittis.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Pastor's refusal to delegate in prior appointments** _(personal)_ — his independent insistence on doing everything himself robbed others of the opportunity to do a mitzvah  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **Pastor's shy nature and dream vacation** _(personal)_ — The preacher's shyness, dislike of phone calls, and reading-on-a-mountain dream vacation show that courage to evangelize comes only from God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **Pastor's teenage independence by limiting his needs** _(personal)_ — His form of teenage rebellion was refusing to spend money or admit needs, illustrating how refusing to admit need keeps you from being helped or saved.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **Patek Philippe watch and jet boat** _(other)_ — Luxury possessions that will burn away contrast with a neighbor's love that endures into the new creation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-do-we-hope-for|What do we hope for?]]
- **Paths painted with fake gold** _(other)_ — Destructive ways of life look appealing like fake gold but tear us from community, neighbor, and God.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Patient as a noun at the doctor** _(other)_ — A wordplay on 'patient'—the doctor's patient asked to be patience incarnate—to show love is patient and patience is love.  → Elizabeth and Scott
- **Patrons painted into biblical scenes** _(historical)_ — Rich benefactors had painters insert themselves and their families into biblical scenes, showing how a society distorts sacred images to flatter itself.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eye-of-the-camel|Eye of the Camel]]
- **Patterns of grief like wearing black** _(other)_ — Cultural mourning customs show grief is a natural and valued response, not a negativity to be avoided.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Patton on fixed fortifications** _(historical)_ — General Patton's quip that fixed fortifications are 'a monument to man's stupidity' illustrates that a wall is useless without people to man it.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-gate|The Gate]]
- **Paul Simon, '50 Ways to Leave Your Lover'** _(pop-culture)_ — The 1975 song's litany of escape routes frames the sermon's 12 ways we 'leave our lover'-the God who loves us-by avoiding resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]], [[covenant-umc/2024/12-ways-to-avoid-resurrection|12 Ways to Avoid Resurrection]]
- **Paul's résumé / CV of credentials** _(biblical)_ — Paul lists his Pharisaic credentials like a job application, yet even the 'best' could not save himself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **Pea-Soup Fog Driving** _(other)_ — Suffering limits vision like creeping through thick fog where you can barely recognize what is right in front of you.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Peace/Piece homophone** _(literary)_ — The homophone peace/piece (whole vs. part) frames how Jesus gives true peace and how we honor a piece of Glynis.  → Glynis Frye Memorial
- **Pecan pie out of a hot oven** _(nature)_ — Grabbing a hot pan without a mitt and getting burned shows the world's immediate action/reaction consequences, contrasted with the delayed consequences of Matthew 25.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/new-year-s-eve-christ-the-king-sunday|New Year's Eve (Christ the King Sunday)]]
- **Pelagius** _(historical)_ — The 5th-century English writer's emphasis on a moral life apart from grace exemplifies the tragedy of works-righteousness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-should-we-do-to-be-saved|What Should We Do to Be Saved?]]
- **Pennsylvania Brethren baptism schism** _(historical)_ — A church split over whether baptism required moving or still water, conveniently aligning with each congregation's distance from a river, showing how absurd-seeming divisions felt vital to those involved.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **Pennsylvania Brethren baptism split** _(historical)_ — A sect dividing over baptism in moving versus still water shows how geography and context shaped the Jew-Samaritan divide.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Pennsylvania judge's sentencing before lunch** _(current-events)_ — A study found a judge gave harsher sentences right before lunch and lighter ones after, showing how low blood sugar affects judgment.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/first-attempt-temptation-of-jesus|First Attempt (Temptation of Jesus)]]
- **Pentecost tongues of fire** _(biblical)_ — The disciples letting the Spirit in at Pentecost illustrates transformation that comes from lowering our guard in worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **People at Safeway and Costco** _(personal)_ — The strangers we pass at Safeway, Costco, and restaurants are the ordinary people to whom we have freedom to be light.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
- **People we pass at HEB and Costco** _(personal)_ — The freedom in Christ frees us to love the ordinary people we pass daily rather than walking by them in anxiety.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Perfume covering the smell of rot** _(other)_ — Herod's fine purple cloth, gold, and perfumes covered a heart at war with itself and the smell of rot.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Personal brushes with the justice system** _(personal)_ — His minimal encounters (family court, traffic stops, cops checking a backyard fire) show experience shapes one's idea of justice.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Personal budgets reveal desires** _(other)_ — How we spend our money and time reveals what we truly value and whether we live by generosity or scarcity, hope or fear.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Pestering the lapsed about God** _(personal)_ — Nagging someone who has turned from God only pushes them away; like the waiting father, we must wait and show love instead.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/being-sheep|Being Sheep]]
- **Peter and John's foot race to the tomb** _(biblical)_ — John (the 'beloved disciple') winning the race to the empty tomb in his own Gospel, showing John's account as uniquely eager and coy.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-concrete-faith-of-thomas|The Concrete Faith of Thomas]]
- **Peter Bohler conversations** _(historical)_ — Bohler's confident assurance of faith moves Wesley toward his own  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Peter Comforting Jesus Like a Child** _(other)_ — Peter rebukes Jesus the way one consoles a child teased by 'Mary Margaret,' missing that Jesus must walk the road to the cross.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Peter drawing his sword for Jesus** _(biblical)_ — Peter's misguided sword-drawing illustrates a disciple seeking Jesus' good even without understanding.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/wherever-you-go|Wherever You Go]]
- **Peter Maurin: 'make it easier for people to be good'** _(historical)_ — The French mystic (Catholic Worker co-founder) cited as the preacher's vision for the church removing barriers rather than guilting people into goodness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Peter praised then rebuked as Satan** _(biblical)_ — Jesus praising Peter for confessing the Messiah and then saying 'Get behind me Satan' shows Jesus sees us for who we are, not who we think we are.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/new-year-s-eve-christ-the-king-sunday|New Year's Eve (Christ the King Sunday)]]
- **Peter preaching at Pentecost** _(biblical)_ — Many had heard the resurrection message before yet were transformed by the Spirit at Pentecost, showing the Spirit changes those who receive it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Peter says it's too early to be drunk** _(biblical)_ — Peter's claim that the disciples aren't drunk because it's only 9am, joked as showing Peter hadn't met many real drunks, illustrating the crowd's confusion at Pentecost.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Peter the denier preaching at Pentecost** _(biblical)_ — A fisherman who denied Jesus three times became the voice through whom God transformed the world at Pentecost.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Peter wanting to build a house on the mountain** _(biblical)_ — Peter's desire to stay and build dwellings illustrates the temptation to cling to the mountaintop instead of descending toward the cross.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/who-is-jesus-the-transfiguration|Who Is Jesus? The Transfiguration]]
- **Peter's Pentecost sermon and the 9am drunk joke** _(biblical)_ — Peter jokes the disciples can't be drunk at 9am, framing Pentecost as the birthday of the church.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Peter's threefold denial** _(biblical)_ — The eager disciple who saw the glory later denies Jesus, showing that failure does not negate a genuine encounter with God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/who-is-jesus-the-transfiguration|Who Is Jesus? The Transfiguration]]
- **Peter, James, and Paul leaving their old lives** _(biblical)_ — Peter and James leaving their nets and Paul abandoning his life as persecutor show that following the Spirit means change and letting go.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Pharaoh who did not know Joseph** _(biblical)_ — A new king who no longer remembers Israel's story sees them only as a numerous, unknown threat and moves to oppress them, illustrating fear of what is not understood.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **Pharisees are fair, Sadducees are sad** _(other)_ — English mnemonic distinguishing the legalistic Pharisees from the Sadducees who made a deal with Rome  → [[covenant-umc/2025/under-the-wing|Under the Wing]]
- **Philippians 2 Christ hymn** _(biblical)_ — Quoted to show Peter wanted Jesus to exploit, not empty, his equality with God — the theological heart of the rebuke.  → Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Pick a room and examine its objects** _(personal)_ — Practical invitation to choose one room, look at the objects, and prayerfully ask whether each draws you toward or away from God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Picky Youngest Child at Bedtime** _(personal)_ — His son claims hunger but refuses fruit, showing the difference between need and mere want.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
- **Picturing a chair, cat, or baby vanishing into nothing** _(other)_ — Conveys the absolute void before creation by imagining a familiar object ceasing to exist.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]]
- **Pilgrims and Roman numerals in old-city Jerusalem** _(nature)_ — Groups of pilgrims tracing the stations marked by Roman numerals on Jerusalem's walls embody entering deeply into Christ's passion.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Pious old woman (Aquinas)** _(literary)_ — Aquinas's line that all theology articulates what one pious old woman believes shows theology serves faith, not vice versa.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Pirate Treasure Map** _(pop-culture)_ — Pirates chase a mapped treasure, an image of the goals that drive our stories.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Pirelli tires and regulated power units in Formula 1** _(pop-culture)_ — Because power units and tires are standardized, aerodynamics is what separates teams, just as how the church meets the world distinguishes it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Pizza Hut as largest kale buyer** _(pop-culture)_ — In the 90s Pizza Hut bought the most kale just to decorate salad bars, proving tastes and habits can change.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **Plato's Allegory of the Cave** _(literary)_ — Plato's prisoners watching shadows on the wall depicts humanity mistaking shadows for reality and needing to climb out to see the truth, a salvation narrative.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Plato's Cave** _(literary)_ — Plato's allegory of the cave (paideia vs logos) frames darkness and light as ancient metaphors for ignorance and truth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/animals-and-god-s-promise-children-s-message-the-true-light-already-shines|Animals and God's Promise (Children's Message): The True Light Already Shines]]
- **Playing basketball with his kids** _(personal)_ — Blocking his sons' shots vs. getting rejected illustrates how rejection feels bad but can be moved past  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-harvest|The Harvest]]
- **Playing Hunter / keeping a promise to play** _(personal)_ — Children's message: agreeing to play together but not following through isn't fun, illustrating that following God means doing what we say we'll do.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/antichrist-becoming-the-villain|Antichrist: Becoming the Villain]]
- **Playing poker with grandfather John Huebel** _(personal)_ — Childhood poker games (five card draw 'Dr. Pepper' wild cards) set up the poker adage about deception and being the sucker.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-believes-all|Love Believes All]]
- **Playing the Ball Where It Lies** _(other)_ — The golf temptation to move a bad lie when nobody is watching illustrates honest confession of sin versus pretending to God that the bad shot never happened.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/start-from-where-you-are|Start From Where You Are]]
- **Plugging ears singing la-la-la** _(other)_ — We plug our ears and sing 'La, la, la!' to avoid uncomfortable truths — image of willful deafness to reality.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-opened|Be Opened]]
- **Plugging your ears, la la la** _(other)_ — The antichrist move of ignoring the world's pain just to feel good about oneself  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **Plutonium** _(other)_ — Plutonium doesn't look powerful yet is deadly by radiation even before becoming a bomb, illustrating tremendous power hidden in unremarkable-looking things.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/spicy-mustard|Spicy Mustard]]
- **Pogroms and Jews expelled to Poland** _(historical)_ — Centuries of antisemitic pogroms (often scapegoating Jews for plagues) and the 14th-century expulsion from Germany illustrate the deadly fruit of supersessionism.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/arise-shine-the-light-of-epiphany-and-the-grafting-in-of-the-gentiles|Arise, Shine: The Light of Epiphany and the Grafting In of the Gentiles]]
- **Point de vue du Gras by Nicéphore Niépce** _(historical)_ — The earliest photograph (held in Austin) marks how humans began capturing images in a new way.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/all-peoples-and-creatures|All Peoples and Creatures]]
- **Polishing a car under grackles** _(nature)_ — A freshly polished car ruined by a colony of grackles shows what baptism is NOT—not a wash job undone by later sin.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **Politicians racing to talking points** _(current-events)_ — Modern politicians and online users regurgitating talking points show the collapse of honest debate.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Pope Francis's field hospital after battle** _(current-events)_ — Pope Francis's image of the church as a field hospital that heals wounds first rather than checking cholesterol, modeling mercy-centered community over purity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/field-hospital|Field Hospital]]
- **Possessing a car, watch, or cat** _(other)_ — We cannot possess Jesus the way we possess a car, watch, or cat, raising the question of what it means to 'have' God's Son.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/having-the-son-living-a-more-real-life-by-faith|Having the Son: Living a More Real Life by Faith]]
- **Post-COVID churchgoers who never returned** _(current-events)_ — People who got used to sleeping in during COVID closures and never came back illustrates drifting faith and the question 'why are you here.'  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Potter Stewart 'know it when I see it'** _(historical)_ — Justice Stewart's famous phrase illustrates how justice often goes unarticulated yet recognizable.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Pour one out** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor of dumping out and pouring out every non-love desire (anger, pride, jealousy) to show those things are not who we truly are.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-is-christ|Who is Christ?]]
- **Practicing 10,000 hours and still not matching elite athletes** _(pop-culture)_ — No amount of practice makes one throw like Aaron Rodgers/Tom Brady or shoot like Steph Curry, showing the limits of will against given ability.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-in-christ|Freedom in Christ]]
- **Practicing double bass for four hours** _(personal)_ — The preacher lost himself practicing string bass so completely he forgot to eat, illustrating how the crowd was so absorbed in Jesus's word they weren't thinking about food.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/how-many-people-did-jesus-feed|How many people did Jesus feed?]]
- **Praise music vs. hymns** _(other)_ — Distinguishing the modern genre of praise music from biblical praise in Psalm 150.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Praising a newborn or a dog** _(personal)_ — Everyday praise (good boy, muddy dog) illustrates praise as response after the fact.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Pray for the Longhorns to win** _(personal)_ — McCabe's counsel to pray for what you actually want so God can shape your desires  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Pray, Preach, or Die** _(personal)_ — A teacher's saying that a Christian preacher should always be ready to pray, preach, or die points to the stark question of where one's faith lies.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]]
- **Prayer as Entirely Useless Time** _(other)_ — A teacher's image of prayer as unproductive lingering at the beach with someone you love, no heavenly resume points gained.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **Prayer as honored ambassador** _(other)_ — Prayer stands before God as an honored ambassador, giving joy to the spirit and peace to the heart.  → Prayer and Converse with God Is a Supreme Good
- **Prayer as lingering at the beach** _(other)_ — A teacher's image of prayer as 'entirely useless time'—like lingering at the beach with a loved one—illustrates prayer as unproductive, loving presence with God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Prayer is like falling in love / lingering at the beach** _(other)_ — extended metaphor that prayer is unproductive, timeless presence with a beloved  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Prayer is like throwing up to God** _(personal)_ — A teacher's image that prayer means putting everything out before God without self-consciousness.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Prayer journal during COVID** _(personal)_ — Elizabeth wrote Scott's name in her prayer journal during COVID and prayed 'Lord, where have all the good men gone,' illustrating answered prayer.  → Elizabeth and Scott
- **Prayer of Saint Francis choir anthem** _(literary)_ — The choir's anthem on St. Francis's prayer models the self-giving life of love that consoles, understands, and gives.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Prayer stained glass window at Covenant UMC** _(personal)_ — the church window anchoring this entry in the sermon series, framing prayer as a response to God  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Praying instead of saying I'll pray for you** _(personal)_ — Show love by actually praying, loving, and forgiving rather than merely announcing it.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/cantata-show-don-t-tell|Cantata: Show, Don't Tell]]
- **Preacher always wanting to remain silent** _(personal)_ — The preacher admits he's bad at these games and instinctively chooses silence/cooperation over the 'rational' betrayal.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-of-god-love-of-neighbor|Love of God, Love of Neighbor]]
- **Preacher has never murdered but fails to love** _(personal)_ — Confession showing the love commandments are far harder than the Ten Commandments  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
