- **Preaching the lectionary vs. self-chosen series** _(personal)_ — His move from lectionary preaching to elaborate self-chosen sermon series, which became too focused on himself, illustrates the value of receiving a text rather than choosing the easy path.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/not-easy-to-watch|Not Easy to Watch]]
- **Premarital counseling, telling the love story** _(personal)_ — Asking a couple to each tell how they met yields fresh perspectives, like the two creation accounts.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Preppers stockpiling for disaster** _(current-events)_ — Being ready for the Son of Man is not hoarding food and water like a doomsday prepper but refusing to delay love, service, and worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/mercy-revealed|Mercy Revealed]]
- **Pretending to run out of gas on the lake** _(other)_ — Refusing help while stranded and claiming 'I meant to run out of gas' pictures how we cling to our vices and victimhood even after encountering Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/how-many-people-did-jesus-feed|How many people did Jesus feed?]]
- **Prevent Defense in Football** _(pop-culture)_ — Teams late in a game play a fearful prevent defense focused on tomorrow's outcome, like Christians who mitigate injustice instead of boldly confronting it in the present.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]], [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Priests as roughnecks** _(other)_ — Levitical priests worked two-weeks-on, two-weeks-off like oil roughnecks because the bloody slaughter work was so hard.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Prodigal son's older brother** _(biblical)_ — The resentful older brother seeking his own glory illustrates the jealousy faithful Christians feel toward latecomers receiving grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-to-us|Not to Us]]
- **Professor on Orthodox-Catholic reunion** _(personal)_ — A professor predicted the Eastern Orthodox and Catholic churches will never reunite because they would never agree on who controls the property.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **Prophet of the stock market** _(other)_ — A market 'prophet' predicts the future, unlike Isaiah, showing biblical prophecy is not mere future-prediction.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Pros and cons list** _(personal)_ — The discomfort of weighing arguments for and against introduces the idea of counting the cost of following Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-cost|The Cost]]
- **Protective gear and the X-ray room** _(personal)_ — Wearing protective gear (lab gloves, X-ray shielding) signals an activity isn't safe, illustrating that difficult faith requires the armor of God.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Pruitt residence phone answering as a child** _(personal)_ — Answering the home phone that was never for him shows how little we expect a call to be for us.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Pruitt-Igoe Housing Project** _(historical)_ — The state-of-the-art St. Louis housing complex funded to build but with no funding to maintain illustrates that the real cost of any building is in upkeep and rebuilding, not construction.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-and-see-the-trouble-come-and-start-rebuilding|Come and See the Trouble — Come and Start Rebuilding]]
- **Psyche vs. Zao Greek word study** _(literary)_ — Distinguishes psyche (soul/essence of who you are) from zao (mere biological living) to argue Jesus means loving your true self, not loving being alive.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/fragments|Fragments]]
- **Psychology as 'study of souls'** _(literary)_ — The Greek 'psyche' behind 'psychology' shows the soul (nephesh) is the life-force, not a body-separated entity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Pulled Over by Lakeway PD** _(personal)_ — Getting pulled over for going 31 on Lakewood Boulevard right after doing a good turn shows we live in a broken world where rain falls on just and unjust alike.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Pulling the Not-Carrot** _(personal)_ — The instinct to yank an obvious weed so the carrots can grow illustrates our natural urge to judge and uproot others.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Pulling weeds by the roots** _(nature)_ — Pulling only the green tops leaves the roots, so weeds return, explaining 'radical' change to the roots.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-bread-of-life-what-are-you-hungry-for|The Bread of Life: What Are You Hungry For?]]
- **Putting on Our Nicodemus Hats at the Table** _(personal)_ — Imagining communion through an outsider's shock ('Are you cannibals?') argues the faithful response is to take the sacrament more seriously, not less.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **QR code to website stations page** _(current-events)_ — A handout QR code links to an online stations walkthrough so the mobility-challenged can still pray through Christ's passion.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Quentyn and plot armor in A Dance with Dragons** _(pop-culture)_ — George R.R. Martin's character Quentyn believes he has 'plot armor' and is the story's center, but is burned by a dragon instead of becoming king, illustrating the deadly illusion of thinking we are the center of the story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Quid pro quo / 'friend in Jesus'** _(other)_ — The transactional 'do this and Jesus will do that' bargaining that instrumentalizes Jesus, which the Transfiguration refuses.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Quirinius, Governor of Syria** _(historical)_ — The biographical detail of Quirinius (consul, tutor to Augustus's heir, governor) shows the named Roman power-figures of history are precisely the ones God did NOT enter history through.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **Rabbi Seated to Teach** _(historical)_ — Second Temple teaching took place with the rabbi seated and students gathered around, so to bring children to Jesus was to seek to have them taught.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/like-a-child|Like a Child]]
- **Rachel and Tanner met through work** _(personal)_ — Rachel noticed Tanner at morning meetings in the same work unit, then moved into the same apartment, before a non-work text led to their first date.  → Rachel and Tanner
- **Radical means getting to the roots** _(literary)_ — The etymology of 'radical' (root) illustrates that Christian faith stands on nothing beneath Jesus Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Raising chickens and vegetables in Jolleyville** _(personal)_ — Childhood self-sufficiency, growing food with family after moving to Jolleyville.  → Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff, A Service of Death and Resurrection for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Rare cancer treatment news** _(other)_ — Hearing of a new treatment for a rare cancer and joining a study as a rare example of news that is earnestly something you genuinely need.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Rashi on Hagar as Pharaoh's daughter** _(literary)_ — Rashi's midrash that Hagar was a daughter of Pharaoh who chose to be a handmaid in Sarah's house, illustrating the rabbinic reading of Genesis 16.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/hagar-sarah-and-the-birth-of-a-people|Hagar, Sarah, and the Birth of a People]]
- **Rashi on naming Ishmael** _(literary)_ — Rashi's claim that the Holy Spirit rested on Abram so he named Ishmael though he had not heard the angel's words.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/hagar-sarah-and-the-birth-of-a-people|Hagar, Sarah, and the Birth of a People]]
- **Rashi on the angels sent to Hagar** _(literary)_ — Rashi's note that a separate angel was sent for each statement to Hagar, explaining the repeated 'and an angel said to her.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/hagar-sarah-and-the-birth-of-a-people|Hagar, Sarah, and the Birth of a People]]
- **Raspberries on Isle Royale** _(personal)_ — Eating ripe wild raspberries alone for an hour after dodging a moose, illustrating the strange power of a fruit-bearing tree in full fruit.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Rasputin, theosophy, and Aleister Crowley** _(historical)_ — The early-1900s occult revival (Crowley influencing Hubbard/Scientology and an Ozzy Osbourne song) shows astrology never went away.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Ravens and lilies of the field** _(nature)_ — Jesus' images of birds God feeds and lilies God clothes, illustrating freedom from anxiety over consumption.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-jesus|The Gift of Jesus]]
- **Raymond Brown on the magi** _(literary)_ — Scholar's reflection that the Gentile magi meet God through nature but need the Scriptures to find the Messiah.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Raymond Brown's planetary conjunction theory** _(literary)_ — Brown's The Birth of the Messiah argues a planetary conjunction best explains the unusually bright Christmas star.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Raymond Carver, What We Talk About When We Talk About Love** _(literary)_ — Carver's story names the ambiguity of the English word 'love' that Jesus' command does not share.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-new-commandment|A New Commandment]]
- **Reading Bible stories to preschoolers** _(personal)_ — Young children's literalism (Noah, wise man on the rock, This Little Light of Mine) shows how the capacity for symbol develops.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Reading Hebrew in Genesis 1 vs Genesis 2** _(personal)_ — The preacher can read Genesis 1 but not Genesis 2 in Hebrew, showing how different the two accounts are.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Reading John 14 at funerals** _(personal)_ — The pastor's habit of reading John 14 at memorial services illustrates the passage's power to offer hope and consolation in the face of trouble.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-joy-of-the-lord-is-our-strength|The Joy of the Lord is Our Strength]]
- **Reading the Bible as a college freshman** _(personal)_ — Personal discovery that the Ten Commandments aren't central and 'the law' means all of Torah  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Reading the Bible like a mystery novel with a known ending** _(literary)_ — A teacher's advice to read Scripture like a whodunit whose twist (Christ is risen) you already know, so you can spot all the clues.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Reading The Hobbit aloud as a family** _(personal)_ — frames the sermon: the quiet, character-focused life mirrors the undramatic work of sanctification  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Real life Minecraft (Hunter)** _(personal)_ — Brief reference to playing real-life Minecraft with Hunter, evoking imaginative child's play that holds a real and unreal world together.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/having-the-son-living-a-more-real-life-by-faith|Having the Son: Living a More Real Life by Faith]]
- **Reality TV and Survivor** _(pop-culture)_ — Reality shows hand the audience someone to root for and despise, the same black-and-white framing we wrongly impose on Scripture.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/election-not-selection|Election, Not Selection]]
- **Rear guard military term** _(historical)_ — A rear guard protects a marching army's flank so the rest can face forward, picturing God as our rear guard freeing us to shine.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Rear naked choke and the ukulele intro video** _(personal)_ — Wilson's Brazilian jiu-jitsu practice as family discipline frames faith as showing up through difficulty, not mastery.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Receiving a gift and shelving it** _(other)_ — thanking someone for a gift then forgetting it on a shelf, illustrating how we neglect the undeserved gift of the Spirit given at baptism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **Receiving visitors mid-cleaning** _(personal)_ — the discomfort of guests arriving while your house is torn apart, paralleling the household's distraction when Jesus comes  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **Recounting a trip in full detail** _(other)_ — Relaying every detail of a trip would take as long as the trip itself, so stories must be summarized to be meaningful.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Red Sea movies** _(pop-culture)_ — The Ten Commandments and The Prince of Egypt making the Red Sea iconic, contrasted with the lesser-known rock-at-Meribah miracle done by the same staff.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/give-me-water|Give Me Water]]
- **Redwood seed comparison** _(nature)_ — If Jesus had preached on the Pacific coast he might have used the redwood seed — small but growing into the largest living organism — to make the same point about small faith yielding immense growth.  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Refilling the Oil Lamp** _(literary)_ — An oil lamp runs out unless refilled, picturing the need to replenish our light through prayer, worship, scripture, and serving together.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod-2|Broken Rod]]
- **Refilling the oil lamp** _(biblical)_ — Keeping a first-century oil lamp lit by refilling it pictures sustaining our light through prayer, worship, scripture, and service.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/broken-rod|Broken Rod]]
- **Refiner's Fire** _(other)_ — The Spirit as a refining fire that burns away what is not love, also the fire of creation and Pentecost still spreading today.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Reformation cutting of feast days as inefficient labor market** _(historical)_ — Protestant suppression of saints' days and holidays explains why Christians came to be seen as joyless rather than celebratory.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **Refusing to ask for advice on plumbing or technology** _(personal)_ — The preacher's stubborn pride in solving home problems alone illustrates how hard and humbling it is to admit need.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/lead-us-not-into-temptation|Lead Us Not into Temptation]]
- **Rehearsal of Concept (R.O.C.) military dry walk-through** _(historical)_ — The US military's pre-rescue planning practice illustrates the complex logistical effort behind the cave rescue and salvation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Reimagining property insurance** _(other)_ — An extended metaphor framing a church's existential risk through the lens of property insurance.  → Shifting A Church's Risk Profile
- **Remembering baptism through those who loved you** _(personal)_ — Those baptized as infants remember through the people present, illustrating that faith is communal memory  → [[covenant-umc/2025/remember-and-be-thankful|Remember and Be Thankful]]
- **Remembering Bob Weeks and Bob Fossick** _(personal)_ — The preacher's gratitude for two departed members models inhaling and being inspired by the memory of the saints.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Remodeling a home vs. building up** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor that an addition or remodel is not a true build-up, contrasting superficial change with love that constructs from the ground up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-builds-up|Love Builds Up]]
- **Removing rocks from a child's path** _(personal)_ — A parent clearing stumbling stones he once tripped on illustrates making goodness easier without forcing it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-of-the-future|The Church of the Future]]
- **Renaissance painting of a camel inside a needle** _(historical)_ — A rich Renaissance patron commissioned a painting of a tiny camel drawn inside a needle's eye to exempt himself from Jesus' warning to the rich.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eye-of-the-camel|Eye of the Camel]]
- **Render unto Caesar** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' teaching on coins bearing Caesar's image illustrates biblical citizenship as the question 'who are your people?'  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-gate|The Gate]]
- **Renting AFI top-100 films from Hastings as a kid** _(personal)_ — Preacher's boyhood habit of systematically renting every classic film introduces his love of movies and sets up the Casablanca illustration.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
- **Repainting the altar / changing the chair** _(other)_ — An altar repainted or a chair re-cushioned stays itself, illustrating accidents versus the substance that makes a thing what it is.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Rescued from drowning** _(other)_ — The asymmetry between thanking a stranger and thanking the one who saves you from drowning shows the deeper, asymmetrical praise owed to God.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Resentful cousin and leaf-blowing neighbor** _(personal)_ — Everyday grudges show that giving God our heart means concretely letting go of resentment.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Resetting a frozen computer** _(personal)_ — Growing up with an Apple IIe and early Macintosh, learning that a stuck computer just needs a reset without asking why, contrasted with how much harder it is to reset our lives.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Resetting a frozen computer or router** _(personal)_ — Growing up with an Apple IIe and Macintosh; the genius-bar fix of just resetting a stuck machine, no ego, models how a new year lets us reset our lives.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Restless in the barber's and dentist's chair** _(personal)_ — Childhood inability to sit still illustrates restlessness as the mind longing to be elsewhere doing other things.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **Restless Scrolling and Switching Hobbies** _(current-events)_ — People skim Facebook, Instagram, and TikTok and switch sports and hobbies, restlessly seeking the bread of life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
- **Restoring an old desk with toxic wood stripper** _(personal)_ — God strips and rebuilds us like a carpenter disassembling, stripping, and regluing furniture  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-mercy-of-fire|The Mercy of Fire]]
- **Resurrected Christ showing his scars** _(biblical)_ — Christ kept his wounds after resurrection, showing healing does not erase our experiences and we can look broken to the world yet whole to God.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Retirement from the French for retreat** _(other)_ — The word's origin shows there is a legitimate Christian place for retreat and stepping away to clear the head.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-for-neighbor|Freedom for Neighbor]]
- **Revelation read since c.390 as the struggle within the human heart** _(historical)_ — Since about 390 some Christians have read Revelation not as us-versus-them but as the struggle within the heart between Babylon and the one on the throne, putting responsibility on us to live a heavenly life now.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Revoked Visitor Card** _(personal)_ — Pastor tells new members their visitor card is revoked and they must now welcome others, illustrating membership as servanthood.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **Rich Uncle's Estate / Severance Package** _(other)_ — Treating God's love like inheriting a rich uncle's estate or a three-month severance illustrates the wrong, passive way to receive the gift of God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/us-and-them|Us and Them]]
- **Rich young man told to sell everything** _(biblical)_ — Jesus' undetailed command to "sell everything" shows how his teachings usually lack specifics, making the very precise colt instructions strikingly odd.  → Palm Sunday: From Hosanna to Passion, [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Rich Young Ruler and Zacchaeus** _(biblical)_ — The rich young ruler put a limit on his generosity and stopped short, while Zacchaeus let God transform him without limit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Richard Bauckham on creator and lamb as theological center** _(literary)_ — Scholar Richard Bauckham's claim that the creator and the lamb are the theological center of Revelation, framing the chapter 4 throne vision at the heart of the created order.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Righteous posters vs. righteous people** _(current-events)_ — Social-media commenting and judging people online, illustrating that God calls us to build up neighbors on the internet, not to be righteous posters.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-builds-up|Love Builds Up]]
- **Rivendell / the Last Homely House** _(literary)_ — good days are 'soon told and not much to listen to,' like the gradual, undramatic story of sanctification  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Road to Emmaus breaking of bread** _(biblical)_ — Jesus breaking bread opens the disciples' eyes, underscoring the bodily nature of the resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]]
- **Road to Emmaus unrecognized Jesus** _(biblical)_ — The disciples not recognizing the risen Jesus shows the life to come is a transformed, different kind of life.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Robert Altman and the M*A*S*H loudspeaker/PA announcements** _(pop-culture)_ — Altman's director's commentary on adding the base loudspeaker and Radar O'Reilly's announcements as a unifying visual thread — 'otherwise we might as well call it radio' — illustrates that faith must be lived/visible action, not mere internal voice-over.  → Faith the Size of a Mustard Seed
- **Robert Caro on the Texas hill country** _(literary)_ — Caro's LBJ biography calls the hill country 'a trap baited with water'—green-looking but too dry to farm, like rocky soil that looks good but can't sustain roots.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **Robert Frost's 'The Road Not Taken'** _(literary)_ — two diverging roads, read as a paraphrase of Jesus' narrow/wide gate, showing we cannot travel both paths at once  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **Robert Grosseteste's Optics** _(historical)_ — A medieval monk wrote the West's first scientific text on light, driven by Scripture's centrality of light to who God is, marking a breakthrough in empirical science.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/arise-shine-the-light-of-epiphany-and-the-grafting-in-of-the-gentiles|Arise, Shine: The Light of Epiphany and the Grafting In of the Gentiles]]
- **Rock bottom as a cave, not a valley** _(other)_ — Unlike a valley you can see, a cave hides the floor, so you only know rock bottom after you've hit it and start coming back up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Rocky training montage** _(pop-culture)_ — Jacob has no off-page training montage; his 'technique' is grit and refusal to let go, not preparation.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Roman Consuls and the January Calendar** _(historical)_ — Julius Caesar's calendar and the one-year, prosecution-immune Roman consulship illustrate why the new year was tumultuous and politically charged, including possible influence on US inauguration timing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **Roman emperor in the Coliseum** _(historical)_ — Thumbs up/down emperor rejected as a false image of God who instead is the God of resurrection.  → Drawn by the Father
- **Roman roads** _(historical)_ — Rome endured by maintaining roads, but building them stripped the soil—roads link communities yet are themselves barren, like trampled hearts.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **Roman triumph slave whispering Memento Mori** _(historical)_ — The slave reminding the victorious general 'you will die' warns against the intoxication of self-glory and ignoring God.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Romans 8's 'more than conquerors'** _(biblical)_ — Paul's assurance that nothing — including angels or demons — can separate believers from the love of God, used as the climactic ground for letting go of fear about spiritual forces.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Romans Road / sinner's prayer** _(other)_ — Common evangelism methods that stop at justification, used as a foil for the fuller Wesleyan understanding of salvation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-salvation|What is Salvation?]]
- **Rome claiming every story and the stolen Menorah** _(historical)_ — Rome swept across the world claiming each people's story as its own (stealing the Temple Menorah, granting citizenship with bread and trial rights), a life-denying counterfeit to the throne of heaven that Revelation exposes.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **Rome's hunger for honor in battle** _(historical)_ — Ancient men needed war every 20-40 years to prove their worth, showing the worldly link between conflict and identity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Romeo and Juliet balcony scene ("What's in a name?")** _(literary)_ — Juliet's "a rose by any other name" speech frames the sermon's central question of what a name is and whether it matters.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/god-told-adam-and-eve|God Told Adam and Eve]]
- **Rorschach test** _(other)_ — Jesus functioning as a Rorschach test of sorts illustrates how people project and read themselves onto Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/we-are-god-s-children-but-we-are-not-finished-yet|We Are God's Children, But We Are Not Finished Yet]]
- **Roster of conquering generals** _(historical)_ — Napoleon, Robert E. Lee, Grant, Pershing, Patton, and Nimitz represent the worldly power our God refused, choosing to lose so our souls could win.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **Roughneck on an Oil Rig** _(other)_ — Zechariah's rotational temple priesthood compared to two-weeks-on, two-weeks-off rig work, both intense and bloody.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-ascended-into-heaven|He Ascended into Heaven]]
- **Rowan Williams debating Richard Dawkins** _(current-events)_ — Williams telling Dawkins that a God who is just part of material existence cannot save us illustrates that the Creator must transcend creation to be Savior.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Rowan Williams interview on judgment** _(current-events)_ — The former archbishop argues judgment is on love, not rule-keeping, and 'the next thirty seconds are decisive,' framing the sermon's title.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-next-thirty-seconds|The Next Thirty Seconds]]
- **Rowan Williams quote on blessedness** _(literary)_ — 'To be blessed is simply to be where God would have you be' defines blessedness as God's presence, not achievement.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Rowan Williams, Tokens of Trust** _(literary)_ — Williams's reflection on knowing God as faithful love and mercy illustrates a God who refuses to be fitted into our categories.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **Roxanne Quimby and Burt's Bees land donation** _(historical)_ — Founder of Burt's Bees donated 87,500 Maine acres, framing the trail's setting  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **Royce Gracie winning the first UFC** _(pop-culture)_ — The unassuming 'guy in pajamas' who wasn't even the best in his family won by showing up, mirroring Jacob's unlikely strength.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Rudyard Kipling's poem 'If'** _(literary)_ — Its vision of manhood and keeping one's head offers a virtue-based reason not to panic, short of the Christian one.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Rule of threes (three hours/days/weeks)** _(other)_ — The survival adage about shelter, water, and food all serve the larger goal of being rescued.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Rumbling bellies when a preacher runs long** _(personal)_ — Churchgoers always notice missed lunch and grow restless — unlike the crowd so hungry for the word they ignored their hunger.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/why-do-we-worship-the-assembly-and-the-bread-of-life|Why Do We Worship? The Assembly and the Bread of Life]]
- **Running again after COVID and ankle surgery** _(personal)_ — The preacher's loss of running to a bone tumor and ankle surgery, then resuming training for a half marathon, frames love as a practice requiring discipline and recovery.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Running hills around Lakeway** _(personal)_ — Struggling with unpracticed hill runs shows that loving enemies and forgiving the unforgivable takes practice.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Running pace-setters** _(personal)_ — Pace-setters who run segments alongside an athlete illustrate fellow Christians who walk the journey of faith with us and for whom we set the pace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-race|The Race]]
- **Ruth the welcomed foreigner** _(biblical)_ — Ruth the alien and stranger who clung to Naomi and became grandmother of David and a cornerstone for Christ, a model of clinging to Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whose-name|Whose Name?]]
- **Ruth, the non-Jewish great-grandmother of David** _(biblical)_ — Ruth's inclusion proves Matthew's genealogy is not about pure Jewish blood but God's relationship with the world.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Sabbath-Washing** _(other)_ — First-century parallel to cross-washing: keeping one ritual habit to claim superiority over even the anointed one of God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/privilege|Privilege]]
- **Sack of Rome** _(historical)_ — The sack of Rome serves as an example of the external threats the church contends with during the present millennial age.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/revelation-notes|Revelation Notes]]
- **Sacrificing the queen in chess** _(other)_ — Treating 'be last to be first' as a winning strategy misses the point, like a temporary sacrifice to ultimately dominate.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **Sailboat on Lake LBJ** _(personal)_ — Taking out his cousin's unfamiliar sailboat and being unable to do nothing once launched illustrates that the question of God leaves no neutral, do-nothing option.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-1|The Wager (Part 1)]]
- **Saint Crispin's Day speech from Shakespeare's Henry V** _(literary)_ — Henry's 'we band of brothers' battle speech, contrasted with the life of faith where the battle is already won — our job is to witness to Christ's victory, not fight for it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-world|Reset Your World]]
- **Saint Francis selling everything** _(historical)_ — God's call to Francis to sell all and give to the poor, raised to argue that the God who called Francis is the same God who calls us today.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]]
- **Saint Francis selling everything for the poor** _(historical)_ — Shows God still calls people to radical transformation; the God of Francis is not different from God today.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Sainte-Chapelle in Paris** _(historical)_ — Cited as a favorite example of Christian stained-glass art  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]]
- **Sales and 'getting to the close'** _(other)_ — The salesperson chasing the close stands for the peddler Paul warns against, contrasted with being a people of sincerity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]]
- **Salespeople trying to get to the close** _(other)_ — Contrasts a peddler chasing the sale with the church as a people of sincerity seeking transformation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Samaritan woman at Jacob's well** _(biblical)_ — The unclean Samaritan offered living water shows God's grace reaches us where and as we are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Samaritan's Purse nonprofit** _(current-events)_ — The large relief nonprofit named for the Samaritan shows how the figure gets idealized in culture.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Samson as the Jewish Goliath** _(biblical)_ — Samson, given great strength but undone by pride, foreshadows the danger of being the powerful one who is blinded by his gifts.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Samuel anointing David over his brothers** _(biblical)_ — God passes over the strong, handsome brothers to choose young David, because God sees the heart, not the outside.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-does-not-seek-its-own|Love Does Not Seek Its Own]]
- **Samuel Wesley's Latin commentary on Job** _(historical)_ — Samuel Wesley hiring curates so he could work on his Job commentary sets up how John came home to serve as curate of Wroot.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **Santa Pudenziana apse mosaic** _(historical)_ — Post-Constantine majesty creeping into depictions of Jesus  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Sarah and Elizabeth's symmetry** _(biblical)_ — Elizabeth (like Sarah) is too old and Mary too young—two barrennesses God overcomes.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Saul of Tarsus at Stephen's stoning** _(biblical)_ — Saul's seemingly fixed, predictable life shows God can intervene and change anyone, countering the assumption people can't change.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Saving yourself with the Longhorns or Cowboys** _(other)_ — Trying to give meaning to life through sports teams, job, family, or politics will fall short and lose your life.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/take-up-your-cross|Take Up Your Cross]]
- **Schadenfreude** _(other)_ — The German term for delight in another's pain illustrates the violence in the heart that makes God's peace seem abhorrent or scary.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/what-does-god-ask-of-us|What Does God Ask of Us?]]
- **Schadenfreude over the lost sheep** _(other)_ — Our delight in others' comeuppance contrasts with the shepherd who seeks even the sheep that 'deserved' to be lost.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/being-sheep|Being Sheep]]
- **Schmemann on fasting as a real fight** _(literary)_ — Schmemann's image of starting the fast over after each failure, with patience, illustrates that holiness has no shortcut.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **School Spirit at A&M Consolidated** _(personal)_ — The preacher's high school spirit days playing 'Eye of the Tiger' on Fridays, contrasted with the Holy Spirit to show how 'spirit' is an ambiguous word.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Schumacher's 1994 and 1997 last-race collisions** _(historical)_ — Schumacher knocking out Damon Hill (1994) and crashing into Villeneuve (1997) illustrates wins won by trickery that leave a bad taste.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Schweitzer's quest for the historical Jesus** _(historical)_ — Seekers of Jesus' face keep finding their own reflection looking back (eisegesis)  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Science fiction and fantasy prophecies** _(pop-culture)_ — Dune's Lisan al-Gaib, Star Wars, DragonLance, Wheel of Time, and Game of Thrones all hinge on prophecy, framing how we hear the word 'prophecy.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-prophecy|The Prophecy]]
- **Scopes trial and Inherit the Wind** _(historical)_ — Bryan vs. Darrow over teaching evolution in Tennessee, which became Inherit the Wind, illustrates the cultural baggage attached to the word 'fundamentalist.'  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Scott calmed by the Facebook page** _(personal)_ — Scott said visiting the Facebook page calmed him down, showing the patient devotion practiced on the way to marriage.  → Elizabeth and Scott
- **Screen-every-time phone call** _(personal)_ — The person whose name on the screen you never answer names the everyday enemy we all actually have.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Screening phone calls and spam robocalls** _(personal)_ — The preacher's preference for texts over calls and screening unknown numbers illustrates how we screen God's call.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **Scriptures as swaddling clothes, not swords** _(other)_ — Scripture is not a sword to beat people with but the swaddling clothes that hold the baby Jesus offered for us.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/we-are-one|We are One]]
- **Scrooge in A Christmas Carol / Scrooged** _(pop-culture)_ — the classic dramatic conversion story that movies prefer over the slow work that follows  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Scylla and Charybdis** _(literary)_ — the Odyssey's twin monsters illustrate the twin errors of over- and under-believing in the supernatural  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **Sea of Galilee compared to Lake Travis** _(nature)_ — The Sea of Galilee is large enough (twice Lake Travis's surface) that a storm can rage on one side while the other stays calm, showing why fishermen feared uncontrollable storms.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Secret society with a secret handshake** _(other)_ — An in-group club guarding secret knowledge illustrates the false gnostic idea that God's truth is reserved for a select few.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **Seeing through a glass darkly** _(literary)_ — We are blind to our own blind spots, but Jesus still sees and loves us  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Seinfeld - Jerry dates his female double** _(pop-culture)_ — Jerry falls for a woman identical to himself, showing that loving someone is not making them just like yourself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-does-not-seek-its-own|Love Does Not Seek Its Own]]
- **Seinfeld finale Good Samaritan law** _(pop-culture)_ — The show's last episode hinging on a Good Samaritan law illustrates the cultural shorthand of the Samaritan.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Seinfeld rental car reservation** _(pop-culture)_ — Jerry's bit on knowing how to take a reservation but not hold it illustrates that anyone can claim Christianity but the key is holding to the faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/holding-the-faith-doing-what-you-say|Holding the Faith: Doing What You Say]], [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Selecting the perfect peach** _(nature)_ — Picking the ripe peach by rejecting the bruised and unripe ones illustrates that selection is exclusive, unlike God's election.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/election-not-selection|Election, Not Selection]]
- **Self-driving cars and liability** _(current-events)_ — Self-driving cars are stalled not by technology but by the legal question of who is liable in a crash, illustrating that faith cannot be on autopilot.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Selling something called 'ordinary'** _(other)_ — A mock sales call pitching a product as 'ordinary' shows how nobody wants the ordinary, framing the scandal of naming half the year ordinary time.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Seminary book on food in the Bible** _(personal)_ — The preacher's own seminary thesis that biblical food is about who you eat with, not what it does to you, reframing food away from moralism.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **Seminary summer at the Duke campus bookstore** _(personal)_ — Working a low-pressure bookstore job (Harry Potter campers, absent-minded professors) gave the preacher time to research a Bible-and-food book.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/opened-eyes|Opened Eyes]]
- **Seminole Canyon Cave Drawings** _(nature)_ — Ancient cave drawings near Del Rio illustrate how caves have long shaped the human imagination.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **SEO Job at a Plumbing Company** _(personal)_ — The preacher's post-seminary SEO job optimizing plumbing-part words illustrates the constant manipulation of language to capture attention.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Serious Musicians' Lightness** _(personal)_ — Real musicians focus on expression beyond the notes because execution is taken care of, illustrating recognizable mastery and trust.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/come-and-see-2|Come and See]]
- **Sermon on the Mount as unachievable ideal** _(biblical)_ — Treating the Beatitudes as impossible sells short God's grace; Scripture's commands are meant to be achievable and transforming.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Servant untying sandals** _(historical)_ — Untying a master's sandal was a servant's task, so John feeling unworthy even of that shows the immense gap to Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-baptism-of-the-lord|The Baptism of the Lord]]
- **Setting and resetting the table** _(other)_ — Reset is a plain English word, like setting a table, then resetting it after something happens.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house-2|Reset Your House]]
- **Seventh-grade football as offensive guard** _(personal)_ — Being placed where the least athletic kids go, ending his athletic career, as part of the contingent shape of his life.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **Shakespeare vs TikTok reels** _(pop-culture)_ — The stylistic gap between Genesis 1 and 2 is like Shakespeare versus a set of TikTok reels, illustrating how translation flattens a text's character.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]]
- **Shared hobbies that don't unite the church** _(personal)_ — Loving UT, Formula 1, boats, knitting, or pickleball is not what binds the church; the word of God is what cuts through the world's drag.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/aerodynamics|Aerodynamics]]
- **Sharing about a new baby** _(personal)_ — People naturally love to share when they or their children have a baby, modeling the joy that should drive sharing our faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/good-news-what-is-evangelism|Good News: What Is Evangelism?]]
- **Sharing Location to Find a Friend** _(personal)_ — Meeting a man at the same establishment but in different buildings, found only by sharing phone location, shows how easily we can be lost even when we think we are not.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Shaving in the mirror** _(personal)_ — The preacher's discomfort with mirrors and how a reflection distorts us illustrates how hard it is to see ourselves as we truly are.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i|Who Am I?]]
- **She never really cussed** _(personal)_ — Her quiet, gracious character — the preacher wishing he could have been more like her.  → Memorial for Estalena Ferguson Aylieff
- **Sheep are stupid and vulnerable** _(nature)_ — Sheep's unintelligence and defenselessness (drowning in ponds, easy prey for wolves) shows humans' real condition before the shepherd.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/being-sheep|Being Sheep]]
- **Sheep without a shepherd (children's message)** _(nature)_ — Domesticated sheep can't survive or protect themselves alone, like the aimless crowds Jesus had compassion on.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/election-not-selection|Election, Not Selection]]
- **Shepherd seeking the lost sheep and father celebrating the returning son** _(biblical)_ — God will not chop us down but seeks and celebrates our return  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]]
- **Shepherds and the Little Drummer Boy at the stable** _(other)_ — Unlike the kings with prepared gifts, the shepherds and drummer boy show the overwhelmed believer asking what they can give the God who is love.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Shibboleth at the bridge crossing in Judges** _(biblical)_ — The tribal password test in Judges illustrates how the virgin birth is misused as a litmus-test shibboleth.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **Shibboleth at the Jordan (Gileadites and Ephraimites)** _(biblical)_ — A password that sorts insider from outsider illustrates how we mark people as safe or unsafe.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Shoddy Pool Foundation** _(personal)_ — A family's pool cracked because the concrete was poured before the rebar was set, illustrating the cost of a bad foundation laid in haste.  → A Foundation of Water
- **Shopping list with macadamia nuts and cranberries** _(personal)_ — Questioning whether you really need macadamia nuts or cranberries, evoking the fussy specialness of Christmas preparations.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **Short-form social video** _(current-events)_ — TikTok, Instagram reels, YouTube shorts of K-pop Demon Hunters dancing in a Target are present then gone with no subtext to interpret.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-lamb-of-god|The Lamb of God]]
- **Show Don't Tell writing adage** _(personal)_ — From the preacher's college English major: good writing shows rather than tells, just as Jesus shows who he is by his deeds rather than a yes/no answer.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/cantata-show-don-t-tell|Cantata: Show, Don't Tell]], [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **Shrub in the desert** _(biblical)_ — Jeremiah's image of the one who trusts in his own strength, parched when droughts come.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Siddhartha by Hermann Hesse** _(literary)_ — The line that fasting and praying let you out-wait anyone inspired his strategy of arriving early, illustrating dogged commitment.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **Sign of Jonah** _(biblical)_ — A symbol understood only retrospectively, like Christ's death and resurrection.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Signaling from an island** _(nature)_ — On an island you signal rather than exhaust yourself, modeling conserving energy in a survival situation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/rescue|Rescue]]
- **Signing 50 pages at a house closing** _(other)_ — Pointless closing documents from an 1853 lawsuit contrast with faith, which needs no concrete signed contract about our future with God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **Simeon's song (Nunc Dimittis)** _(biblical)_ — Simeon's spontaneous song, sung nightly worldwide since at least 516 AD, models worship as thanks.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]], [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Simeon's song (Nunc Dimittis)** _(biblical)_ — Simeon's spontaneous song of praise sung nightly since at least 516 AD models thanking God through worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **Simon and Garfunkel 'The Sound of Silence'** _(pop-culture)_ — 'Hello darkness my old friend' echoes the bleak close of Psalm 88, the only psalm ending in unrelieved darkness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Simon Magus and the sin of simony** _(biblical)_ — Simon's attempt to buy the Spirit (Acts 8) exemplifies the false lights that dazzle but cannot save.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Simon the magician in Acts** _(biblical)_ — Simon's wish to buy apostolic power shows the perennial exploitation of God's good for profit.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **Singing together in a crowd** _(other)_ — The good feeling of all singing the same song at a concert or church service illustrates the powerful, often un-understood pull of the praising crowd shouting Hosanna.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Sinking Ship Full of Ice Cream** _(other)_ — Gorging on ice cream as a boat sinks feels good but isn't good, illustrating feel-good news versus genuinely transforming good news.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/binding-the-strongman|Binding the Strongman]]
- **Sinner's prayer as get-out-of-jail-free card** _(pop-culture)_ — Salvation wrongly imagined as a Monopoly-style card you earn by saying the sinner's prayer, contrasted with holistic, sanctifying salvation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-will-come-to-judge-the-living-and-the-dead|He Will Come to Judge the Living and the Dead]], [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Sir Gawain and the Green Knight** _(literary)_ — Gawain only survives the axe once he removes the protective belt and stops clinging to life, illustrating that letting go of self-preservation brings freedom from the fear of death.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/what-does-god-ask-of-us|What Does God Ask of Us?]]
- **Sky Daddy / Genie in a Bottle** _(other)_ — We must not treat God as a genie who grants our wishes; prayer returns us to our source to align our will with God's.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **Slavery and family-values idolatry** _(historical)_ — Southern slavery and domestic violence justified by 'Christian/family values' show values becoming idols that distort the gospel.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]]
- **Slumped fans at eliminated playoff games** _(pop-culture)_ — The camera panning to fans with slumped shoulders and tearful eyes when hope of victory is lost illustrates hanging your head in desperation.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/why-do-we-worship-the-assembly-and-the-bread-of-life|Why Do We Worship? The Assembly and the Bread of Life]]
- **Small farm plots of Western North Carolina** _(personal)_ — Seminary-era memory of small, land-constrained acreages contrasted with industrial monoculture scale.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **Snatch (Guy Ritchie film) Antwerp diamond scene** _(pop-culture)_ — Opening scene of Hasidic Jews debating the Isaiah 7 'almah' translation frames the virgin/young-woman ambiguity as a Christian 'gotcha'.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **Snow Day in Texas That Won't Last** _(nature)_ — The fleeting Texas snow, gone by next week's 70-degree weather, illustrates the mountaintop experience the disciples wrongly wanted to last forever.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **So Many Rules** _(personal)_ — Wilson's service on standing rules committees for both conference and jurisdiction, played as ironic contrast to the rule-free retreat.  → October Pastor Report
- **Soap opera characters dying and returning** _(pop-culture)_ — Soap operas normalize characters dying and coming back, unlike the genuinely jarring reality of resurrection.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/opened-eyes|Opened Eyes]]
- **Social clubs with a different story** _(other)_ — Golf clubs, pickleball, Kiwanis, and Rotary gather around different stories, unlike the church gathered solely around Jesus Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Social media high-performer routines (CEOs, Olympians)** _(current-events)_ — Modern fascination with elite daily routines parallels Wesley's holy-life models, but aimed at transformed lives.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/follow-me|Follow Me]]
- **Social media influencer self-care** _(current-events)_ — No amount of optimized routine or influencer life-hacks can self-actualize us away from sin  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **Socks vs. heirloom pearls and watch** _(personal)_ — Simple gifts (socks) versus heirloom gifts that carry past, present, and future meaning, framing Jesus as a gift across all three.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-jesus|The Gift of Jesus]]
- **Solomon and the divided baby** _(biblical)_ — The Hebrew word nigzar ('cut/divide') first appears in 1 Kings 3, illustrating Christ's total separation from the land of the living.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Someone screaming during sermon under I-35** _(personal)_ — A man standing and screaming at a Church Under the Bridge service illustrates the agency people have in the present moment.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-present|The Church in the Present]]
- **Someone you admire as holiness** _(personal)_ — Picturing an admired person of courage and care redefines holiness as sharing love, available to everyone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Song of Simeon** _(biblical)_ — Simeon's gratitude over the infant Jesus shows non-rivalry, paralleling Eli's gladness that God speaks through the child Samuel.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Sorting at cafeteria tables** _(personal)_ — People sitting at different tables and choosing who is worthy of their time shows our constant self-sorting that the Trinity calls us to abandon.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Sorting at the cafeteria tables** _(personal)_ — Kids and adults sorting themselves by preference shows how church wrongly sorts the likable from the unlikable.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]]
- **Sorting good guys and bad guys into buckets** _(other)_ — Labeling countries, states, and teams good or bad is the neat dualism that removes the need for faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/anoint-the-dust|Anoint the Dust]]
- **Sourdough Starter and Wild Yeast** _(nature)_ — Leaving flour and water out lets wild yeasts grow, illustrating why matzah must be made in under 19 minutes.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/give-us-this-day-our-daily-bread|Give Us This Day Our Daily Bread]]
- **South Park: Jesus vs. Satan wrestling match** _(pop-culture)_ — The Pay-Per-View bout (pasty hippy Jesus vs. a giant red beast) shows how worldly we are conditioned to picture power as muscles and might.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Special forces qualifying tests** _(other)_ — Faith is sometimes wrongly presented like special forces training, a series of qualifying tests for a heavenly reward, contrary to grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Special forces qualifying training** _(other)_ — Faith wrongly presented as a series of qualifying tests for a heavenly reward, illustrating the temptation of works-righteousness against grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting-2|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Speck in your neighbor's eye, log in your own** _(biblical)_ — Fixating on others' faults blinds us to our own  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-sign|A Sign]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Speeding ticket and intention** _(personal)_ — An officer judges your speed, not your intention, showing how the letter of the law cares only about the words on the page.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eye-of-the-camel|Eye of the Camel]]
- **Spending $300 at Hobby Lobby** _(current-events)_ — Resetting your house is not about buying religious decor or painting over things to hide them.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-house|Reset Your House]]
- **Spicy mustard with a kick** _(other)_ — The pastor's favorite spicy mustard with a little kick, an extended metaphor for a faith with bite that refuses domination and seeks to witness rather than win.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/spicy-mustard|Spicy Mustard]]
- **Spilled coffee and the meaning of 'accident'** _(personal)_ — An accidentally spilled coffee illustrates Aristotle's category of 'accident' (inessential) versus essential substance.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]]
- **Spirit Halloween and Ghosts** _(pop-culture)_ — Halloween costumes, ghosts, and New Year's 'spirits' illustrate the cultural meanings of 'spirit' as invisible-yet-present, leading into the Spirit as fully God yet unseen.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Spirit set on fire with longing** _(other)_ — One who tastes the heavenly food of prayer burns with eternal longing for the Lord, his spirit aflame.  → Prayer and Converse with God Is a Supreme Good
- **Splanchnizomai as a bowel movement** _(other)_ — Notes the Greek word for the father's compassion derives from 'bowels,' literally a gut-level, visceral mercy.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-prodigal-son-and-the-ministry-of-reconciliation|The Prodigal Son and the Ministry of Reconciliation]]
- **Spock's sacrifice in The Wrath of Khan** _(pop-culture)_ — 'the needs of the many outweigh the needs of the few' as the utilitarian logic underlying Judas's objection  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **Spontaneous neighborhood pickup games** _(personal)_ — Boys on the block meeting up unplanned to play football and baseball as a picture of organic neighbor connection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Sports all-time records and childhood Olympic obsession** _(personal)_ — As a kid loving home-run and rushing records and Olympic champions, illustrating the human fixation on finding 'the best.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **Sports cliche on getting back up** _(pop-culture)_ — The cliche that it's not how you fall down but how you get back up that counts, framing grace as the way we rise after temptation.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **Sports preparation and blown calls** _(pop-culture)_ — Ads praising preparation over the game, and 'don't put yourself in position to need that call,' illustrate preparing our lives to be interrupted by neighbor-love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **Sports Radio Politics** _(current-events)_ — Over 30 years the language of sports radio took over American politics, turning it into a team sport, illustrating dangerous tribalism.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/us-and-them|Us and Them]]
- **Sports rivalries (Cowboys-Giants, OU-Texas)** _(pop-culture)_ — Famous sports rivalries frame the deeper rivalry between Herod and Jesus, Caesar and God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/rivals|Rivals]]
- **St. Aelred on enlarging the horizon of love** _(historical)_ — Aelred's call to expand our love by contemplating the humanity of the Lord answers the restlessness of acedia.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **St. Andrews New Testament Professor on Paul the Physicist** _(personal)_ — A friend's interview describing Paul's interest in the physical world and the puzzle of Paul's 'third heaven' in 2 Corinthians 12.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-ascended-into-heaven|He Ascended into Heaven]]
- **St. Cyril of Jerusalem on the two comings** _(historical)_ — Cyril's preaching on a first patient coming and a glorious second illustrates Advent looking back and forward at once.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **St. Luke's Houston pastor on habits not values** _(personal)_ — A clergy-call colleague's church having 'habits' instead of 'values' inspires the call to make values into action.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **St. Paul's Cathedral Christmas Eve attendance, 1804** _(historical)_ — Only four people attended Christmas Eve in 1804 London, showing there's no simple progress from 'primitive' to modern belief.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Stale chili powder** _(nature)_ — Five-year-old chili powder with no spice left updates Jesus' 'salt that lost its saltiness' for the faith that no longer flavors a life.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Stalin's 'one death is a tragedy' quote** _(historical)_ — We can relate to one death but not a million, because grief flows from particular love.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Stanley Hauerwas on going to church to be told we have no enemies** _(literary)_ — His quip that cosmic struggle sounds like a child's video game exposes our wish to deny we have any enemies to pray for.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Staph infection under a bandage** _(other)_ — Bandaging an open wound without treating the infection keeps you on the surface, like believing in Christ without the descent.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Star of Bethlehem as two aligning planets** _(nature)_ — The star can be a planetary alignment and a sign of God's love at once, illustrating an overflowing rather than flat reality.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Star of Bethlehem as two planets aligning** _(nature)_ — The Bethlehem star can be a natural planetary conjunction and also a sign of God's love, illustrating that more than one thing can be true at once.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Star Trek 5 / Starship to Heaven** _(pop-culture)_ — We can't visit heaven via a starship; heaven is not another planet but the source in whom we live and move and have our being.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-ascended-into-heaven|He Ascended into Heaven]]
- **Star Trek V: Flying to Heaven** _(pop-culture)_ — Captain Kirk flying the Enterprise to heaven illustrates that God is not a far-off material place but the source in whom we live and move and have our being.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]]
- **Star Wars / Luke Skywalker** _(pop-culture)_ — Anyone can be a hero, but there comes a point when it's just not going to happen.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/antichrist-becoming-the-villain|Antichrist: Becoming the Villain]]
- **Star Wars expanded universe fandom** _(personal)_ — The preacher's youthful obsession with reading all 45 Star Wars novels and online debates illustrates how foolishness is always relative and contextual.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **Star Wars rebellion against the evil empire** _(pop-culture)_ — Preacher's first encounter with the idea of rebellion; rebellion makes sense when there is an evil empire to fight against.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/sin-as-rebellion-the-freedom-not-to-rebel|Sin as Rebellion: The Freedom Not to Rebel]]
- **Starting and Tending a Fire** _(nature)_ — Fire needs spark, fuel, and oxygen, and must be relit and fed daily, picturing how love must be continually tended to keep us in the light.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/keeping-the-fire-of-love-burning|Keeping the Fire of Love Burning]]
- **Starting small with a 40-day fast** _(personal)_ — Advising a beginner to fast a meal rather than 40 days illustrates starting the means of grace where you are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Stations of the Cross pilgrims in Jerusalem** _(other)_ — Groups of pilgrims walking the stations in the Holy Land illustrate the ancient practice of entering deeply into Christ's last moments, which the congregation will do via QR code.  → Palm Sunday: From Hosanna to Passion
- **Stations of the Cross, Westminster Cathedral design** _(historical)_ — The sanctuary display modeled on Westminster Cathedral's stations illustrates the ancient pilgrim practice of walking Christ's last moments.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waving-from-the-sideline|Waving from the Sideline]]
- **Stealing fruit as a kid** _(personal)_ — Being caught in a childhood mistake of stealing fruit illustrates the loneliness of being caught in sin and the urge to deny, divert, or distract.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/jesus-our-advocate|Jesus Our Advocate]]
- **Stephen Ministers program at Bee Creek** _(personal)_ — The congregation's Stephen Ministers who walk with and listen to the hurting exemplify being active participants in God's transformation rather than fixers.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/why-is-he-coming|Why Is He Coming?]]
- **Stepping into a warm room by a fireplace** _(nature)_ — Coming in cold to the bones and warming by a fire pictures the felt presence of God dissolving fear and giving safety.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/trinity-sunday|Trinity Sunday]]
- **Stepping into a warm room from the cold** _(nature)_ — Coming in cold and feeling the fire's warmth depicts the felt safety of the Holy Spirit's presence.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/wisdom-s-call|Wisdom's Call]]
- **Steve Bienkowski, retired NYPD rescue diver** _(personal)_ — Live interview showing how rescued people can become their own worst enemy in the water, paralleling how we get in our own way with God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-should-we-do-to-be-saved|What Should We Do to Be Saved?]]
- **Steve Earle, 'I Ain't Ever Satisfied'** _(pop-culture)_ — The song illustrates the restless human heart that is never satisfied apart from God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **Stoic mountain-climbing philosophy with his kids** _(personal)_ — Telling his kids '15 miles to go' even on a 2-mile hike, so they don't make the end unbearable by expecting it too soon.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Stone too big for God to lift** _(other)_ — The logical contradiction exposes the false assumption that God is on the same material plane as creation.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Stonewall Jackson outwitting Frémont** _(historical)_ — A Civil War valley passed on the hike, underscoring the diversity of places and cultures crossed.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **Storms on the Sea of Galilee** _(nature)_ — The disciples being more familiar with sudden Galilee storms than with Jesus illustrates how we trust the passing of life's storms more than the word of God.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/what-does-god-ask-of-us|What Does God Ask of Us?]]
- **Straightening a crooked building with a crowbar** _(other)_ — Trying to make ourselves righteous by works is like trying to straighten a crooked building with only a crowbar, which cannot happen.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/justification|Justification]]
- **Stranded in Lake Travis** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor of God reaching out a hand to someone flailing in the middle of Lake Travis, who suffers if they refuse to admit they need help and grab it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins-2|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Stuck on the side of the road** _(personal)_ — Wilson's own experience being stranded contrasts with the far graver trapped-in-a-cave need for rescue.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-salvation|What is Salvation?]]
- **Student of the Year in Walking** _(personal)_ — The preacher's lone high-school award amid friends bound for Stanford and West Point shows our worth is not measured against our peers' resumes.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **Sudden $100,000 windfall** _(personal)_ — An unexpected no-strings gift forces a choice between freedom and insecurity, mirroring the servants' response to the talents.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/do-or-do-not|Do Or Do Not]]
- **Sufjan Stevens' 'The Transfiguration'** _(pop-culture)_ — Lyrics ('Lost in the clouds, a voice / Son of Man, turn your ear') quoted to evoke the awe of the heavenly voice at the transfiguration.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]], [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Sufjan Stevens, "The Transfiguration"** _(pop-culture)_ — Stevens' song that retells the Transfiguration and invites listeners into its chorus reopened the preacher's attention to the event and frames the sermon's close.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/who-is-jesus-the-transfiguration|Who Is Jesus? The Transfiguration]]
- **Sufjan Stevens, 'Casimir Pulaski Day'** _(pop-culture)_ — A song about a girlfriend dying of cancer, wrestling with hope and faith amid suffering, illustrating complex faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Summer kids' sleep schedule** _(personal)_ — Children waking unpredictably (7:30 vs 5:30am) as everyday evidence experience denies pure predetermination.  → Drawn by the Father
- **Summer of George (Seinfeld)** _(pop-culture)_ — The Seinfeld 'Summer of George' plot illustrates squandering or corrupting a good gift through complacency.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/us-and-them|Us and Them]]
- **Summer of George (Seinfeld)** _(pop-culture)_ — George Costanza's hyped 'summer of George' collapses into disappointment, illustrating how our anticipated future time to shine rarely arrives as planned.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]]
- **Summer pastorate at High Rock Lake** _(personal)_ — Wilson's seminary-summer lake ministry near Lexington, NC, where he did twelve funerals for strangers who welcomed him in, teaching him to speak truthfully and with dignity over the dead.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/who-do-you-say-that-i-am|Who Do You Say That I Am?]]
- **Summit Fever** _(nature)_ — The burst of energy climbers feel near a mountaintop, illustrating the Spirit's empowering push at Pentecost.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Sun, moon, and tides** _(nature)_ — Light, seasons, and tidal cycles God set in the dome of the sky are the conditions trees need to bear fruit.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Sunday as the eighth day / little Easter** _(other)_ — Worshiping on the day Jesus rose marks the start of the New Creation.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Sunday school classes in 1745** _(historical)_ — Early Sunday schools teaching children to read valued children as opportunities not instruments, modeling enduring values over trends.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]]
- **Sunday Sense vs Monday Sense** _(personal)_ — Not having a separate 'church sense' and 'business sense' shows truth-telling as a resurrection mindset with real-world consequences.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **Super Bowl sports cliches** _(pop-culture)_ — Football platitudes like 'one game at a time' and 'it's not how you start but how you finish' set up the contrast between finite games and the open-ended length of a life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/spilling-things-god-offers-us-a-chance-for-a-do-over|Spilling Things: God Offers Us a Chance for a Do-Over]]
- **Superhero / Iron Man suit of grace** _(pop-culture)_ — God does not zap you with 'superhero juice' to do whatever you want; grace walks with you rather than leaving you where you are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]], [[covenant-umc/2024/palm-sunday|Palm Sunday]]
- **Superhero needs a supervillain** _(pop-culture)_ — The narrative craving for a villain of equal power illustrates how dualism wrongly inflates the devil into God's rival.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **Superheroes (Avengers / X-Men / Captain America / Wonder Woman)** _(pop-culture)_ — The unreachable gap between us and comic-book superheroes contrasts with sainthood, which anyone can attain by grace rather than by birth or super strength.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Superman heating toast with laser vision** _(pop-culture)_ — Jesus refuses to use divine power for his own convenience  → [[covenant-umc/2025/no-distinction|No Distinction]]
- **Supermarket bags of fruit** _(personal)_ — Store-bought bags of grapes and oranges hide the hands that pick them and make fruitfulness only a distant metaphor.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Supporting actors in the movie of our life** _(other)_ — Other people are not supporting actors in the movie of our life but individual agents made in God's image.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Supreme Court Justice dream with an island on the Potomac** _(personal)_ — The preacher's childhood plan to become a Supreme Court Justice with an island on the bay illustrates the 'what do you want to be' question and how our aspirations differ from God's concern for the heart.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/we-are-god-s-children-but-we-are-not-finished-yet|We Are God's Children, But We Are Not Finished Yet]]
- **Surveys of those who left the church** _(current-events)_ — The number one reason people give for leaving the church is hypocrisy or judgmentalism of Christians.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Susanna and Samuel Wesley's fight over the king** _(historical)_ — Susanna refusing to say Amen to a prayer for King William illustrates conscience-bound conviction in Wesley's formative household  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Sweeping beer bottles at Resort Ranch** _(historical)_ — Members arriving early to clean a party venue before Palm Sunday worship illustrates the church's take-care-of-business DNA.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **Swimming across Lake Travis to prove God's love** _(personal)_ — We don't have to swim Lake Travis or volunteer for everything to earn God's love; sometimes faith's first move is simply to rest.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **Swimming pool rules vs. Wesley's General Rules** _(other)_ — Wesley's General Rules aren't prohibitions like 'no running at the pool' but active tools for living out God's promise on our life.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-not-to-make-jesus-king|How not to make Jesus King]]
- **Synchronizing watches in movies** _(pop-culture)_ — The countdown to synchronize watches shows how mechanical clock time replaced sun-based living.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/when-will-he-come|When will he come?]]
- **T.S. Eliot's 'The Cultivation of Christmas Trees'** _(literary)_ — Eliot's 1954 poem on childlike wonder at the tree, linking the first-remembered Christmas to a final great joy and fear, the first coming to the second.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-cultivation-of-christmas-trees|The Cultivation of Christmas Trees]]
- **T.V. Munson grafting saves French wine from phylloxera** _(historical)_ — A Texan agronomist grafted French vines onto pest-resistant wild Texas rootstock, saving Europe's vineyards while both grape varieties stayed themselves — a picture of Gentiles grafted into Israel's promise without replacing it.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/arise-shine-the-light-of-epiphany-and-the-grafting-in-of-the-gentiles|Arise, Shine: The Light of Epiphany and the Grafting In of the Gentiles]]
- **Taking off shoes vs. washing feet** _(personal)_ — The discomfort of letting someone wash your feet or refusing to remove your shoes illustrates the pride that runs both directions.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Taking shoes off for no one** _(personal)_ — Comfort washing another's feet but refusing to bare your own illustrates pride running both directions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Taking sons Dominic and Ephraim to first Astros game** _(personal)_ — Bringing his boys to their first game (Astros got 20 hits) frames the pastor's investment and rising anxiety as the season turned.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-we-fill-our-waiting|How We Fill Our Waiting]]
- **Tall poppy syndrome in Australia** _(personal)_ — Preacher's miserable year at Newcastle High after moving to Australia in eighth grade, where peers tore down anyone standing out, illustrating tearing down vs. building people up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-builds-up|Love Builds Up]]
- **Tamar and Judah / Perez** _(biblical)_ — A child of incest in the line shows the genealogy is not a 'blood prophecy' of good blood.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **Tanner asking the friend if Rachel was single** _(personal)_ — Tanner asked Rachel's friend if she was single, and the friend feigned not knowing.  → Rachel and Tanner
- **Tax collectors and idolatrous coins** _(historical)_ — Explains why tax collectors were despised — they handled coins bearing Caesar's face, making them impure in that culture.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-prodigal-son-and-the-ministry-of-reconciliation|The Prodigal Son and the Ministry of Reconciliation]]
- **Tax collectors and prostitutes entering the kingdom first** _(biblical)_ — Illustrates that those judged outsiders by external law-keeping standards are welcomed ahead of the self-righteous.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Taylor Swift inviting a fan on stage** _(pop-culture)_ — Being invited to participate though you feel unworthy, like communion's invitation  → [[covenant-umc/2025/communion|Communion]]
- **Teacher on never knowing who you marry** _(personal)_ — A teacher's claim that you can't predict a spouse 20 years out shows that love is sustained by daily presence, not prediction.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/easter-sunday-works-of-love|Easter Sunday: Works of Love]]
- **Teacher's apricot guess** _(personal)_ — A teacher who thought the forbidden fruit was an apricot because she loved apricots, illustrating how the fruit's real meaning is its location on the tree, not the food itself.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
