- **Teacher's line on regret** _(literary)_ — 'Someone who has no regrets is someone not fully human' frames honest confrontation of our own failures.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Teacher's quotation on forgiveness as medicine** _(literary)_ — A quoted teacher describes forgiveness as an all-powerful medicine that can heal any wound, but only if we wish to be healed.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **Teacher's quote on love that will not play fair** _(literary)_ — A quote that God's dangerous love demands we give away what we think is ours to feed and shelter others.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Teachers counting kids at BeeLoved childcare** _(personal)_ — Childcare teachers constantly counting children shows that in small groups it's easier not to leave anyone behind.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **Teaching children not to trespass on yards** _(personal)_ — Explaining to children why you don't cut through a stranger's Texas yard illustrates the everyday meaning of trespass as entering property without permission.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Teaching children to say thank you at Christmas** _(personal)_ — His kids practicing manners and gratitude for Christmas gifts frames the whole sermon's question of how to respond to a gift.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-to-say-thank-you|How to Say Thank You]]
- **Teaching kids to say thank you** _(personal)_ — His children practicing manners and gratitude at Christmas models responding to the gift rather than the object.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]], [[covenant-umc/2023/three-ways-of-saying-thank-you|Three Ways of Saying Thank You]]
- **Teaching the kids poker** _(personal)_ — Teaching his sons card games by playing fast rather than dwelling on rules illustrates that practice precedes theory.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
- **Teddy Roosevelt's 'The Man in the Arena'** _(historical)_ — Roosevelt's 1910 speech contrasts the critic with the one who strives in the arena, calling believers to be in the arena with Christ.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/a-sermon-on-endings-the-patience-of-simeon|A Sermon on Endings: The Patience of Simeon]]
- **Telling a Struggling Friend to Be Patient** _(personal)_ — Advice to wait is easy to give but hard to receive when you are the one jobless, single, or awaiting a medical specialist.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Temple priesthood as a roughneck two-weeks-on rotation** _(other)_ — Comparing temple service to oilfield shift work explains why the priest and Levite were heading downhill home past the beaten man  → [[covenant-umc/2025/ask-me-anything-2|Ask Me Anything]]
- **Ten Commandments on the capitol lawn, not the Beatitudes** _(current-events)_ — Why governments post the Decalogue but never the Beatitudes, revealing how Christianity is reduced to universal moral law disconnected from Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Ten men needed for a synagogue** _(historical)_ — The Jewish minyan tradition is traced to Abraham's bargaining for ten righteous people in Sodom, illustrating his mercy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **Ten-year-old Wilson preferred receiving presents** _(personal)_ — His childhood love of receiving gifts over giving sets up the contrast with the heart-gift God truly wants.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Terminator's nude arrival** _(pop-culture)_ — Arnold Schwarzenegger arriving nude from the future and seeking clothes, used to imagine the risen Jesus' body without his stripped garments.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-concrete-faith-of-thomas|The Concrete Faith of Thomas]]
- **Terry Eagleton on Optimism** _(literary)_ — Eagleton's critique of optimism as 'moral evasion' and the enemy of hope, distinguishing Christian hope that can face grave truth from cheerful psychological disavowal.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/laborers-in-the-vineyard-grace-community-and-mortality|Laborers in the Vineyard: Grace, Community, and Mortality]]
- **Terry Eagleton on optimism vs. hope** _(literary)_ — Quotation from Eagleton's Hope Without Optimism distinguishing the optimist's temperament from genuine hope rooted in God.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eternal-life|Eternal Life]]
- **Tertullian on prayer as the offering** _(historical)_ — Tertullian's quote that prayer is the offering God asked for, dedicated with the whole heart and crowned with love, illustrating prayer as the true sacrifice God desires.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/fragments|Fragments]]
- **Texas A&M and the 12th Man (E. King Gill)** _(historical)_ — A&M symbols and the 1922 12th Man story illustrate how symbols deepen relationship and belonging.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Texas A&M and the 12th Man (E. King Gill)** _(historical)_ — The 1922 Aggie 12th-man story (anyone willing and ready can be called in) parallels the common 'you can be David' reading of the story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/david-and-goliath|David and Goliath]]
- **Texas cedar allergies** _(personal)_ — A local aside that congestion forces mouth-breathing, grounding the breathing illustration in the congregation's life.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Texas ERCOT winter blackout** _(current-events)_ — The February 2021 Texas power crisis where blackouts fell on just and unjust alike, illustrating how we respond to extreme situations and care (or not) for neighbors.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Texas freeze and the generator temptation** _(current-events)_ — The frozen winter storm week illustrates restlessness and the temptations to pride or self-doubt over having (or not) a generator.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **Texas industrial farming and roadside peaches** _(personal)_ — Modern farming's scale makes it feel impersonal, abstracted from the people who eat the food.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **Texas public school testing bureaucracy** _(current-events)_ — Administrators structuring everything around test results pre-categorize kids by their expectations, like clay already baked.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/subterranean-hometown-blues|Subterranean Hometown Blues]]
- **Texas Sports Rivalries** _(personal)_ — The preacher growing up surrounded by Oilers/Cowboys, College Station/Bryan, and UT/A&M rivalries illustrates the human instinct to divide into 'us and them.'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/us-and-them|Us and Them]]
- **Texas y'all and Pittsburgh yinz** _(personal)_ — Regional dialect aside illustrating Jesus honing his question from 'who do people say' to the pointed 'who do you say I am.'  → [[covenant-umc/2024/take-up-your-cross|Take Up Your Cross]]
- **The 28.8 modem downloading a 3MB song** _(personal)_ — Generational gap in technology illustrates how alien the Temple's significance is to us now  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-portico-of-solomon|The Portico of Solomon]]
- **The 80/20 principle** _(other)_ — When 20% of people do 80% of the work, the church cannot be at its best, like an F1 car with a bad driver who keeps crashing.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **The Adams family and General David Adams** _(personal)_ — Claimed Revolutionary-War-general ancestor illustrates the cultural prestige attached to lineage.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/emmanuel-mercy|Emmanuel = Mercy]]
- **The Anarchy and 'new king, same as the old king'** _(historical)_ — Medieval succession wars (the Anarchy, Empress Matilda vs. Stephen, the War of the Roses) where peasants shrugged 'new king, same as the old king' — illustrating that resetting our world means focusing on life right where we are, not on distant centers of power.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-world|Reset Your World]]
- **The angel's 'thigh lock' submission** _(personal)_ — A submission with no equivalent in jiu-jitsu technique, used to show the encounter with God as supernatural, beyond technique, exposing human limitation as the site of grace.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **The Apostles' Creed empirically tested** _(literary)_ — Most creedal claims (virgin birth, Pilate) are unobservable and unrepeatable, unlike sin which we witness again and again  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **The B-I-B-L-E nursery song** _(pop-culture)_ — How a children's song ('I stand alone on the word of God') shapes a flawed, individualistic imagination of the Bible.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **The Big Lebowski / The Dude Abides** _(pop-culture)_ — The Dude, who only cares about his rug and 'abides,' is used to explore what it means to abide in God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/learning-to-abide-the-means-of-grace|Learning to Abide: The Means of Grace]]
- **The book of Job and the wager with the Satan** _(biblical)_ — Job's loss of family, property, and health following the wager between God and the Accuser illustrates an endless loop of shipwrecks where God, not the sufferer, makes the bet.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]]
- **The boy with loaves and fishes** _(biblical)_ — Offering what little he had (not giving up, not pretending to have more) becomes the means Jesus uses to feed the crowd.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **The broad way and the narrow way** _(biblical)_ — Media culture's 'be happy, take it easy' broad road contrasted with Christ's narrow way of fighting and suffering toward genuine happiness.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **The Brothers Karamazov and Ivan vs. Alexei** _(literary)_ — Dostoevsky's novel of failure: Alexei answers Ivan's argument from the problem of evil not with refutation but by embracing his brother and seeking out Father Zosima.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **The Canaanite woman** _(biblical)_ — The woman who persisted and pressed Jesus for crumbs, unlike the rich young ruler, models faith that does not walk away from the hard encounter.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/not-easy-to-watch|Not Easy to Watch]]
- **The child pretending to be a pirate** _(personal)_ — A child who pretends to be a pirate (stick as sword, box as sailboat) balances two worlds, illustrating how faith lets us live in this world as citizens of a more real one without it being mere pretend.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/having-the-son-living-a-more-real-life-by-faith|Having the Son: Living a More Real Life by Faith]]
- **The childhood horse** _(personal)_ — Chris had a horse as a child and loved it, showing her lifelong devotion to caring for animals.  → A Service of Death and Resurrection for Christine Renée Jarl
- **The children's song 'In the Beginning'** _(personal)_ — Wilson's own children's song distills creation to its essentials so kids can sing and remember God's loving creative work.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]]
- **The Church's One Foundation hymn** _(literary)_ — The hymn naming Jesus Christ as the church's one foundation is affirmed as true, with Jesus's parable adding another way to see that foundation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-chassis|The Chassis]], A Foundation of Water
- **The cold open** _(pop-culture)_ — Like SNL's first sketch or a podcast's opening segment, you can't start with the climax; Advent is the getting-ready before Christmas.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-four-questions-who-is-coming|The Four Questions: Who Is Coming?]]
- **The college question for teenagers** _(personal)_ — Pruitt recalls dreading 'where do you want to go to college?' until realizing people just wanted a story to fill in, showing how the future can feel ominous in moments of transition.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]]
- **The contingent moments of Gettysburg** _(historical)_ — Oliver Howard's stand, Chamberlain's 20th Maine on Little Round Top, and Pickett's Charge as forks where the nation's direction hung in the balance.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **The continuous gardener** _(nature)_ — A gardener who tills, weeds, and composts only once and never again grows nothing—tending the soul's soil is an ongoing affair.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **The Daily Examen at Evensong** _(historical)_ — The classical Examen prayer practice models building a lexicon of gratitude to talk about one's soul.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **The Daniel Plan diet book** _(pop-culture)_ — A bestselling Christian diet book (Atkins/Paleo repackaged) used to show how the Bible isn't actually about what you eat.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/opened-eyes|Opened Eyes]]
- **The devil quoting scripture to Jesus** _(biblical)_ — Quoting scripture doesn't make what you say good; accountability is needed  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **The devil's temptation of Jesus** _(biblical)_ — Williams reads the temptation stories as the devil urging Jesus to take his eye off the present for an imagined future.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-next-thirty-seconds|The Next Thirty Seconds]]
- **The divine watchmaker found in a field** _(historical)_ — The 18th-century deist argument the sermon rejects in favor of God's continual sustaining of creation.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/trinity-sunday-in-the-beginning-song-of-creation|Trinity Sunday: In the Beginning (Song of Creation)]], [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]], [[covenant-umc/2026/three-whats|Three Whats?]]
- **The Dodgers Probably Winning Again** _(pop-culture)_ — The gap between baseball's universal optimism and the reality that the favorite usually wins underscores how easily hope deflates.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **The don't-miss Halloween house** _(personal)_ — Her elaborate Halloween decorations made her home a 'don't miss this house' stop, illustrating her generosity and delight.  → Glynis Frye Memorial
- **The Donatist controversy and Augustine** _(historical)_ — Augustine's response to the Donatists' traditores dispute teaches that the church holds wheat and chaff and only Christ may judge.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/rivals|Rivals]]
- **The dove and the end of the flood** _(biblical)_ — The Spirit descending as a dove echoes Noah's dove announcing the flood's end, signaling new creation  → [[covenant-umc/2025/remember-and-be-thankful|Remember and Be Thankful]]
- **The Dude abides in The Big Lebowski** _(pop-culture)_ — Jeff Bridges' character 'the Dude' saying 'the Dude abides' illustrates the rare word abide, meaning to remain or live.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **The edge/aperture of a photograph** _(other)_ — Extended metaphor: every image has a limit beyond its edge, unlike God's love which has no limit.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/all-peoples-and-creatures|All Peoples and Creatures]]
- **The electric chair as a modern cross** _(other)_ — Imagining carrying your own electric chair to your execution while the town watches, to recover the terrible meaning of taking up the cross.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/take-up-your-cross|Take Up Your Cross]]
- **The end of The Sopranos** _(pop-culture)_ — Mark's abrupt cut-off before the Good News is shared is like the famously incomplete Sopranos finale that leaves viewers to fill in the missing pieces.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]]
- **The enemy you avoid at HEB** _(personal)_ — Defining an enemy as the person you dread seeing at the grocery store or answering the phone for makes 'love your enemies' concretely hard.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **The English Civil War and execution of Charles I** _(historical)_ — Charles I's high view of kingship, defeat at Naseby, and 1649 execution under Cromwell show humanity learning it does not need a king.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/how-not-to-make-jesus-king|How not to make Jesus King]]
- **The Ethiopian eunuch reading Isaiah (Acts 8)** _(biblical)_ — Solitary Bible reading drives us toward community and guidance  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **The Exorcist and demon-possession horror films** _(pop-culture)_ — cinema shapes our demon imagery more than the Bible does  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **The forgiven teenager thought experiment** _(personal)_ — Imagining a 15-16 year old told they'll be forgiven no matter what illustrates antinomianism, while noting God transforms the will rather than controlling behavior.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-race|The Race]]
- **The four W's of finding wild shelter** _(other)_ — Survival rule (wood, water, wind, widowmakers) is mapped onto evaluating the health of the church.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/shelter|Shelter]]
- **The Funeral Industrial Complex** _(current-events)_ — Five companies owning 95% of U.S. funeral homes and exploiting the bereaved illustrates how the vulnerable are taken advantage of around death.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/jesus-wept|Jesus Wept]]
- **The Glorious Revolution of 1688** _(historical)_ — James II fleeing the Dutch invasion frames the political loyalty conflict shaping Wesley's upbringing  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **The Godfather and Don Corleone's Favors** _(pop-culture)_ — The mob boss who grants favors with a future debt due illustrates the false image of God as one who extracts a quid pro quo, contrasted with God who only gives.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/what-does-god-ask-of-us|What Does God Ask of Us?]]
- **The golden calf** _(biblical)_ — Israel's well-intentioned attempt to honor God by 'doing something' becomes idolatry, showing we need not feel useful to be loved.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-should-we-do-to-be-saved|What Should We Do to Be Saved?]]
- **The Golden Calf at Sinai** _(biblical)_ — Impatient Israelites worship the messenger instead of the message, the pattern of idolatry.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **The golden calf comparison** _(biblical)_ — Abraham and Hagar likened to the golden calf as humanity's well-intentioned attempt to follow God on its own terms.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/hagar-sarah-and-the-birth-of-a-people|Hagar, Sarah, and the Birth of a People]]
- **The good tree and its fruit** _(biblical)_ — A tree is known by its fruit, not its roots, illustrating that we are known by what we do, by grace bearing fruit even from a broken root system.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i|Who Am I?]]
- **The gospel 'hinge' moments toward Jerusalem** _(biblical)_ — Each gospel has a turning point where Jerusalem and Golgotha pull Jesus back  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **The Gospel of Mark's many 'ands'** _(literary)_ — A literal Mark would have three times as many 'ands'; English translators smooth them out, losing the text's character, as also happens in Genesis.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]]
- **The Gospel Spreading from Paul to Spicewood** _(historical)_ — Paul carried Jesus across the Mediterranean and Thomas to India, and the same story has now come to Spicewood, showing the new song reaching the whole world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/half-a-world-away|Half a World Away]]
- **The grafting into Israel and the church's persecution of the Jews** _(historical)_ — Gentiles grafted into Israel yet turning on the Jewish people with persecution and mass murder, a confession of the church's anti-Judaism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-service-for-good-friday|A Service for Good Friday]]
- **The Grand Inquisitor in The Brothers Karamazov** _(literary)_ — Dostoevsky's poem frames the three temptations as miracle, mystery, and authority, the powers that hold human conscience captive.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/first-attempt-temptation-of-jesus|First Attempt (Temptation of Jesus)]]
- **The great chain of believers** _(other)_ — An extended metaphor of an unbroken chain from Jesus and the apostles through the present room into the future, conveying the communion of saints.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **The harrowing of Hell (1 Peter 4, the Apostles' Creed)** _(biblical)_ — Christ's descent to preach to the dead is used to argue that even hell's population is not settled or triumphant over God's mercy.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **The healed man born blind getting a job** _(biblical)_ — The man healed in John 9 still had to find work afterward, showing miracles have an ordinary aftermath and don't stop at the story's end.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **The Hill Country / Robert Caro** _(historical)_ — The Texas Hill Country seeming too good to be true (per Robert Caro) as another example of a deal that isn't what it appears.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/god-s-love-a-deal-too-good-to-be-true|God's Love: A Deal Too Good to Be True]]
- **The Holy Club at Oxford** _(historical)_ — Charles and John's disciplined group living a Christian life shows calling and service preceding assurance  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **The Holy of Holies in the Jerusalem temple** _(biblical)_ — The innermost temple room entered only once a year shows what it means to be most set apart from the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **The Holy Spirit is not the Force in Star Wars** _(pop-culture)_ — God does not seek balance between good and evil, rejecting the dualism that treats wickedness as God's equal.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **The hymn 'In the Garden'** _(literary)_ — A mid-19th-century camp-meeting hymn quoted as lovely sentiment but with little theology or biblical connection.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **The Iliad opening on wrath** _(literary)_ — Homer's epic begins with the word for wrath, showing how central anger was to the ancient Greek imagination versus its near-absence in scripture.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-foolishness-of-the-cross-jesus-cleanses-the-temple|The Foolishness of the Cross: Jesus Cleanses the Temple]]
- **The Inner Game of Tennis / The Inner Game of Music** _(literary)_ — Barry Green's adaptation of the tennis book frames performance as potential minus interference, paralleling the life of faith and hope.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **The invention of jaywalking** _(historical)_ — Auto companies lobbied in the 1920s to criminalize jaywalking and shift liability to pedestrians, showing the world's arrangements are chosen, not inevitable.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **The investment-pitch conversation** _(personal)_ — Running into an old friend who shifts from talking about kids and travels to an investment opportunity shows how stakes change the moment money enters a conversation.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-and-see-the-trouble-come-and-start-rebuilding|Come and See the Trouble — Come and Start Rebuilding]]
- **The Konami Code / Contra cheat code** _(pop-culture)_ — The 30-lives cheat code from Contra (up-up-down-down-left-right-left-right-B-A-Start) as the central metaphor for treating God's grace as a way to escape consequences.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eternal-life|Eternal Life]]
- **The Konami code in Contra** _(pop-culture)_ — The Up-Up-Down-Down cheat granting 30 extra lives illustrates a faith that pretends grace removes all consequences for our actions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-do-we-hope-for|What do we hope for?]]
- **The Late Great Planet Earth by Hal Lindsey** _(literary)_ — The 1960s-70s bestseller mapping the Soviet Union and China onto Daniel illustrates modern misreadings of Revelation as end-times prediction.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/alpha-and-omega|Alpha and Omega]]
- **The least of these (Matthew 25)** _(biblical)_ — Christ identifying with the hungry, thirsty, stranger, naked, sick, and imprisoned to expose the church's neglect  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-service-for-good-friday|A Service for Good Friday]], A Service for Good Friday
- **The least of these (sheep and goats)** _(biblical)_ — Matthew 25's righteous who fed, clothed, and visited Jesus unawares model good soil as faith put into action.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **The Lego building downtown** _(current-events)_ — A downtown Austin building that steps up and to the side yet still rises straight, reinforcing the image of structural uprightness as justification.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/justification|Justification]]
- **The Little Drummer Boy** _(pop-culture)_ — An invented, out-of-place carol contrasted as the wrong kind of gift versus the gift of the heart.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **The lost sheep as addict or scoundrel** _(other)_ — The shepherd searches night after night even for a sheep that was an addict, abuser, or scoundrel, undeserving of rescue.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/being-sheep|Being Sheep]]
- **The lullaby with an agenda** _(personal)_ — Rocking a sleepless infant and singing the same lullaby what feels like 6,000 times illustrates how grace invites us simply to rest.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/go-to-sleep-isaac|Go to Sleep, Isaac]]
- **The Magi Travel from Far Away** _(biblical)_ — The magi came a long way (plural in Greek, not kings nor three) to show the Christ-story reaching across distance and prompting a new song.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/half-a-world-away|Half a World Away]]
- **The man born blind in John 9** _(biblical)_ — 'I was blind, but now I see' models revival as dead souls coming alive, not church-membership transfer.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **The manger as a dirty feed trough** _(biblical)_ — Animal feed not from Petco and saliva-filled trough underscores that Jesus' birth was one of the least hygienic moments in history.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-far-country-god-s-journey-to-us|The Far Country: God's Journey to Us]]
- **The Marathon of Life** _(other)_ — Being mid-marathon with miles still to go pictures the present struggle even as eternal rest lies ahead.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **The Marshmallow Test** _(historical)_ — The famous self-control experiment, since disproven, showing patience is a practiced habit rather than an innate predictor of success.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **The Martyrdom of Stephen** _(biblical)_ — Stephen joyfully proclaiming Jesus and praying for his executioners while being stoned models a Spirit-formed faith worth striving toward.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **The meat grinder of existence** _(other)_ — extended metaphor for a cruel world that will not stop smashing the rejected until God acts to make all well  → [[covenant-umc/2025/extravagant-faith-mary-s-anointing-and-the-gift-of-the-mitzvah|Extravagant Faith: Mary's Anointing and the Gift of the Mitzvah]]
- **The mind-changing meme photo** _(pop-culture)_ — A meme-ified photo underscores how genuinely hard it is to change anyone's mind by logic alone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **The Mismatched Roommate** _(other)_ — A tidy person gaining a roommate who never cooks or cleans, showing that life together is not about efficiency or 50/50 fairness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **The Moon Landing** _(historical)_ — Older listeners' vivid memory of TVs wheeled into school for a man on the moon, framing wonder at a human moving through space toward the question of where Jesus ascended.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-ascended-into-heaven|He Ascended into Heaven]]
- **The Music Man's 'Trouble in River City'** _(pop-culture)_ — The sermon title alludes to the musical's 'trouble' to frame the Holy Spirit as the bringer of good, disruptive trouble.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **The mustard seed growing enormous** _(biblical)_ — Like the tiny mustard seed in Mark, the small bread and juice of communion is God doing something great that will grow, so feeling insignificant does not define who you are.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-sits-upon-the-throne|Who Sits Upon the Throne]]
- **The nearly-chosen music performance major** _(personal)_ — Having the forms signed to switch to a music major was a crossroads where his life could have gone another way.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **The newborn as bundle of needs** _(nature)_ — A newborn who can do no work, fight no battles, or play football pictures the vulnerability God chose in the Incarnation.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **The newborn infant as a bundle of need** _(nature)_ — Newborns never clean the kitchen, make messes, and wake everyone at all hours; they are pure neediness, which is how God enters the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/a-bundle-of-need|A Bundle of Need]]
- **The objective Martian observer** _(other)_ — An alien judging you only by what you actually do illustrates that identity is revealed by deeds, not excuses or origins.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/who-am-i|Who Am I?]]
- **The Office 'Corporate needs you to tell the difference' meme** _(pop-culture)_ — The identical-pictures meme opens the point that distinctions matter even when things look alike.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **The Passover Four Questions (Haggadah)** _(historical)_ — The youngest at the Seder asks 'Why is this night different?'; the Advent sermon series reframes it as 'Why is this season different?'  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-four-questions-who-is-coming|The Four Questions: Who Is Coming?]]
- **The pastor's evolving planning tools** _(personal)_ — His cycle through productivity, bullet, and atomic planners down to a Field Notes book illustrates how we plan around our identity and that our plans won't fit the new creation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/new-creation|New Creation]]
- **The pediatrician's growth-percentile chart** _(personal)_ — Checking a child's height/weight percentile is one of the everyday 'signs' we anxiously seek about our lives.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **The poker adage about the sucker at the table** _(other)_ — 'If you can't point out the sucker, you are the sucker' frames the worldly fear of being deceived that love overturns.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-believes-all|Love Believes All]]
- **The potter mashing down imperfect clay to start again** _(biblical)_ — being mashed down and remade is uncomfortable for the clay but is a mercy of God  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-mercy-of-fire|The Mercy of Fire]]
- **The preacher's own dog and cat at peace** _(personal)_ — A current household where a dog and cat enjoy each other models the rare familiarity and peace pointing toward Isaiah's vision.  → Pet Blessing
- **The preacher's recent non-milestone birthday** _(personal)_ — his own ordinary birthday opens the riff on how we misuse 'milestone' to mean only major life events  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **The pressure to be perfect** _(personal)_ — The burden of perfect scores, schools, jobs, and spouses, contrasted with the biblical teleios meaning completed rather than mistake-free.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/glorification|Glorification]]
- **The principal as 'prince-apal'** _(personal)_ — Grade-school memory of a regimented day controlled by the 'prince-apal,' picturing life without free will.  → Drawn by the Father
- **The professional who won't think hard at church** _(personal)_ — People with demanding careers in engineering or business come to church not wanting to think, treating worship as a feel-good boost rather than loving attention to God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **The promised large gift not yet received** _(personal)_ — Not counting on a large promised gift until it actually arrives parallels Thomas's reasonable doubt about a resurrection claim he hasn't yet seen.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-and-see-the-trouble-come-and-start-rebuilding|Come and See the Trouble — Come and Start Rebuilding]]
- **The Quirinius census problem in Luke** _(historical)_ — The mismatch between Luke's birth narrative and Roman records (Herod, Quirinius, Augustus, the census) shows faith rests not on one-to-one verification but on the resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **The Quran as dictation** _(other)_ — Islam's view of the Quran dictated by Gabriel illustrates the first error: reading the Bible as dictated rather than inspired.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **The Reproaches: Christ's Lament Against His Faithless Church** _(literary)_ — Ancient liturgical text voiced by Christ recounting God's saving acts (Exodus, manna, vineyard) answered by the church's betrayal, indicting the gathered church's faithlessness  → [[covenant-umc/2025/a-service-for-good-friday|A Service for Good Friday]], A Service for Good Friday
- **The Rescue (2018 Thai cave documentary)** _(pop-culture)_ — The boys must be anesthetized to be rescued, illustrating that salvation cannot come from inside the cave or from our own effort.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-should-we-do-to-be-saved|What Should We Do to Be Saved?]]
- **The Rescue (Thai cave rescue documentary)** _(current-events)_ — The 2018 Thai cave rescue of a trapped soccer team illustrates humanity's concrete need to be saved and inability to save itself.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-salvation|What is Salvation?]]
- **The Rescue / Thai Cave Disaster** _(current-events)_ — The 2018 Thai cave rescue documentary clip (boys trapped, divers searching) frames salvation as being trapped and lost and needing help to be free.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/where-are-we|Where Are We]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **The Rescue documentary final clip** _(pop-culture)_ — The Thai cave rescue's last and smallest boy, whose standard mask didn't fit, shows rescuers refusing to leave anyone behind.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **The reverse parable of the sower** _(other)_ — Extended original metaphor: instead of scattering seed indiscriminately, the church now hoards it for fear of the road or the bush.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **The Rich Fool's Bigger Barns** _(biblical)_ — The landowner who tore down barns to build bigger ones embodies the prideful, self-satisfied earthly mind Paul warns against.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **The rich man and his storage units** _(biblical)_ — Retelling of the parable of the rich fool who keeps buying storage units only to lose his life that night, showing things cannot save the soul.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/take-up-your-cross|Take Up Your Cross]]
- **The Rich Young Ruler** _(biblical)_ — The pious man who kept the commandments but walked away sad rather than sell his possessions, showing a sacred story that brackets off generosity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]], [[covenant-umc/2023/the-next-thirty-seconds|The Next Thirty Seconds]], [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]], [[covenant-umc/2024/not-easy-to-watch|Not Easy to Watch]], [[covenant-umc/2024/screening-god-s-call|Screening God's Call]]
- **The Rich Young Ruler** _(biblical)_ — The rich young ruler of Luke 18 contrasts the grand dramatic gesture with the day-by-day generosity of Acts, and warns against putting limits on what we give God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story|How to Live Our Story]]
- **The Richly Laden Table and Fatted Calf** _(biblical)_ — The Easter feast spread for fasters and non-fasters alike, illustrating the abundance and inclusivity of resurrection joy.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/easter-sunrise-service-the-paschal-homily-of-st-john-chrysostom|Easter Sunrise Service: The Paschal Homily of St. John Chrysostom]]
- **The Scouring of the Shire in Lord of the Rings** _(literary)_ — Tolkien ends not at Mount Doom but in the aftermath in the Shire, showing that even righteous victory leaves consequences that can't be tied in a neat bow.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/get-ready-for-something-big|Get Ready for Something Big]]
- **The seashell window symbol for baptism** _(other)_ — The seashell in the stained glass marks baptism and invites remembrance of one's own baptism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/baptism|Baptism]]
- **The Ship of Theseus (Plutarch)** _(literary)_ — Plutarch's puzzle of the rebuilt ship dramatizes the substance-versus-accident question underlying transubstantiation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/do-this|Do this!]], [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **The smudged iota** _(historical)_ — The smallest Greek letter easily blurring into alpha or omega on old papyrus illustrates how something seemingly insignificant (an iota of the Law) still matters to Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **The South Park movie** _(pop-culture)_ — even secular comedy draws on demon imagery, showing its cultural saturation  → [[covenant-umc/2025/legion|Legion]]
- **The Spark of Friendship** _(personal)_ — The exciting spark of a new friendship doesn't last on its own, illustrating that initial feeling must give way to sustaining fuel.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/keeping-the-fire-of-love-burning|Keeping the Fire of Love Burning]]
- **The spiritual 'If you cannot preach like Peter'** _(literary)_ — The old spiritual that you can still tell the love of Jesus even if you can't preach like Peter or pray like Paul illustrates that we need not be as holy as Job.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-wager-part-2|The Wager (Part 2)]]
- **The stained glass Bible window** _(personal)_ — The church's stained glass window reminds the congregation the Bible is a gift to be received with humility.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]]
- **The Tax Collector** _(biblical)_ — The tax collector who offered humility instead of good deeds and was relieved of his sins, illustrating that a humble life takes sin away.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/where-two-or-three|Where two or three…]]
- **The Ten Commandments film** _(pop-culture)_ — Most of the movie precedes Exodus 3, showing how the burning-bush moment is the essential preface the film only sets up.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-communion-of-the-saints|I Believe in the Communion of the Saints]]
- **The Tetragrammaton and Hashem at the Bar** _(historical)_ — Jews never speak the four-letter name, saying Adonai in prayer or 'the name' (Hashem) in casual talk, illustrating the holiness of God's name.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **The thief on the cross** _(biblical)_ — praying before knowing Jesus, showing prayer as an act of faith in someone beyond yourself  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **The Thomas Jefferson Bible** _(historical)_ — Jefferson cutting out gospel parts he disliked illustrates the 'Bible Buffet' à la carte error.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-bible|The Bible]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-power-unit|The Power Unit]]
- **The Trust Clause and Methodist Splinter Groups** _(historical)_ — The 1796 US trust clause (church property held in common trust across the connection) distinguishes UMC churches from breakaway groups like the Free Methodists, Wesleyans, and Nazarenes, illustrating Methodism's real history beyond mere respectability.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **The two-trombonists audition cartoon** _(pop-culture)_ — 'The person next to you at an audition has practiced an hour more every day of their life' — the warning that turned him from the music life.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **The Upper Room devotional resources** _(current-events)_ — Online pandemic prayer resources offered as a practical entry into contemplation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/love-neighbor|Love Neighbor]]
- **The Wall of Being Decent** _(other)_ — Everyone of any religion hits a wall just trying to be a good person, which Christian discipleship is meant to surpass.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]]
- **The warm-up act and the headliner** _(other)_ — No one goes to a show for the warm-up; John the Baptist is the warm-up act who readies the crowd for the headliner, Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-four-questions-who-is-coming|The Four Questions: Who Is Coming?]]
- **The Waterboy (1990s film)** _(pop-culture)_ — Adam Sandler's Louisiana hero channeling anger into football illustrates modern culture's positive portrayal of anger.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-foolishness-of-the-cross-jesus-cleanses-the-temple|The Foolishness of the Cross: Jesus Cleanses the Temple]]
- **The whirlpool of what-ifs in work, marriage, and grief** _(personal)_ — Suffocating 'what if I had done this' counterfactuals over jobs, marriage, and loss that Christ's love releases us from.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-rescue|The Rescue]]
- **The Whole-Brain Child / Name It to Tame It** _(literary)_ — Parenting book's storytelling technique for calming a child illustrates how telling our story integrates and heals the brain.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **The widow's mite** _(biblical)_ — The widow's two pennies, given not in competition with the loud Pharisees, model giving as venerating God rather than winning.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/last-place|Last Place]]
- **The widow's two coins** _(biblical)_ — The woman giving her two coins out of joy gives more than the rich out of abundance, showing sacrificial giving as habit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **The Widow's Two Mites** _(biblical)_ — The widow who put her two mites in the box, proving poverty is no obstacle to the path of repentance through almsgiving.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/where-two-or-three|Where two or three…]]
- **The woman with the sick daughter clinging to Jesus** _(biblical)_ — Her persistence in not letting go, resulting in healing, parallels Jacob's refusal to release the angel.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **The word for "dog" across languages** _(other)_ — English dog vs. Greek, Hebrew kelev, French chien, Chinese gou, German Hund show names differ by language yet point to one reality, illustrating the mystery of Adam's naming.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/god-told-adam-and-eve|God Told Adam and Eve]]
- **The world's media versus the narrow way** _(other)_ — Modern media saying 'be happy, take it easy, follow the broad way' contrasted with Christ's call to the narrow way of struggle.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **The Worldly Philosophers** _(literary)_ — Heilbroner's book on Ricardo, Malthus, and Adam Smith sparked his interest in economics.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-the-forgiveness-of-sins|I Believe in the Forgiveness of Sins]]
- **The Worldly Philosophers book** _(literary)_ — Reading this history of economics led him to major in economics as a practical way to study philosophy.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/drop-your-nets|Drop Your Nets]]
- **The youngest brother who never lifted a sword** _(literary)_ — The unlikely youngest son out in the fields who looks powerless, illustrating that God chooses what appears weak.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/spicy-mustard|Spicy Mustard]]
- **Theologian on Christ standing with sinners** _(literary)_ — A quoted theologian (Barth) describes Christ joining the evil generation in John's baptism not for his own sake but for ours.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-from-sin-in-the-waters-of-baptism|Freedom from Sin in the Waters of Baptism]]
- **Theologian on God as no bargain** _(literary)_ — A theologian's quote that Jesus assaults the idea of a transactional relationship with God, to whom we give everything and from whom we only receive with gratitude.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-foolishness|Restless with Foolishness]]
- **There Was No Year Zero / Calendar History** _(historical)_ — The AD/BC dating system (coined by Dionysius Exiguus in the 6th c. and BC by Werner Rolevinck in 1474) wasn't built on historical evidence of Jesus's birth, undercutting the assumption that the calendar pins down the nativity.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **Theudas and the Roman crackdown** _(historical)_ — Rome sent troops to behead Theudas for leading a crowd, contrasting with Jesus' entry that provoked no Roman panic.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/palm-sunday-practicing-living|Palm Sunday: Practicing Living]]
- **Thief in the night** _(biblical)_ — Christ's coming arrives unexpectedly like a thief, so Advent is a season of staying ready.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/mercy-revealed|Mercy Revealed]]
- **Things Around the House** _(other)_ — Just as living in a home means doing chores like dishes and cleaning, abiding in God's house means practicing the means of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/learning-to-abide-the-means-of-grace|Learning to Abide: The Means of Grace]]
- **Things we do when nobody is watching** _(personal)_ — Unhygienic private habits (nose-picking, etc.) frame the question of whether we are more or less ourselves when unobserved, like the women coming to the tomb alone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/easter|Easter]]
- **Think of the worst thing you've ever done** _(personal)_ — An invitation to recall one's worst act, then receive the twist that God offers mercy and you are not your mistakes.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/mercy-revealed|Mercy Revealed]]
- **Third-Century Writer Against Celebrating Birthdays** _(historical)_ — An early Christian writer held it contrary to faith to celebrate birthdays, since Christians should mark baptism or martyrdom instead.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-cultivation-of-christmas-trees|The Cultivation of Christmas Trees]]
- **Thirsty little ones in Austin's summer** _(nature)_ — Austin's drying lake and summer heat make the 'cup of cold water' literal and a sign of thirst for the water of life.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/welcome|Welcome]]
- **Thirty Years War and belief-based religion** _(historical)_ — Post-war 17th-18th c. Protestant framing redefined religion as private belief rather than practice, sidelining practices like Sabbath.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-voice-of-sabbath|The Voice of Sabbath]]
- **This is fine dog meme** _(pop-culture)_ — The dog sitting in a burning building denying danger pictures our refusal to admit our need to be saved.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-salvation|What is Salvation?]]
- **This Little Light of Mine** _(pop-culture)_ — Kids can sing it but not yet make the metaphorical leap to being light themselves.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/symbols-and-signs|Symbols and Signs]]
- **Thomas Aquinas and the faithful old woman** _(historical)_ — Aquinas said his vast theology was just an attempt to write down what the faithful old woman already knows in her heart, illustrating that faith is not measured by books read.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Thomas Aquinas's four conditions of hope** _(historical)_ — The medieval philosopher's criteria (good, future, difficult, possible) are helpful but insufficient because hope rests on the living God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/living-hope|Living Hope]]
- **Thomas Aquinas's four conditions of hope** _(historical)_ — Aquinas defines hope as a future, arduous, possible good, a helpful but ultimately unsatisfying definition.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/what-is-hope|What is Hope?]]
- **Thomas Malthus and the East India Company famines** _(historical)_ — Malthusian scarcity logic and the El Niño famines that killed ~30 million Indians while cotton shipped to England show how humans decide who deserves to live or die.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-gets-to-be-saved|Who Gets to Be Saved]]
- **Thomas Mann's Joseph novels** _(literary)_ — Notes Mann wrote four enormous novels on the Joseph story, underscoring its narrative weight and strangeness.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Thomas seeing the scars** _(biblical)_ — The risen Christ's scars, seen by Thomas, show the past is not erased but healed in resurrection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Thor and Zeus** _(pop-culture)_ — Pagan gods who would never let themselves be killed, highlighting the scandal that the Christian God does.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Three components of fire** _(nature)_ — Fire needs fuel, oxygen, and a spark (the spark can come from another candle) as an image of how light is kindled and shared.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **Three hours, three days, three weeks rule** _(other)_ — The survival rule of thumb (3 hrs without shelter, 3 days without water, 3 weeks without food) frames the whole sermon series order.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/shelter|Shelter]]
- **Three-page Airbnb rules list** _(personal)_ — A long list of prohibitions doesn't feel like freedom, illustrating how rules can be experienced as burden rather than gift.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Thunder Bay and English variation** _(personal)_ — Different English across countries (e.g. Thunder Bay) shows how each person's story has its own grammar and vocabulary.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]]
- **TikTok 'How Often Do You Think About the Roman Empire' Trend** _(pop-culture)_ — The viral trend of men thinking about the Roman Empire daily (the preacher admits he would too) is a hook for taking the Roman office of consul seriously.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/christmas-eve-god-enters-history|Christmas Eve: God Enters History]]
- **TikTok subtitle context clues** _(pop-culture)_ — Modern readers use context clues to decode unknown words, a luxury unavailable for words that appear only once in ancient Hebrew.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/what-s-next-builders-and-names|What's Next? (Builders and Names)]]
- **Tilling the soil for costly fruit** _(nature)_ — Being tilled by God is costly but trades weeds and easy plants for fruit, the only way to bear a harvest.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Time machine to see Jesus** _(other)_ — Wishing to take one's kids in a time machine to see Jesus so they'd believe, illustrating the longing to see rather than believe unseen.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-concrete-faith-of-thomas|The Concrete Faith of Thomas]]
- **Time Magazine 'Is God Dead?' cover** _(historical)_ — The 1966 Time cover that drew record letters, used to frame the death-of-God question that the Christian faith actually places at its center.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Tipping off the other team at a UT-A&M game** _(other)_ — the Pharisees warning Jesus about Herod feels like an opponent revealing the next play, showing how surprising their help is  → [[covenant-umc/2025/under-the-wing|Under the Wing]]
- **Tolkien and the Battle of the Somme** _(historical)_ — WWI losses behind The Hobbit explain why Tolkien made a small hobbit, not a warrior, the hero  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Tom Hanks and the pickle jar** _(pop-culture)_ — SNL Celebrity Jeopardy sketch where Tom Hanks won't let go of the pickle and stays stuck in the jar, illustrating the comforts we refuse to release.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/walking-along|Walking Along]]
- **Too Much Candy** _(personal)_ — Candy is fun but eating too much makes you sick, illustrating that worldly things can be abused, unlike God's love of which you can never have too much.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/you-can-never-have-too-much-love|You Can Never Have Too Much Love]]
- **Too proud to ask for help unloading groceries** _(personal)_ — Pride goes both ways: too proud to kneel for another and too proud to accept help carrying groceries.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Too proud to ask for help with groceries** _(personal)_ — Pride can keep you from accepting help unloading groceries or from offering it, mirroring the two-way sin of pride.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Too proud to unload the groceries** _(personal)_ — Being too proud to ask for help carrying groceries or to offer help shows how pride blocks mutual service.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/maundy-thursday|Maundy Thursday]]
- **Total darkness in Carlsbad Caverns** _(personal)_ — On a tour the guides cut all the lights and he couldn't see his hand in front of his face, illustrating darkness as the genuine absence of light.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/creation-from-nothing-genesis-1-and-john-1-together|Creation from Nothing: Genesis 1 and John 1 Together]]
- **Touching the hot stove** _(personal)_ — Painful memories keep us from repeating mistakes, showing why memory of the bad matters too  → [[covenant-umc/2025/remember-and-be-thankful|Remember and Be Thankful]]
- **Touching the hot stove** _(personal)_ — Don't stick your hand on the pot and get burned to learn there's danger; receive Jesus' word instead of learning the hard way.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/subterranean-hometown-blues|Subterranean Hometown Blues]]
- **Tower of Babel reversed** _(biblical)_ — Where Babel scattered people and confused their languages, Pentecost draws people together to understand each other through the Spirit.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **Tower of Babel reversed at Pentecost** _(biblical)_ — Babel scattered one-language people for their vanity; Pentecost gathers many-language people to build a temple of the heart.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Traffic on 620 after school lets out** _(personal)_ — Everyday traffic contrasts with true survival stakes to show not all fears carry equal weight.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **Trail Lite Reduces the Shoulder** _(personal)_ — A fellow hiker whose only credential was a doctor dad and an article he'd read popped the shoulder back in, illustrating both desperation in suffering and the help that comes from others.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-suffering|Restless with Suffering]]
- **Trail manners with the boys** _(personal)_ — Teaching his sons to be courteous on the trail and step aside for others models the mutual space-making that forgiveness requires.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Transfiguration faith vs the bleeding woman's faith** _(biblical)_ — Peter and John needed little faith on the mountaintop where Jesus shone, but the woman who touched Jesus' tunic showed great faith by believing when it was neither easy nor obvious.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Transitive vs Intransitive Verbs** _(other)_ — Grammar of 'saves' as a transitive verb shows we are saved from and for something specific, not generally.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/binding-the-strongman|Binding the Strongman]]
- **Transitive vs intransitive verbs (walk, open, play)** _(other)_ — grammar lesson distinguishing intercessory prayer (transitive) from contemplative prayer (intransitive)  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Translucent Ghosts in TV and Movies** _(pop-culture)_ — White or semi-translucent ghosts on screen versus invisible ghosts in books illustrate how non-material beings defy physical laws, present yet unseen.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/credo-i-believe-in-the-holy-spirit|Credo: I Believe in the Holy Spirit]]
- **Transparent Substances in Sunlight** _(nature)_ — Basil's analogy of clear objects shining when sunlight falls on them, picturing souls made radiant and grace-bearing by the indwelling Spirit.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/a-burning-sensation-summit-fever-a-pentecost-sermon|A Burning Sensation (Summit Fever: A Pentecost Sermon)]]
- **Traveling Hungary with Alina translating** _(personal)_ — Visiting Hungary with Alina, who could translate, shows how radically different it is to be accompanied by someone who speaks the language — like being accompanied in our story.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Traveling salesmen and snake oil** _(historical)_ — The history of traveling salesmen, snake oil peddlers, and US evangelism as examples of deceivers selling false goods.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/advent-don-t-be-deceived|Advent: Don't Be Deceived]]
- **Treasure at the End of the Rainbow** _(pop-culture)_ — A hollow treasure goal, like the pot at the rainbow's end, that keeps us moving toward nothing lasting.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Treasure in clay pots** _(biblical)_ — Paul's image of bodies as fragile clay pots that crumble to dust yet will be remade gloriously.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Tree rhymes with bee, arbre with marbre** _(literary)_ — differing rhymes across languages illustrate how stylistic norms make translation imperfect  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-crown-of-salvation|The Crown of Salvation]]
- **Trendy diets, workouts, churches, and investments** _(current-events)_ — Paleo/keto/crossfit/pilates, trendy churches like Summit and Soma, and chasing 'the next Apple/Tesla/Uber' illustrate our craving for the next big thing instead of the eternal God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/not-a-new-commandment-god-s-love-is-old-not-trendy|Not a New Commandment: God's Love Is Old, Not Trendy]]
- **Tribes of Israel competing in Joshua** _(biblical)_ — After entering the promised land the tribes fought and pined for the good old days, a picture of post-success church conflict.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Trojan horse / ransom to the devil** _(literary)_ — The devil thinks he gains a holy man to control but gets God in flesh and is defeated, illustrating Christus Victor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **True crime podcast confession** _(pop-culture)_ — The killer confessing when caught illustrates the narrow, after-the-fact view of confession Lent corrects.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **Trunk or Treat** _(personal)_ — Brenda's well-attended October trunk-or-treat event that Wilson and his kids loved, cited as fruit of her hard work.  → October Pastor Report
- **Trusting a person with your children's lives** _(other)_ — Saying you trust someone versus putting your children in their hands illustrates the difference between believing a story and living it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/holy-conferencing|Holy Conferencing]]
- **Trusting doctors to declare death** _(personal)_ — We accept reports of death on the authority of doctors and family, showing that even declaring death is an act of faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/was-crucified-died-and-was-buried|Was Crucified, Died, and Was Buried]]
- **Trusting the Boat's Captain** _(other)_ — Trusting a captain who knows the destination even if it isn't where you expected captures the risk and trust of faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]]
- **Trying to drive last year's model** _(other)_ — The church tries to drive an outdated car not equipped for today's roads, though Jesus and the resurrection are timeless.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Tucker at the bus stop in UT gear** _(personal)_ — Meeting her grandson Tucker off the bus every afternoon in UT clothing illustrates her devotion to family and her Longhorn pride.  → Glynis Frye Memorial
- **Turkey-induced nap** _(nature)_ — A big turkey dinner making you sleepy illustrates obvious dietary consequences versus the hidden consequences of spiritual food.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/food|Food]]
- **Turtles All the Way Down (John Green novel)** _(literary)_ — Green's novel about grief and mental illness is invoked as another resonance of the 'turtles' phrase, connecting the cosmic question to lived emotional pain.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Turtles All the Way Down (William James anecdote)** _(literary)_ — The apocryphal William James exchange with an old lady claiming the earth rests on turtles 'all the way down' illustrates infinite regress and whether a flat or God-sustained reality answers the question of origins.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-service-is-the-world-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve Service: Is the World Flat or Overflowing?]], [[bee-creek-umc/2022/christmas-eve-is-reality-flat-or-overflowing|Christmas Eve: Is Reality Flat or Overflowing?]]
- **Twelve-step first step / Bill Wilson and AA** _(historical)_ — Admitting powerlessness is a step of transformation; AA's roots trace through the Oxford Groups and Keswick holiness Methodism  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **Two kids told to empty the dishwasher and care for each other** _(personal)_ — Picking the clear task over the vague one shows the temptation to do the defensible thing rather than the loving thing.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/sabbath-and-prayer|Sabbath and Prayer]]
- **Two Teachers in the Darkness** _(other)_ — Two teachers in the dark, one needing external light and the other being light itself, contrasts ordinary guidance with Jesus who is the light.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/animals-and-god-s-promise-children-s-message-the-true-light-already-shines|Animals and God's Promise (Children's Message): The True Light Already Shines]]
- **Two-Edged Sword of the Word** _(biblical)_ — Hebrews' two-edged sword cuts through the costumes society tells us to wear.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-my-sacred-story|What Is My Sacred Story?]]
- **Two-Face in The Dark Knight** _(pop-culture)_ — 'You either die a hero, or you live long enough to see yourself become the villain' shows that becoming an antichrist can happen to any of us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/antichrist-becoming-the-villain|Antichrist: Becoming the Villain]]
- **TwoSetViolin's mythical LingLing** _(pop-culture)_ — The joke about the over-practicing prodigy LingLing mocks the relentless drive to give up everything for success.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacrifice|Sacrifice]]
- **Tyconius and Augustine's reading of Revelation** _(historical)_ — Tyconius and Augustine established reading Revelation as a vision of the church's struggle throughout the present age, ending only at resurrection and last judgment.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/revelation-notes|Revelation Notes]]
- **U2 'I Still Haven't Found What I'm Looking For'** _(pop-culture)_ — Lyrics about climbing mountains and scaling walls illustrate the restless search that is only satisfied when we find Jesus.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/take-up-your-cross|Take Up Your Cross]]
- **Ukraine Kharkov offensive** _(current-events)_ — The 2022 Ukrainian breakthrough and salient against Russian lines shows military language is continuous across centuries, grounding Jesus' suffering in real concrete history.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-suffered-under-pontius-pilate|Who Suffered Under Pontius Pilate]]
- **UMC baptism liturgy 'when nothing existed but chaos'** _(literary)_ — The baptismal prayer ties the waters of baptism back to the waters of creation.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **UMC clergy birthday group chat** _(personal)_ — Pastor recalls a birthday group chat with UMC clergy enthusing about the virgin birth, rebutting claims Methodists deny it.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/who-was-conceived-from-the-holy-spirit-and-born-of-the-virgin-mary|Who was conceived from the Holy Spirit and born of the Virgin Mary]]
- **UMC membership vows** _(other)_ — The membership questions ('will you be loyal... by your prayers, presence, gifts, service and witness') echo baptismal language, framing church-joining as a worthwhile detour.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **Underpaid US shot putter sponsored by Flavor Flav** _(current-events)_ — An Olympian who couldn't make rent until Flavor Flav covered it, versus rich stars, shows athletes depend on a supporting team.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-cup|The Cup]]
- **Unequally yoked at church camp** _(personal)_ — A Paul-class anecdote about 2 Corinthians 6 being misread at church camp as a rule against dating outside your youth group, showing how scripture gets bent toward relationships.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/if-you-fall-down|If You Fall Down]]
- **Unused road bike in the garage** _(personal)_ — The bike bought and ridden only twice illustrates that prayer is a habit that must be practiced, not stored away.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless|Restless]]
- **Unwritten manuscript on food and dieting in the Bible** _(personal)_ — His never-edited book project that concluded there is no Jesus diet or one weird trick to lose weight.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/opened-eyes|Opened Eyes]]
- **Updike's Seven Stanzas at Easter** _(literary)_ — Updike's poem insists Christ rose bodily—molecule reknit, amino acids rekindled—or the Church will fall.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/on-the-third-day-he-rose-again|On the Third Day, He Rose Again]]
- **UT basketball superfan and camping out** _(personal)_ — His stubborn devotion to UT basketball (camping out for seats, riding to Denver) shows being part of something meaningful but not sacred, contrasted with church membership not being fanaticism.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/what-is-our-sacred-story|What is our sacred story?]]
- **UT coffee-shop and message-board debates** _(personal)_ — College obsession arguing free will vs. determinism, framing the sermon's central question.  → Drawn by the Father
- **UT English major and poetry workshop** _(personal)_ — A substitute poetry instructor's rule that only the text on the page matters taught Wilson to let the Bible speak for itself (exegesis over eisegesis).  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-and-see|Come and See]]
- **UT football and patience** _(personal)_ — Glynis's love of UT football, which 'has taken a lot of patience over the last decade,' illustrates the patience that love of children and family requires.  → Glynis Frye Memorial
- **UT football championship run** _(current-events)_ — A playful aside about Texas's national championship hopes contrasts full football gear with flag-football's lighter equipment.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Verrocchio and Leonardo's Baptism of Christ** _(historical)_ — Renaissance realism and naturalism entering portrayals of Jesus' body  → [[covenant-umc/2025/if-jesus-drew-a-portrait-of-you|If Jesus Drew a Portrait of You]]
- **Verses Not on a Hobby Lobby Sign** _(current-events)_ — The Bible's unsettling verses won't be found decorating a kitchen wall; we must wrestle with them.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/go-from-your-country|Go From Your Country]]
- **Vessel versus dam in ministry** _(other)_ — Being a vessel that lets God flow rather than a dam that blocks love depicts the minister's true role and human nature's urge for comfort.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-abides|Love Abides]]
- **Vestige as Latin for footprints** _(other)_ — We see footprints of God in creation, but creation is not God.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/distinctions-message-and-messenger-in-creation|Distinctions: Message and Messenger in Creation]]
- **Vinegar and gall, the pierced side** _(biblical)_ — The vineyard branches given the water of salvation respond with vinegar, gall, and a spear-thrust to Christ's side.  → A Service for Good Friday
- **Visiting Google and Apple headquarters** _(personal)_ — The family toured Google and Apple only because they were convenient, unlike the deliberate detour pilgrims made to reach John.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/waters-above-waters-below|Waters Above, Waters Below]]
- **Visiting people in hospice care** _(personal)_ — Hospice patients reduced to small actions are not limited in faith, prayer, or humanity as children of God.  → Every Act of Faith
- **Volunteering Sue for health phone calls** _(personal)_ — The pastor volunteers a congregant, Sue, as a contact for advice on physical health, making the 'do something' call concrete.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]], [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **Von Balthasar on contemplation as looking inward toward God** _(literary)_ — a teacher's words framing contemplation as finding God and discovering oneself in him  → [[covenant-umc/2025/prayer|Prayer]]
- **Waiting for Friends Who Are Late** _(personal)_ — You only wait for friends who haven't arrived because you trust they're coming, showing waiting is itself an act of faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/waiting|Waiting]]
- **Walk to Emmaus** _(biblical)_ — Invoked as the model of full conversion and encounter that disciples wish to stay within forever.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Walk to Emmaus / midwinter moment** _(other)_ — Comparisons for the preeminent mountaintop conversion experience the disciples wanted to stay in.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Walking away from a car wreck unscathed** _(other)_ — Crediting God for surviving a crash because you have no other explanation exemplifies the impersonal deist 'God of the gaps' rather than the present Holy Spirit.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/pentecost|Pentecost]]
- **Walking onstage alone at UIL orchestra** _(personal)_ — the preacher's high-school humiliation of walking out with his bass to an empty stage before 300 people, illustrating how humiliation is busted expectations plus shame  → [[covenant-umc/2025/under-the-wing|Under the Wing]]
- **Walking the greenbelt trails** _(personal)_ — Walking trails like the Hamilton Greenbelt and Canyonlands, where paths cross and people bump into one another, reframes trespass as straying off our path into someone else's.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/forgive-us-our-trespasses|Forgive Us Our Trespasses]]
- **Walton family breeding a microscopic camel at A&M** _(other)_ — A hypothetical 40-year lab effort to breed a needle-sized camel parodies the rich exploiting loopholes to game Jesus' test.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/eye-of-the-camel|Eye of the Camel]]
- **Wanting to fast-forward through events** _(personal)_ — The wish to skip a party, job interview, or lunch to 'be in the future now' illustrates impatience with the present.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-our-future|Restless with Our Future]]
- **Warner Sallman's Head of Christ (1950)** _(historical)_ — The famous 1950 portrait with 500 million copies is held up as the image furthest from the biblical Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-jesus-christ-his-only-son-our-lord|I Believe in Jesus Christ, His Only Son, Our Lord]], [[covenant-umc/2023/who-is-christ|Who is Christ?]]
- **Washrooms in Thunder Bay, Canada** _(personal)_ — Searching a Thunder Bay Walmart for a 'restroom' that Canadians call a 'washroom' illustrates deceptive fluency — assuming shared understanding where differences hide.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Water filter on the Appalachian Trail** _(personal)_ — The preacher sent his water filter home and learned to read good vs. bad springs, illustrating discerning good vs. bad sources of spiritual water.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/water|Water]]
- **Waterhouse's 'Lady of Shalott' / Pre-Raphaelites** _(historical)_ — Background on the Pre-Raphaelite movement's rejection of Raphael and Michelangelo, situating Rossetti's artistic milieu.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/saying-yes-to-god|Saying Yes to God]]
- **Wayne's World — "We're not worthy!"** _(pop-culture)_ — Wayne and Garth bowing to Alice Cooper illustrates the natural sense of unworthiness in the presence of beauty.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/the-baptism-of-the-lord|The Baptism of the Lord]]
- **We Are Climbing Jacob's Ladder** _(literary)_ — The hymn referenced alongside Jacob's earlier ladder dream as the congregation's shared song memory.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/healing-wounds|Healing Wounds]]
- **We are Lazarus quotation** _(literary)_ — A teacher's words that the baptized are Lazarus, unbound from the shroud of death to live a dangerous Lazarus life of witness.  → Every Act of Faith
- **Wedding at Cana** _(biblical)_ — Jesus turning water into choice wine points to the abundant feast of wine awaiting us eternally with God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **Wedding ring as a sign of love** _(other)_ — A ring of wood or diamond shows the gift's value lies in being a sign of something deeper, like God's love.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-gift|The Gift]]
- **Wednesday morning Bible-in-a-Year small group** _(personal)_ — References the church's small group working through Kings and Chronicles as the source of the Jehoram example of persistent disobedience.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/come-down-don-t-delay|Come Down, Don't Delay]]
- **Weeds I do not like (Dr. Seuss riff)** _(literary)_ — A Green-Eggs-and-Ham-style litany ('not in my yard, not on a barge') opens the sermon to show weeds are low-effort, high-return, and not all growth is good.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **Wemby and a Spurs championship parade** _(personal)_ — Even a hoped-for Spurs title and San Antonio parade would fade like everything else, illustrating the unsatisfying transience of worldly markers.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/get-ready-for-something-big|Get Ready for Something Big]]
- **Wendell Berry and Wes Jackson** _(literary)_ — Seminary reading in American agrarian writers that shaped the preacher's thinking on land and faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/one-straw-revolution|One Straw Revolution]]
- **Wer weiss wie nahe mir mein Ende** _(literary)_ — The 17th-century German countess's stark hymn on mortality set by Bach for this Sunday's cantata, linking the vineyard parable to the inevitability of death and grace freely given.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/laborers-in-the-vineyard-grace-community-and-mortality|Laborers in the Vineyard: Grace, Community, and Mortality]]
- **Wesley and the singing Moravians in the storm** _(historical)_ — The Moravians calmly singing hymns amid a deadly storm gave Wesley a faith he wanted, sparking his small-group spirituality.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Wesley brothers' Holy Club and the 22 Questions** _(historical)_ — The Oxford Holy Club's daily self-examination questions illustrate the Methodist pursuit of holiness.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Wesley denying communion to Sarah Hopkey** _(historical)_ — Wesley's passive-aggressive refusal of communion to his former fiancee illustrates the sin of moralism and living in the past.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/restless-with-the-past|Restless with the Past]]
- **Wesley vs. Whitefield controversy of 1744** _(historical)_ — the predestination split and Moravian dispute frame Wesley's insight that grace is a growing thing, not finished at its start  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-reign-of-the-spirit|The Reign of the Spirit]]
- **Wesley's 'almost Christian'** _(historical)_ — The commandments don't even make us an 'almost Christian' in John Wesley's language  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-ten-commandments|The Ten Commandments]], [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]], [[covenant-umc/2024/pentecost|Pentecost]], [[covenant-umc/2024/eye-of-the-camel|Eye of the Camel]]
- **Wesley's 'Duty of Constant Communion'** _(historical)_ — Wesley's sermon argues for frequent communion because Jesus commands it and we meet God there.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Wesley's 'Metaphysical Sermons' and the Model Deed** _(historical)_ — Wesley's later sermons on angels, devils, and the nature of humanity were excluded from the 1760 Standard Sermons required by the Model Deed and instead published in the Arminian Magazine, so they've been dismissed for 248 years as non-doctrinal.  → Jacob's Ladder
- **Wesley's 1738 conviction about grace (Aldersgate)** _(historical)_ — Wesley's realization that holiness is not about what you do but about being fully loved by God grounds the message of grace.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/saints-and-sinners|Saints and Sinners]]
- **Wesley's Covenant Service and prayer** _(historical)_ — John Wesley's New Year Covenant Service, where people make a two-way covenant with God, models the mutual yielding the congregation prays together.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/arise-shine-the-light-of-epiphany-and-the-grafting-in-of-the-gentiles|Arise, Shine: The Light of Epiphany and the Grafting In of the Gentiles]]
- **Wesley's Duty of Constant Communion** _(historical)_ — Wesley's sermon answers why Methodists commune: because Jesus commands it and because it is good.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/this-teaching-is-difficult|This Teaching is Difficult]]
- **Wesley's edited Book of Common Prayer (1784)** _(historical)_ — Wesley sending his edited Anglican liturgy to the new American church illustrates the structured roots of Methodist worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Wesley's editing of the Book of Common Prayer** _(historical)_ — Wesley's quick pen cut the 39 Articles to 25 and shaped the 1784 Sunday Service, explaining why early American Methodists cut and later restored the descent clause.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/he-descended-into-the-dead|He Descended into the Dead]]
- **Wesley's failed mission to Georgia** _(historical)_ — His arrest and flight from Georgia shows that calling is not measured by external success  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-calling-and-the-called|The Calling and the Called]]
- **Wesley's General Rules riffing on Micah 6:8** _(historical)_ — Wesley's three rules map onto Micah's do justice / love mercy / walk humbly with God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/justice|Justice]]
