- **Wesley's heart strangely warmed** _(historical)_ — The same Spirit at Pentecost settled on Wesley and spread Methodism, and is still at work at Bee Creek today.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-church-in-the-past|The Church in the Past]]
- **Wesley's Primitive Physic** _(historical)_ — John Wesley's home health book and plain rules on air, food, and water show health as part of showing the light of Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body|Reset Your Body]]
- **Wesley's three General Rules** _(historical)_ — do no harm, do all the good you can, follow the ordinances of God as concrete guides for sanctified living  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **Wesleyan Covenant Prayer** _(historical)_ — The early-Methodist practice of gathering at the new year to recommit to God, recited together at January 2 worship.  → Notes from the Pastor: Resetting Yourself with God in the New Year
- **Wesleyan Covenant Service prayer** _(historical)_ — The 'I am no longer my own, but thine' covenant prayer models new-year recommitment that only makes sense beyond individualistic freedom.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **Wesleys as tired teenagers at Oxford** _(historical)_ — Charles Wesley and friends, fed up with hypocrisy, recruited older brother John, modeling living as if the gospel were true.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/whoever-is-not-against-us-denominations-control-and-how-you-follow-jesus|Whoever Is Not Against Us: Denominations, Control, and How You Follow Jesus]]
- **Wesleys preaching at the coal mines** _(historical)_ — John and Charles Wesley preaching before 6am shifts and organizing meetings illustrates uniting heart and head against complacent faith.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-a-new-covenant-for-a-new-year|Freedom: A New Covenant for a New Year]]
- **West Texas roads built for rare rain** _(personal)_ — Far West Texas highways are engineered around rainfall that comes only a few times a year, because storms hit fast and destroy roads not built for runoff just as the house on sand washes away.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **What have you done for me lately** _(other)_ — A sarcastic quote capturing how humans sneak works-righteousness in, which is not how God looks at us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/i-believe-in-the-resurrection-of-the-body-and-the-life-everlasting|I Believe in the Resurrection of the Body and the Life Everlasting]]
- **What Would Jesus Do (WWJD)** _(pop-culture)_ — a helpful but vague rule of faith, contrasted with Wesley's more concrete General Rules  → [[covenant-umc/2025/sanctification|Sanctification]]
- **What Would Jesus Do Bracelets** _(pop-culture)_ — The WWJD bracelets of the preacher's youth framed faith as ethical behavior rather than martyrdom-style discipleship.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/discipleship|Discipleship]]
- **When Harry Met Sally 'I'll have what they're having'** _(pop-culture)_ — Wesley wanting the Moravians' faith is like wanting what someone else is having.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Whiskey advent calendars starting December 1** _(current-events)_ — Commercial advent calendars ignore the real Advent (starting Nov 30) to stay shelf-stable, illustrating how Advent has been secularized.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **WHO drowning statistics for men** _(current-events)_ — Men drown at twice the rate of women due to riskier behavior, contrasting modern recklessness with the disciples' realistic fear of storms.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/be-still|Be Still]]
- **Why can't we see God? (the child's question)** _(personal)_ — The earnest questions children ask about seeing, knowing, and hearing God, which adults learn to ignore, frame the whole sermon.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/shining|Shining]]
- **Wild (the movie / Pacific Crest Trail)** _(pop-culture)_ — Reference to the film Wild's Pacific Crest Trail setting, contrasted with the harder-to-resupply Appalachian Trail, framing the rigor of his hike.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/love-covers-a-multitude-of-sins-2|Love Covers a Multitude of Sins]]
- **Wild apple tree in Virginia** _(personal)_ — Sitting for an hour eating fresh apples off a wild tree, illustrating fruitfulness experienced firsthand rather than as supermarket abstraction.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/fruitfulness|Fruitfulness]]
- **Wild berries, mushrooms, and field-dressing game** _(nature)_ — Unknown berries, mushrooms, and sick game animals can knock you out faster than bad water, illustrating how harmful spiritual food endangers the soul.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/food|Food]]
- **Wild cats larger than wild dogs** _(nature)_ — Though domesticated dogs outsize domesticated cats, lions and tigers dwarf the largest wolves, and wild predators never cohabit gracefully.  → Pet Blessing
- **Wild Kratts villain Polly Paver** _(pop-culture)_ — The PBS show's asphalt-loving villain who cheers Joni Mitchell's paved paradise illustrates the trampled-road soil where seed is pecked away.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/growth|Growth]]
- **William Morgan visiting prisoners** _(historical)_ — An Oxford student's prison visits became a core practice of the Holy Club, embodying concrete love of neighbor.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-holy-club|The Holy Club]]
- **William Seymour's conversion at Simpson Chapel** _(historical)_ — The Azusa Street Revival leader's 1895 conversion in a Methodist chapel links Methodism to Pentecostal worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **William Temple on original sin as empirically proven** _(historical)_ — The wartime Archbishop of Canterbury's line that original sin is the only empirically proven doctrine, seen in hearts and institutions alike.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/lost-found-lost|Lost & Found: Lost]], [[covenant-umc/2024/let-s-talk-about-the-devil|Let's Talk About the Devil]]
- **William Temple's empirical doctrine joke** _(historical)_ — Original sin is the only empirically observable Christian doctrine because we can see brokenness repeated everywhere  → [[covenant-umc/2025/original-sin|Original Sin]]
- **William Temple: original sin as the only empirical doctrine** _(historical)_ — Temple's WWII remark grounds original sin in the visible evil of the era  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **Wilson's call to ministry in Peru** _(personal)_ — Running up a sand hill outside Lima and feeling his ambitions turn to dust was the preacher's own Aldersgate moment of redirection.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/aldersgate|Aldersgate]]
- **Wilson's college and seminary acceptances** _(personal)_ — The relief of getting into UT Austin, seminary, and ordination illustrates our craving to be settled in an in-group.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/the-next-thirty-seconds|The Next Thirty Seconds]]
- **Wilson's mother saying life's not fair** _(personal)_ — His mother's refrain 'life's not fair, Wilson' captures the hard truth that gifts are not doled out equally.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/freedom-in-christ|Freedom in Christ]]
- **Wilson's own humbling early jiu-jitsu training** _(personal)_ — Getting armbarred/choked as a beginner illustrates the humility and long failure before any traction in a life of faith.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/jacob-s-grappling-technique|Jacob's Grappling Technique]]
- **Wimber, Arder, and the conversion of Yoni Adonyi and Alina's family** _(personal)_ — A chain of revival-born love (Vineyard movement to Bill Arder to Yoni Adonyi) that led to the preacher's wife Alina becoming a Christian, showing revival changes lives quietly across the world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/revival|Revival]]
- **Wine as purified water in Jesus's day** _(historical)_ — Ancient winemaking made bad water safe, underscoring that Jesus was no teetotaler and genuinely came to celebrate.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/jesus-likes-to-party|Jesus Likes to Party]]
- **Wings of eagles (Isaiah 40)** _(biblical)_ — The closing benediction borrows Isaiah's imagery of mounting up on eagles' wings to bless renewal in worship.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/trouble-in-river-city|Trouble in River City]]
- **Wipe the dust off your shoes** _(biblical)_ — Jesus telling disciples to move on shows he was not a maximalist, freeing us to serve purposefully rather than exhaustively.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-birth-of-jesus|The Birth of Jesus]]
- **Wise men's gift of incense** _(biblical)_ — The magi bringing incense to the infant Jesus grounds the use of incense in worship from the very beginning.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/what-we-remember|What We Remember]]
- **Wool combed thousands of times** _(other)_ — The Greek image for Christ's brilliance, like wool combed thousands of times, conveying the dazzling whiteness of the vision.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/the-transfiguration|The Transfiguration]]
- **Workplace Mindfulness for Productivity** _(current-events)_ — Companies push meditation to make workers more efficient, showing how prayer gets distorted into something centered on ourselves rather than God.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2023/our-father-who-art-in-heaven-hallowed-be-thy-name|Our Father, Who Art in Heaven, Hallowed Be Thy Name]]
- **Works seasoned with the salt of God's love** _(other)_ — Acts of charity become a palatable offering to God when seasoned with the salt of his love.  → Prayer and Converse with God Is a Supreme Good
- **World fire myths** _(literary)_ — Prometheus, Maui, Azazel in Enoch, and Loki all bringing/stealing fire, showing humanity's universal fascination with fire as divine.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/light-of-the-world|Light of the World]]
- **World War I and the cycle of national humiliation** _(historical)_ — France, Russia, and Austria each entering 1914 with something to prove demonstrates how the ancient warmongering instinct persisted into the modern world.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/heavenly-battles|Heavenly Battles]]
- **Worship is not a weekly TED talk** _(pop-culture)_ — Worship is not an entertaining TED talk with music; the pastor cannot drive the church alone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/the-driver|The Driver]]
- **Worship the series of truths, not Jesus (teacher's saying)** _(personal)_ — A seminary teacher's warning that clinging to proofs of the resurrection makes an idol of the proofs rather than Jesus.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/how-we-are-saved|How We Are Saved]]
- **Writing Children's Bible Songs** _(personal)_ — The preacher's five years of writing Bible-based children's songs for chapel models sharing good news plainly for all ages.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
- **Writing preconceptions and dissolving them in water** _(personal)_ — The congregation writing preconceptions of Revelation and of God's limits, then dissolving the papers in water stations, enacts God's freedom from self-imposed limits.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/alpha-and-omega|Alpha and Omega]]
- **Wrong Mountain Hike near Mt. Wilson** _(personal)_ — Hiking alone before dawn with a headlamp, the pastor climbed halfway up the wrong mountain and had to shimmy along an exposed ridge, illustrating the folly of going it alone.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/the-way-walking-together|The Way: Walking Together]]
- **WWII fire bombings of Dresden and Tokyo** _(historical)_ — Atrocities by Nazis, Japanese, British, and Americans alike illustrate that no nation stands outside original sin  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-holy-innocents-and-the-problem-of-evil|The Holy Innocents and the Problem of Evil]]
- **Year Compass Booklet** _(other)_ — A free reflection booklet for reviewing the past year and planning the next, used as a tool for spiritual self-location ('if we don't know where we are, it is hard to know where we are going').  → Notes from the Pastor: Resetting Yourself with God in the New Year
- **Year-old bread in 18th-century France** _(historical)_ — Pre-modern peasants baked bread once a year and ate rock-hard loaves to survive, showing how radically the human diet has changed.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-body-2|Reset Your Body]]
- **Yeast leavening three measures of flour** _(biblical)_ — Jesus's parable of hidden, pervasive growth of the kingdom of God.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/the-gift-of-the-trinity|The Gift of the Trinity]]
- **Yoda's 'do or do not, there is no try'** _(pop-culture)_ — Yoda's line to Luke contrasts the third servant who merely tried with the first two who did.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/do-or-do-not|Do Or Do Not]]
- **Yoni, father-in-law from communist Romania** _(personal)_ — His mandatory army service made him hate camping, showing different people have good reasons for their aversions.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/don-t-panic|Don't Panic]]
- **You and Jesus are BFFs** _(personal)_ — The complacent Model Two believer who assumes they're on the good team and need do nothing different.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/upside-down|Upside Down]]
- **You can't be half pregnant** _(other)_ — Just as no one can be partly pregnant, you can't be 'kinda saved' or 'kinda loved' by God; salvation claims all of you.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/show-us-the-father|Show Us the Father]]
- **You can't sell a fast** _(current-events)_ — There are no Lenten chocolates or Lententines because Lent's call to give up things resists consumer marketing.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/ash-wednesday|Ash Wednesday]]
- **You give off an aroma** _(other)_ — Riffing on Paul's image that believers spread the aroma of Christ (not just post-workout sweat) to show we carry Christ's identity.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/advent-don-t-be-deceived|Advent: Don't Be Deceived]]
- **Youth answering the sermon questions** _(personal)_ — The pastor hands the sermon over to the congregation's youth, who answer prepared questions, modeling hope by listening to and honoring those already called by God.  → [[covenant-umc/2026/the-joy-of-the-lord-is-our-strength|The Joy of the Lord is Our Strength]]
- **Youth football team that never won** _(personal)_ — Kids stayed joyful losing until they got competitive and started crying over losses, showing that proximity to success is what makes defeat painful.  → Peter Wants the Warmth Without the Weight
- **Zacchaeus and the children's song** _(biblical)_ — The repentant tax collector who pledges restitution and joy in response to Jesus' grace, modeled (via the Sunday-school song) as the opposite of the rich young ruler.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/sacred-story-aligning-our-lives-with-who-we-claim-to-be|Sacred Story: Aligning Our Lives with Who We Claim to Be]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/our-sacred-story|Our Sacred Story]]
- **Zacchaeus's pledge** _(biblical)_ — Zacchaeus changing his story by giving up control and self-centeredness models finding a purpose worth living for.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story-2|How to Live Our Story]], [[bee-creek-umc/2020/how-to-live-our-story|How to Live Our Story]]
- **Zach Greinke Pulled in Game 7** _(personal)_ — The pastor's frustration at watching the manager pull a sharp Zach Greinke at the 2019 World Series, only for relievers to give up the lead, sets up the sermon's grudging aside about rest and readiness.  → [[covenant-umc/2023/come-to-me-and-find-rest|Come to Me and Find Rest]]
- **Zao and the modern zoo** _(other)_ — Notes zao shares a root with our word 'zoo'—where living things are—to illustrate it just means biological aliveness like a monkey or mouse.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/fragments|Fragments]]
- **Zechariah struck mute fails the test** _(biblical)_ — A lifelong holy man can't handle the angel and is struck mute, while young Mary says yes—contrasting faithful response.  → [[covenant-umc/2024/magnify|Magnify]]
- **Zero-sum giving and sacrifice** _(other)_ — Giving costs a meal out or a vacation day, making generosity an act of faith since our gifts are zero-sum but God's are not.  → [[covenant-umc/2025/praise|Praise]]
- **Zeus without lightning bolts** _(literary)_ — The mistaken image of God as a powerful but distant Zeus illustrates the false projection of a far-off deity onto the God of the Bible.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2022/i-believe-in-god-the-father-almighty-maker-of-heaven-and-earth|I Believe in God the Father Almighty, Maker of Heaven and Earth]]
- **Zodiac signs of the preacher's family** _(personal)_ — The preacher noting he and his children are Libra, Scorpio, Virgo opens the topic of astrology's hold on us.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/the-star|The Star]]
- **Zombie movies / heroism in collapse** _(pop-culture)_ — The fantasy that if everything collapses I could finally be a hero, exposing how we all picture ourselves as heroes not villains.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2020/antichrist-becoming-the-villain|Antichrist: Becoming the Villain]]
- **Zoom interview manners** _(current-events)_ — Pandemic-era etiquette about not being a mouth-breather illustrates how breathing matters in close conversation.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/reset-your-soul|Reset Your Soul]]
- **Zuckerberg Weighting 'Congratulations'** _(current-events)_ — Zuckerberg personally tweaked the algorithm to boost 'congratulations' posts, showing how news is engineered for engagement.  → [[bee-creek-umc/2021/tell-everyone-that-jesus-is-alive|Tell Everyone That Jesus is Alive]]
